# Constitution of Zimbabwe (2013) — Tonga translation

This is a translation of the original 2013 text of theConstitution of Zimbabwe. It does not include amendments made by Acts of Parliament in 2017 and 2021; the validity of those Acts is still the subject of litigation in the Constitutional Court.

KKATA LYAMULAWU WAZIMBABWE

ZIMBABWE

KKATA LYAMULAWU WAZIMBABWE Kkata lyaMulawu waZimbabwe lyakasimbwa mbuuli Mpampikwa yaKkata lyaMulawu waZimbabwe Mbambululo (No. 20) Mulawu, 2013 (Mulawu 1 wa2013). Lyakabambululwa aMilawu iitobela— Mweelwe amwaaka

Mutwe wamakani

Buzuba Bwakutalika

10 wa2017

Kkata lyaMulawu waZimbabwe (No.1) Mulawu, 2017

7.9.17

2 wa2021

Kkata lyaMulawu waZimbabwe (No.2) Mulawu, 2021

7.5.21

## Section 345

Kuteendelana kwazyaambwa aamabala muKkata lyaMulawu. MPAMPIKWA NTAANZI: Ndembela yaChisi, Lwiimbo lwaChisi, Pande lyaChisi antoomwe aChidindo chaChisi.

MPAMPIKWAYABILI: Kutyolwa kwazyeelelelo muchiindi champyopyongano. MPAMPIKWA YATATU: Zikonke aNzumino. MPAMPIKWA YANE: Nzibeelede Kubaazyo kuti Bantu Balembesye Kuvoota. MPAMPIKWA YAMUSANU: Zitobelezegwa mukuchita maBills azimwi zyaambo muPalyamenti. MPAMPIKWA YAMUSANU AKAMWI: Kutalika kubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli aKutambikizyania Nguzu kwaF ulumende. NTALISYO Swebo tubantu bamuZimbabwe, Katukamantanisigwa, mukusiyana kwesu , achiyandisyo chakwaangunuka, bululami akweelana kabiyo akukaka nkutwakaka budyaamizi, nsaluulakkanda antoomwe azyoonse nzila zyabudyaamizi, Katulumbayizya akukankayizya basichaamba babaalumi bakapedekezya buumi bwabo muchiindi chankondo zyakulilela zyoonse,

abanakazi

Katulemeka bensiku bakabelekela nyika yesu changuzu kuti isumpuke kusika mpiibede sunu, Katubona bubotu bwakusimatizya bulelo bwajandabanji, bulelo bubalangala kabuzikitide kabiyo bulemeka mulawu, Katulipeda kabiyo kukusumpula akukwabilila zyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu zyaampandamunsaka, Katuliyeezya buvule bwabuvubi bwazilengwaleza zyesu, Katusekelela kupona kwatunsiyansiya twesu azimwi zyabasikale Katulipeda kuzunda boonse buyumuyumu azimwi zijalila lusumpuko lwesu, Katukomezya kwaangunuka, kweelana, luumuno, bululami, kulekelelana, kusumpuka akuyandisya nyika yesu katuyanduula kusumpuka kwanyika yesu mulukamantano, Katuliyeezya bupati kampatila bwaSikabumba Leza, mumaanza aakwe lyajuunza lyesu mulibede, Katulipeda kwiinda muli zeezyo zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli kuti tuyake nyika yesu ikamantene, yabululami iyakidwe aajulu kwandemeko zyabulelo bwajandabanji, Kabiyo, katuyanduula lugwasyo luzwa kuli Sikabumba Leza, tulaano twabumba Kkata lyaMulawu eeli katulipeda lwesu kuli ndilyo Kkata lyaMulawu wanyika yesu iiyandwa. CHAANDAANO 1 BWAALO BWAMILAWU 1

IRipabbuliki Nyika yaZimbabwe nimbulumbumbe bwajandabanji kabiyo yakalilela.

yachisi,

chilaabweendelezi

Bupati-kampatila bwaKkata lyaMulawu

(1) Kkata lyaMulawu eeli ngumulawu mupati-kampatila nkaako toonse tulawulawu, inchito zyabantu antoomwe aziyanza ziteendelani azeezyo zilembedwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, zilaanze kwamulawu nkabela tazikwe manguzu.

(2) Zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli zyeelede kuchitwa tweelede twazichita toonse tubantu atubunga kujatikizya fulumende antoomwe azibeela zyakwe zisiyenesiyene zibikkikizya basilutwe, bapanga milawu a Babetesi atumwi tutabitabi twafulumende kuzibeela zyoonse zyanyika kweelede zyazuzikizya mulawu ooyu mbuuli mbuwamba. 3

Bwaalo Bwandemeko

(1) Nyika yaZimbabwe yakabumbwa kwiinda mukulemeka ndemeko zitobela —

(a) bupati-kampatila bwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(b) kulemekwa akutobelezegwa kwamilawu yaNyika;

(c) zyeelelo zyabantu zyaampandamunsaka antoomwe angwangunuko zyabo;

(d) kusiyana kwatunsiyansiya twabantu baNyika yaZimbabwe antoomwe abuvule bwanzila nzyakombwa aazyo Leza amakkalilo abantu;

(e) kulemeka bulemu abuntususu bwawumwi awumwi muntu;

(f) kulemeka kweelana kwabantu boonse;

(g) kweelanisya myeenya yabaalumi abanakazi mubukkale bwabuumi.

(h) njendelezyo yanyika atubunga mbotu, kabiyo

(i) akulemekwa kwankondo yakulilela.

(2) Ndemeko zyachisi anjendelezyo yamasena izikitide, ikamantanisya tubunga ambunga zyafulumende kumasena woonse antoomwe azibeela zisiyene-siyene kubikkikizya —

(a) lulelo lwanyika lwajandabanji lusungwaazya kubumbwa kwatubunga twiingi twakweendelezya nyika.

(b) mabambe ansaluuzyo zyabazulwidi ayeeme a —

(i) antoomwe akweelana kwa nsaluulo/kwamavooti aabantu boonse;

(ii) kweendelezya zyamavooti muchiindi cheelede munzila iwangunukide kabiyo kiibalangala; a

(iii) kwiimikililwa kuzwide kwabantu bavoota.

(c) kutambikizyana zyuuno zyabuzulwidi kuzwa nizyachitwa nsaluuzyo zyanjizya bazulwidi muzyuuno;

(d) kulemeka zyeelelo zyatubunga toonse twanjendelezyo yanyika;

(e) kulemeka kusiyana kwamanguzu aapedwe zibeela zyafulumende zisiyenesiyene;

(f) kulemeka bulumbu bwaZimbabwe oobo bupa nguzu zyakweendelezya nyika eeyi;

(g) kubeleka milimu munzila ilaantanganana, yabululami, ibalangala antoomwe akuswiilizya makanze aabantu mbibazulwida mumibeleko isiyene-siyene.

(h) kusungwaazya kukamantana kwanyika, luumuno akuzikitila kwanyika kwiinda mukusumpula milaka isiyenesiyene, tunsiyansiya antoomwe abukkale busiyenesiyene bujanika munyika;

(i) kulemekwa kwazyeelelo zya —

(i) tubunga tusiyenesiyene twabantu bamisyobo-misyobo, tunsiyansiya tusiyenesiyene, milaka isiyenesiyene antoomwe anzila zyakukomba Leza zisiyenesiyene;

(ii) bantu balemene munzila zisiyenesiyene;

(iii) banakazi, bantu bakachembaala, bakubusi abana;

(iv) bakalwana nkondo yakulilela;

(j) kwaabana bweelene kwazilengwa-leza zyamuchisi kubikkikizya nyika yakulima;

(k) kulemeka zyeelelo zipati; kabiyo

(l) kupa nguzu kumitabi yafulumende ili muzilikiti amumabbooma. 4

Ndembela Yachisi, Lwiimbo Lwachisi, Chidindo Chachisi antoomwe aPande Lyachisi Chisi chaZimbabwe chijisi Ndembela, Lwiimbo lwaChisi, Pande lyaChisi antoomwe aChidindo chaChisi zyoonse eezi zili pandulwidwe muMpampikwa Ntaanzi.


Zibeela zyafulumende kuzwa kujulu kuloosika aansi Zibeela zisiyenesiyene zyafulumende waZimbabwe kuzwa kujulu kuloosika aansi zyiimvwi boobu —

(a) fulumende waChisi choonse mbuchizulwa;

(b) fulumende wachilikiti amaKkansili amadolopo mapati;

(c) fulumende wamaKkansili maniini awaMabbooma —

(i) fulumende weendelezya dolopo (kkansili yamudolopo) kabona zyabukkale bwabantu mudolopo eelyo; kabiyo

(ii) fulumende weendelezya bbooma kabona bukkale bwabantu bamumyuunzi.


Milaka

(1) Milaka iitobela; Chewa, Chibarwe, English, Kalanga, Koisan, Nambya, Ndau, Ndebele, Shangani,Shona, Sign Language, Sotho, Tonga,Tswana,Venda aXhosa, njiimilaka izibikene zili mumulawu waZimbabwe.

(2) Mulawu wakapangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kusiyanisya milaka eeyi munzila zyobile; imwi ibe milaka itanoobelesegwe mumalembe ali mumulawu kakuli imwi milaka kiili milaka biyo izibikene kilemekwa zili mumulawu.

(3) Fulumende antoomwe amitabi yakwe yoonse atubunga twakwe toonse kuzibeela zyazilikiti akumabbooma woonse weelede—

(a) kubona kuti yoonse milaka iizibikene zili mumulawu ibelesegwa akujatwa bweelene;

(b) kubona kuti bantu bamubusena bumwi abumwi babelesya mulaka ngubayanda mukwaambuzyania antoomwe amilumbe iiza kuli mbabo.

(4) Fulumende weelede kusungwaazya akusumpula kubelesegwa kwamilaka yoonse ibelesegwa munyika yaZimbabwe, kubikkikizya Mulaka Ubelesya Zizo, mpawo weelede kubona kuti milaka yoonse ilookusumpulwa.

Kusungwaazya luundu kuti luzibe zili muKkata lyaMulawu Fulumende weelede kusungwaazya luundu kuti luzibe zili muKkata lyaMulawu, kapati kwiinda—

(a) mukutolekela Kkata lyaMulawu eeli mumilaka isiyenesiyene yoonse izibikene zili mumulawu kabiyo malembe aayo amwaywa mubantu kuti bajane luzibo aatala amulawu ooyu mumasena woonse;

(b) kubona kuti zili muKkata lyaMulawu eeli zilokuyiisigwa muzikolo, kabiyo kazilo kubikkwa muzyiiyo zyabasilumamba, bakapokola tuswiilizya bavwiya antoomwe ababelesi baluundu bajanika muzibeela zyoonse zyafulumende; kabiyo; [Fuka lyabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

(c) kusungwaazya bantu boonse, tubunga toonse kuti tumwaye milumbe munyika yoonse aatala azili muKkata lyaMulawu eeli. CHAANDAANO 2 MPALAMO ZYACHISI 8

Mpalamo zizulwida fulumende antoomwe atoonse tubunga-bunga amitabi yafulumende

(1) Mpalamo ziyalidwe muChaandaano eechi zizulwida fulumende, tubunga twafulumende antoomwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse tujanika muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse, mukubumba akweendelezya milawu igwasizya, kusumpula akusungwaazya kuyakwa kwachisi chiimvwi mubululami, chaangunukide kabiyo kachilaalulelo lwajandabanji, bantu mubaangunukide kababotelwa akupona buumi buzwide bujisi nzibayanda.

(2) Mukuyanda kumvwisisisya mikuli yafulumende yakugwasizya luundu yaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli ayimwi milawu, kweelede twalangisisya mpalamo ziyalidwe muChaandaano eechi. 9

Buzuluzi buzikitide

(1) Fulumende weelede kubumba antoomwe akweendelezya milawu isungwaazya bunkutwe, bubalangale mumabelekelo amilimu yaluundu, kusumpula bwime abuntususu bwamuntu antoomwe akusungwaazya kusyomeka kumabelesezyo aamali mumitabi yafulumende, mutubunga twafulumende tujanika muzibeela zyoonse zyanyika kapa—

(a) bantu banjizigwa mumilimu yafulumende kweelede kabajisi luzibo aatala amilimu njibapegwa peepe chinjizyenjizye.

(b) ntaamo kweelede zyatolwa kuti kukwabililwe, kubbongononwe kabiyo kugwisigwe yoonse misyobo yabuboli antoomwe akubelesya bubi nguzu kwabazuluzi bajisi zyuuno zyakweendelezya nyika abaabo babelekela fulumende.

(2) Fulumende weelede kubona kuti tubunga twakwe, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe muzibeela zyoonse zyanyika, kapati maKKomishini atumwi tubunga twakaanzwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, tweelede twapegwa zibelesyo nkukuti tukonzye kubeleka milimu yato munzila iiluleme, ibalangala kilaabusichaamba.

Lukamantano lwachisi, Luumuno akunzinzama

Fulumende antoomwe amuntu woonse, kubikkikizya tubunga twanzidwe kwiinda mumulawu, yoonse mitabi yafulumende muzibeela zyoonse zyanyika, zyeelede kusungwaazya lukamantano, luumuno akulyiiba kwachisi. 11

Kusumpula zyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka antoomwe angwangunuko

Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo zyoonse zyakukwabilila zyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka antoomwe angwangunuko zyabantu mbuuli mbuziyalidwe muChaandaano 4, kabiyo asungwaazye kuzuzikizigwa kwazyeelelo eezi mbuuli mbukweelede. 12

Bulidilano bwazisi

(1) Bulidilano bwanyika yaZimbabwe azimwi nyika kweelede kabuzulwidwa andemeko zitobela —

(a) kusumpula akukwabililwa kwazintu antoomwe amizeezo igwasya nyika yaZimbabwe;

(b) kulemekwa kwamilawu iswaanizya akujatikizya manyika aandeene;

(c) kukkala muluumuno azimwi zisi;

(d) kuzyotololana akumana luzyokola akati kamanyika/kazisi munzila yaluumuno.

(2) Fulumende weelede kusungwaazya kusumpulwa kwabuntususu atunsiyansiya antoomwe akumvwanana kwanyika zyamuAfrica. Uleelede kabiyo kubelekela aamwi azimwi nyika antoomwe atumwi tubunga-bunga twamuchilawu twiimvwilidi zyaluumuno abukkale bubotu muchilawu, muchisi chaAfrica choonse mbuchizulwa akubona zyakusumpuka kwabantu mbubazulwa. 13

Lusumpuko lwachisi

(1) Fulumende antoomwe amitabi yakwe atubunga twakwe muzibeela zyoonse zyachisi weelede wasungwaazya lusumpuko lweelene koonse, kapati weelede kubweza ntaamo kuchita zitobela —

(a) kusungwaazya bantu kuti balipangile mizeezo yalusumpuko lwabo beni antoomwe akubelesya zibelesyo azilengwa-leza zili muluwa lwabo;

(b) kusungwaazya kusumpuka kwazyabulimi, zyamakwebo, zyamandastili, zyabusyaabusongo(tekinoloji), azyasayensi;

(c) kusungwaazya kuzwidilila kwamandastili amakwebo kakuli ninzila yakubusilizya bana bachisi chaZimbabwe; kabiyo

(d) kweeta lusumpuko lweelene mumasena aandeene muchisi, kapati kusumpulwa kwamumyuunzi amumadolopo munzila yeelene.

(2) Ntaamo amabambe alusumpuko zyaambidwe muchibeela eechi kubota zyeendelezegwa antoomwe abantu bamuluwa nkukuti bagwasizye mukweeta lusumpuko lwabo beni.

(3) Ntaamo zyakweeta lusumpuko zyaambidwe muchibeela eechi kweelede kazisumpula cheelelo chabantu, kapati bamakayintu, kuti bapegwe myeenya yeelene abaalumi muli zyalusumpuko.

(4) Fulumende weelede wabona kuti bantu bamuluundu balookujana mpindu kuzwa muzilengwa-Leza zijanika mubusena bwabo.

Kusumpulwa kwabantu antoomwe akupangwa kwamilimu

(1) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede watola ntaamo zyakusumpula bantu, kwiinda munzila zikonzeka mubusena oobo, munzila ibalangala, ilaantanganana, yabululami kabiyo igwasizya akuchebuka baabo bakadyaamikizidwe mumazuba aayinda munyika yaZimbabwe.

(2) Choonse chiindi, Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede wabona kuti zyoonse zikonzya kuchitwa zyachitwa kuti bana Zimbabwe bajane milimu yakubeleka, kapati bamakayintu abakubusi. 15

Kubawo kwachakulya Fulumende weelede —

(a) kusungwaazya bantu kuti balime akubamba chakulya chiingi;

(b) kupanga zimumbwa zyanyika zyakubambila chakulya chiingi chikonzya kusanina luundu lwanyika; kabiyo

(c) kusungwaazya akusumpula malimino aachakulya chilaamunse kwiinda mukuyiisya bantu azimwi nzila zyeelede.


Tunsiyansiya

(1) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede wasumpula akulondola/kubamba tunsiyansiya tuyaka buntususu, bukkale bubotu bwabantu akusumpula kweelene kwabantu boonse muchisi.

(2) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyachisi zyoonse antoomwe abana Zimbabwe boonse kweelede basoleeka kubamba akukwabilila nzyakatupa Leza.

(3) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede twatola ntaamo zyakubona kuti buzuluzi bwabulongo muluundu bwalemekwa. 17

Kupana myeenya yeelene yabaalumi abanakazi

(1) Fulumende weelede kusumpula kweelana kwabaalumi abanakazi mumyeenya yazyabukkale munyika yaZimbabwe kapati —

(a) fulumende weelede wasumpula bamakayintu kuti banjile akubeleka muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse bweelene abaalumi;

(b) fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo zyoonse, kubbikkikizya kupanga milawu, yeelede kubona kuti —

(i) banakazi abaalumi balajanika muli fulumende, mumitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse; kabiyo

(ii) banakazi mbiingi kusika muchisela chabantu bajanika mutubunga twamaKkomishini antoomwe atumwi tubunga twafulumende twaanzidwe kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli naakuti Mulawu wapangwa aPalyamenti

(c) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede watola ntaamo zyoonse kubona kuti banakazi bajana zibelesyo ziyandikana, kubikkikizya nyika yakulima, bweelene abaalumi.

(2) Fulumende weelede watola ntaamo mbotu zyakuzyotolola musiyano wakujana myeenya kwabaalumi abanakazi zyakali kupangwa aatunsiyansiya antoomwe amilawu yansiku yabudyaamizi. 18

Kwiimikililwa kweelene kwazilawu munyika

(1) Fulumende weelede kusungwaazya kwimikililwa kweelene kwazilawu zyachisi zyaZimbabwe zyoonse mutubunga twakwe toonse atutabitabi twakwe toonse tujanika muzibeela zyachisi zyoonse kuzwa kujulu kuyoosika aansi.

(2) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede zyatola ntaamo zyakubona kuti boonse bantu bakkala muluwa bajana zibelesyo zyakweendelezya lusumpuko muluundu lwabo. 19

Bana

(1) Fulumende weelede kupanga milawu atulawu-lawu kubona kuti kunkani zyabana, eezyo ziyandikana kuti bana bapone buumi buntuloko zyalemekwa akuchitwa.

(2) Fulumende weelede kupanga milawu atulawu-lawu, ikonzya kuzuzikizigwa kakulangwalangwa zibelesyo ziliwo, kuti bana—

(a) bakomena kababambwa kabotu abazyali mumpuli naakuti kubambwa kabotu nibagwisigwa mumpuli zyabo;

(b) bajana chiyusilo achakulya chilomene azigwasizya bukkale bwamwana mbuuli zyalwiiyo, kusilikwa azimwi zilangene azyabukkale;

(c) bakwabililwa kuzwa kukupenzegwa, kujatwa bubi naakuti kuli bumwi bubi bukonzya kupambanisya bukkale bwamwana); kabiyo

(d) bakonzya kujana lwiiyo antoomwe akwiiya mulimu wamaboko /ikkoosi.

(3) Fulumende weelede kupanga milawu akutola zimwi ntaamo —

(a) kukwabilila bana kuzwa kukubelesegwa mbuuli bazike mumilimu; kabiyo

(b) kubona kuti bana tabazumizigwi pe kubeleka naakuti kuchita milimu —

(i) iteendelani pe aminyaka yabana; naakuti

(ii) ibikka bana mubuyumuyumu, kabatachijani zintu zili mbuuli lwiiyo, kukkalinkana muboongo amumuuya, antoomwe akukomena kwabo kwamubili. 20

Bakubusi

(1) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede watola ntaamo, kubikkikizya mabambe akusumpula bakadyaamikizidwe nsiku, zyeelede kubona kuti bakubusi, bana balaaminyaka ilaakati kakkumi (10) kuyoosika kuminyaka iili makumi otatwe alaamusanu yaminyaka (35)—

(a) bajana lwiiyo antoomwe amweenya wakuyiisigwa mulimu;

(b) bajana myeenya yakuswaanana, kwiimikililwa antoomwe akubamo muzibeela zyabuumi zyoonse mbuuli zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika, zyabukkale azyabuvubi;

(c) bapegwa myeenya yakubeleka ilabasumpula muli zyabuvubi;

milimu

ayimwi

myeenya

iinga

(d) bajana myeenya yakulisangalizya akujana myeenya yakusika kumasena aakulisangalizya; kabiyo

(e) bakwabililwa kuzwa kutunsiyansiya tukonzya kubanyonyoona, kubelesegwa buzike, azimwi nchito zikonzya kubanyongania buumi bwabo.

(2) Mulawu wapangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kudoma akwaamba mabambe aanyika anga alagwasizya kusumpula bakubusi.

(3) Ntaamo amabambe zyaambwa muchibeela (1) a (2) zyeelede kugwasizya bakubusi boonse kakutalangwi tubunga bakubusi nkubajanika twakweendelezegwa kwanyika.

Bantu bakachembaala

(1) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede watola ntaamo, kubikkikizya kupanga milawu, zyakubona kuti bantu bakachembaala balemekwa, bakwabililwa antoomwe akugwasigwa kuti bajatane abamwi mubuumi bwaluundu mubakkala.

(2) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse kweelede, kakulangwalangwa zibelesyo ziliwo—

(a) kusungwaazya bakachembaala bwaluundu mubajanika;

kuti

bajatane

abamwi

mubuumi

(b) kubajania zinga zilabagwasya mbuuli chakulya akubambwa kabotu kwabantu bakachembaala;

(c) kuchita mabambe agwasizya machembele abapa mweenya wakulyoolola kwiinda mukugwasizya mpubakonzya kumanguzu amuluzibo ndubalaalo antoomwe anzibayanda kuchita lwabo; kabiyo

(d) kusungwaazya tubungabunga tugwasizya kusumpula buumi abukkale bwabantu bakachembaala. 22

Bantu balemene

(1) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse kweelede walemeka zyeelelo zyabantu balemene mubili naakuti muboongo, kapati cheelelo chabo chakujatwa aabulemu alulemeko.

(2) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede, kakulangwalangwa zibelesyo ziliwo, wagwasizya bantu balemene mubili naakuti muboongo kuti bajane lugwasyo lubapa myeenya yakubelesya zipo nzibalaazyo akuchesya budyaamizi mbubaswaanana aabo mubuumi bwabo.

(3) Kapati, fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse kweelede—

(a) wapanga mabambe agwasizya bantu balemene mubili aboongo, kapati mabambe akuti balibeleke kakweendelana abulema mbubajisi antoomwe anzibalimvwa kabayanda kuchita lwabo beni;

(b) kulangisisya zintu ziyandwa abantu balemene munzila zyaandeene kuti zibe nzizyo zigwasizya mukuchita mabambe agwasya balemene;

(c) kusungwaazya misyobo yakwaambuuzyania iisiyene-siyene njibakonzya kubelesya kakweendelana abulema mbubajisi).

(d) kusungwaazya tubungabunga tugwasizya kusumpula buumi abukkale bwabantu balemene.

(4) Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo kubona kuti mayake azimwi zyakulisangalizya kwaluundu zyayakwa munzila njibakonzya kubelesya balemene abalabo kabataswaanani abuyumuyumu pe. 23

Bakalwana nkondo yakulilela

(1) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibela zyanyika zyoonse weelede kupa lulemeko kubantu bakalwana nkondo yakulilela, kakwaambwa kuti—

(a) aabo bakalwana munkondo yakulilela;

(b) aabo bakagwasizya basikulwana nkondo yakulilela; kabiyo

(c) aabo bakaangidwe mumajele kakulwanwa nkondo yakulilela.


kabaangilwa

zyanyika

muchiindi

(2) Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo, kubikkikizya kupanga milawu, igwasizya bukkale akusumpulwa kwabantu bakalwana nkondo yakulilela muli zyabuvubi. 24

Zyabubelesi akubelekelana

(1) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede wapanga milawu azimwi ntaamo, kakulangisigwa zibelesyo ziliwo, kuyanda kugwasizya woonse muntu kuti abeleke mubusena bwaangunukide kabelekela mpuli yakwe munzila yabulemu.

(2) Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede wabona kuti—

(a) kuli milimu yakubeleka;

(b) wagwisya zintu zikonzya kukataazya babelesi kuti batabeleki kabotu mumilimu yabo;

(c) wagwasizya bantu kwiinda muzyiiyo zyamaboko kubikkikizya bantu balemene;

(d) kweendelezya ntaamo zyakugwasizya mpuli kuchitila kuti bamakayintu bakonzye kujana chiindi chakubeleka kabotu kabaangunikide. 25

Kukwabililwa kwampuli

Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede wakwabilila akusungwaazya chiyanza champuli, kapati weelede kubona, kakulangisisigwa zibelesyo ziliwo, kuti kwatolwa ntaamo zyaku—

(a) bikka mabambe akugwasizya bamamama, mataata abamwi bajanika mumpuli bakonzya kubamba bana;

(b) kukasya kupenzyania kwabakwetene kwiinda mukulwana, kutukilana akwiimana zibelesyo azimwi nzibeelede kugwasyania mulukwatano. 26

Lukwatano Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo kubona kuti—

(a) taakwe lukwatano luchitwa basikukwatana kabatayandi kukwatana pe lwabo;

(b) bana tababelesegwi pe mukukwatizya naakuti kubbadala zikwelete akubbadala ngozi;

(c) kuli kweelana kwazyeelelo mulukwatano aakati kabasikukwatana muchiindi nibakwetene amuchiindi nibalekana;

(d) kuti bakwetene balekana, kwiinda mukufwa kwawumwi naakuti balekana luzutu, mabambe kweelede achitwa akukwabilila bana naakuti aabo bakakwetene.


Lwiiyo

(1) Fulumende weelede kutola zyoonse ntaamo zikonzeka zyakusumpula —

(a) bana kuti baangilwe kujana lwakumatangunino; kabiyo

lwiiyo

kabatabbadali

pe,

lwiiyo

(b) lwiiyo lwajulu antoomwe azyamaboko

(2) Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo kubona kuti bana bachisimbi bapegwa myeenya ikozyania abalombe mukujana lwiiyo muzibeela zyoonse zyalwiiyo.

Chiyusilo

Fulumende, mitabi yakwe atutabitabi twakwe toonse muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse weelede kutola ntaamo zyakupanga milawu azimwi nzila, kakulangwalangwa zibelesyo ziliwo, kubona kuti woonse muntu wajana chiyusilo chilomene. 29

Zyabulondosi

(1) Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo zyoonse kubona kuti bantu balookujana kusilikwa muzibbadela amumakkilinika munyika yoonse yaZimbabwe.

(2) Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo kubona kuti taakwe muntu ukakilwa kusilikwa kuti waswaanana abuyumuyumu buyanda kusilikwa chakufwambaana nikuba kulichili achili chibbadela naakuti kkilinika.

(3) Fulumedne weelede kutola zyoonse ntaamo, kakulangwalangwa zibelesyo ziliwo, kukuyiisya luundu aatala akukwabilwa kwakutabukila kwamalwazi. 30

Zyalugwasyo abukkale bwabantu

Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo, kakulangwalangwa zibelesyo ziliwo, kubona kuti aabo bayanda lugwasyo nibachembaala naakuti batachibeleki balujana lugwasyo oolo. 31

Lugwasyo lwakwiimikililwa munkuta asimulawu

Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo, kakulangwalangwa zibelesyo ziliwo, zyakugwasizya bantu kwiinda mukupa basimulawu banga baliiminina bafwaba munkuta zyamulawu. 32

Zyamisobaano akulisangalizya

Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo zyoonse zikonzeka kuti asungwaazye zyamaSpoti akulisangalizya kwabantu, kubikkikizya kupangwa kwamasena akuchitila maspoti akulisangalizya kwabantu boonse bayanda. 33

Kubambwa kwaluzibo lwansiku

Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo zyakubamba kabotu, kukwabilila akusungwaazya Luzibo lwaNsiku kubikkikizya luzibo lwatala aminsamu yakusilika yansiku antoomwe aminsamu yamulusaka ibelesegwa aabantu basiyenesiyene. 34

Kweetwa kwamilawu yamanyika aandeene mumilawu yanyika yaZimbabwe

Fulumende weelede kubona kuti yoonse milawu ijatikizya manyika aandeene yakasayinwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kayimvwilidi Zimbabwe yabikkikizigwa mumilawu yaZimbabwe.

CHAANDAANO 3 BWENICHII 35

Bwenichisi

(1) Bantu bajana bwenichisi kwiinda munzila eezi: kuzyalilwa munyika yaZimbabwe, kuzyalwa abazyali bajisi bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe (mubuzukululu), kujana bunanyika kwiinda mukulembesya kubupegwa.

(2) Boonse bantu bajisi bwenichisi beelede kujatwa bweelene akujana lugwasyo loonse lweelede, kabiyo balaamukuli wakuchita yoonse milimu yakusumpula nyika yabo.

(3) Boonse bajisi bwenichisi beelede kujana zyeelelo alugwasyo, ajulu kwazimwi zinga zilaambwa amilawu imwi—

(a) kujana lukwabililo kuzwa kuli fulumende waZimbabwe fumbwa nkubabe;

(b) kujana mapepa asiyenesiyene (mapasipoti awamwi);.

akweenzya

kumanyika

aandeene

(c) kujana mapepa akuzyalwa antoomwe awamwi mapepa akuzibikana kwamuntu munyika aajanwa kuli fulumende.

(4) Bajisi bwenichisi balaamukuli wakuchita milimu eeyi, ajulu kwayimwi njibanga balapegwa ayimwi milawu —

(a) kusyomeka akulipeda kunyika yaZimbabwe;

(b) kuswiilila Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, kulemeka nzilyaamba antoomwe akulemeka tubunga twakabumbwa kwiinda mumulawu ooyu;

(c) kulemeka ndembela yanyikai antoomwe alwiimbo lwanyika; kabiyo

(d) kukwabilila kulilela kwanyika yaZimbabwe munzila nzibakonzya zyoonse. 36

Bwenichisi mukuzyalwa

(1) Bantu bajana bwenichisi kwiinda mukuzyalilwa munyika yaZimbabwe, antoomwe akuti chiindi nibakazyalwa —

(a) umwi wabazyali (baama naakuti ndeende) wakajisi bwenichis; naakuti;

(b) umwi wabamanyinakulu amasyanyinakulu wakajisi bwenichisi bwakuzyalilwa munyika yaZimbabwe naakuti bakabujana kwiinda mubuzukululu.

(2) Bantu bakazyalilwa aanze kwanyika yaZimbabwe bajana bwenichisi kwiinda mukuzyalwa kuti kakuli muchiindi chakuzyalwa kwabo umwi wabazyali wakajisi bwenichisi; kabiyo —

(a) wakakkede munyika yaZimbabwe;

(b) wakali kubelekela aanze kwanyika yaZimbabwe kabelekela fulumende waZimbabwe naakuti kabelekela mbungano ijanika mumanyika aandeene.

(3) Mwana wajanika muZimbabwe, ulaaminyaka ilaansi kwakkumi amusanu utazibikene bwenichisi bwakwe, ngawabwezwa kuba bwenichisi kwiinda mukuzyalwa. 37

Bwenichisi kwiinda mubuzukululu

Kwiinda muli zeezyo zilembedwe muchibeela 36(2), bantu bakazyalilwa aanze kwanyika yaZimbabwe bajana bwenichisi kwiinda mubuzukululu kuti kakuli muchiindi chakuzyalwa kwabo —

(a) umwi wabazyali babo naakuti bayinakulu asyayinakulu wakajisi bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe kwiinda mukuzyalwa naakuti bazyali bakalaabunanyika bwaZimbabwe; naakuti

(b) umwi wabazyali babo wakajisi bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe kwiinda mukulembesya, kabiyo kuzyalwa ooko kakulembesedwe muZimbawe mumulawu wakulembesya zyakuzyalwa.

Bwenichisi kwiinda mukulembesya

(1) Fumbwa muntu uteensi munaZimbabwe pesi kakwetene amunaZimbabwe kwaminyaka iyinda musanu, kabiyo muntu ooyo kazuzikizya zyoonse ziyandikana amulawu uleelede kupegwa bunanyika bwaZimbabwe, kuti walembela kuli fulumende kakumbila kupegwa bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe.

(2) Fumba muntu uteensi munaZimbabwe pesi ukkede munyika yaZimbabwe zili mumulawu kwaminyaka iyinda kkumi, kabiyo muntu ooyo kazuzikizya zyoonse ziyandikana amulawu uleelede kupegwa bwenichisi, kuti walembela kuli fulumende kakumbila kupegwa bwenichisi.

(3) Mwana uteensi munaZimbabwe, pesi watolwa kuti alelwe amunaZimbabwe ulacheelelo chakupegwa bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe kuti walembela kuli fulumende kakumbila kupegwa bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe. 39

Kunyangwa bwenichisi

(1) Bwenichisi kwiinda mukulembesya bukonzya kunyangwa kuti —

(a) muntu wakabupedwe wakabupegwa kwiinda munzila italuleme; naakuti

(b) muchiindi chankondo yakalwanwa anyika yaZimbabwe, muntu ooyo wakawuzya nyika yaZimbabwe, munzila zisiyenesiyene, kuli basinkondoma bakali kulwana anyika yaZimbabwe.

(2) Bwenichisi bwakajanwa kwiinda mukuzyalwa bukonzya kunyangwa kuti —

(a) muntu wakabupegwa kwiinda munzila italuleme; naakuti,

(b) kumuntu uwambidwe muchibeela 36(3), bunanyika bwakwe bwazibikana naakuti bazyali bakwe bazibikana kuti bakajisi bwenichisi bwayimbi nyika.

(3) Bwenichisi tabukonzyi kunyangwa kuti kakuli sikunyangwa taakwe bumbi bwenichisi mbwanga ulasyaala aabo nanyangwa bwenichisi. 40

Kutabunda kwabwenichisi kwiinda mulukwatano naakuti kulekana

Muntu takonzyi kusweekelwa bwenichisi nkaambo kakunjila mulukwatano naakuti kufwa kwalukwatano. 41

Bwenichisi aMbunga ilanga bantu bazwa kumanyika aambi kabayanda kuzookkala muchisi

Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zyakubumbwa kwa Mbunga zilanga beni chisi kuli aamwi manyika zyazya bwenichisi ijisi sichuuno antoomwe abamwi bantu babili basalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika, bbodi eeyi kilaamulimu wa :—

(a) kupa akunyanga bwenichisi bujanwa kwiinda mukulembesya;

(b) kuzumizya bantu, batajisi bwenichisi, kuti bakkale akubeleka munyika yaZimbabwe antoomwe akubapa malayilile ngibeelede kutobelezya mumakkalilo aabo beenzu munyika yaZimbabwe; kabiyo

(c) kuchita imwi milimu njibatanooli bapegwa aMulawu ubumbwa aPalyamenti. 42

Manguzu aPalyamenti aatala azyabwenichisi

Mulawu ubumbwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupandulula zyiingi, pesi kawuyendelana azili muChaandaano eechi, aatala —

(a) azyeelede kutobelezegwa kuti muntu ajane bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe kwiinda mukulembesya;

(b) akuboozya bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe amuntu wakabupedwe kwiinda mukulembesya;

(c) azyeelede kutobelezegwa mukunyanga muntu bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe mbwakali wapegwa kwiinda mukulembesya;

(d) kupegwa bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe mbwakali wanyangwa muntu;

(e) akukasigwa kwakuba abwenichisi bwamanyika obile kubantu balaabwenichisi bwakajanwa kwiinda mubuzukululu amukulembesya; kabiyo

(f) akubona kuti zyaambidwe muChaandaano eechi zilookubeleka. 43

Kweendelela nembo abwenichisi antoomwe akuboozegwa kwabwenichisi bwakali bwanyangwa

(1) Woonse muntu wakajisi bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe kuzwa buunsi nuwakadindwa mulawu ooyu, ulaya nembo kali munaZimbabwe.

(2) Woonse muntu wakazyalilwa munyika ya Zimbabwe kuutanadindwa mulawu ooyu wakaba munaZimbabwe kwiinda mukuzyalwa kuti kakuli—

(a) umwi wabazyali naakuti bobile bazyali bakajisi bwenichisi bwanyika ili muMbunga yaSouthern African Development Community yakabumbwa kwiinda muchizuminano chakasayinwa ku Windhoek munyika yaNamibia mubuunsi bwa 17 Itwi, 1992; naakuti

(b) wakakkede kali munyika yaZimbabwe kale buunsi nuwakadindwa mulawu ooyu.

CHAANDAANO 4 MOOMPOLOLO WAZYEELELO

CHIBAKA 1 MABELESEZYO ABUPANDULUZI BWACHAANDAANO 4 44

Mukuli wakulemeka zyeelelo zyabantu zyaampandamunsaka angwangunuko

Fulumende amuntu woonse, kubikkilizya tubunga tuli mumulawu, antoomwe amitabi yafulumende iijanika muzibeela zyoonse zyanyika kweelede kulemeka, kukwabilila, kusungwaazya akuzuzikizya zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaambidwe muchaandaano eechi. 45

Mvwisisisyo yaChaandaano 4

(1) Chaandaano eechi chaanga fulumende, bazuluzi bafulumende, basikubumba milawu yanyika, babetesi antoomwe azibeela zyafulumende zijanika muzibaka zisiyenesiyene zyanyika, kuti boonse bantu aaba balemeke zyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka.

(2) Chaandaano eechi chisungwaazya antoomwe akubangila muntu woonse mubuntu bwakwe antoomwe atubunga tuli mumulawu kuti kulemekwe akuzuzikizigwa zyeelelo eezi munzila njizyeelede kulemekwa kakulangwa kuti nziizili zyeelelo angwangunuko umwi awumwi nzyeelede kuzuzikizya amilimu njeelede kuchita mukuzuzikizya zyeelelo eezi.

(3) Tubunga toonse tuli mumulawu antoomwe abantu beelede kujana zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaambidwe muchaandaano eechi munzila njizyeelede kuzuzikizigwa akuchitwa kubantu boonse antoomwe atubunga tuli mumulawu.

Imbelesezyo abupanduluzi bwaChaandaano 4

(1) Kuti nikupandululwa Chaandaano eechi, nkuta, imbeta abamwi babetesi beelede—

(a) kweezya kuzuzikizya akulemeka zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaambidwe muChaandaano eechi;

(b) kusungwaazya bwaalo bwabukkale bubumba bbambabbwe lyaluundu lweendelezegwa munzila yajandabanji, iilemeka lulelo lubalangala, bululami, bulemu bwamuntu, kweelanisigwa kwabantu akwaangunuka kwabo, kapati bwaalo bwazyandemeko azilemekwa mbuuli mbukupandulwidwe muChibeela 3 chaKkata lyaMulawu.

(c) kulemeka milawu yakazuminanwa aamanyika woonse alaansi aano kiili milawu eeyo yakatambulwa afulumende waZimbabwe;

(d) kulemeka zibeela zyoonse zyaKkata lyaMulawu wanyika (national constitution) kapati bwaalo bwamulawu antoomwe ampalamo zijanika muChaandaano 2 chaKkata lyaMulawu wanyika.

(e) kulemeka milawu yaamwi manyika iiyendelana kabiyo kiinjililana amilawu yesu;

eezi zyoonse zichitwa aajulu kwakulangisisya zintu zinji zyeelede kuchitwa mukubelesya akumvwisisisya Kkata lyaMulawu wanyika.

(2) Kuti nikubelesegwa umwi mulawu, anikupangwa milawu yalunji, milawu yabukkale bwabantu, inkuta zyoonse, tubunga tubeteka, abamwi babetesi beelede kusungwaazya kabiyo balulamikwe aamuuya ampalamo zyaChaandaano eechi. 47

Chaandaano 4 tachikasyi pe kuzuzikizigwa akubawo kwazimwi zyeelelo

Chaandaano eechi tachikasyi kuzuzikizigwa akulemekwa kwazimwi zyeelelo pe angwangunuko zyeelede kuzuzikizigwa akulemekwa kwiinda muliimwi milawu iiliwo, fumbwa milawu eeyo kayiyendelana aziyandwa aKkata lyaMulawu wanyika.

CHIBAKA 2 ZYEELELO ZYABANTU ZYAAMPANDAMUNSAKA ANGWANGUNUKO 48

Cheelelo chakupona

(1) Muntu woonse ulacheelelo chakupona.

(2) Mulawu ulaako kuzumizya kupa chisubulo chalufu kumuntu wajanika kalaamulandu wakujeya muntunyina achaali, kabiyo —

(a) mulawu weelede kuzumizya nkuta kuti ipe chisubulo chakujeegwa kwamuntu naakuti pe kakusiyana aabwiime bwamulandu ulookubetekwa;

(b) chisubulo chalufu chichitwa luzutu kuti kakuli nkuta yamumulawu yapa chisubo eecho;

(c) inkuta tayikonzyi kupa chisubulo chalufu pe kubantu batobela—

(i) kumuntu woonse wasunda mulandu kajisi minyaka ilaansi kwamakumi obile awumwi.

(ii) kumuntu woonse ujisi minyaka iiyinda makumi musanu amakumi obili;

(d) chisubulo chalufu tacheelede kupegwa kumuntu wachanakazi; kabiyo

(e) muntu wapegwa chisubulo chalufu ulacheelelo chakukumbila lulekelelo kuzwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

(3) Mulawu wapangwa aPalyamenti weelede kukwabilila bana batana tumbukwa nkaambo abalabo balijisi cheelelo chakupona Mulawu uzoobikkwa upandulula aawo mpukweelede kuti mwanakazi agwisye bula/ida zili mumulawu. 49

Cheelelo changwangunuko yakwe mwini

(1) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakwaangunuka kwakwe. Eezi zibikkilizya —

(a) cheelelo chakutabikkwa mubwaange kutabetekedwe pe ankuta yamulawu.

naakuti

muchitookesi

(b) cheelelo chakutanyangwa ngwangunuko yako kakutakwe nchuwasunda. pe. 50

(2) Taakwe muntu uunga ulasungwa nkaambo kakutyola chizuminano naakuti cheene Zyeelelo zyabaange abali muchitookesi

(1) Woonse muntu wasungwa —

(a) weelede kwaambilwa mulandu ngwaasunda/ngwaawangilwa;

(b) weelede kuzumizigwa, achakufwambaana —

(i) kuzibisya banakwe/mulumaakwe, naakuti nzubo zyakwe, simulawo/ Loya unga ulamwiiminina naakuti nguni ngwaayanda kuzibisya aatala akusungwa kwakwe. Kufonena bantu aaba kweelede kwabbadalilwa afulumende/kakubelesegwa mfoni yafulumende.

(ii) kalibbadalila lwakwe, ulacheelelo chakubonana aSimulawu/Loya ngwayanda uunga ulamwiiminina munkuta naakuti kubona dokotela ngwayanda;

(c) weelede kujatwa aabulemu bweelede;

(d) weelede kugwisigwa muchitookesi kakutakwe zimwi nzyeelede kuchita naakuti kakuli zimwi zisyoonto nzyeelede kuchitwa, kachilinda kubetekwa kwamulandu wakwe. Ukonzya biyo kubambwa mubwaange/ muchitookesi kuti kwaboneka zimwi ziyoowelwa kuti zilachitika kuti wagwisigwa abetekwe kalaanze; kabiyo

(e) weelede kuzumizigwa kutongawuka aatala akwaangwa kwakwe kuti kakuli takaangwa zili mumulawu.Weelede wagwisigwa kuzwa muchitookesi kuti kwaboneka kuti wakaangwa zilaanze kwamulawu.

(2) Woonse muntu wasungwa/wajalilwa muchitookesi —

(a) kuchitila kuti abambilwe kweetwa kunkuta yalubenta; naakuti

(b) nkaambo kamulandu ngwaategwa wakasunda;

weelede kutolwa kunkuta yalubeta achakufwambaana mukati kachiindi chitiindi mawola aali makumi aane alaamusanu atutatu (48) kuzwa chiindi nchaasungwa aacho naakuti kuzwa aawo naajalilwa muchitookesi. Eezi tazikwe ndaba pe kuti muntu waangwa kumamanino aamviki kiinjila muMugibelo, Nsondo naakuti mubuzuba bwakulyookezya kwabantu.

(3) Muntu watetwa kunkuta yalubeta mukati kachiindi chamawola aasika makumi aane alaamusanu atutatu (48) weelede wagwisigwa muchitookesi adomane kuti kubambwa kwakwe muchitookesi kwazumizigwa aankuta.

(4) Woonse muntu wasungwa naakuti wajalilwa nkaambo kakuyeeyelwa kuti wasunda mulandu ulacheelelo—

(a) chakuumuna naasungwa;

(b) chakwaambilwa achakufwambaana;

(i) cheelelo chakwe chakuumuna; kabiyo

(ii) zinga zilachitika kuli nguwe kuti wawumuna akuti taawumuna naasungwa;

(c) chakutaangilwa kuzumina mulandu; kabiyo

(d) natolwa kunkuta kwalutanzi, weelede kuzibisigwa mulandu ngwaakasanda naakuti kwaambilwa chinga chilapa kuti abweedezegwe mubwaange naakuti agwisigwe mubwaange.

(5) Woonse muntu uuli mubwaangemuchitookesi, kakubikkilizigwa ooyo wakapegwa kale chisubulo, ulacheelelo —

(a) chakuzibisigwa cheecho chapa kuti abambwe mubwaange;

(b) kabelesya mali yakwe, kubonana mukavwindi asimulawu wakwe uunga ulamwiiminina munkuta yalubeta, kabiyo weelede kwaambilwa achakufwambaana cheelelo chakwe eechi;

(c) kwambuuzyania akuswayigwa abantu batobela aaba —

(i) mwanakazi wakwe/mulumi wakwe;

(ii) luzubo lwakwe;

(iii) mukambawusi ngwaalisalila lwakwe;

(iv) simulawu ngwaalisalila lwakwe

(v) dokotela ngwaalisalila lwakwe; kabiyo

(vi) abamwi bantu mbayanda kuti bamuswaya, pesi kazisiyana aakuti balatambulika na kubapati babona zyakubambwa kwabaange kuchitila kuti kubambwa kwabaange kweendelezegwe kabotu;

(d) kubambwa mubusena bukkalika, busalala kabiyo butondeezya bulemu bwamuntu, kupegwa mweenya wakulyoolola akuchita zeezyo zisimya mubili, kupegwa, kazibbadalilwa afulumende, malo aakulala azulide, zimbuzi, busanambi bweelede antoomwe akusilikwa malwazi; kabiyo.

(e) kutongawuka, kwiinda munkuta, aatala akubambwa kwakwe mubwaange kuti kabwene kuti kubambwa kwakwe mubwaange kulaanze kwamulawu, mpawo akugwisigwa mubwaange chakufwambaana kuti kwategwa agwisigwe.

(6) Woonse muntu wabambwa mubwaange katabetekedwe pe mane kwiinda chiindi cheelede (48) weelede kugwisigwa kuzwa mubwaangwe. Kugwisigwa ooku kulakonzya kuchitwa kakutakwe zyaambwa pe zyeelede kuchenjelelwa naakuti kakuli zimwi zyaambwa zyeelede kutobelezegwa amwaange kuchitila kuti naagwisigwa—

(a) azoosike kulubeta lwankuta;

(b)atazookolonganyi bukamboni bweelede kubelesegwa kunkuta buunsi bwalubeta; kabiyo

(c) teelede kusunda uumbi pe mulandu muchiindi kachilinda kubetekwa mulandu ngwalaawo kale.

(7) Kuti kwaba aantanganana kuti mwaange ubambidwe muchitookesi zilaanze kwamulawu naakuti takuzibikani kuti mwaange ubambidwe kuchitookesi chili, nikuba nabani muntu ulakonzya kwiinka kuNkuta Yaajulu akakumbile pepa lyamvumo—

(a) yakuti mwaange agwisigwe mubwaange bwakwe naakuti ayetwe kunkuta azoobetekwe kuchitila kuti bwaangwe bwakwe bupandululwe kabotu bubonwe kuti buli mumulawu na, naakuti mwaange adombolwe nkwasisidwe nkwataboneki; naakuti

(b) yakwaamba kuti bwaange bwamuntu bulaanze kwamulawu nkaako mwaange ngagwisigwe muchitookesi achakufwambaana;

Kabiyo Inkuta Yaajulu ikonzya kupa mvumo eeyo kazyeendelana aakuti bwiime bwakwaangwa kwamwaange bweendede biyeni.

(8) Bwaangwe bwamuntu buteendelani azeezi zyaambwa muchibeela eechi bunooli bulaanze kwamulawu.

(9) Woonse muntu wakaangwa zitali mumulawu naakuti wakabikkwa mubwaange zitali mumulawu ulacheelelo chakuliigwa/kubbadalwa amuntu ooyo wakamwaangide zitali mumulawu. Pesi mulawu ukonzya kukwabilila bantu batobela kuti bataanzigwi—

(a) silubeta wakazichita mbukuyandikana;

katobelezya

mulimu

wakwe

mbuuli

(b) woonse mubelesi waluundu utanooli wazichita katobelezya ziyandwa aamulimu wakwe katachitide bubeya pe. 51

Cheelelo chakujatwa aabulemu

Muntu woonse uzyedwe kajisi bulemu mubuumi bwakwe boonse kabiyo cheelelo chakulemeka bulemu bwamuntu cheelede kukwabililwa akuzuzikizigwa. 52

Cheelelo chakujana lukwabililo lwakwe muntu

Muntu woonse ulacheelelo chakuchita mbabona kazimweelede aatala amubili wakwe antoomwe amayeeyelo aakwe. Eezi zibikkilizya zitobela—

(c) cheelelo chakutanjila mumpyompyongano antoomwe amulumamba;

(d) cheelelo chakuchita mbayanda muntu aatala azyakuzyala bana bakwe;

(e) cheelelo chakutabelesegwa muzyiiyo zyakufunuuka akuyanduula bwiime bwazintu zipya, naakuti kugwisigwa kwazipanzi zyamubili muntu katazumide kuti zigwisigwe. 53

Kwaangunuka kuzwa mukusonsegwa, kusubulwa akujatwa bunkala munzila iigwisya bulemu bwamuntu

Taakwe muntu uunga ulasonsegwa pe aamubili naakuti muboongo bwakwe. Taakwe muntu weelede kupegwa chisubulo chimugwisya bulemu bwakwe pe. 54

Kwaangunuka kuzwa kubuzike akujatwa bunkala

Taakwe muntu weelede kujatwa buzike naakuti kujatwa bunkala. 55

Ngwangunuko kuzwa kukwaangilwa kubeleka mulimu kutayandi

Taakwe muntu weelede kwaangilwa kuchita mulimu katayandi pe/ ngwaatalisalila pe mwini. 56

Kweelana kwabantu antoomwe akutasaluulana

(1) Woonse muntu uleelene kunembo lyamulawu kabiyo woonse muntu weelede kukwabililwa amulawu.

(2) Boonse baalumi abanakazi bajisi myeenya iikozyania mubuumi kuzintu zyoonse zichitikana mubuumi; muli zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika, zyabuvubi, zyabukkale bwabantu azyatunsiyansiya.

(3) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakutasaluulwa pe abamwi kwiinda muzintu zisiyenesiyene mbuuli bunanyika bwakwe, mubala wachikumba chakwe, mulaka wakwe, musyobo wakwe, nkombyo yakwe, kabunga kazyanyika nkanjide, tunsiyansiya twakwe, nkwazwa kubusena, bwanakazi/bwaalumi bwakwe, bulema bwakwe, minyaka yakwe, kuba aada kwakwe, mayeeyelo aakwe, nzila njaakazyalwa aayo, azimwi zintu zinga zilapa kuti muntu asaluulwe.

—

(4) Muntu nga ulati wasaluulwa mbuuli kwaambidwe mukabeela (3) aajulu kuti kakuli

(a) wajatwa muliimwi nzila naakuti wakasigwa chimwi chintu bamwi bantu nchibazumininwa, naakuti;

(b) bamwi bantu bapegwa mbwaatapegwa pe we.

chimwi

chintu

naakuti

bumwi

bubotu

(5) Lusaluulo lwaambidwe aajulu mukabeela (3) lunooli taluli mumulawu pe kuti teelwachitwa kwiinda munzila zibalangala mubukkale bwajandabanji bweendelezegwa munzila iibalangala, yabululami, yabulemu iiyelanisya bantu boonse akulemeka ngwaangunuko zyabantu.

(6) Fulumende weelede kupanga milawu iiwamba kuti bantu kweelede bajatwe bweelene mubuumi. Weelede kuchita mabambe akukwabilila akusungwaazya bantu badyaamizidwe kwiinda mukusaluulwa; kabiyo—

(a) eezi zyeelede kuchitwa kuti kakuyanda kweelanisigwa bamwi bantu bakadyaamizidwe nsiku;

(b) eezi kuti zyachitwa takunooli kwategwa nkusaluula pe nkaambo nkuyanda kugwasya bantu bakadyaamizigwa kwiinda mukabeela (3). 57

Cheelelo chakuba aansiswa

Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakuba aakavwindi/nsiswa, eezi zibikkilizya zitobela—

(a) kutanjilwa kwalubuwa lwako, muunzi wako, ng’anda yako amuntu utakwe mvumo;

(b) kutasechwa kwamubili wako, ng’anda yako, alubono lwako kakutakwe mvumo pe;

(c) kutabezelwa lubono lwako pe;

(d) kutanjililwa muzyaambo zyako nzuwambuula aaluwayile, aanjobolamakani mumagwalo aako, awumwi muntu katakwe mvumo; naakuti

(e) kubbulula bwiime bwamalwazi aamuntu we mwini katayandi kuti zibbululwe kubantu. 58

Waangunuka kwakuswaanana, kulyidilana akubungana kwabantu

(1) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakubungana, kulyidilana akuswaanana abamwi bantu mbayanda. Kabiyo muntu ulacheelelo chakusala kutayanda kuswaanana, kulyidilana antoomwe akubungana abamwi bantu mbatayandi mwini.

(2) Muntu takweelede kuti waangilwa kunjila mukabunga nkatayandi naakuti kunjila mumiswaangano njatayandi. 59

Kwaangunuka kwamuntu kuti atongawuke antoomwe akulemba aapepa eezyo zyamuchima

Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakutongawuka akung’ung’una aawo mpajatwa bubi akutondeezya kutabotelwa kwakwe kwiinda mukuzilemba apepa eezyo nzyatayandi mpawo pepa walipa baabo bakonzya kuzizyotolola. Pesi kutongawuka akung’ung’una ooku kweelede kwachitwa muluumuno. 60

Kwaangunuka muli zyakulibeteka akulilya moyo

(1) Woonse muntu ulaangunukide kuti alilye moyo antoomwe akulibeteka mwini, eezi zijatikizya —

(a) kwaangunuka kwamuntu kusala nzila yakukomba njayanda, kuba amayeeyelo amaboneno aakwe; kabiyo

(b) kukomba zintu nzyayanda/Leza ngwayanda aantanganana naakuti munsiswa alikke naakuti aamwi abenziina.

(2) Taakwe muntu uunga ulaangilwa kuchita chikonke chiteendelani anzila yakukomba Leza wakwe.

(3) Bazyali antoomwe abaabo balelede bana bateensi babo balacheelelo chakugwasizya mwana nzyeelede kukomba akuchita mubuumi. Pesi eezi tazyeelede kutyola cheelelo chamwana kuti ajane lwiiyo, lukwabililo, zyakusilikwa abukkale bubotu.

(4) Basinkombyo balakonzya kuyaka mayake oomo mubatanoochitile nkombyo zyabo. Mayake aabo akonzya kujana lugwasyo lwamali kuzwa kuli fulumende. 61

Ngwangunuko yakulyaambwida zilaamoyo a ngwangunuko mukubelesegwa kwazibelesyo zyakumwaya milumbe

(1) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakwaangunuka kuti ambuuzyanie abamwi nzyayanda, eezi zibikkilizya zeezi —

(a) kwaangunuka kwamuntu kuti ayanduule, akumwaya mizeezo yakwe ayimwi milumbe;

atambule

antoomwe

(b) kwaangunuka kupanga zikozyanisyo, kuchita mfukununo, antoomwe akubumba zintu; kabiyo

(c) kujana kwaangunuka mulizyalwiiyo.

(2) Woonse muntu ulaangwangunuko yakubelesya zimwaya milumbe, ngwangunuko eeyi ibikkilizya kukwabililwa kwacheelelo chakutabbongonona bantu bapa basimusunzatwaambo makani.

(3) Maanda aasikapepele aamwaya milumbe alakonzya kupangwa kakutakwe chikachizya, pesi ayelede kutobelezya milawu yakujana mapepa aapa mvumo yakutalika kubeleka antoomwe—

(a) akukwabilila mabelesezyo azyamuluuwo ansimbi kweendelezya mayiwe aazyakumwaya milumbe/twaambo;

zigwasizya

(b) akubeleka kabataseyamide kuli fulumende pe naakuti kabataseyamide kutubunga twazyanyika pe naakuti kuli bazyamakwebo.

(4) Basikumwaya twaambo babelekela fulumende tabeelede pe —

(a) kutola mabazu muli zyatubunga twazyakweendelezegwa kwanyika.

(b) beelede kuchita mulimu wabo mbuuli mbubawubona kakweelede nikuba kuti mayeeyelo aabo anooli kakazyania amizeezo yafulumende, tazikwe ndaba; kabiyo.

(c) basungwaazye bamwi kuti bamwaye milumbe, maboneno amayeeyelo aabo asiyene antoomwe amaboneno abaabo bayeeya zisiyene azyabamwi.

(5) Ngwangunuko zyakwambuuzyania antoomwe akumwaya milumbe atwaambo takweelede pe kuti —

(a) zyabanzila zyakusungwaazya lupyopyongano;

(b) kumwaya majwi aalusulano;

(c) kubisya chiimo abuntu bwamuntu antoomwe abulemu bwakwe; naakuti

(d) kuvwanyanga cheelelo chamuntu chakuba ansiswa.

Kujana luzibo antoomwe amilumbe

(1) Woonse munaZimbabwe naakuti wakapegwa mvumo yakuchita kakkalila muZimbabwe, ulacheelelo chakujana luzibo antoomwe amilumbe zibambidwe afulumende kapati kuti luzibo oolo luyandilwa kugwasizya luundu kuti lubelesye luzibo oolo mukumvwisisisya mabelekelo afulumende.

(2) Woonse muntu, kubikkilizya basimumwaya twaambo bamuZimbabwe, ulacheelelo chakujana luzibo loonse lubambidwe anikuba muntu uuli, kubikkilizya mitabi yafulumende fumbwa luzibo oolo kaluyanda kubelesegwa kuzuzikizya naakuti kukwabilila cheelelo chabantu.

(3) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakululamikilwa malembe/luzibo lwakwe naakuti kuziminwa malembe aataluleme alaatala anguwe naakuti luzibo lwakubeja lujiswi afulumende naakuti lujiswi anikaba kali kabunga kalyiimikilide naakuti mutabi wafulumende muzibeela zyoonse zyanyika, malembe aayo ayeelede alulamikwa.

(4) Milawu yeelede kupangwa yakusimatizya cheelelo eechi. Milawu eeyi ilakonzya kukasya lumwi luzibo kuti lutapegwi bantu boonse pe nkaambo kakuti ninsiswa yafulumende yakukwabilila nyika mbiizulwa naakuti ninsiswa yazyamilimu. Kukasya ooku kweelede kwachitwa munzila iibalangala munyika yeendelezegwa munzila yajandabanji muli bubalangale, bululami bulemu bwamuntu, kweelanisigwa kwabantu alwaangunuko. 63

Mulaka antoomwe atunsiyansiya Muntu woonse ulacheelelo —

(a) chakubelesya mulaka ngwayanda;

(b) chakutobelezya tunsiyansiya ntwayanda;

pesi mukutobelezya cheelelo eechi muntu tayelede kutyola zyeelelo zyabamwi pe bantu mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe muChaandaano eechi. 64

Ngwangunuko yakulisalila milimu iiyiilwa, zyamabbindawuko naa imwi biyo milimu

Woonse muntu weelede kulisalila antoomwe akuchita mulimu ngwayanda mwini antoomwe akuchita zyakuuzizyania. Pesi mabelekelo aamulimu ooyo akuuzizyania kweelede kakutobelezya milawu iiliwo. 65

Zyeelelo zyababelesi ababelekelwa

(1) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakujatwa kabotu kakutobelezegwa milawu yakubelekelana aakati kabasikubelekelwa ababelesi antoomwe akubbadalwa mali iiyelede mulimu ngwalookubeleka mubelesi.

(2) Woonse muntu, aanze kwabaabo babeleka milimu yakukwabilila nyika (basilumamba, tuswiilizyabavwiya amapulisa), ulacheelelo chakupanga kabunga antoomwe akunjila mukabunga kakapangwa kale kalaatala akwiimininwa kwababelekelwa naakuti babelesi aamilimu yabo.

(3) Woonse muntu, aanze kwabaabo babeleka milimu yakukwabilila nyika (basilumamba, tuswiilizyabavwiya amapulisa), ulacheelelo chakutongawuka aatala amabelekelo aakwe kuti taajatwa kabotu aamulimu. Eezi zijatikizya cheelelo chakutakata mulimu akutakata kwiindila mukukkalila mubelekelwa kabatabeleki pe bantu. Pesi mulawu ulakonzya kukasya bamwi babelesi bachita iimwi milimu yakuvuna buumi bwabantu kuti tabatakati milimu yabo.

(4) Woonse mubelesi weelede kubeleka mulimu akujatwa aamulimu munzila iiyandikana amulawu.

(5) Aanze kwabaabo babeleka milimu yakukwabilila nyika, woonse mubelesi asikubelekelwa, kabunga kayimina babelesiakabungakayiminababelekelwa, ulacheelelo—

(a) chakujatana abamwi mukukombelezya kutansikilwa mali yamufolo.

(b) kweendelezya; antoomwe

(c) akupanga naakuti kunjila mutubunga twiimina babelesi ababelekelwa.

(6) Banakazi abaalumi bajisi zyeelelo zikozyania kabiyo beelede bafola mali iiyelene nibabeleka milimu iikozyania.

(7) Banakazi beelede kupegwa mazuba akuya kukulyookezya nichasika chiindi chakuyanda kutumbuka kuli baabo banooli balaada. Bulamfu bwachiindi eechi nchibeelede kupegwa busika kumyeezi iitatu. 66

Ngwangunuko yakweendeenda akukkala munyika

(1) Woonse munaZimbabwe ula —

(a) cheelelo chakunjila munyika yaZimbabwe;

(b) cheelelo chakutagwisigwa pe kuzwa muZimbabwe; kabiyo

(c) cheelelo chakujana Pasipoti antoomwe awamwi mapepa akweenzya.

(2) Woonse munaZimbabwe awoonse ooyo wakanjila munyika yaZimbabwe zili mumulawu ulacheelelo —

(a) chakweendeenda kaangunukide fumbwa nkwayanda kuya munyika yaZimbabwe;

(b) chakukkala fumbwa nkwaayanda munyika; antoomwe

(c) akubbuda munyika nichiba chili chiindi nchaayanda. 67

Zyeelelo zyameendelezyo aanyika

(1) Woonse munaZimbabwe ulacheelelo —

(a) chakunjila munsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi zichitwa munzila iibalangala kazichitwa chiindi cheelede kakuchitwa munzila iitobelezya mulawu wanyika;

(b) chakusala zeezyo nzyayanda aatala ameendelezyo eenyika.

(2) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu, bantu bamuZimbabwe balacheelelo —

(a) chakupanga akunjila mutubunga twakweendelezya nyika amutubunga tuliwo ntubayanda;

(b) chakukombelezya bantu muluumuno kuti bavootele kamwi kabunga;

(c) chakunjila mumabambe muluumuno.

azyakweendelezya

nyika

aasiyenesiyene

(d) chakuswaanana aabantu antoomwe akunjila mutubunga kulaawulikke naakuti kamuli kabunga muzichitiko zyaluumuno zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika kamuyanda kutongawuka naakuti kusungwaazya mabelekelo afulumende naakuti kamwi kabunga kazyanyika nikuba chinzi nchimuyanda kuchita.

(3) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu wanyika eeli, woonse munaZimbabwe uujisi minyaka iisika akwiinda kkumi lilamusanu atutatu (18) ulacheelelo —

(a) chakuvoota munsaluuzyo zyoonse amalefelendamu woonse aayitilizigwa kwiinda mumilawu yanyika iisiyenesiyene kabiyo kavoota munsiswa;kabiyo

(b) chakuyanda kuvootelwa kuti ajane chuuno chabuzulwidi nchayanda.Kuti wasalwa ulacheelelo chakweendelezya chibaka nchaasalilwa abantu.

(4) Munzila yakusungwaazya kuvula kwatubunga twakweendelezya nyika, kweelede kwapangwa mulawu uuzumizya fulumende kuti ape mali kutubunga twazyakweendelezya nyika kusungwaazya kuti tuvule. 68

Cheelelo chakweendelezegwa kwazyabululami

(1) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakweendelezegwa kwazyabululami munzila iili mumulawu amububalangale.

(2) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakwaambilwa kufwambaana kwiinda mumalembe aatala akutyolwa antoomwe akunyonganisigwa kwameendelezezyo aazyabululami.

(3) Mulawu wabumbwa aPalyamenti weelede kusimatizya zyeelelo eezi kabiyo uyelede—

(a) kuchita ndangamusule yameendelezezyo azyabululami mumabelekelo aankuta, eezi zyeelede kuchitwa ababetesi balisantide;

(b) kupa mulandu amukuli kuli fulumende kuti azuzikizye zyeelelo zyaambidwe mutubeela (1) a(2); kabiyo,

(c) kusungwaazigwe kweendelezegwa kwazyabululami munzila ilaamunse. 69

Cheelelo chakubetekwa chooma

(1) Woonse muntu watamizigwa mulandu ulacheelelo chakubetekwa chooma munzila iilaantanganana kabiyo kiibalangala muchiindi chifwiifwi munkunta yamulawu yabwini.

(2) Mukulangwalangwa kwazyeelelo zyabukkale abulyidilane bwabantu, woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakujana kubetekwa chakufwambaana kunembo lyankuta iilisantide iitakwe bbazu ndyitola naakuti kubetekwa akabunga kamwi kali mumulawu.

(3) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakwiinka kunkuta zyalubeta kabiyo alisalile lwakwe simulawu/loya utamwiiminine munkuta yamulawu.

(4) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo, kalibbadalila, kusala simulawu/loya unga ulamwiimina munkuta naakuti kamwi kabunga kabeteka. 70

Zyeelelo zyabantu batamizigwa milandu

(1) Woonse muntu watamizigwa mulandu ulazyeelelo zinji zimwi nzeezi —

(a) kujatwa mbuuli muntu uutakwe mulandu domane nkuta kalaamulandu muntu kwiinda mulubeta;

yamujana

(b) kuzibisigwa achakufwambaana mulandu nguwasungilwa muntu, kabiyo kupegwa mweenya wakulisakwida kumulandu nguwasungulwa.

(c) kupegwa chiindi chiingi chakuti zyakuloolisakwida munkuta yalubeta;

muntu

alibambile

zyaambo

(d) cheelelo chakuyanduula simulawu uunga ulamwiimikilila munkuta yamulawu kabiyo kamubbadala lwakwe simulandu;

(e) cheelelo chakuti fulumende ayandwiide sikutamizigwa mulandu, simulawu uunga ulamwiimikilila munkuta yalubeta kabbadalwa afulumende;

(f) kuzibisigwa achakufwambaana zyeelelo nzyalaazyo muntu mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe muminchali (d) a (e) aajulu.

(g) mulandu wakwe weelede kubetekwa kaliwo lwakwe sikutamizigwa.

(h) kukaka antoomwe akuzumina bukamboni bweetwa munkuta yalubeta.

(i) kuumuna naakuti kutaangilwa pe kwaambuula zyaambo akutapa bukamboni bunga bulakusungilila kumulandu ngulaawo munkuta;

(j) cheelelo chakubetekwa mumulaka ngumvwa kabotu nusikutamizigwa mulandu;

(k) cheelelo chakutabetekwa mulandu wakatali mulandu kutaanguna nichakachitwa

(l) cheelelo chakutabetekelwa mulandu wakali wasunsikwa kale naakuti nguwakali wajanika kutakwaawo.

(m) cheelelo chakutabetekelwa mulandu nguwakali walekelelwa kale nsiku naakuti wakuuzwa kale naakuti wakapegwa chisubulo kale aatala anguwo;

(n) kupegwa chisubulo chilaansi kulicheecho chaambidwe amulawu kuti kakuli bwiime abulemu bwamulandu bwakasandulwa kuzwa mpuwakasundwa mulandu kuzoosika nuwapegwa chisubulo.

(2) Aawo mpukweelede kuti muntu apegwe luzibo —

(a) luzibo oolo lweelede kupegwa muntu mumulaka ngwamvwa; kabiyo

(b) kuti muntu ooyo katakonzyi kulemba akubala, luzibo ndwapegwa lulaamalembe lweelede kupandululwa kabotu munzila njamvwisisisya muntu uuyanda luzibo oolo.

(3) Mukubetekwa kwamuntu, bukamboni bwakajanwa munzila iityola zyeelelo zyaambwa muChaandaano eechi tabweelede kubelesegwa pe munkuta yalubeta nkaambo kuti bwabelesegwa bukamboni oobo lubeta loonse lunooli teelwenzegwa mumulawu pe.

(4) Cheelelo chakupegwa mapepa ajisi malembe alaatala akweendelezegwa kwalubeta lwako muchiindi chifwiifwi nilwamana lubeta. Pesi kweelede wabbadala kamali nikancheya kuti uwapegwe mapepa alaamalembe.

(5) Fumbwa muntu wajanwa kalaamulandu ankuta yalubeta ulacheelelo, nikuba kuti ngakwalangwalangwa imwi milawu, kuti, —

(a) mulandu wakwe ulangwelangwe ankuta yaajulu;

(b) mulandu wakwe utansikwe kunkuta yaajulu kuti taakkutikana achisubulo nchapegwa ankuta yaansi. 71

Zyeelelo zyaatala alubono

(1) Muchibeela eechi —

“luliyo lwa pencheni” chaamba luliyo lwapegwa muntu kaluli lwapencheni, mali yakulumba muntu nuwamana mulimu naakuti mali zibbadalwa kuzuzikizya kulumba mubelesi wakali kubeleka mulumu, kiili mali eeyo ibbadalwa —

(a) kumuntu naba uuli kiizwa muNkomo Mpati yafulumende;

(b) kulumbwa kwamubelesi nkwaakachita;

asikubelekelwa

nkaambo

kakubeleka

(c) nkaambo kakuti muntu wachisilwa naakuti kulichisila aamulimu;

(d) nkaambo kakuti muntu wasika aakusiya mulimu nkaambo kakuchembaala, malwazi naakuti chimwi chapa kuti asiye mulimu; eezi zibikkilizya kuliigwa kwamali akupegwa mali nzyatanooli muntu wakali kubbadala kupencheni yakwe azimwi mali zipegwa mubelesi naasiya mulimu; “lubono” chaamba fumbwa chintu muntu nchalaacho nchati nchichakwe/ lubono muntu ndwajisi anzila njaluyanda lubono oolo.

(2) Kwiinda muli zeezyo zyaambidwe muchibeela (72), woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakuula lubono ndwayanda, kubamba lubono, kuuzya lubono, kupa bamwi bantu lubono

lwakwe akujatisya bamwi bantu lubono lwakwe. Zyoonse eezi zikonzya kuchitwa muntu kalaalikke naakuti kabali kabunga.

(3) Kwiinda muli zeezyo zili muchibeela eechi achibeela (72), taakwe muntu weelede kunyangwa lubono lwakwe pe adomane kwiinda munzila zitobela—

(a) kunyangwa lubono lwakwe kwachitwa kakutobelwa mulawu;

(b) kunyangwa lubono lwakwe kweelede kuchitwe nkaambo kachimwi chazintu zitobela —

(i) nkaambo kakuyanda kukwabilila luundu/bantu, kubamba luumuno lwaluundu, kubamba busanambi bwaluundu antoomwe akutobelezya mabambe aalusumpuko abwiime bwadolopo.

(ii) nikuyanda kuti kuchitwe mabambe aalusumpuko naakuti kubelesya lubono oolo munzila iigwasizya luundu;

(c) mulawu uwamba kuti aabo bayanda kutola lubono bachite zitobela —

(i) kwaambilizya akupa chizibisyo chakuyanda kubweza lubono kakuchili chiindi kubantu balaachakuchita aatala alubono luyanda kutolwa, kuchitila kuti boonse bantu balaachakuchita alubono lutolwa bazibe;

(ii) kweelede basikutola lubono babbadale mali yakuliya kutola lubono oolo kabatana lutola naakuti nikwiinda chiindi chifwiifwi kabali balubweza lubono oolo; kabiyo

(iii) kuti kutolwa kwalubono oolo kulookukakwa abeni lubono, kweelede bayanda kutola lubono oolo balembela kunkuta yamulawu kabayanda kujana mvumo izwa kunkuta kabatana kulubweza/ kulutola pe lubono oolo, naakuti kuti kabali balutola kale kweelede kakutaniinda mazuba makumi otatwe (30) kabali balubweza lubono oolo balembela kunkuta yamulawu kutondeezya kuti bakabweza lubono;

(d) mulawu upa mvumo kumuntu watolelwa lubono kuti alembele kunkuta yamulawu kayanda kuti lubono lwakwe luboozegwe kuti kakuli nkuta yakakasya kubwezegwa kwalubono lwakwe; kabiyo

(e) mulawu upa mvumo kumuntu uuyanda kubbadalwa kuti alembele kunkuta yamulawu kayanda kuti zintu zitobela zilangwelangwe—

(i) kutondeezya kuluyanda lubono antoomwe abwiime bwaluyando ndwalaalo kulubono oolo;

(ii) kutolwa kwalubono oolo kwachitwa mumulawu na; a

(iii) bwiingi bwaluliyo ndubeelede kubbadalwa; Kabiyo kulembela kunkuta kuyanda mvumo yakubbadalwa kufwambaana luliyo lwalubono lulookutolegwa.

(4) Aawo muntu mpalaacheelelo chakubbadalwa lugwasyo lwapencheni, mulawu ukasya kuti cheelelo eechi tachizuzikizigwi, nkaambo kakabeela (3) kalaajulu, unooli mulawu usungwaazya kutolwa kwalubono achanguzu. 72

Cheelelo chakujana nyika yakulima akuvuba mbono

(1) Muchibeela eechi — “nyika yakulima” chaamba nyika yakulima naakuti iiyelede kubelesegwa kulima mbuuli zisyu, minsamu, kuvuba baswi, kuvuba banyama (lubono), kubikkilizya —

(a) kuvuba banyama bamuunzi, banyama bamusokwe, inkuku, antoomwe anzuki; naakuti

(b) kuchezya banyama bamuunzi naakuti banyama bamusokwe;

Pesi nyika yakulima iwambwa tiibikkilizyi nyika yamuluzeva mukkala luundu pe naakuti nyika iizyungulukide dolopo/kkansili, naakuti iijanika mukati katawundi mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mumulawu uubona zyakuyakwa kwamadolopo antoomwe akuchita mabambe aakusumpulwa kwamadolopo akubelesegwa kwanyika; “nyika” chaamba choonse chijanika /chimena aanyika naakuti chijanika aanyika; “chipanzi chanyika yakulima” chaamba chipanzi chanyika yakulima chilembesedwe aachilikke mumalembe aajanika kuDeeds Registry.

(2) Aawo nyika yakulima naakuti kwatondeezegwa chiyandisyo chakujana nyika yakulima/bulongo kuchitila kuti kugwasigwe baluundu kubikkilizya —

(a) kwaabanisya bantu nyika yakulima naakuti nyika yakuchita imwi milimu yazyakulima;

(b) kubambululwa kwamabelesezyo aanyika/bulongo, kukwabililwa kwaminsamu amasaka, kukwabililwa kwazituzyungulukide naakuti kubelesegwa kwabanyama bamusokwe antoomwe azimwi zyakalengwa aaSikabumba musokwe nzibanga balabelesya bantu; naakuti,

(c) kulonzegwa kwabantu banyangwa nyika yabo yakulima nkaambo kakuti nyika yabo yayanda kubelesegwa zimbi zintu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mufuka (a) naakuti (b); nyika eeyo ikonzya kutolwa changuzu afulumende kabelesya nzila yakubikka chizibisyo mumapepatwaambo chakutondeezya kuyanda kutola nyika eeyo. Kuti kwachitwa boobo, yika eeyo inooli yaba yafuluemende kuzwa buzuba chizibisyo nichabikkwa/ nichatondeezegwa mumapepatwaambo.

(3) Aawo nyika yakulima niyabwezwa changuzu kuchitila kuti ibeelesegwe zyaambwa mukabeela (2) kaambwa aajulu —

(a) kwiinda muchibeela 295(1) a (2), taakwe luliyo lubbadalwa pe mukubweza nyika eeyo pesi sikutolelwa nyika ulakonzya kupegwa luliyo lwakulumba lusumpuko ndwatanooli wakachita aanyika eeyo kayitana tolwa;

(b) taakwe muntu uunga ulalembela kunkuta yamulawu kayanda kujana zyaluliyo lwakutolwa kwanyika yakwe anooli kayanda kujana luliyo lwalusumpuko ndwaakachita aanyika eeyo kwiinda mukuyaka mayake azimwi. Kabiyo taakwe nkuta iinga ilaswiilizya kulila kwamuntu uuyanda kubbadalwa kubwezegwa kwanyika yakwe kiili yabwezwa aafulumende kakutobelwa nzila yapandululwa eeyi; kabiyo

(c) kubwezwa kwanyika eeyi takunga kulategwa nkusaluula muntu wabwezelwa pe mbuuli zyaambidwe aatala akubinda kusaluulwa kwabantu muchibeela 56

(4) Yoonse nyika yakulima yaka —

(a) lembwa mu Mpampikwa Yamusanu Tubili (7) muKkata lyaMulawu lyachiindi; naakuti

(b) tondekwa kale kwiinda muchibeela16B(2)(a)(ii) naakuti (iii) chaKkata lyaMulawu wansiku, mulawu mupya ooyu kuutana talika kubeleka; inooli ninyika yafulumende kabiyo taakwe luliyo lunga lulabbadalwa pe kuti yabwezwa aafulumende dobiyo kubbadala lusumpuko lwakachitwa aanyika eeyo kiitana bwezwa.

(5) Achakufwambaana, mbiitabwezwe biyo nyika kwiindila munzila yapanduluwa aajulu mukabeela (2), mubelesi, uubona zyakulembesegwa kwanyika akupegwa mapepa atondeezya mweni bulongo, weelede kwiilembesya bupya nyika eeyo katondeezya kuti eeyo nyika yaba nyika yafulumende tiichili nyika yamuntu watolelwa pe.

(6) Mulawu wapangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kuchita kuti kube mulandu kumuntu uutanooli wayanda kutola naakuti kubelesya, katapedwe mvumo, nyika yakabwezegwa afulumende kwiinda munzila yapandululwa muchibeela eechi;

(7) Kuti kakuchitwa mabambe aakubwezwa kwanyika changuzu afulumende kakuyanda kukkazikwa bupya bantu kwiindila mumabambe afulumende aakukkazika bupya bantu,zintu zitobela nzintu zipati loko zyeelede kulangisisigwa—

(a) muchiindi chafulumende wamakuwa wabudyaamizi, bantu bamuZimbabwe bakatolelwa nyika zyabo zyakulima azimwi zilengwa zyaLeza kabatabbadedwe pe luliyo;

(b) nkaambo kakutolelwa nyika zyabo zyakulima, bantu bakabweza zilwanio kabayanda kulwana amakuwa kuchitila kuti batole nyika yabo yakali yabwezwa aafulumende wamakuwa antoomwe akuyanda kulyaangununa kuzwa mubudyaamizi bwakali bweetwa aafulumende wamakuwa. Nkondo eeyi yakazooyeta kulilela kwanyika yaZimbabwe mumunyaka wa1980;

(c) nkako banaZimbabwe beelede bazumizigwa kuti batole nyika yabo njibakali banyangwa; kabiyo—

(i) fulumende wanyika yamakuwa aakakkede muZimbabwe (Britain) ulaamukuli wakuliya/kubbadala baabo bamakuwa bakatolelwa changuzu nyika yakulima afulumende wanyika yaZimbabwe kuchitila kuti nyika yakatolwa ikkalwe bupya abantu batakwe nyika yakukkala akulima, kwiinda munkomo yamali ilisantide yakaanzilwa zintu zili boobu; kabiyo

(ii) kuti fulumende wamakuwa wakachilwa kubabbadala luliyo aabo bamakuwa bakatolelwa nyika yakulima, fulumende waZimbabwe takonzyi kubabbadala pe luliyo lwabo aabo mbaakabwezela nyika yakulima kayanda kukkazika bupya bantu batakwe aakukkala.

(8) Chibeela eechi chisungwaazya fulumende wamakuwa kuti abbadale makuwa aayo aakatolelwa nyika yakulima kakuyanda kukkazikwa bupya bantu bakatalikwe nyika yakulima. 73

Zyeelelo zyazituzyungulukide

(1) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo —

(a) chakukkala mubusena butakwe malwazi akonzya kupambanisya bulondozi amubili wakwe;

(b) chakukwabilila zituzyungulukide kuchitila kuti mazyalane aalino awajuunza agwasikane kuzintu zituzyungulukide kwiinda mukubikka mulawu azimwi nchito—

(i) zipa kuti takubi kusofwaazikwa kwamaanzi, muuya antoomwe anyika munzila ipa kuti tazibeleseki;

(ii) zisungwaazya kukwabilila akuyobolola zituzyungulukide;

(iii) zisimatizya bwiime bwazituzyungulukide antoomwe amabelesezyo mabotu aazintu zyakaanzwa aSikabumba kuchitila kuti bantu basumpuke mubukkale bwabo.

(2) Fulumende weelede kuchita mabambe, kuti kakuli zibelesyo, aanga alapa kuti zyeelelo eezi zitatyolwi mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe muchibeela eechi.

Ngwangunuko yakutayandogwisigwa mpubede achanguzu

Taakwe muntu uunga ulalonzegwa aamuunzi wakwe naakuti amwayilwe muunzi wakwe kakutakwe mvumo iizwa kunkuta yamulawu itanooli yalangalanga bulingolingo boonse buyandikana kulangisisigwa kabatana kulonzegwa bantu. 75

Cheelelo chakujana lwiiyo

(1) Woonse munaZimbabwe abaabo bakapegwa mvumo yakuchita kakkalila muZimbabwe bajisi cheelelo —

(a) chakujana lwiiyo lwakumatalikilo kalubbadalilwa afulumende. Eezi zibikkikizya lwiiyo lwabantu bakakomena.

(b) chakwiiya kuya kunembo kwiinda mukugwasigwa afulumende kakubelesegwa milawu azimwi zinga zilachitwa. Fulumende weelede kuchita kuti kube kuuba kujana lwiiyo munyika.

(2) Woonse muntu ulacheelelo chakupanga zikolo zilisantide pesi zikolo eezi tazyeelede kusaluula bantu pe kwiinda munzila zisiyenesiyene zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu wanyika.

(3) Mulawu ulakonzya kwaamba nzila zyeelede kutobelezegwa kuti zikolo zilisantide zilembesegwe kumutabi wafulumende mbu uli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (2) chilaajulu antoomwe akupandulula nzila njizinga zilajalwa aayo zikolo eezyo kuti teezyatobelezya mulawu wabikkwa wakuti zilembesye.

(4) Fulumende weelede kuchita mabambe, kuti kakuli zibelesyo, aanga alapa kuti zyeelelo eezi zitatyolwi mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe mukabeela (1). 76

Cheelelo chakujana kusilikwa muzibbadela

(1) Woonse munaZimbabwe antoomwe abaabo bakapegwa mvumo yakuchita kakkalila muZimbabwe, ujisi cheelelo chakujana mpanga ulasilikwa malwazi akulangwalangwa zyabwiime bwamibili yabo. Eezi zibikkikizya cheelelo chakugwasigwa muli zyakutumbukwa kwabana.

(2) Woonse muntu uupona kachiswa bulwazi butola chiindi kupona ulaacheelelo chakujana lugwasyo lwakusilikwa zyabulwazi bwakwe.

(3) Taakwe muntu uunga ulakasigwa kusilikwa muchiindi naanjila muntenda nikuba kuli chili chibbadela.

(4) Fulumende weelede kuchita zyoonse nzyakonzya kuti zyeelelo eezi zizuzikizigwe kuti kaziliwo zibelesyo. 77

Cheelelo chakujana chakulya antoomwe ameenda Woonse muntu ulacheelelo —

(a) chakujana maanzi aatakwe tombe; antoomwe

(b) akujana chakulya chizulide; Fulumende weelede kupanga milawu azimwi zintu zinga zilapa kuti cheelelo eechi chizuzikizigwe kuti kaziliwo zibelesyo.


Zyeelelo zyatala alukwatano

(1) Woonse muntu ulaaminyaka ilaajulu kwakkumi amusanu aminyaka yotatwe (18 years) ulacheelelo chakukwata/kukwatwa mpawo atalike kuyaka muunzi wakwe.

(2) Taakwe muntu uunga ulaangilwa kukwatana aamuntu ngwatayandi pe lwakwe.

(3) Baalumi/Banakazi tabakonzyi kukwatana aabalikke.

CHIBAKA 3 BUPANDULUZI BUZWIDE BWAZIMWI ZYEELELO 79

Mabelesezyo aachibeela 3

(1) Chibeela eechi muKkata lyaMulawu chipa bupanduluzi bwiindilide aatala azimwi zyeelelo antoomwe angwangunuko kuchitila kuti bantu bazimvwisisisye kabotu akuzitobelezya.

(2) Mpingu eeyi tayeelede kuboneka aanga ikasya kuzuzikizigwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zijanika muMpingu 2 ilaajulu. 80

Zyeelelo zyabanakazi

(1) Woonse mwanakazi ujisi bulemu bwachiimo chamuntu buzwide mbuuli mbwalaabo mwaalumi. Banakazi bajisi zyeelelo zyeelene azyabaalumi kabiyo zyeelelo eezyo zijatikizya cheelelo chakupegwa myeenya iiyelene ayabaalumi mubuumi, muzibeela zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika, mubuvubi bwanyika amubukkale bwabantu.

(2) Banakazi abalabo bajisi zyeelelo, mbuuli baalumi, zyakulela antoomwe akukkala aabana. Ipalyamenti ilakonzya kupanga mulawu uupandulula bulingolingo bweelede kuchitwa kuti cheelelo eechi chizuzikizigwe kabotu.

(3) Yoonse milawu atunsiyansiya twabukkale bwabasikale tudyaamizya akutyola zyeelelo zyabanakazi zili muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, zyeelede kusweegwa nkaambo zinooli zikazyania aamulawu mupati wanyika. 81

Zyeelelo zyabana

(1) Woonse mwana (mulombe naakuti musimbi ulaaminyaka ilaansi kwakkumi lilaamusanu atutatu (18) ulacheelelo —

(a) chakujatwa bweelene abamwi bantu kunembo lyamulawu wanyika, antoomwe cheelelo chakuswiilizigwa;

(b) chakupegwa zina amutupo naazyalwa;

(c) kuli baabo bana —

(i) bakazyalilwa muZimbabwe; naakuti

(ii) bakazyalilwa aanze kwanyika yaZimbabwe pesi kalabunanyika bwaZimbabwe kwiinda mubuzukululu nelukata; balacheelelo chakujana pepa lyakuzyalwa achakufwambaana;

(d) cheelelo chakujana lukwabililo akuvubwa abazyali naakuti abamwi bantu;

(e) cheelelo chakukwabililwa kuzwa kukuchitwa muzike mbuuli kubelesya bana muli zyabusangu, kubelesegwa ziyumu, kusonsegwa, kutakatwa, azimwi nzila zyakujata bana bubi;

(f) cheelelo chakujana lwiiyo akusilikwa, kulya chakulya cheelede antoomwe akujana chiyusilo;

(g) cheelelo chakutaangilwa kunjila mutubunga twabasilumamba akulwana nkondo kabachili bana;

(h) cheelelo chakutanjizigwa mutubunga twazyameendelezyo aazyanyika;

(i) cheelelo chakutabikkwa muchitookesi/mujele adomane kwabulika nzila zimbi zinga zilatobelwa—

(i) kuti wabikkwa muchitookesi/mujele, kweelede kaali mabambe aachiindi chifwiifwi biyo,

(ii) kabiyo kabikkidwe aalikke muchitookesi katasweenene abantu bapati bali muchitookesi antoomwe akujatwa kweelede.

(iii) kubambwa akujatwa munzila amubusena bweendelene aaminyaka yakwe.

(2) Muzintu zyoonse zilaachakuchita amwana, eezyo nzyayanda mwana nzizyo zitobelwa.

(3) Bana beelede kujana lukwabililo choonse chiindi ankuta zyamulawu kapati Nkuta Yaajulu. 82

Zyeelelo zyabantu bakachembaala Woonse muntu ulaaminyaka iisika kumakumi musanu awabili (70) ulacheelelo chakujana —

(a) lugwasyo nilucheya luzwa kuli fulumende antoomwe aluzwa kumpuli yakwe;

(b) lugwasyo lwazyakusilikwa luzwa kuli fulumende; kabiyo

(c) lugwasyo lwamali luzwa kuli fulumende lugwasizya mubukkale bwabo; kakale

kabiyo Fulumende weelede kuchita zyoonse nzyakonzya kuti zyeelelo eezi zizuzikizigwe kuti kaziliwo zibelesyo. 83

Zyeelelo zyabantu balemene

Fulumende weelede kuchita nzyanga ulachita, kuti zibelesyo kaziliwo, kugwasya bantu balemene kuti bajane nzibayanda akubelesya luzibo ndubalaalo antoomwe azipego zyabo mubuumi bwabo. Nzyeelede kuchita fulumende zijatikizya—

(a) kubagwasizya kuti bakonzye kulibelekela milimu iibapa mpindu;

(b) kubagwasizya kuti bakkale aampuli zyabo antoomwe akuti babe aachakuchita muzintu zyiingi zichitikana mubukkale bwampuli zyabo azyakulikondelezya kwabo;

(c) kubakwabilila kuzwa kukusonsegwa akujatwa buzike abamwi bantu;

(d) kubagwasya kuti bajane kusilikwa kumalwazi aamubili antoomwe akubachita kuti bakkalinkane mumiyeeyo yabo;

(e) kubagwasizya mayake azikolo akayakwa kagwasizya balemene nkukuti bajane lwiiyo; kabiyo

(f) kubagwasizya kuti bajane lugwasyo lwakuyiisigwa afulumende aawo mpukweelede. 84

Zyeelelo zyabantu bakalwana nkondo yakulilela

(1) Bantu bakalwana nkondo yalwaangunuko —

(a) mbaabo bakalwana munkondo yakulilela kwaangununa Zimbabwe kuzwa kumakuwa,

(b) bakagwasizya basikulwana nkondo yakulilela munzila zisiyenesiyene; kabiyo

(c) bakaangidwe kwachiindi chilamfu kabaangilwa zyanyika muchiindi niyakali kulwanwa nkondo yakulilela;

bantu aaba beelede kuzibikana kuti bakalwana nkondo yakulilela antoomwe akupegwa bulemu bweelede. Nkaako beelede kuchitilwa zintu zili mbuuli kupegwa mali yakubelesya yabantu bakaleka kubeleka akujana kusilikwa nibachiswa.

(2) Ipalyamenti yeelede kupanga mulawu upandulula kuti banoozijana biyeni zyoonse eezi zyaambidwe mukabeela (1).

CHIBAKA 4 KUZUZIKIZIGWA KWAZYEELELO ZYABANTU ZYAAMPANDAMUNSAKA ANGWANGUNUKO 85

Kuzuzikizigwa kwazyeelelo zyabantu zyaampandamunsaka angwangunuko

(1) Kufumbwa nabani wabantu aaba —

(a) uulyiimikilide kachita zigwasya nguwe alikke;

(b) uuyimikilila umwi muntu uutakonzyi kulyiimikilila;

(c) muntu wakabunga naakuti uyimikilila ziyandisyo zyakabunga naakuti musyobo wabantu;

(d) muntu uuyimikilila ziyandwa aluundu;

(e) kabunga kiimikilila ziyandwa aabantu bako;

ulacheelelo chakwiinka kunkuta, akuyomang’ala kuti cheelelo naa ngwangunuko yaampandamunsaka iiili mu Chaandaano eechino yakatyolwa, naakuti ilakonzya ku nyonyonganisigwa alimwi nkuta ilakonzya kupa nzila nzyakugwasizya zili mbuuli zyeelelo naakuti batyolelwa zyeelelo babbadalwe.

(2) Kutyola kwamuntu mulawu takumukachizyi pe kuyoomang’ala kunkuta kuti ajane lugwasyo mbuuli zyaambidwe mukabeela (1) aajulu.

(3) Tulawu-lawu twankuta zyoonse tweelede kupa bweendelezi butobelezegwa muchiindi bantu nibayanduula lugwasyo mbuuli makanze ali mukabeela (1), tulawu ootu tweelede kubona kuti—

(a) cheelelo chakumang’ala kunkuta mbuuli zyaambidwe mukabeela (1) cheelede kweendelezegwa kabotu;

(b) kweendelezegwa kwalubeta lwankuta antoomwe akutalika kwalubeta, kweelede zyafwiinsigwa muchiindi chifwiifwi loko/chiniini;

(c) inkuta, nikuba kuti yeelede kutobelezya tulawu-lawu twakubeteka kabotu, tiiyelede kuchelezegwa aatulawu ootu twabweendelezi oobu; alimwi

(d) muntu ulaaluzibo, kuti wazumizigwa ankuta, ulakonzya kunjila munkuta kagwasizya.

(4) Kubulika kwatulawu-lawu twaambidwe mukabeela (3) takukonzyi kukachizya cheelelo chakutalika bweendelezi bwaambidwe mukabeela (1) akuchita kuti mulandu ubetekwe munkuta.

CHIBAKA 5 MPUZYEELEDE KULEKWA KUZUZIKIZIGWA ZYEELELO ZYAAMPANDAMUNSAKA 86

Mpuzigolela zyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka angwangunuko

(1) Zyeelelo zyabantu zyaampandamunsaka angwangunuko zijanika muChaandaano eechi zyeelede kuzuzikizigwa akutobelezegwa kabotu pesi kakulemekwa zyeelelo angwangunuko zyabamwi bantu.

(2) Zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zijanika muChaandaano eechi zilakonzya kudyaamizigwa biyo kwiinda mumulawu umwi wabelesegwa fumbwa mulawu ooyo kuboneka kuuyelede kudyaamizya zyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka alimwi acheecho chipa kuti zyeelelo angwangunuko zidyaamizigwe kweelede kachili chintu chiboneka cheendelana abweendelezi bwachisi chajandabanji chitobelezya bubalangale, bululami, kulemeka bulemu bwamuntu, kweelanisya bantu, kwaangunuka kwabantu, azimwi kubikkilizya—

(a) bube bwacheelelo naakuti ngwangunuko yadyaamizigwa;

(b) mpalamo yakudyaamizya cheelelo eecho, akubona kuti kuleelede na kudyaamizya cheelelo eecho nkaambo kakuyanda kukwabilila nyika, kukwabilila luundu, bukkale bwabantu muluumuno, kukwabilila zyabulondozi/busanambi bwabantu, mabambe akweendelezegwa kwamadolopo antoomwe aziyandwa abanabunji;

(c) bube abuyumu bwakudyaamizigwa kwacheelelo;

(d) chiyandisyo chakubona kutegwa kunoneezegwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko abamwi bantu takudyaamizyi zyeelelo angwangunuko zyabambi bantu;

(e) bulidilane buliwo aakati kakudyaamizigwa kwazyeelelo ampalamo yakudyaamizya zyeelelo;

(f) naakuti taakwe na zimwi nzila zitadyaamizyi loko zyeelelo zyabantu zinga zilabelesegwa pesi impalamo yakudyaamizya zyeelelo kiichizuzikizigwa anzila eezyo.

(3) Taakwe mulawu uukonzya kudyaamizya zyeelelo zitobela mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe muChaandaano eechi, alimwi taakwe muntu weelede kuzityola pe —

(a) cheelelo chakupona, domane mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela (48) muChaandaano eechi;

(b) cheelelo chakulemeka bulemu bwamuntu;

(c) cheelelo chakutasonsegwa naakuti kutajatwa bunkala, zitakwe bulemu naakuti zisubulo zisampula muntu;

(d) cheelelo chakutajatwa buzike naakuti kujatwa bunkala;

(e) cheelelo chakubetekwa munzila iiluleme kiibalangala;

(f) cheelelo chakuya kunkuta kukujana mvumo iizumizya mwaange kuti agwisigwe muchitookesi, naakuti atolwe kunkuta kukabonwe kuti kujalilwa kwakwe kuli mumulawu na, naakuti kujana mvumo yakuziba kuti muntu ujalilidwe usisidwe kuli nkwataboneki, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 50(7)(a). 87

Zyeelelo mbuzilekwa kulemekwa muchiimo chalupyopyongano

(1) Aajulu kwanzila zizumizigwa kudyaamizya zyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela (86), zyeelelo angwangunuko eezi zikonzya kudyaamizigwa kabiyo kwiinda mumulawu wakalembwa antaamo zyeelede kuchitwa muchiindi achiimo chalupyopyongano, pesi kudyaamizigwa ooku kweelede kuchitwa munzila yakazumizigwa kwiinda muchibeela eechi antoomwe amuMpampikwa Yabili ijanika muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(2) Mulawu uulembedwe waambwa mukabeela (1) kalaajulu antoomwe azimwi zili mumulawu zinga zilachitwa, zyeelede kubikkwa mupepatwaambo lyaluundu lyafulumende litegwa niGazette.

(3) Kufumbwa kudyaamizigwa kwacheelelo kwaambidwe amulawu ulembedwe mukabeela (1) muchaandaano eechi, takweelede pe kwiindilizya eezyo zyeelede kuchitwa kuti kakulangwa bwiime bwalupyopyongano buliwo.

(4) Taakwe mulawu uuzumizya koompololwa kwachiimo chalupyopyongano, kabiyo taakwe zimwi zili mumulawu zyeelede kuchitwa nkaambo kakoompololwa kwachiimo chalupyopyongano zinga zila—

(a) kwabilila, naakuti kuzumizya fulumende naakuti chibeela chafulumende nikuba kuchibaka chili chanyika , naakuti naba uuli muntu, kuti achite zintu zitali mumulawu; naakuti

(b) dyaamizya nichiba chili chazyeelelo zyaambidwe muchibeela 86(3) , naakuti kuzumizya kudyaamizigwa akutyolwa kwazyeelelo eezi.

CHAANDAANO 5 BASILUTWE BAFULUMENDE

CHIBAKA 1 MANGUZU AABASILUTWE BAFULUMENDE 88

Manguzi aaBuzuluzi

(1) Manguzu a Basilutwe bafulumende azwa kubulumbu bwaZimbabwe kabiyo ayelede kubelesegwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(2) Manguzu a basilutwe bafulumende munyika yaZimbabwe apedwe Muzulwidi waNyika uwabelesya, kwiinda muNkamu yaBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende (Kkabbineti), katobelezya Kkata lyaMulawu eeli.

CHIBAKA 2 MUZULWIDI WANYIKA ABAMUTOBELA 89

Muzulwidi Wanyika Muzulwidi waNyika ngusilutwe waNyika aFulumende kabiyo nguDuundumuntule waBasilumamba bakwabilila nyika.


Milimu yaMuzulwidi waNyika

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kusumpula, kukwabilila, kutobelezya akulemeka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli mbuuli mulawu mupati-kampatila wachisi chaZimbabwe nkaako kweelede wabona kuti Kkata lyaMulawi eeli antoomwe amilawu imwi yanyika zyalemekwa akutobelezegwa mbuuli mbukweelede.

(2) Muzulwidi waNyika weelede—

(a) kusungwaazya lukamantano aluumuno muchisi kuchitila kuti boonse banaZimbabwe bakkale kabotu muluumuno;

(b) kuziba akulemeka bulemu andemeko zyankondo yalwaangunuko;

(c) kukwabilila zyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka angwangunuko antoomwe akulemekwa kwamulawu munyika; kabiyo

(d) kulemeka busiyane bwamisyobo yabantu ijanika muZimbabwe. 91

Nzyayelede kubaazyo kuti muntu asalwe kuba Muzulwidi waNyika akuba mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika

(1) Muntu uyelelwa kusalwa kuba Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti kubikwa mbuuli Uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika kuti kakuli — [Kabeela kakabambululwa achibeela 3(a) waMbambululo yaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(a) ujisi bwenichisi mubuzukululu;

bwaZimbabwe

kwiinda

mukuzyalwa

naakuti

(b) ujisi minyaka isika naakuti kwiinda makumi aane;

(c) ukkala munyika yaZimbabwe;

(d) ulembesezye kuvoota.

(2) Muntu takonzyi kwiimikila kuti asalwe kuba Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti kubikwa kuba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika kuti kakuli wakali waba Muzulwidi waNyika kale tubili aansi kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kazitobelana naakuti muzidyunkuzidyunku zyamyaaka. Aansi kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli kuti muntu wakali waba Muzulwidi waNyika kwaminyaka itatu kaziyanembo chaamba kuti unooli wakaba Muzulwidi waNyika kwamweenya uzwide. [kabeela kakabambululwa achibeela 3(b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]


Kusalwa kwaMuzulwidi waNyika [Mutwe wakagwisigwa achibeela 4(a) yaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(1) Kusalwa kwaMuzulwidi waNyika waNyika kweelede kwachitwa mukati kachiindi chaambidwe muchibeela 158.

(2) …

[Kabeela kakabambululwa achibeela 4(b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(3) … [Tubeela (2) a(3) tubambululwa achibeela 4 (c ) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(4) Ziyandikana kuti muntu alembesye kuyovoota mukusala Muzulwidi waNyika zili pandulwidwe muMpampikwa Yane. [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 4 (d) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(5) Kusalwa kwaMuzulwidi waNyika kweelede kuchitilwa aamwi ansaluuzyo zyabamwi bazulwidi bali mbuuli bayiminina bantu kung’anda yaPalyamenti (MP), bayimikilila bantu kuChilikiti abayimikilila bantu kuBbooma. [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 4 (e ) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]
