Kutondeezya kutakkutikana kunsaluuzyo zyakusala Muzulwidi waNyika

(1) Kwiinda muchibeela eechi, woonse muntu watakkutikana anzila njaasalwa aayo Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kukazya zyazwa munsaluuzyo zyakusala bantu aaba kwiinda mukutola kutongawuka kwakwe kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu mukati kamazuba musanu awabili kakuli koompololwa zina lyamuntu wakazunda bamwi munsaluuzyo zyakachitwa.

(2) ...

[ Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 5 (a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021] [Kabela kabambululwa achibeela 5(b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(3) Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu yeelede kuswiilizya ntongawuko yeetwa kuli njiyo, atala akusalwa kwaMuzwilidi waNyika, antoomwe akumana/kugomena kaambo aaka mukati kamazuba kkumi amazuba aane (14) kuzwa buunsi mbukeetwa kaambo kakutongawuka kuNkuta. Eezyo zyazwa mulubeta lwaNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu tazikazigwi pe.

(4) Mukulangisisya butongawusi bweetwa kuli njiyo Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu mbuuli kwaambwa mukabeela (1), inkuta eeyi ikonzya —

(a) koompolola wakazunda boonse munsaluuzyo yakachitwa;

(b) kuzimaazya nsaluuzyo zyakachitidwe kuchitila kuti zichitwe bupya, aawo mpukwategwa ngazichit we mupya kweelede zyachitwa mukati

kamazuba atayindi makumi musanu akkumi limwi (60) kuzwa buunsi nkuta niyazimaazya nsaluuzyo zyakachitidwe.

(c) kwaamba nzyikonzya kwaamba mbuuli maboneno aayo ayendelana azyakachitika.

(5) … [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 5(c) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]


Kutalika mulimu kwaMuzulwidi waNyika

(1) Muntu wasalwa kuba Muzulwidi waNyika utalika mulimu musule akutola chikonke kunembo lyaDunduumuntuule wabaJaji naakuti umwi Mubetesi uuliwo ulachuuno chaajulu kulibamwi mbuuli mbukwaambidwe kuMpampikwa yaTatu kakuli chikonke Muzulwidi waNyika nchayelede kuchita—

(a) buzuba bwamusanu abuzuba bwane musule akuti wasalwa; naakuti

(b) naa kwaba kutakkutikana kwakusalwa kwakwe, aakati kwamahola makkumi one alamusanu aatatu musule akuti Nkuta yaKkata lyaMulawu yoongolola kuti nguwe wazunda. [Kabeela kasowegwa achibeela 6 (a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Ooyo walaachuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika ulaya nembo kakkede achuuno kusikila buunsi ooyo mupya Muzulwidi waNyika natalika mulimu kwiindila mukabeela (1).

(2a) Kufwambaana naatalika mulimu, kweelede asale bantu batiindi babili kuba batobeli bakwe alimwi bali bantu bali aaziyandikana kuti baslawe mbuuli Muzulwidi waNyika kakutobelwa chabaka 91(1) alimwi batachite chikonke chakuba Batobeli baMuzulwidi waNyika kunembo lyaDunduumutule waJaji naakuti umwi uuliwo ulachuuno chaajulu kulibamwi mbuuli mbukubambidwe kuMpampikwa yaTatu. [ Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 6 (b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(3) Mutobeli wa Muzulwidi waNyika wabweza chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika kuti wakaliwo wafwa, wasiya mulimu naakuti wagwisigwa, ukonzya kutalika mulimu kuti kakuli wachita chikonke chachuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika kunembo lyaDuundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti umwi mubetesi umutobela mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yatatu . Chikonke eechi uyelede kuchichita kufwambaana mukati kama-awa ali makumi aane alaamusanu awatatu (48) kakuli chuuno chaMuzulwiid waNyika chasyaala kachitachikwe muntu. 95

Bulamfu bwachiindi chakubeleka kwa Muzulwidi waNyika antoomwe aBamutobela

(1) Bulamfu bwachiindi chakubeleka kwaMuzulwidi waNyika butalika buunsi nachita chikonke mbuuli mbukwaambwa muchibeela 94(1)(a) naakuti 94(3). [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 7 (a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Bulamfu bwachiindi chakubeleka kwaMuzulwidi waNyika —

[Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 7 (b) (i) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(a) buunsi naasiya chuuno naakuti naagwisigwa achuuno; naakuti

(b) nikwachitwa nsaluuzyo kakuli wasalwa lubo naakuti uumbi muntu wasalwa kuba Muzulwidi waNyika. Aanze kwazyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli bulamfu bwakukkala achuuno cha Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede kwaba minyaka musanu, eezi kweelede zyeendelana abulamfu bukkalwa abayiminini bamuPalyamenti. [Fuka libambululwa achibeela 7(b) (ii) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(3) Chiindi chakuba achuuno chaMutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika chitalika mubuzuba aawo mpabikwa aMululwidi waNyika alimwi musule akuchita chikonke kakutobelwa Chibeela 94(2a).

(4) Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika usiya mulimu wakwe musule akusiya mulimu naakuti musule akugwisigwa achuuno aMuzulwidi waNyika. [Tubeela (3) a(4) tubikwa aChibeela 7 ( c) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]


Kusiya mulimu kwa Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti Uumutobela

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kusiya chuuno chakwe kwiinda mukulemba lugwalo kuli Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu (National Assembly- Speaker). Sichuuno waNational Assembly uyelede kumwaya mulumbe ooyo, kuluundu lwaZimbabwe, wakusiya mulimu kwaMuzulwi waNyika achakufwambaana mukati kazuba limwi /mukati kamaawa makumi abili atune (24) kali wajana lugwalo lwakusiya mulimu kwaMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Mutobeli wa Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kusiya mulimu kwiinda mukulemba lugwalo kuMupati waNyika uyelede kwaambila bulumbu bwaZimbabwe aatala akusiya mulimu kwawutobela Muzulwidi waNyika mukati kama –awa asika kumakumi abile atune

(24). 97

Kugwisigwa kwa Muzulwidi waNyika [Mutwe wamakani ulabambululwa achibeela 8 (a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(1) Ing’anda Yaansi Yamilawu aNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu (Senate) kazili zyakkala aamwi zyavoota zikonzya kuzuminana kuti Muzulwidi waNyika ugwisigwe muchuuno chakwe kuti kakuli kwazuminana bwiingi bwabantu busika chisela chaboonse bajanika mumaanda amulawu wobilwe aaya. Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kugwisigwa kuti kakuli— [kabela kabambululwa achibeela 8(b)(i) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(a) wachita mulandu mupati loko;

(b) wakachilwa kutobelezya, kusumpula akukwabilila Kkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(c) watyola Kkata lyaMulawu eeli achaali; naakuti

(d) wakachilwa kuchita mulimu wakwe nkaambo kakuchembaala, kulemana naakuti kusondoka; weelede kulangisisigwa kwiinda muchibeela eechi

(e) katacheelede kuba achuuno chakuba Muzulwidi waNyika kakutobelwa chibeela 91(1)(c), naakuti kali mweni chisi (kunze kwakuba bumwenichisi nkabuli bwakupegwa) kwachimwi chisi muchiindi kali muchuuno. [Kabeela kabikwa achibeela 8 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Kakuli bazuminana kwiinda mukabeela (1), iKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti iyelede kugala kakkomiti kaswaanizya bantu bazwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu amuNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu basika kumusanu abane kabasweenene mbuuli mbutwiimvwi tubunga twakweendelezegwa kwanyika tuli muPalyamenti. Kkomiti eeyi iyelede kulangisisya kugwisigwa achuuno kwaMuzulwidi waNyika. [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 8 (c ) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(3) Kuti —

(a) kakuli ikkomiti yakasalwa kuti ilangisisye kugwisigwa kwaMuzulwidi waNyika, mbuuli zyambidwe mukabeela (2) kalaajulu, yaamba kuti ngagwisigwe achuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika [Fuka libambululwa achibeela 8 (d)(i) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(b) kakuli iseneti anational assembly, nizyaswaanana zyazuminana kugwisya Muzulwidi waNyika achuun; muzulwidi waNyika ulasiya mulimu [Fuka libambululwa achibeela 8 (d)(ii) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

Aboobo Muzulwidi waNyika anga wazwa achuuno [Fuka libambululwa achibeela 8 (d) (iii) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

Kukwabililwa kwa Muzulwidi waNyika kuli zyakwaangwa

(1) Kuti kachili achuuno, Muzulwidi waNyika tayelede kwaangwa akutolwa kunkuta pe nkaambo kabulewusi mbwachita naakuti nzyaaleka kuchita lwakwe.

(2) Ukonzya kwaangwa akutolwa kunkuta naasiya chuuno chakuba Muzulwidi waNyika kaangilwa nzyakachita anzyakaleka kuchita katanaba Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti naakali Muzulwidi waNyika.

(3) Zikwelete zyaMupati waNyika zyakazwa muchiindi katanaba Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti nakaba Muzulwidi waNyika ngazyachunzikwa kubbazu nachili achuuno.

(4) Mukubetekwa kwakwe naazwa achuuno wakali Muzulwidi waNyika kabetekelwa nikuba kabakali kaambo nkakachita ankakaleka kuchita kachili amulimu wakwe, ukonzya kulisakwida kaamba kuti wakazichita naakuti wakaleka kuzichita kachitila kukwabilila bukkale bwanyika. 99

Milimu yaBatobela Muzulwidi waNyika

Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika bagwasizya Muzulwidi waNyika kuchita milimu yakwe kabiyo balachita ayimwi milimu njibatumwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kazibikkilizya kweendelezya nikuba uuli Mutabi wafulumende naakuti Mulawu waPalyamenti ngubanooli bategwa bawuyendelezye. 100 Ujisilidi Muzulwidi waNyika

(1) Choonse chiindi kuti Muzulwidi waNyika katamo munyika naakuti katakonzyi kuchita milimu yakwe kwiinda mukuchiswa naakuti nkaambo kachimwi chintu, milimu yakwe yeelede kuchitwa —

(a) aMutobeli mutaanzi waMuzulwidi waNyika;

(b) aawo Mutobeli mutaanzi wa Muzulwidi waNyika mpatakonzyi kuchita milimu eeyo, Mutobeli wabili weelede kwiichita; naakuti

(c) kuti kakuli takukwe Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika uukonzya kuchita milimu yaMuzulwidi waNyika, umwi wabaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende—

(i) wagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuti achite mulimu waMuzulwidi waNyika; naakuti

(ii) wasalwa aNkamu yabaSilutwe baMitabi yafulumende (kkabbineti), kuti kakuli taakwe mweendelezi wamutabi wafulumende wakasalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kwiinda mufuka (i) lilaajulu.

(2) Aanze kwakuti zilokweendelana aachizuminano chakachitwa abanji baNkamu yabaSilutwe baMitabi yafulumende (kkabbineti) , muntu ujisilidi chuuno cha Muzulwidi waNyika kwiinda mukabeela (i) tayelede kubelesya manguzu aMuzulwidi waNyika—

(a) kutuma Basilumamba kunkondo kuti bakalwane;

(b) kusayina chizuminano chanyika yaZimbabwe chijatikizya manyika manji;

(c) kusala naakuti kugwisya achuuno Mutobeli wa Muzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende; naakuti

(d) kupa milimu naakuti kusandula milimu yaMutobeli wa Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti uMutobela kubikkikizya kweendelezegwa kwaMulawu waPalyamenti naakuti

chipanzi chamutabi wafulumende naakuti kunyeya akuzimaazya milimu njakali kuchita umwi wabaaba bantu baambwa aajulu. 101 Kutolwa kwachuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika aBamutobela kuti wafwa, wasiya mulimu naakuti katachikonzyi kubeleka

(1) Kakutobelwa chibeela (2) a(3), naa muntu waslwa kuba Muzulwidi waNyika kuli iili nsaluuzyo kuti wafwa, wasiya mulimu nnaakuti wagwisigwa achuuno —

(a) Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti Bbatobeli naa kakuli batobeli babili, Muitobeli waMuzulwidi wakaba wakuchinka kujata chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika kakutobelwa Chibeela 100, ulajata chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika mane kukabikwe Muzulwidi waNyika mupya kakutobelwa kabela (2); alimwi

(b) Chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika chitakwe muntu cheelede kubwezwa amuntu waslwa akabunga kazyabuleli bwanyika lyiimvwilidwi aMuzulwidi waNyika chiindi nchamuzundano walusaluulo.

(2) Kabunga kazyabuleli bwanyika kalacheelelo chakusala muntu kakutobelezegwa kabela (1)(b) keeled kazibya Sichuuno waNg’anda yaMulawu Yaansi aatala azina lyawoyo ngukasala kakutanayinda mazuba makkumi muisanu amakkumi one musule akuzwa achuuno kwaMuzulwidi waNyika waliwo achuuno. Kuzwa wwawo waslwa ulatalika mulimu mbuuli Muzulwidi waNyika musule akuchita chikonke chaMuzulwidi waNyika kakutobelwa Chibeela 94 kachili chikonke waslaw nchayelede kuchita kakutanayinda mahola makkumi one alamusanu aatatu musule akuti Sichuuno waNg’anda yaMulawu Yaansi kali wapegwa zina lyakwe.

(3) Naa ooyo wasalwa kuba Muzulwidi waNyika kuti wafwa, wasiya mulimu naakuti wagwisigwa achuuno alimwi nkatalikwe kabunga kazyabuleli bwanyika naakasalwa, Mutobeli wakwe naakuti kabali babili, Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika wakaba wakuchinka kujata chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika kakutobelwa Chibeela 100 ubweza chuuno chakuba Muzulwidi waNyika. [Kabeela kasowegwa achibeela 9 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

102 Mufolo wa Muzulwidi waNyika aBamutobela

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika aBamutobela beelede kujana mifolo, lumwi lugwasyo lwamali lupegwa babelesi, mpencheni azimwi zyaambidwe muMulawu waPalyamenti.

(2) Mufolo amali zyakubelesya zya Muzulwidi waNyika abamutobela zyeelede kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende.

(3) Muntu wakali Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti Uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kujana—

(c) Mpencheni iyelene amufolo ufolwa a Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti Uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika uchili aachuuno; kabiyo

(d) lumwi lugwasyo lwamali lupegwa babelesi amulimu azimwi zijanwa mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMulawu waPalyamenti. 103 Muzulwidi waNyika aBamutobela abamwi bakali bajata zyuuno eezi tabeelede kubeleka uumbi pe mulimu nibasiya zyuuno zyabo Muzulwidi waNyika aBamutobela abamwi bakali bajata zyuuno eezi tabeelede kubeleka uumbi pe mulimu wafulumende nibasiya zyuuno zyabo naakuti kuchita uumbi mulimu kuti kabachipegwa mpencheni izwa kuli fulumende yakuba wakali Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika.

CHIBAKA 3

BASILUTWE BAMITABI YAFULUMENDE, BABATOBELA ANKAMU YABASILUTWE BAMITABI YAFULUMENDE 104 Kugalwa kwaBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende aBabatobela

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika nguwe ugala akupa milimu yaBasilutwe baMitabi yafulumende, kubikkilizya kweendelezegwa kwamilawu isiyene-siyene yakapangwa aPalyamenti. Pesi Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kulyeendelezezya lwakwe imwi milawu njayanda kweendelezya lwakwe.

(2) Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kugala Batobeli baBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende batanoogwasizye kweendelezya mitabi yafulumende.

(3) Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela bagalwa kuzwa kubantu bakasalwa kuti bayiminine masena muNg’anda Yamilawu (Palyamenti) kabali maSenata antomwe abaabo bajanika muNational Assembly. Pesi Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kubweza bantu, basika kumusanu ababili, balaanze kwaPalyamenti kuti babe Basilutwe bamitabi yafulumende batanooli basalilwa luzibo ndubajisi. [Kabeela kalabambululwa achibeela 10 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(4) Mukugala Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela, Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede wabona kuti bantu bazwa kumasena asiyenesiyene munyika bagalwa antoomwe akubona kuti banakazi abaalumi bagalwa bweelene.

(5) Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela batakasalwa kuba bayiminini babantu mung’anda yakupangwa kwamilawu (Palyamenti) bakonzya kukkala akuyooma mung’anda yakupangwa kwamilawu pesi tabakonzyi kuvoota muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu (Senate) amuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(6) Kabatana kutalika mulimu bakagalwa kuba Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela kweelede bachita chikonke kunembo lya Muzulwidi waNyika, chikonke chaBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende mbuuli mbuchilembedwe muMpampikwa Yatatu. 105 Nkamu Yabasilutwe Bamitabi Yafulumende

(1) Kuli Nkamu ya Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende ijatikizya; Muzulwidi waNyika, kali nguSilutwe waNkamu eeyi, Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika antoomwe aBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende boonse bakagalwa.

(2) Sichuuno wamiswaangano yaNkamu yaBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende Muzulwidi waNyika pesi kuti katawo umwi waBatobela baMuzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kuba sichuuno wankamu eeyi kuti kabatawo umwi waBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende ukonzya kuba sichuuno mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 100(1)(c). 106 Mbubeelede kubeleka akulijata Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika, Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela

(1) Boonse Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika, Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela beelede kuchita milimu yabo antoomwe akulijata munzila iyendelana aKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(2) Muchiindi nibachili muzyuuno zyabo Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika, Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela tabakonzyi,—

(a) kubeleka uumbi mulimu wafulumende naakuti kubeleka uumbi mulimu ubapa mufolo;

(b) kulijata munzila isiyene aziyandwa amilimu yabo naakuti kuliyanika kuzintu zinga zilabalwanisya ziyandwa aamilimu yabo azeezyo nzibayanda beni mubuumi, naakuti

(c) kubelesya zyuuno zyabo naakuti luzibo ndubalaalo lweendelene azyamilimu yabo kuti balinotye lwabo naakuti bajane zimwi zintu munzila itali kabotu kababelesya zyuuno zyabo.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti weelede kupandulula kabotu mbubeelede kulijata Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika, Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela. 107

Kufolomwa kwa Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika, Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela

(1) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, boonse Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika, Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela bafolomwa aMuzulwidi waNyika ooyo ubona mabelekelo aabo,

(2) Boonse Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika, Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela beelede kunjila mung’anda yakupangwa kwamilawu muPalyamenti antoomwe amumakkomiti aPalyamenti nkukuti kabagwasizya kusandula mibuzyo iyendelana amitabi njibazulwida. 108 Bulamfu bwachiindi chakubeleka mulimu waBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela

(1) Chuuno chaBusilutwe bwaMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uumutobela chikonzya kusyaala kachitakwe muntu kuti —

(a) Muzulwidi waNyika waFulumende;

wagwisya

ooyo

wali

Silutwe

waMutabi

(b) kuti wajisi chuuno chaBusilutwe bwaMutabi waFulumende wasiya mulimu akuyanda kwakwe kwiinda mukulemba chizibisyo kuMuzulwidi waNyika chaamba kusiya mulimu kwakwe;

(c) kwanjila Muzulwidi waNyika mupya.

(2) Kwiinda mukabeela (4), Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uumutobela wakali mwiiminini mung’anda yamulawu naakagalwa kuba Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uumutobela, usiya chuuno chakwe kuti waleka kuba mwiiminini wabantu mung’anda yakupanga milawu (Palyamenti).

(3) Kwiinda mukabeela (4) Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uumutobela wakatali mwiiminini mung’anda yamulawu naakadomwa kuba Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uumutobela, usiya chuuno chakwe kuti waba mwiiminini wabantu mung’anda yakupanga milawu (Palyamenti).

(4) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kuti Palyamenti yazimaazigwa/ yamwayika, Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela beelede kweendelela nembo kabachita milimu yabo kusikila Muzulwidi waNyika mupya wataanguna mulimu nikwiinda nsaluuzyo. 109

Vooti yakutondeezya kubula lusyomo muli Fulumende

(1) Ing’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kazisweenene zikonzya kupanga chizuminano chazuminanwa abantu basika kuntompa-chaala yabantu boonse bajanika mumaanda obile, bakonzya kutondeezya kubula lusyomo muli fulumende.

(2) Mutwe wanjoomo yakuyanda kutondeezya kubula lusyomo muli fulumende ikonzya kutalikwa kwaambuulwa kuti—

(a) kwapegwa chizibisyo kuli Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu chakuyanda kuzoonjizya njoomo eeyo mumazuba musanu awabili aaza; kabiyo

(b) kakuli chizibisyo chanjoomo chakasayinwa abantu bayinda chisela chabaabo bajanika muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(3) Njoomo yakuyanda kutondeezya kubula lusyomo muli fulumende —

(a) iyelede kuyoomwa kazisweenene Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu mukati kamazuba makumi abili aazuba limwi (21) kali wakatambula chizibisyo Sichuuno; kabiyo

(b) iyelede kuvootwa akugomenwa/kumaninsigwa mukati kamiswaangano itayindi musanu ayibili pe kuzwa mpubakatalika kuswaanana kuyooma kaambo aaka, kuti teebachita boobo njoomo eeyi ilayangala.

(4) Kuti Palyamenti yazwa achizuminano chakubula lusyomo muli fulumende, Muzulwidi waNyika weelede, mukati kamazuba kkumi awane kuzwa mpuchakapangwa chizuminano chaPalyamenti —

(a) wabagwisya azyuuno zyabo boonse Basilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela, kuti kakuli teebakali basiya kale abalikke milimu kuzwa buunsi chizuminano chaPalyamenti nichakachitwa; naakuti

(b) kutatuula Palyamenti mpawo akwiitilizya nsaluuzyo zimbi mukati kamazuba asika kumakumi ali musanu amakumi aane (90).

(5) Kuti Muzulwidi waNyika taachita zyaambidwe mukabeela (4) mukati kamazuba ali kkumi aamazuba aane (14) kakuli chizuminano chaPalyamenti chachitwa chakutondeezya kubula lusyomo muli fulumende, Palyamenti inooli yayangala kale zili mumulawu.

CHIBAKA 4 MILIMU YABASILUTWE BAFULUMENDE 110 Milimu yaMuzulwidi waNyika aNkamu yaBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika ujisi manguzu ngapegwa aKkata lyaMulawu eeli antoomwe amulawu waPalyamenti naakuti imwi milawu kuti ayendelezye milimu yachuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, Muzulwidi waNyika ulaamukuli wakuchita milimu eeyi—

(a) kuzumina akusayina milawu;

(b) kutola malembe amulawu kuNkuta yaKkata lyaMulawu kuti amvwe maboneno aankuta eeyi atala amalembe amulawu aayo;

(c) kwiita bayiminini bamuNg’anda Yaajulu yaMilawu abamuNg’anda Yaansi yaMilawu naakuti Palyamenti kuti baswaanane muchiindi nchibatayelede kuswaanana kuchitila kuti bachite mulimu ngubatanooli batumwa mung’anda yakupanga milawu;

(d) kugala bantu kuti banjile muzyuuno zyaambidwe aKkata lyaMulawu aawo mpukuyandikana kuti Muzulwidi waNyika achite mbubo;

(e) kwiitila nsaluuzyo kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(f) kwiitila malefulandamu kuti achitwe aawo mpaayelede kuchitwa kwiinda mumulawu;

(g) kutuma Basilumamba kunkondo/kumulimu;

(h) kupa bantu zipo zyabulemu nkaambo kamilimu mipati njibakachitila nyika;

(i) kugala bayiminini banyika kulaamwi manyika, abamwi bazyuuno zyeendelene akwiimikilila nyika kuli zimwi nyika; kabiyo

(j) kutambula baabo bayimvwilidi nyika zimbi munyika yesu;

(3) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, Nkamu yaBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende bajisi mukuli wa —

(a) kweendelezya milimu yaMitabi yaFulumende;

(b) kweendelezya milimu yafulumende muPalyamenti;

(c) kubambila, kutalika akweendelezya milawu yanyika;

(d) kupanga akweendelezya mapolisi anyika; kabiyo

(e) kupa Muzulwidi waNyika busongo amizeezo atala azintu zinji.

(4) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kuchita zizuminano amanyika /bafulumende bamanyika aambi antoomwe atubunga tujatikizya manyika manji.

(5) Nzyawamba kuti zichitwe Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede zyabikkwa mumalembe nkukuti zitambulike.

(6) Mukuchita milimu yakwe Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede kapegwa mizeezo aNkamu yaBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende, aanze kwakuti ulokuchita milimu iyendelana azyaambidwe mukabeela (2) ajulu. 111 Nkondo aluumuno

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika ulaamanguzu akutalika nkondo akwiimikizya nkondo kabiyo weelede kwaambila Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu mukati kamazuba musanu awabili (7) kuti wabelesya manguzu aakwe kutalika nkondo akwiimikizya nkondo.

(2) INg’anda Yamilawu Yaajulu aNg’anda Yamilawu Yaansi kuti zyaswaanana zyachita chizuminano chabantu basika kuntompa-chaala yabantu bajanika mumaanda obile (Palyamenti), bakonzya kuzuminana kwiimikizya nkondo yali yatalikwa a Muzulwidi waNyika.

(3) Aawo Palyamenti mpuyachita chizuminano chakwiimikizya nkondo, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kutola ntaamo zyakwiimikizya nkondo yali yatalika pesi kalokubona kabiyo kuti banaZimbabwe boonse antomwe alubono lwanyika zili kwabilidwe. 112 Nguzu zyakulekelela

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika, kwiinda mukuzuwa aNkamu yabaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende, ukonzya kubelesya nguzu zyakulekelela, eezi kazyaamba —

(a) kulekelela naba uuli muntu wajanwa kajisi mulandu nikuba wawuli mulawu;

(b) kulekelela kuti muntu tapegwi chisubulo chamulandu ngwali wajatwa kalaawo;

(c) kusandula chisubulo chali chilemu chibe chuuba nchali wapegwa muntu wasunda; naakuti

(d) kusowa yoonse naakuti chipanzi chachisubulo chali chapegwa muntu wajanwa kalaamulandu; ukonzya kwaamba mbukweelede kweenzegwa kulekelelwa kwachitwa muntu ooyo.

(2) Kuti muntu wamu Zimbabwe wajanwa kalaamundu ankuta yakunyika iimbi nkwakasundila mulandu kwiinda mumilawu yanyika eeyo, Muzulwidi waNyika yaZimbabwe ukonzya kwaamba kuti mulandu wakasundwa kunyika ooko teemulandu pe mumulawu wamuZimbabwe.

(3) Kulekelela kwachitwa aMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti kuubulwa akugwisigwa kwachisubulo kweelede kudindwa akumwaywa mupepa lyaluundu litegwa Gazette.

113 Chiimo chalupyopyongano lwaluundu

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya koompolola chiimo chalupyopyongano lwaluundu munyika naakuti muli chimwi chibeela chanyika yaZimbabwe.

(2) Koompololwa kwachiimo chalupyopyongano lwaluundu kulayangala kuti kakuli taakwe chachitwa mukati kamazuba kkumi amazuba aane kuzwa buunsi bwamoompololo, kudindwa akumwaywa chiimo chalupyopyongano. Pesi kuti katanayinda mazuba kkumi amazuba aane koompololwa kwachiimo chalupyopyongano kwasimatizigwa aPalyamenti kazisweenene Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kwiinda mubwiingi bwabantu basika kuntompa-chaala bwabayiminini bamasena bajanika mumaanda aPalyamenti.

(3) Kuti Palyamenti yazimaazigwa mukati kamazuba aayo ali kkumi amazuba aane kachili choompololwa chiimo chalupyopyongano, moompololo ooyo ulayangala nikwiinda mazuba asika makumi abili alazuba limwi (21) kubikkikizya zuba lyakudindwa akumwaywa kwamoompololo, aanze kwakuti mukati kamazuba ngawaayo moompololo wasimatizigwa lubo abunji bwabayiminini bamuPalyamenti kazisweenene Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(4) Moompololo wachiimo chalupyopyongano wachitwa kwiinda mutubeela (2) naakuti

(3) unolaanguzu kusika kwiinda chiindi chamyeezi itatu kuzwa buunsi moompololo nuwadindwa akumwaywa, aanze kwakuti wakakwa moompololo ooyo.

(5) Kuti kakuli moompololo wachiimo chalupyopyongano tewazumininwa aPalyamenti naakuti nkaambo kabumwi bupanduluzi tewazumininwa aPalyamenti mukati kamazuba awambidwe muchibeela eechi, Muzulwizi waNyika weelede kuti mukati kamazuba musanu awabili wazimaazya moompololo ngwakali wachita.

(6) Kuti kakuli Palyamenti yazuminana, kazili zyaswaanana ng’anda zyakupangwa kwamilawu yaansi ayakujulu, kuti moompololo wachiimo chalupyopyongano—

(a)weelede uyinkilile kunembo kwachiindi chitayindi myeezi yotatwe,Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede achakufwambaana walampya bulamfu bwachiindi chamoompololo kwiinda mukudinda muGazette;

(b)weelede uzimaasigwe naakuti ubeleke mubusena buniini, Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede achakufwambaana wazimaazya moompololo ooyo naakuti wachita moompololo ulangene abusena buniini peepe nyika yoonse.

(7) Inkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kuti yeetelwa kaambo awumwi muntu kalangene amoompololo, ikonzya kupa maboneno aayo aatala —

(a) abwiime bwamoompololo wakachitwa wachiimo chalupyopyongano mumulawu;

(b) kuyungizigwa kwachiindi chamoompololo wachiimo chalupyopyongano.

(8) Nikuba iili nkuta ikonzya kulangalanga bwiime bwamulawu wakapangwa naakuti zyakachitwa nuwakachitwa moompololo wachiimo chalupyopyongano.

CHIBAKA 5 NABBATULE WABASIKUSOSELA MILANDU MUNKUTA 114 Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta

(1) Kuli Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika

(2) Muntu wagalwa kuba Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta utaanguna mulimu naamana kuchita chikonke chijanika muMpampikwa Yatatu kunembo lyaMuzulwidi

waNyika naakuti umwi muntu wapegwa mvumo aMuzulwidi waNyika kuti amuchitisye chikonke.

(3) Muntu uyelela kugalwa kuba Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta kuti kakuli ulaazyo ziyandikana kuti agalwe kuba Jaji wakuNkuta Yaajulu.

(4) Milimu yaNabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta njeyi—

(a) kuba mupati–kampatila mukupa mizeezo aatala azyamilawu kuli fulumende;

(b) kwiimikilila fulumende munkuta;

(c) kulemba milawu kayimvwilidi fulumende;

(d) kusumpula, kukwabilila akulemeka mulawu antoomwe akukwabilila zeezyo zigwasya luundu muli zyamilawu; kabiyo

(e) kuchita niiba iili milimu njanga ulapegwa kwiinda mumulawu upangwa aPalyamenti. Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta ukonzya kuchita milimu yoonse eeyi lwakwe naakuti kwiinda mubabelesi balaansi lyakwe

(5) Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta ukonzya —

(a) kukkala mumiswaangano yaBasilutwe (kkabbineti), pesi tajisi nguzu zyakuvoota;

baMitabi

yaFulumende

(b) kukkala akwaambuula muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu (Senete) amuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu , pesi tajisi nguzu zyakuvoota pe; kabiyo

(c) kulidilana ankuta mumilandu itanjizyi fulumende pe. 115 Kugwisigwa kwaNabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kugwisya Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta fumbwa chili chiindi nchaayanda.

CHAANDAANO 6 BASIKUPANGA MILAWU

CHIBAKA 1 NGUZU ZYAKUPANGA MILAWU 116 Basikupanga Milawu Basikupanga milawu muZimbabwe babikkilizya Palyamenti antoomwe aMuzulwidi waNyika bachita milimu yabo kwiinda muChaandaano eechi. 117 Bube abwiime bwanguzu zyakupanga milawu

(1) Nguzu zyakupanga milawu muZimbabwe zizwa kubulumbu bwanyika kabiyo zyeendelezegwa abasikupanga milawu kwiinda mumalayilile ajanika muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(2) Nguzu zyakupanga milawu zipegwa basikupanga milawu kwiinda mumanguzu—

(a) akubambulula Kkata Laymulawu eeli kwiinda muchibeela 328;

(b) akupanga milawu yakweeta luumuno akweendelezegwa kabotu kwanyika yaZimbabwe, kabiyo

(c) akupa nguzu zyakupanga milawu kuli zimwi mbunga naakuti tubunga kwiinda muchibeela 134.

CHIBAKA 2 PALYAMENTI 118 Palyamenti Palyamenti ijisi maanda obile:- Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu. 119 Mulimu waPalyamenti

(1) Palyamenti iyelede kukwabilila Kkata lyaMulawu eeli antoomwe akusumpula bulelo bwajandabanji muZimbabwe.

(2) Palyamenti ijisi manguzu akubona kuti zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli zyasumpulwa kabiyo tubunga ambunga zyafulumende kuzibeela zyoonse zyanyika, kuzwida kujulu kuyoosika aansi, zyatobelezya Kkata lyaMulawu aziyandwa aachisi mbuchizulwa.

(3) Kwiinda mukabeela (2), Palyamenti ijisi manguzu akubona mabelekelo ambunga antomwe atubunga twafulumende toonse kuzibeela zyoonse zyanyika.

CHIBAKA 3 NG’ANDA YAAJULU YAMILAWU 120

Bubumbwe bwa Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu

(1) Ng’anda Yaajulu YaMilawu ijisi bantu basika kumakumi musanu alaamakumi otatwe (80), kakuli —

(a) bali musanu awumwi ,6, basalwa kuzwa muZilikiti zyamuZimbabwe zyoonse kwiinda muchiimbo chakwiimikililana kakuya abwiingi bwabantu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (2);

(b) bali kkumi amusanu awumwi ,16, mbaSimwaami kakuli bobile basalwa aKkansili YaBaami yachimwi achimwi chilikiti aanze kwazilikiti zyamumadolopo mapati.

(c) Muzulwidi waKkansili aWumutobela;

yaBaami

Yanyika

Yoonse

antoomwe

(d) babili ,2, bayimvwilidi bantu balemene mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe muMulawu waNsaluuzyo.

(2) kusalwa kwamaSeneta kweelede kuchitwa kwiinda muMulawu waNsaluuzyo (Electoral Law) zyakuti kweelede kwabonwa kuti kusalwa kwabo kuchitidwe munzila ibikkilizya tubunga twazyanyika antoomwe akwiimikililwa kwabantu.

(a) zyeendelana amavooti akajanwa mukusalwa kwabayiminini batubunga twazyanyika muli chimwi achimwi chilikiti munsaluuzyo zyakuyanduula bazulwidi bayiminina bantu muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu; kabiyo

(b) zyakuti boonse banakazi abaalumi bayanda kwiiminina kusalwa kuba bazulwidi balembwa bweelene pesi zina taanzi mukulembwa kalili lyamwanakazi.

121 Ziyandikana kuti muntu asalwe antoomwe azinga zilapa kuti akakilwe kwiimikila bantu muNg’anda Yaajulu yaMilawu

(1) Muntu ukonzya kwiimikila kuti asalwe kuba Seneta mbuuli zyaambwa muchibeela 120(1)(a) naakuti (d) kuti kakul —

(a) ulembesezye kuvoota;

(b) ujisi minyaka isika naakuti kwiinda makumi aane; aanze kwakuti wakasigwa kwiiminina bantu kwiinda mukabeela (4) naakuti (5).

(2) Muntu ukonzya kwiimikila kuti asalwe kuba Seneta Mwaami mbuuli zyaambwa muchibeela 120(1)(b) kuti kakuli:(a) ujisi chuuno chabwaami muluundu;

(b)ulembesezye kuvoota; aanze kwakuti wakasigwa kwiiminina bantu kwiinda mukabeela (4) naakuti

(5).

(3) Muntu ukonzya kwiimikila kuti asalwe kuba Seneta mbuuli zyaambwa muchibeela 120(1)(b) kuti kakuli muntu uulemene mbuuli mbukupandulwidwe muMulawu waNsaluuzyo, aanze kwakuti ulakasigwa kwiinda mukabeela (4) naakuti (5).

(4) Muntu ukasigwa kwiimikila kuti asalwe kuba Seneta kuti kakuli —

(a) ulakasigwa kwiinda muMpampikwa Yane kuti alembesye kuvoota;

(b) mukati kaminyaka musanu kakutana kuchitwa nsaluuzyo, wakasiya chuuno chabuSeneta naakuti chakwiimikilila bantu muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kwiinda muchibeela 129(1)(i) nkaambo kakuti wakajanika kalaamulandu ankuta..

(5) Muntu ukasigwa kwiimikila kuti anjile munsaluuzyo zyakusala mwiiminini wakazwa/wakafwa wakali Seneta kuti kakuli uyimvwilidi bumwi kale busena muPalyamenti. 122 Muzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu yaMilawu

(1) Buunsi butaanzi nibaswaanana, mung’anda yamilawu, bantu bakasalwa kuba maSeneta kweelede, kabatana chita chimwi chintu, basala umwi wabo kuti abe Muzulwidi waSeneti utabe sichuuno nibachita njoomo zyabo mung’anda yamilawu eeyi.

(2) Choonse chiindi chuuno chaMuzulwidi waSeneti nichasyaala kachitakwe muntu kweelede kwasalwa uumbi muntu sikukkala achuuno eechi.

(3) Muntu ukonzya kusalwa kuba Muzulwidi waSeneti kuti kakuli wakali waba Seneta kale naakuti ulazyo zipa kuti asalwe kuba Seneta.

(4) Nsaluuzyo zyakusala Muzulwidi waSeneti zyeelede kweendelezegwa aMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti kagwasigwa aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe. Nsaluuzyo yansiswa kweelede yabelesegwa nikusalwa Muzulwidi waSeneti kabiyo butebuzi bwansaluuzyo bwazibisigwa mpawaawo.

(5) Katana talisya mulimu wakwe Muzulwidi waSeneti kweelede wachita chikonke chamulimu wakwe kunembo lyaDuundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti umwi mubetesi umutobela uliwo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yatatu.

(6) Muntu wasalwa kuba Muzulwidi waSeneti ngampawo wasiya chuuno chakwe chabuSeneta nkaako kweelede kwachitwa nsaluuzyo mubusena mbwakali kwiimikilila kuti kusalwe umbi Seneta utayiminine busena oobo.

(7) Muzulwidi waSeneti ukonzya kusiya chuuno chakwe kuti wayanda, kwiinda mukuzibisya maSeneta lwakwe. Pesi kuti Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu njazulwida kitalokubeleka chiindi nayanda kusiya chuuno chakwe, ukonzya kupa Mulembi Mupati waPalymenti chizibisyo chakwe chilembedwe aatala akuyanda kusiya chuuno chakwe.

(8) Muzulwidi waSeneti weelede kusiya chuuno chakwe kuti—

(a) buunsi Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu njazulwida niyaswaanana kwakutaanguna kuzwa nikwachitwa nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene bapya naakuti maSeneta mapya;

(b) naatambula chimbi chuuno chamulimu wafulumende naakuti kuti naanjila uumbi mulimu uteensi wafulumende;

(c) naaba Mwiiminini wabantu mu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu naakuti kuba Sichuuno wa Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(d) naaba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende;

(e) nikwaba zinga zilamwaangila kuti asiye chuuno chakwe kuti kakuli wakali Seneta; naakuti

(f) kuti kakuli kwaba chizuminano akati kamaSeneta chakumugwisya achuuno chakwe, chizuminano eechi chachitwa amaSeneta asika kuntompa-chaala yamaSeneta woonse ajanika mung’anda eeyi. 123 Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu yaMilawu

(1) Mbubatamane biyo kusala Muzulwidi waSeneti, maSeneta ayelede kusala umwi wabo kuti abe Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waSeneti.

(2) Choonse chiindi kuti wazwa walaachuuno chaMutobeli waMuzulwidi waSeneti kweelede basala uumbi Seneta kuti abweze chuuno eechi.

(3) Nsaluuzyo zyakusala Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waSeneti zyeelede kweendelezegwa aMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti kagwasigwa aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe. Nsaluuzyo yansiswa kweelede yabelesegwa nikusalwa Muzulwidi waSeneti kabiyo butebuzi bwansaluuzyo bwazibisigwa mpawaawo.

(4) Katana talisya mulimu wakwe Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waSeneti kweelede wachita chikonke chamulimu wakwe kunembo lyaDuundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti umwi mubetesi umutobela uliwo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yatatu.

(5) Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waSeneti ukonzya kusiya chuuno chakwe kuti wayanda kwiinda mukuzibisya Seneti lwakwe mwini. Pesi kuti Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu njazulwida kitalokubeleka chiindi nayanda kusiya chuuno chakwe, ukonzya kupa Muzulwidi waSeneti chizibisyo chilembedwe. Pesi kuti katawo Muzulwidi waSeneti ukonzya kupa Mulembi Mupati waPalymenti.

(6) Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waSeneti weelede kusiya chuuno chakwe kuti—

(a) nikwamana kuba Seneta kwakwe;

(b) naaba Sichuuno wamu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(c) naaba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende;

(d) kuti kakuli kwaba chizuminano akati kamaSeneta chakumugwisya achuuno chakwe, chizuminano eechi chachitwa amaSeneta asika kuntompa-chaala yamaSeneta woonse ajanika mung’anda eeyi.

CHIBAKA 4 NG’ANDA YAANSI YAMILAWU 124 Bubumbwe bwaNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu

(1) Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu iyimvwi boobu —

(a) bantu basika myaanda yobile ilaakkumi lyabantu mbaabo banga basalwa kwiinda munsaluuzyo kuzwa kumasena ngibayiminina mung’anda eeyi asika kumyaanda yobile ilaakkumi akagaanunwa munyika yaZimbabwe yoonse; kabiyo

(b) muchiindi chansaluuzyo zyone zitazoochitwe kazitobelana kuzwa buunsi Kkata lyaMulawu eeli nilyatalika kubeleka, kweelede kakusalwa banakazi basika kumakumi musanu alimwi (60). Musanu awumwi ,6, kabazwa muli chimwi achimwi chazilikiti zyaZimbabwe basalwa kwiinda mutubunga tweendelezya zyanyika kwiinda muchiimbo chakwiimikilana mubuvule kaziya amavooti ajanwa kamwi akamwi katubunga tweendelezya zyanyika muzilikiti. [Fuka libambululwa achibeela 11 (a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(c) bamwi biiminini babachikula balikkumi, zyaamba abntu balaminyaka yakuzyalwa iitalika kumakkumi obile awumwi kuyojinka kumakkumi atatu alamusanu, chilikiti chimwi achimwi chaZimbabwe chilamwiiminini womwe uusalwe munsi ampampikwa yakabunga kazyabuleli bwachisi munsi aanzila yakwiiminina yakweelenanisya kuzwa kumyeelwe yabantu bakavotela kabunga kazyabuleli bwanyika muchilikiti eecho—

(i) iyeme munsi anzila yakwiiminina yakweelanisya kwatubunga twabuleli bwachisi amyeelwe yamavoti atanoli ajanwa kunsaluuzyo yabiiminini bachilikiti; alimwi

(ii) bazundana bachaalumi abachikayintu babikidwe kabiinzizyania: Naa kakuli kuti tubunga twabuleli bwachisi tulasisimpizya kuti —

(a) biiminini bachikayintu balikkumi aakati kwamakkumi musanu akkumi lyomwe bali aansi amyaaka makkumi otatwe alamusanu;

(b) bachikayintu balabulema biimininwe ampampikwa zizwa kutubunga twazyabuleli bwachisi twabo

(c) bachikayintu bachikula balabulema biimininwe kumpampikwa yatibunga twabo twazyabuleli bwachisi kakutobelwa Mulawu waPalyamenti. [Fuka lyabikwa achibeela 11(b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Nsaluuzyo zyabayiminini babantu bakkala mu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kweelede zyachitwa kakutobelezegwa Mulawu waNsaluuzyo.

(3) Nzyayelede kuba aazyo muntu kuti alembesye kuvoota kabiyo akuvootela ngwayanda munsaluuzyo yabayiminini babantu mu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, zilaambidwe muMpampikwa Yane. 125 Ziyandikana kuti muntu asalwe antoomwe azinga zilapa kuti akakilwe kuba mwiiminini wabantu mu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu

(1) Muntu ulatambulwa kuti ayimine kusalwa kuba mwiiminini wabantu mu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kuti kakuli —

(a) ulembesezye kuvoota;

(b) ujisi minyaka isika naakuti iyinda makumi obile awumwi (21);aanze kwakuti wakasigwa kwiiminina bantu kwiinda muli zyaambidwe mukabeela (2) naakuti (3).

(2) Muntu ulakasigwa kuti asalwe kuba mwiiminini wabantu mu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kuti kakuli —

(a) wakasigwa kwiinda muMpampikwa Yane kuti alembesye kuvoota;

(b) mukati kaminyaka musanu kakutana kuchitwa nsaluuzyo, wakasiya chuuno chabuSeneta/naakuti chakwiimikilila bantu muNg’anda Yaansi

Yamilawu kwiinda muchibeela 129(1)(i) nkaambo kakuti wakajanika kalaamulandu ankuta.

(3) Muntu ukasigwa kwiimikila kuti anjile munsaluuzyo zyakusala mwiiminini wakazwa/wakafwa wakali MP kuti kakuli uyimvwilidi bumwi kale busena kali MP muPalyamenti. 126 Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu

(1) Buunsi butaanzi nibaswaanana bantu bakasalwa kuba bayiminini bamu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kweelede, kabatana chita chimwi chintu, basala Sichuuno utanoobe sichuuno nibachita njoomo zyabo mung’anda yamilawu eeyi.

(2) Choonse chiindi naasiya chuuno chabu Sichuuno kweelede, achakufwambaana, kwasalwa uumbi sikukkala achuuno eechi.

(3) Muntu ukonzya kusalwa kuba Sichuuno wa Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kuti kakuli wakali waba MP kale nsiku naakuti ulaazyo zipa kuti asalwe kuba MP.

(4) Nsaluuzyo zyakusala Sichuuno zyeelede kweendelezegwa aMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti kagwasigwa aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe. Nsaluuzyo yansiswa kweelede yatobelezegwa nikusalwa Sichuuno kabiyo butebuzi bwansaluuzyo bwazibisigwa mpawaawo.

(5) Katana talisya mulimu wakwe Sichuuno kweelede wachita chikonke kunembo lya Duundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti umwi mubetesi umutobela uliwo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yatatu.

(6) Muntu wasalwa kuba Sichuuno ngampawo wasiya chuuno chakwe chabuMP nkaako kweelede kwachitwa nsaluuzyo mubusena mbwakali kwiimikilila kuti kusalwe uumbi MP/ mwiiminini wabusena oobo kakutobelezegwa Mulawu waNsaluuzyo.

(7) Sichuuno ukonzya kusiya chuuno chakwe kuti wayanda kwiinda mukuzibisya maMP lwakwe. Pesi kuti Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu njazulwida kitalokubeleka chiindi nayanda kusiya chuuno chakwe, ukonzya kulemba chizibisyo chakusiya mulimu wakwe wapa Mulembi Mupati waPalymenti,

(8) Sichuuno weelede kusiya chuuno chakwe kuti —

(a) buunsi Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu njazulwida niyaswaanana kwakutaanguna kuzwa nikwachitwa nsaluuzyo zyakusala maMP aambi;

(b) naatambula chimbi chuuno chamulimu wafulumende naakuti kuti waanjila uumbi mulimu utensi wafulumende;

(c) naaba Mwiiminini wabantu mu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu naakuti kuba Muzulwidi waSeneti/waNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu;

(d) naaba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende;

(e) nikwaba zinga zilapa kuti asiye chuuno chakwe; kuti kakuli wakali mwiiminini wabusena muPalyamenti; naakuti

(f) kuti kakuli kwaba chizuminano akati kamaMP chakumugwisya achuuno chakwe, chizuminano eechi chachitwa amaMP asika kuntompa-chaala yamaMP woonse ajanika mung’anda eeyi. 127 Mutobeli wa Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu

(1) Mbubatamane biyo kusala Sichuuno maMP ayelede kusala umwi MP kuti abe Mutobeli waSichuuno mu Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(2) Choonse chiindi kuti wasiya chuuno Uutobela Sichuuno, kweelede basala uumbi muntu kuti abweze chuuno eechi.

(3) Nsaluuzyo zyakusala Mutobeli wa Sichuuno zyeelede kweendelezegwa aMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti kagwasigwa aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe. Nsaluuzyo yansiswa kweelede yabelesegwa nikusalwa Mutobeli waSichuuno kabiyo butebuzi bwansaluuzyo bwazibisigwa mpawaawo.

(4) Katana talisya mulimu wakwe Mutobeli wa Sichuuno kweelede wachita chikonke kunembo lya Duundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti umwi mubetesi umutobela uliwo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yatatu.

(5) Mutobeli wa Sichuuno ukonzya kusiya chuuno chakwe kuti wayanda kwiinda mukuzibisya maMP lwakwe. Pesi kuti Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu njazulwida kitalokubeleka chiindi nayanda kusiya chuuno chakwe, ukonzya kulemba chizibisyo chakusiya mulimu wakwe wapa Sichuuno. Pesi kuti katawo Sichuuno ukonzya kupa chizibisyo eecho kuMulembi Mupati waPalymenti.

(6) Mutobeli waSichuuno weelede kusiya chuuno chakwe kuti—

(a) nikwamana kuba MP kwakwe;

(b) naaba Muzulwidi waSeneti/Ng’anda Yamilawu Yaajulu;

(c) naaba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende;

(d) kuti kakuli kwaba chizuminano akati kamaMP chakumugwisya achuuno chakwe, chizuminano eechi chachitwa amaMP asika kuntompa-chaala yamaMP woonse ajanika mung’anda eeyi.

CHIBAKA 5 BULAMFU BWACHIINDI NCHIBEELEDE KUBA MUWOFESI BAYIMININA MASENA ANSALUUZYO MUPALYAMENTI


Chikonke chaMwiiminini Wabusena bwaNsaluuzyo muPalyamenti

(1) Katana talika mulimu wakwe Mwiiminini wabusena bwansaluuzyo muPalyamenti uyelede kukonka chikonke chabiiminini bamuPalyamenti chijanika muMpampikwa Yatatu.

(2) Chikonke eechi chaambidwe mukabeela (1) cheelede kukonkwa kunembo lyaMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti. 129

Bulamfu bwachiindi nchibeelede kuba muwofesi bayiminina masena ansaluuzyo mupalyamenti

(1) Chuuno chasikwiiminina busena bwansaluuzyo muPalyamenti nga chayanda uumbi muntu kuti kakuli —

(a) Palyamenti yamwaywa/yayangala;

(b) kuti wali mwiiminini busena bwansaluuzyo muPalyamenti wasiya mulimu akuyanda kwakwe kwiinda mukulemba lugwalo kuMuzulwidi waSeneti naakuti Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(c) wali mwiiminini waba Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti waba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika;

(d) wali mwiiminini waba Muzulwidi waSeneti naakuti waba Sichuuno naakuti wanjila muliimbi Ng’anda Yamilawu wasiya njakasalilwa kubamo;

(e) mwiiminini wagwisigwa mumalembe abantu bakonzya kuvoota;

(f) mwiiminini walova kumulimu, katakwe mvumo yazwa kuli Sichuuno naakuti kuMuzulwidi waSeneti, kwamazuba atobelana asika kumakumi obile azuba limwi (21) beenzinyina kabayinka kumulimu. Bayiminini bamung’anda yamulawu mwabede bakonzya kuvoota, abwiingi

bwabazuminana busika kuchisela chabo, kabazuminana kuti chuuno chasikulova ngachipegwe uumbi;

(g) kuti mwiiminini wamuPalyamenti watambula mulimu muli fulumende naakuti wachimwi chipanzi chafulumende, kukkansili yachilikiti naakuti yalimwi dolopo naakuti bbooma;

(h) kuti mwiiminini wamuPalyamenti wakajisi chuuno mulifulumende naakuti muli chimwi chipanzi chafulumende, mukkansili yachilikiti naakuti yalimwi dolopo/bbooma chiindi naakasalwa kuba mwiiminini mpawo wakachilwa kusiya chuuno chakwe mukati kamazuba asika makumi otatwe kuzwa buunsi naasalwa kuba mwiiminini wamu Palyamenti;

(i) kuti mwiiminini wamuPalyamenti wajanika kalaamulandu ankuta—

(i) yamu Zimbabwe, mulandu umugwisya bulemu, kulemekwa, kusyomwa kwakwe abantu, walupyopyongano akulwana; naakuti,

(ii) yaanze kwanyika yaZimbabwe, wanchito iinga kuti yalangwalangwa amumilawu yaZimbawe ipa mulandu ugwisya bulemu bwamuntu, kusyomwa kwakwe abantu, lupyopyongano akulwana, mpawo kali wakapegwa chisubulo chakukkala mujele kwamyeezi musanu awumwi naakuti kwiinda, katapedwe mweenya wakubbadala/kuliya mali naakuti katapedwe chimwi chisubulo chiteensi chakujalilwa mujele, aanze kwakuti mwiiminini wakumbila kuti mulandu wakwe ulangwelangwe lubo ankuta mpawo nuwa langwalangwa lubo kwajanika kuti chisubulo nchakapedwe chakali chipati loko mpawo wapegwa chisubulo chamyeezi ilaansi kwamusanu awumwi naakuti kupegwa chisubulo chitamunjizyi mujele.

(j) kuti mwiiminini wategwa tachikonzyi kutola zikwelete nkaambo kabuvule bwazikwelete nzyalaazyo mubuumi bwakwe kwiinda mumulawu wamuZimbabwe kabiyo katakwe nchachitwa kuti agwasigwe;

(k) kuti mwiiminini wazwa mukabunga kazyanyika nkakayiminide naakasalwa kuba mwiiminini kabiyo kabunga nkakayiminide kalembela kuli Sichuuno naakuti Muzulwidi waSeneti kwaamba kuti mwiiminini wakazwa mukabunga kabo kazyanyika;

(l) kuti mwiiminini taakali mukabunga kazyanyika naakasalwa pesi wazoonjila muli kamwi kabunga kazyanyika kali wasalwa kuba mwiiminini;

(m) kuti mwiiminini wajanika kuti wasondoka zyakuti tachiyeeyi kabotu eezi kazili zyaba abukamboni kwiinda mumilawu yamuZimbabwe; naakuti

(n) kuti mwiiminini wajanika kajisi mulandu uyendelana amulawu wansaluuzyo kabiyo woompololwa aNkuta Yaajulu kuti wagwisigwa mumalembe abantu bavoota naakuti moompololo umukasya kuvoota kunsaluuzyo niziba zili.

(2) Mwiiminini uwambwa mukabeela (1)(i) ooyo wakumbila kuti kujanika kalaamulandu kwakwe kulangwelangwe lubo ankuta yaajulu, ukonzya kujana bukkala achuuno chakwe chabwiiminini nachilinda kuti kulangwalangwa kwamulandu wakwe kuchitwe ankuta yaajulu kusikila nkuta yapa maboneno aayo. Muchiindi eechi nachilinda kumvwa maboneno ankuta yaajulu, mwiiminini ukonzya kuchita milimu yakwe yoonse yalubbe antoomwe akutambula mali aanze kwakuti nkuta yati ngakalindile maboneno ankuta yaajulu kali mujele.

(3) Mwiiminini wasalwa kuba Sichuuno cha Kkansili yaChilikiti ulasiya chuuno chakwe chamwiiminini nchaajisi katanaba Sichuuno waKkansili yachilikiti.

CHIBAKA 6 NGUZU ZYAKUPANGA MILAWU AZIMWI NGUZU 130 Manguzu antoomwe amilimu yaNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu a Ng’anda Yaansi yamilawu

(1) Aanze kwazeezyo zyaambidwe muMpampikwa Yamusanu, mukubelesya manguzu aazyo aakupanga milawu ng’anda zyaPalyamenti zyobile (yaajulu ayaansi) zilaamanguzu akutalika, kubambila, kulangalanga antoomwe akukaka fumbwa nuuba uuli mulawu.

(2) Aajulu kwamilimu yabo yaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, Ng’anda Yaajulu aNg’anda Yaansi zikonzya kuchita imwi milimu njizyapegwa ayimbi milawu. 131 Milawu iPangwa aPalyamenti antoomwe anzila zitobelezegwa mukwiipanga

(1) Manguzu aPalyamenti akupanga milawu abeleka kwiinda mukupanga Milawu ipangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Mulawu waPalyamenti malembe amulawu aka —

(a) langwalangwa akutambulwa amaanda obile aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(b) zuminwa mpawo asayinwa aMuzulwidi waNyika; kakutobelezegwa zilembedwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(3) Mabala awamba kubumba mulawu waPalyamenti ngaya ‘Wakabumbwa aPalyamenti antoomwe aMuzulwidi waNyika yaZimbabwe’ naakuti amwi mabala awamba zikozyania azeezi.

(4) Nzila zitobelwa aNg’anda Yaansi aYaajulu mukupanga milawu ili pandulwidwe muMpampikwa Yamusanu.

(5) Kuti malembe amulawu atambulwa akuzuminwa amaanda amilawu obile kakutobelezegwa zilembedwe muMpampikwa Yamusanu, Muzulwidi waSeneti antoomwe aSichuuno waNg’anda Yamilawu Yaansi, kweelede umwi wabo achakufwambaana—

(a) watumizya malembe amulawu, akatambulwa akuzuminwa amaanda amilawu obile, kuMuzulwidi waNyika kuti akazuminwe akusayinwa antoomwe asetifiketi lyeelede kweenda aamwi amalembe amulawu

(Bill); kabiyo

(b) amwaye mulumbe mumapepa kwaambila luundu buunsi naakatumizya malembe amulawu kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

(6) Kuti malembe amulawu atolwa ku Muzulwidi waNyika kuti akazuminwe akusayinwa kweelede kuti, mukati kamazuba makumi obile azuba limwi, wachita chimwi chazeezi zitobela —

(a) waazumina akwasayina malembe aayo mpawo awadinde akwamwaya muGazette achakufwambaana; naakuti

(b) kuti waabona kuti malembe ngaapegwa taayendelani pe aKkata lyaMulawu naakuti ulaachimwi nchabona chitali kabotu aatala amalembe amulawu ngapegwa ukonzya kwabweedezya kuPalyamenti, kwiinda kuMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti, antoomwe alugwalo lupandulula zizwide zyapa kuti aleke kuwazumina akuwasayina Muzulwidi waNyika malembe amulawu.

(7) Kuti malembe amulawu akakwa aMuzulwidi waNyika mpawo abweedezegwa kuPalyamenti kwiinda mbuuli mbukaamba kabeela(6)(b), Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu uyelede kwiita maMP kuti aze kumulimu kuti—

(a)alangelange malembe amulawu akaboola kuzwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika akulangisisya zyakapa kuti aboozegwe; naakuti

(b)kuzuminana kuti malembe amulawu aaya ali kabotu mbuuli mbaabede taayandi kubambululwa pe. Chizuminano eechi kweelede chachitwa amaMP asika kuntompa-chaala yamaMP akkala muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu; nikuba kuti nziizili nzibachita maMP, Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu ulatumizya lubo malembe amulawu kuMuzulwidi waNyika achakufwaambana kuchitila kuti akazuminwe akusayinwa malembe. Alubo Sichuuno ulamwaya mumapepa kuzibisya luundu buunsi mbwatumizya malembe kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

(8) Kuti malembe amulawu(bill) abwedezegwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika kwiinda mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (7) kaali akabambululwa kubikkikizya nzyakaambide Muzulwidi waNyika, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kwaazumina akwasayina malembe aaya mukati kamazuba makumi obile azuba limwi mpawo awadinde akwamwaya mupepa lyaluundu (Gazeti) achakufwambaana. Pesi kuti Muzulwidi waNyika katana kkutikana abwiime bwamalembe amulawu aboozegwa kuli nguwe, uyelede kuti mukati kachiindi chikozyania, wachita chimwi chazeezi zyaambidwe aansi—

(a) waazumina akwasayina malembe amulawu nikuba katakkutikene angawo; naakuti

(b) kutola malembe amulawu aayo kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kuti akalangwe bweendelene bwawo malembe aaya aKkata lyaMulawu.

(9) Kuti kakuli malembe amulawu akatolwa kuNkuta yaKkata lyaMulawu, kwiinda mukabeela (8), mpawo Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu yaamba kuti malembe amulawu aleendelana aKkata lyaMulawu, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kutola malembe amulawu awazumine antoomwe akwasayina achakufwambaana mpawo akwadinda akwamwaya muGazette.

(10) Kuti malembe amulawu atumizigwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika kuti akazuminwe akusayinwa pesi katakwe setifiketi iyandikana kutumizigwa aamwi amalembe amulawu mbuuli mbulyaamba Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, Muzulwidi waNyika tayelede kuwazumina pe nikuba kwasayina malembe amulawu aayo kusikila setifiketi yeetwa. Pesi Muzulwidi waNyika weelede walembela Palyamenti, kwiinda kuMulembi Mupati waPalyemnti, kuti setifiketi teeyakatumizigwa aamwi pe amalembe amulawu nkaako iyanda kutumizigwa achakufwambaana. 132 Kutalika kubeleka kwaMilawu ipangwa aPalyamenti Mulawu wapangwa aPalyamenti utaanguna kubeleka kumatalikilo kwazuba nuwazibisigwa kuti wabeleka kwiinda muGazette naakuti kumatalikilo kwazuba lyaambidwe mumulawu mupya naakuti buunsi bwakaambwa mulaamwi malembe. 133 Kunjizigwa kwamilawu ipangwa a Palyamenti

(1) Kuti Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti wazuminwa akusayinwa aMuzulwidi waNyika, Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti weelede kutumizya mulawu mupya ulaanzilingo yaMuzulwidi waNyika antoomwe aChidindo chaChisi ku-ofesi yaRejistra waNkuta Yaajulu, kuchitila kuti mulawu mupya ukabikkwe mu-ofesi eeyi. Nkaako malembe amulawu mupya ooyu mbubukamboni kutegwa kwaanzwa mulawu mupya kusikila mulawu ooyu wabambululwa aPalyamenti kabiyo kwiinda mukabeela (2)

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kuba mulawu wabwini naakuti chipanzi chawo, ukonzya kudindwa kakuli wabambululwa kabiyo ukonzya—

(a) kuti chiindi nuwadindwa wabambululwa kuuli ulakozyania awooyo mutaanzi;

(b) malembe akabambululwa ayelede kupegwa wofesi yaRejistira waNkuta Yaajulu ukabambwe; kabiyo

(c) mulawu wabikkwa muwofesi yaRejestira waNkuta Yaajulu nguwo upa bukamboni bwakuti kwakaba mulawu mupya.

(3) Kutaanguna kubeleka kwaMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti naakuti wakabambululwa takulindi kuti mane ukabikkwe mu-ofesi yaRejesitira waNkuta Yaajulu pe. 134 Imwi milawu igwasilizya Palyamenti ikonzya kuti, kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti, yapa manguzu aayo akupanga tulawu-lawu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mumulawu utanooli wabikkwa, pesi —

(a) mukuli waPalyamenti wakupanga milawu tuyelede kupegwa kambi pe kabunga/mbunga;

(b) tulawu-lawu tatweelede kudyaamizya zyeelelo angwangunuko zijanika muZyeelelo Zyampandamunsaka;

(c) tulawu-lawu tweelede kweendelana aMulawu ngutwakabumbwa kuti tugwasizye munsi lyawo;

waPalyamenti

(d) Mulawu waPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula kabotu kujinka manguzu antoomwe azimwi zyeelede kujatwajatwa akalawulawu kapangwa kakweendelana andemeko zyeelede kutobelezegwa mukupanga kalawulawu.

(e) tulawu-lawu tatukonzyi kuba aanguzu zyamulawu domane twadindwa muGazette; kabiyo.

(f) tulawu-lawu tweelede kweetwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu mbuuli malayilile aliwo kakutobelwa Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti. Tweelede kulangwalangwa aKkomiti Yazyamilawu ijanika muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

CHIBAKA 7 MEENDELEZYO AAMILIMU MUPALYAMENTI 135 Silutwe waPalyamenti

(1) Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu nguSilutwe waPalyamenti pesi uyelede kubeleka katobelezya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(2) Kakutobelezegwa Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti, Muzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu nguwe uutobela kuba Silutwe waPlayamenti kuti Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu katawo naakuti kakuli chimwi chachitika. 136

Bantu bazulwida njoomo muPalyamenti

(1) Muntu uzulwida njoomo fumbwa chiindi achiindi nikuyoomwa makani muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu kweelede —

(a) kali Muzulwidi waSeneti, kuti katawo , kweelede umutobela wazulwida; naakuti

(b) kuti katawo Muzulwidi waSeneti naakuti Umutobela, umwi Seneta wasalwa kuti achite mulimu ooyo ulakonzya kuzulwida njoomo fumbwa ooyo wasalwa katali Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende.

(2) Muntu uzulwida njoomo fumbwa chiindi achiindi nikuyoomwa makani muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kweelede —

(a) kali Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, kuti katawo, kweelede umutobela wazulwida; naakuti

(b) kuti katawo Sichuuno naakuti Umutobela, umwi wabiiminini baNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu (MP) wasalwa kuti achite mulimu ooyo ulakonzya kuzulwida mbandiko fumbwa ooyo wasalwa katali Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende.

(3) Sichuuno, naakuti katawo Sichuuno, Muzulwidi waSeneti, umwi wabo weelede kuzulwida mbandiko ijatanisya maanda obile amilawu (Yaansi aYaajulu). 137

Buvule bwabantu beelede kutalika njoomo muPalyamenti

Maanda obile amilawu ayelede kuzwa aNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti zipandulula buvule bwabantu beelede kubawo mumaanda amilawu kuti njoomo zitalike antoomwe akweendelela nembo amulimu wamumaanda amilawu. 138 Kuvoota acheelelo chakumvwigwa muPalyamenti

(1) Aanze kwawaawo Kkata lyaMulawu mpulyaamba busiyene —

(a) zyoonse zyaambo naakuti mibuzyo iyanda kuvootelwa mumaanda amilawo obile zimaninsigwa kwiinda mukutobela zeezyo zyazuminanwa aabantu banji baliwo chiindi chakuvoota;

(b) muntu uzulwida njoomo mumaanda amilawu obile takonzyi kuvoota nikwasika chiindi chakuvootela makani ayanda kugomenwa/ kumaninsigwa;

(c) kuti mavooti atala amakani alikuyoomwa mumaanda amilawu obile ayelana kumabazu obile, chaamba kuti kaambo kali kuvootelwa kayangala nkaambo taakwe bbazu lyazunda limwi pe.

(2) Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika bakonzya kukkala akwaambuula munjoomo zyamuPalyamenti pesi tabakwe nguzu zyakuvoota pe mumaanda amilawu obile.

(3) BaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende abaBatobela bakonzya kukkala akwaambuula munjoomo zyamuPalyamenti pesi tabakwe nguzu zyakuvoota pe muNg’anda Yamulawu mubateensi bayiminini.

(4) Bayiminini bali muKkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti antoomwe awambi makkomiti ajanika muPalyamenti balacheelelo chakukkala akwaambuula munjoomo zya muPalyamenti nikuba muliili ng’anda yamulawu, pesi tabakwe nguzu zyakuvoota pe muNg’anda Yamulawu mubateensi bayiminini.

(5) Aawo mwiiminini wamuPalyamenti, utajisi chuuno chaSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabo waFulumende, wakonzya kunjizya Malembe azyaMulawu muliimwi yamaanda amilawu, ooyo wanjizya malembe azyaMulawu ulacheelelo chakukkala akwaambuula munjoomo zyaatala aMalembe azyaMilawu alokubandikwa mung’anda yamulawu, pesi takwe nguzu zyakuvoota pe muNg’anda Yamulawu mwateensi mwiiminini. 139

Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti

(1) Meendelezyo aanjoomo amilimu yamuPalyamenti mung’anda zyobile zyamilawu azulwidwa amiteto itegwa Nzila Ziyalidwe Mumalembe aPalyamenti. Miteto eeyi ibumbwa abayiminini bayimwi ayimwi ng’anda yamilawu naakuti kazisweenene ng’anda eezi kuswaanana kwazyo kakuli kwasungwaazigwa aKkomiti ibona zya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(2) Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti zikonzya kuzulwida bupanduluzi bwamachitilo atobela —

(a) kupangwa kwamilawu yanyika;

(b) kupangwa amabelekelo amaKkomiti antoomwe akuwapa nguzu zyawo;

(c) mbaabelesegwa manguzu ngibalaawo maMP antoomwe akukwabililwa kwamaMP nibachita njoomo zyabo mumaanda amilawu;

(d) mbubeelede kubuzigwa mibuzyo baSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende ababatobela kababuzigwa abayiminini bamumaanda amilawu;

(e) mbubeelede kulijata maMP/bayiminini kuti kabali muPalyamenti;

(f) kubelesegwa kwamanguzu aluundu kuti batongawuke kuPalyamenti kuti kakuli zitalookubajata kabotu; kabiyo

(g) meendelezyo aamilimu yamuPalyamenti muliimwi ayimwi ng’anda yamulawu naakuti akati kamaanda amilawu.

(3) Kweendelezegwa kwamilimu yamuPalyamenti amumaKkomiti aPalyamenti, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti, kweelede kusungwaazya bubalangale mumachitilo amilimu yaPalyamenti kabiyo kakubikkikizya boonse bayiminini bajanika muPalyamenti bamutubunga twazyanyika tusiyenesiyene toonse tuli muPalyamenti. Mabelekelo aPalyamenti aaya ayelede kusungwaazya luundu kuti lujane mweenya wakubikka mizeezo yalo mumilimu yaPalyamenti. Zyoonse eezi zyeelede kuchitwa munzila iyelanisya boonse bantu munzila yabululami.

(4) Woonse maKkomiti abumbwa kwiinda muNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti ayelede kutondeezya bwiime, bube abubumbwe bwaPalyamenti mutubunga twazyanyika tujanika muPalyamenti antoomwe abwiingi bwabanakazi abaalumi bajanika muPalyamenti naakuti mung’anda yamilawu muzibeleka Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti. 140 Milumbe izwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika kiiya kuPalyamenti

(1) Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kwaambuula aPalyamenti nikuba iili ng’anda yamilawu naakuti kazisweenene ng’anda zyamilawu zyobile (Yaansi aYajulu).

(2) Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kutumizya milumbe nikuba kuliili ng’anda yamilawu, nkaako milumbe eeyo yatumizigwa yeelede kubalwa aMutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti umwi muntu uzulwida njoomo mung’anda yamilawu yatambula milumbe eeyo.

(3) Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kukkala munjoomo zyaPalyamenti kuchitila kuti asandule mibuzyo yabuzigwa mbuuli mbukuyalidwe mu Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(4) Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede wapa ngambo amilumbe kumaanda amilawu kasweenene bumwi naakuti twiingi amunyaka aatala abwiime bwachisi, nkaako Sichuuno aMuzulwidi waSeneti beelede kuchita mabambe akuti bayiminini bajanika mumaanda amilawu obile kuti bazimvwe ngambo amilumbe eezyo zyazwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika. 141

Kubelekelaamwi kwaPalyamenti abulumbu bwaZimbabwe Palyamenti iyelede —

(a) kuchita kuti kuube kuti bulumbu bukonzye kunjizya mizeezo yabo mukupangwa kwamilawu yanyika antoomwe amumabelekelo amakkomiti aPalyamenti;

(b) kubona kuti bantu boonse bayanda kupa mizeezo atala milawu ilokupangwa muPalyamenti bapegwa mweenya wakupa mizeezo yabo aanze kwakuchiti kakuli buyumuyumu bupa kuti bantu tababikki mizeezo yabo mukupangwa kwamilawu muPalyamenti; kabiyo

(c) kuchita milimu yayo munzila ilaantangana ibalangala, kiichita miswaangano yayo amiswaangano yamakkomiti amuPlayamenti mubusyu bwabantu, nikuba kuti ntaamo kweelede zyatolwa kuti—

(i) kubelekeke kube luumuno muPalyamenti;

(ii) kubona kuti luundu abasimusunza twaambo banjizigwa muPalyamenti achiindi cheelede;

(iii) kukasya bulumbu antoomwe abasikuzunza twaambo kunjila mumiswaangano yamakkomiti aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(iv) kusecha bantu banjila muPalyamenti naakuti kukasya bantu kuti tabanjili muPalyamenti bateelede naakuti kugwisya bantu muPalyamenti; pesi ntaamo eezi zyeelede kuchitwa mububalangale kazitondeezya kuti ninyika ilaabulelo bwajandabanji busimpide mubululami, kulemekwa kwabuntu abulemu bwamuntu, kweelanisigwa kwabantu antoomwe akulemekwa kwangwangunuko zyabantu 142

Butambulwe abukakwe bwanjoomo zyaPalyamenti

(1) Kutabawo kwawumwi naakuti kwaandwa kwawumwi mwiiminini mumaanda amilawu takwiimikizyi njoomo pe naakuti kwiimikizya milimu yaPalyamenti mumaanda amilawu obile.

(2) Kuti kwaba umwi muntu, mumaanda amilawu obile, wateelede kubamo munjoomo amukuvootela twaambo muPalyamenti, tazyaambi kuti njoomo eezyo antoomwe azyazuminanwa zyeelede kuyangala pe, zyazuminanwa zinoochitambulika.

CHIBAKA 8 BULAMFU BWAKUKKALA, KUMWAYIKA AKUBELEKA KWAPALYAMENTI 143

Bulamfu bwakukkala akumwaywa kwaPalyamenti

(1) Bayiminini bamuPalyamenti bavootelwa kuti babeleke kwachiindi chisika kuminyaka musanu kuzwa buunsi Muzulwidi waNyika naatalika mulimu kakutobelezegwa zilembedwe muchibeela 94(1)(a) chamulawu ooyu. Palyamenti nga yamwayigwa akati-kabo kwabuzuba nikucha kakuli nsaluuzyo/mavooti akusala bazulwidi bamuzibaka zisiyenesiyene mbuuli kusala umbi Muzulwidi waNyika, bambi bayiminini bamuPalyamenti abambi maKkansela mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 144 chamulawu ooyu.

(2) Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kuti, kwiinda mumoompolo, wamwaya Palyamenti kuti lwawo maanda amilawu obile akkala imwi ayimwi mpawo azuminana kuti Palyamenti imwayigwe. Mavooti aya ayelede kusika kuntompa-chaala yabwiingi bwabayiminini bajanika muliimwi ayimwi ng’anda yamulawu.

(3) Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kumwaya Palyamenti, kwiinda mumoompolo, kuti Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu yakaka kuzumina mulawu uwambidwe muchibeela 305 chamulawu ooyu.

(4) Kumwaywa kwaPalaymenti kwiinda muli zilembedwe mukabeela (3) zikonzya kuti, kwiinda mukulembela kwawumwi mwiiminini wamuPalyamenti, kwabikkwa aabbazu kakuyanda kuti kulangwelangwe kabotu aNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu.

(5) Malembe akuti muzeezo wakumwaywa kwaPalaymenti ulangwelangwe ayelede kutolwa ku Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu mukati kamazuba musanu aabili (7) kuzwa buunsi moompololo nuwachitwa wakumwaya Palyamenti, kabiyo —

(a) iNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kweelede iyoome antoomwe akumaninsya kaambo keetwa kuli njiyo mukati kamazuba asika kukkumi amazuba aane (14) kuzwa buunsi kaambo nikeetwa kunkuta; kabiyo

(b) nikuchilindwa mazembe azwa muNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, moompololo wakumwaywa kwaPalyamenti nga wajana bwiimikizigwa.

Nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene nikwamwaywa Palyamenti

(1) Aawo Palyamenti mpuyatazuminana akumwaywa kwayo mbuuli mbukulembedwe muchibeela 143(2) chamulawu ooyu, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kuti, kwiinda mumoompololo, waamba mazuba akuzoochitwa kwansaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene mukati kachiindi chaambidwe muchibeela 158 chamulawu ooyu.

(2) Aawo—

(a) Palyamenti mpuyazuminana kuti imwaywe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 143(2) chamulawu ooyu;

(b) Muzulwidi waNyika naamwaya Palyamenti kwiinda muchibeela 143(3) chamulawu ooyu;

(c) Muzulwidi waNyika wamwaya Palyamenti kwiinda mukutondeezya kubula lusyomo kakutobelezegwa chibeela 109(4); naakuti

(d) Palyamenti yakkala kiimwayidwe nkaambo kakutondeezya kubula lusyomo kwiinda muchibeela 109(5)a. Muzulwidi waNyika weelede, kwiinda mumoompololo, waamba mazuba akuchitwa kwansaluuzyo yakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene zyeelede kuchitwa mukati kamazuba asika mumakumi musanu amakumi aane (90) kuzwa buunsi Palyamenti niyamwaywa

(3) Mazuba akuchita nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene kakutobelezegwa kabeela (1) naakuti (2) ayelede kwaambwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kabapana mizeezo aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe. 145

Kuswaanana kutaanzi kwaPalyamenti nizyamana nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene

(1) Kuswaanana kwaPalyamenti nikwamana kuchitwa nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene kweelede kuchitwa mubuzuba bwaambwa aMuzulwidi waNyika pesi buzuba oobo bweelede kuba mukati kamazuba makumi otatwe kuzwa buunsi natalika kubeleka Muzulwidi waNyika wakasalwa kakutobelezegwa zyaambidwe muchibeela 94 chamulawu ooyu.

(2) Kusikila kwasalwa Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu naakuti kusalwa kwaMuzulwidi waSeneti muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu, Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti nguwe uyendelezya kuswaanana kwamaanda aaya. 146

Chiindi chakuswaanana achakulyookezya

Imwi ayimwi ng’anda yaPalyamenti ilalibikkila zyiindi abulamfu bwakuswaanana kwabo mung’anda yamulawu antoomwe amazuba akulyookezya, pesi —

(a)Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kwiita Palyamenti kuti iswaanane ibeleke mulimu nguyayitilwa fumbwa chiindi nikwayandikana;

(b)takweelede pe kuti kwiinda mazuba asika kumwaanda omwe amazuba makumi musanu awatatu (180) kilyookezezye Palyamenti. 147

Kuyangala kwamaBills, mitwe yanjoomo azimwi mbandiko zyakali kuchitwa kiitana mwayika Palyamenti

Aakumwayika kwaPalyamenti zyoonse njoomo , ngambo, amabills zyakali kubandikwa nga zyayangala.

CHIBAKA 9 ZIMWI BIYO ZYEENDELENE AAPALYAMENTI

Lukwabililo anzibeelede kuchitilwa bayiminini bamuPalyamenti

(1) Muzulwidi waSeneti, Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu antoomwe abayiminini bamuPalyamenti bajisi ngwangunuko yakwaambuula nzibayanda kuti kabali muPalyamenti, amumakkomiti aPalyamenti. Nikuba kuti bayelede kutobelezya tulawulawu twameendelezyo aPalyamenti, pesi tabeelede pe kwaangwa, kubikkwa mujele naakuti kutolwa kunkuta kuti bakaliye nkaambo kazyaambo nzibaamba kabali muPalyamenti naakuti mumakkomiti aPalyamenti.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya—

(a) kupa zimwi nzibeelede kuchitilwa, lukwabililo awamwi manguzu aPalyamenti antoomwe abayiminini bamuPalyamenti;

(b) kupandulula kabotu nchito zibonwa kazili zyakuubya akunyansya Palyamenti nikuba kazili zyachitwa abayiminini bamuPalyamenti naakuti bamwi bantu luzutu;

(c) kupa cheelelo chakusandulwa, kwiinda muli Sichuuno naakuti Muzulwidi waSeneti, kuli baabo batanooli bamvwa machise nkaambo kazeezyo zyaambwa atala ambabo muchiindi chambandiko zyaPalyamenti. pesi ooyo mulawu waPalyamenti tukonzyi kupa manguzu akuti Palyamenti naakuti bayiminini bamuPalyamenti bape chisubulo cheelene achakutolana kunkuta yamulawu pesi wasunda uyelede kubbadalisigwa biyo nkaambo kakutyola zyeelelo zyabantu bamuPalyamenti naakuti kuubya akunyansya Palyamenti 149

Cheelelo chakutongawukila Palyamenti kwiinda mumalembe

(1) Woonse munaZimbabwe amuntu ulaamvumo yakuchita kakkalila muZimbabwe ulaacheelelo chakutongawukila Palyamenti kwiinda mumalembe aatala anikaba kali kaambo kamukataazya kuzintu nzyiikonzya kuzyotolola Palyamenti, mbuuli kupanga mulawu, kuzyotolola mulawu akukaka mulawu.

(2) Nzila njizyeelede kusika aazyo kuPalyamenti ntongawuko zilembedwe zizwa kubantu kweelede zyapandululwa kabotu kwiinda mu Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti. 150 Mpuyiswaananila Palyementi Palyamenti ikonzya kuswaanana kubusena buteensi bwamazuba woonse pe, pesi kakulangisigwa ziyandwa abulumbu, lukwabililo akusikika kwabusena oobo. 151

Kkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti

(1) Palyamenti iyelede kudoma Kkomiti izibikene kuti niKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti kuchitila kuti —

(a) kibona kweendelezegwa kwamilimu yaPalyamenti;

(b) kupanga Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti;

(c) kulangalanga zyoonse zyaatala abwiime bwaPalyamenti; kabiyo

(d) kuchita nuuba uuli mulimu nguyapegwa Kkomiti eeyi aKkata lyaMulawu naakuti a Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti naakuti uuli mulawu.

(2) Kkomiti ya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti iyelede kubumbwa abantu batobela; Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, Muzulwidi waSeneti antoomwe abayiminini bamuPalyamenti batobela —

(a) Uutobela Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(b) Uutobela Muzulwidi waSeneti;

(c) Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona Mali antoomwe abamwi baSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende babili bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika;

(d) Muzulwidi waZyaambo zyaFulumende muliimwi ayimwi ng’anda yamilawu;

(e) Muzulwidi watubunga twazyanyika tuli muPalyamenti pesi tutali muli fulumende muliimwi ayimwi ng’anda yamilawu;

(f) Bazulwidi batubunga twabuleli bwanyika muNg’anda yaMulawu batubunga twazyanyika tuli muPalyamenti;

(g) Muzulwidi waKkansili yaBaami Yanyika Yoonse;

(h) babili bayiminini bamuPalyamenti bateensi baSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende naakuti babatobela, umwi kali wakuNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu wakagalwa aMuzulwidi waSeneti, uumbi kali wakuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu wagalwa aSichuuno wang’anda eeyi;

(i) bayiminini bamuPalyamenti bali musanu abatatu bateensi baSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende naakuti babatobela, bane basalwa aSeneti kwali kuti bambi bane basalwa aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(3) Bantu beelede kugalwa naakuti basalwa kunjila muKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti achakufwambaana kuzwa niyatalika kuswaanana Palyamenti. Beelede kusalwa nkukuti balangisisye bwiime abubumbwe bwaPalyamenti muli zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika antoomwe abwiingi bwabanakazi abaalumi kuti zyobile ng’anda zyamilawu zyaswanana.

(4) Kkomiti ya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti isalwa kubeleka kwabuumi bwaPalyamenti eeyo boonse.

(5) Kkomiti ya Nzila zyaMeendelezyo aPalyamenti izulwidwa aSichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, pesi kuti katawo Muzulwidi waSeneti nguyizulwida.

(6) Meendelezyo amilimu yaKkomiti ya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti ayelede kupandululwa muNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(7) Kuti kwaba mweenya muKkomiti yaNzila zyaMeendelezyo aPalyamenti kakuyanda kubikkwa umbi/ bambi bantu kweelede muntu/bantu aabo bagalwa/basalwa achakufwambaana kusinka mweenya wabawo. 152

Kkomiti yakulangwalangwa kwaMilawu muPalyamenti

(1) Achakufwambaana kuzwa nibakkala kwalutaanzi muPalyamenti, iKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti iyelede kusala kkomiti itegwa Kkomiti yakulangwalangwa kwaMilawu muPalyamenti, ilaabantu batali aansi kwabatatu bateensi baSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende naakuti baBatobela.

(2) Banji babantu basalwa kunjila muKkomiti yakulangwalangwa kwaMilawu muPalyamenti kweelede kabali bantu bazi zyamilawu muZimbabwe aanze kwakuti kwajanika kuti muPalyamenti bakayiya zyamulawu mbache.

(3) Kkomiti yakulangwalangwa kwaMilawu muPalyamenti iyelede kulangisisya—

(a) woonse malembe amilawu, aanze kwamalembe ali aatala aKkata lyaMulawu, kaatanasika achibaka chakuvootelwa malembe amulawu aaya mumaanda obile amilawu;

(b) fumbwa malembe amilawuabambululwa kuzwa naakalangisisigwa lutaanzi akkomiti eeyi kaatana vootelwa lubo pe mumaanda obile aPalyamenti;

(c) toonse tulawulawu tunga twadindwa mupepa lyaGazette;

(d) woonse malembe amilawu ayetwa kukkomiti aMutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende; kabiyo

(e) tulawulawu tweetwa kukkomiti anikuba uuli ulaamanguzu akupanga tulawulawu ootu. Kkomiti eeyi iyelede kwaambila, Palyamenti, Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti naba uuli wakiipede malambe amulawu kuti ilangisisye, nziyajana mukulangalanga kwayo bwiime bwamalembe amulawu wayetwa kuli njiyo. Ikkomiti nga ilangalanga bwiime bwamalembe mapya amulawu kuti aleendelene na aKkata lyaMulawu wanyika

(4) Niyamana kulangalanga bube bwamalembe amulawu naakuti bwamalembe atanazuminanwa ngiyapegwa Kkomiti yeelede kwaambila, Palyamenti, Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti naba uuli wakiipede malambe amulawu kuti ilangisisye, nziyajana mukulangalanga kwayo bwiime bwamalembe amulawu wayetwa kuli njiyo. Ikkomiti nga ilangalanga bwiime bwamalembe mapya amulawu kubona kuti taakwe na zikazyania aKkata lyaMulawu wanyika mumalembe mapya ayanda kuba mulawu.

(5) Mulawu wapangwa aPalyamenti naakuti Nzila zyaMeendelezyo aPalyamenti zikonzya kupa Kkomiti yakulangwalangwa kwamulawu imwi milimu yakuchita. 153 Mufolo waMuzulwidi wang’anda yaajulu yamulawu, Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu aBayiminini bamuPalyamenti

(1) Mifolo ya Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu aMuzulwidi waSeneti —

(a) yeelede kupandululwa muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kabiyo izwa Munkomo Mpati yaFulumende;

(b) tiyelede kuselusiga pe kuti kabachili muzyuuno zyabo; kabiyo

(c) yeelede kubbadalwa nikuba Palyamenti yamwayika kusikila bazwa azyuuno zyabo.

(2) Mifolo ibbadalwa kubayiminini bamuPalyamenti yeelede kupandululwa muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti. 154 Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti abamwi babelesi

(1) IKkomiti ya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti, kiili yazuminana aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, yeelede kunjizya mulimu muntu achuuno chaMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti utanooyendelezye maanda aPalyamenti kabiyo muntu ooyo kali munsi lyaSichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(2) Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti unjizigwa mulimu kwachiindi chisika minyaka musanu awumwi pesi ukonzya kubweedezegwa achuuno kwaminyaka ikozyenie.

(3) Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti uyelede kusiya chuuno chakwe—

(a) kwiinda muchikumbizyo chazwa kuKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti, bayiminini bamuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu biinda chisela chabaabo bajanika mung’anda mbiizulwa bazuminana kuti nchobeni Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti agwisigwe achuuno;

(b) Mulembi Mupati uyandikana kuti azwe achuuno chakwe aawo kuti kali wakali umwi wabayiminini bamuPalyamenti; naakuti

(c) naamaninsya minyaka isika kkumi atubili (12) kali achuuno chaMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti.

(4) IKkomiti ya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti yeelede yasala uumbi Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti kuzwa mubabelesi mbuuli mbiikubona kakweelela.

(5) Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti abamwi babelesi bamuPalyamenti —

(a)banjizigwa milimu aKkomiti ya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti eeyo kabiyo ibapa malayilile ambubeelede kubeeleka anzibeelede kujana mukubeleka kwabo; kabiyo

(b)babelekela luundu pesi teeBabelesi baLuundu pe. [Fuka libambululwa aMualwu 2 wa2021]

CHAANDAANO 7 NSALUUZYO

CHIBAKA 1 NZILA ZYAKWEENDELEZYA NSALUUZYO 155

Ndemeko zyanzila zyansaluuzyo

(1) Nsaluuzyo, zyeelede kuchitwa chiindi achiindi, antoomwe amalefulandamu zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli zyeelede kuchitwa —

(a) kabaangunukide bantu munzila ilaantanganana;

(b) kuchitwa munzila yansiswa;

(c) boonse bapati bakonzya kuvoota beelede bavoota bweelene; kabiyo

(d) kakutakwe lupyopyongano abumwi bubi buchitwa chiindi chansaluuzyo.

(2) Fulumende weelede kuchita zyoonse ntaamo kubikkilizya kupanga mulawu, kubona kuti ndemeko zyaambwa mukabeela (1), kapati, weelede —

(a) kubona kuti boonse bantu basika achiimo chakuvoota banaZimbabwe bakonzya kuvoota mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yane, balembesya kuvoota;

(b) kubona kuti woonse munaZimbabwe ukonzya kuvoota munsaluuzyo naakuti mulefelendamu wajana mweenya wakuvoota, kabiyo agwasizye kuchita kuti kuvoota kwabantu balemene kwabawubila;

(c) kubona kuti toonse tubunga twazyanyika antoomwe abayiminini bayanda kusalwa bakonzya kuchita zyoonse nzibayanda kuchita zilaatala akulisambala kabayanduula mavooti antoomwe akuvootelwa;

(d) kubona kuti toonse tubunga twazyanyika antoomwe abayiminini bayanda kusalwa bakonzya kusikila zimwaya milumbe nkukuti bakonzye kulisambala kabotu kubantu; kabiyo

(e) kubona kuti kukazyania akutamvwanana kwameendelezyo ansaluuzyo zyazyotololwa muchiindi chifwiifwi. 156 Meendelezyo aansaluuzyo antoomwe amalefelendamu Kunsaluuzyo amalefelendamu woonse, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo yeelede kubona kuti —

(a) fumbwa nzila yabelesegwa mukuchita nsaluuzyo iluleme, tikataazyi pe, ilibalangele kabiyo ibikka aantanganana bweendelezegwe bwansaluuzyo;

(b) butebuzi bwazwa munsaluuzyo naakuti mulefelendamu bwazibisigwa bantu achakufwambaana niyamana biyo nsaluuzyo; kabiyo

(c) nzila zyakweendelezya milimu zyeelede zyabawo—

(i) kuchitila kugwisya akukaka zyalupyopyongano lunga lwabawo chiindi chansaluuzyo abumwi bubeya bweendelene akuchitwa kwansaluuzyo;

(ii) kubona kuti zibelesyo zibelesegwa mukuchita nsaluuzyo zibambidwe kabotu. 157 Mulawu waNsaluuzyo

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti weelede kupandulula kabotu meendelezyo ansaluuzyo antoomwe amalefelendamu zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kapati zintu zitobela —

(a) kweengwa kwamigaanu yamakkonsititwensi antoomwe amawaadi mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 161;

(b) kulembwa kwabantu bakonzya kuvoota, antoomwe azeezyo ziyandikana kuti muntu elembesye kuvoota mumalembe abasikuvoota;

(c) bayiminina kusalwa munsaluuzyo abamwi bantu balaachakuhcita mukweendelezegwa kwansaluuzyo amalefelendamu;

(d) kuba amabambe akwiimininwa kwabantu kwiinda mukubona bwiingi bwabo kubantu basalwa kwiiminina masena muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamulawu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 120(1)(a) azyuuno zibambilidwe banakazi muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 124(1) (b), nzila zyeelede kutobelezegwa mukusalwa kwabantu banjila muzimanga zyabawo. Zimanga eezyo zyeelede kuzuzikizigwa aabantu—

(i) bazwa kutubunga twazyanyika twakajisi zyuuno zyasyaala kazitakwe bayiminini; kabiyo

(ii) bakozyenie awakaliwo kale, kuti wakali mwaalumi ngakunjile mwaalumi kuti wakali mwanakazi ngakunjile mwanakazi;

(e) kusalwa kwabayiminini babantu balemene mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 120(1)(d);

(f) kuchitwa kwansaluuzyo zyamaKkansili antoomwe amumabbooma;

amuzilikiti,

mumadolopo

(g) kweendelezegwa kwantongawuko yaatala abutebuzi bwansaluuzyo.

(2) Chiindo chakwiimininwa kwabantu kwiinda mukubona bwiingi bwabo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (1)(d) kweelede kwabona kuti kuli kwiimikililwa kwabanakazi mumaSeneta mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 120(1)(a).

(3) Mulawu aNsaluuzyo weelede kupa malayilile aatala ambukweelede kusalwa bantu bayanda kwiiminina bantu munsaluuzyo. Eezi zyeelede kuchitwa mukati kamazuba asika kukkumi amazuba aane (14) kuzwa buunsi nikoompololwa zuba lyansaluuzyo. Nsaluuzyo zyeelede kuchitwa mukati kamazuba asika makumi otatwe (30)kuzwa buzuba nibatambulwa bantu bayanda kwiiminina bantu munsaluuzyo.

(4) Taakwe kubambululwa kwaMulawu waNsaluuzyo, naakuti nuba uuli mulawu ulachakuchita ansaluuzyo kweelede kuchitwa pe domane Kkomishini yaNsaluuzyo yapa mizeezo yayo mumbandiko eeyo kabiyo mizeezo njiyapa yatambulwa;

(5) Kuti nsaluuzyo zyoompololwa kuti zizoobawo lili, taakwe masandu aachitwa pe kuMulawu waNsaluuzyo naakuti kuluuli mulawu ulaachakuchita azyansaluuzyo ayelede kuchitwa nkaambo akonzya kusandula mpalamo zyansaluuzyo eezyo.

CHIBAKA 2 CHIINDI CHAKUCHITWA KWANSALUUZYO 158

Chiindi Chakuchitwa kwansaluuzyo

(1) Nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene zyeelede kuchitwa zyakuti takweelede kwiinda mazuba ayinda —

(a) makumi otatwe kakutanasika buzuba nibazwa azyuuno aabo bakasalidwe munsaluuzyo zyakiinda minyaka musanu yayinda mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 143;

(b) aawo Palyamenti mpuyazuminana kuti imwayike kwiinda muchibeela 143(2), mazuba aasika kumakumi musanu alaamakumi aane kakuli kwazuminanwa kumwayika kwaPalyamenti; naakuti

(c) aawo Palyamenti mbuyamwayika kwiinda muchibeela 109(4) naakuti (5) kakutobela kutondeezya kubula lusyomo kwabantu muli fulumende, mazuba aasika kumakumi musanu alaamakumi aane kakuli kwazuminanwa kumwaywa kwaPlayamenti.

(2) Nsaluuzyo zyakusala makkansela kweelede zyachitilwa aamwi azyakusala Muzulwidi waNyika abayiminini bamuPalyamenti.

(3) Nsaluuzyo zyakuzuzikizya chuuno chakasiigwa awumwi mwiiminini muPalyamenti naakuti mumabbooma zyeelede kuchitwa mukati kamazuba makumi musanu amakumi aane

(90) kuzwa buunsi nichasyaala kachitachikwe muntu chuuno, aanze kwakuti chuuno chasiigwa kakuli kwasyaala myeezi musanu ayine (9) kakuya kunsaluuzyo zyakusalwa kwabantu banji yakumana kwaminyaka musanu. Mpukwaba boobo nsaluuzyo zyakuzuzikizya chuuno zilalekwa kulindwe nsaluuzyo zyakusalwa kwabazulwidi basiyenesiyene 159

Kuzuzikizigwa kwazyuuno kwiinda munsaluuzyo
