Aawo mpukweelede kuchitwa nsaluuzyo zyakuzuzikizya zyuuno, fumbwa kuli chili chuuno chasyaala kachitachikwe muzulwidi kachili chuuno eecho cheelede kuzuzikizigwa kwiinda mbuuli zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, aabo balangene akubambila nsaluuzyo zyakuzuzikizya zyuuno beelede bachita mabambe ayelede achakufwambaana aanze kwansaluuzyo zyazyuuno zyaambidwe muchibeela 158.

CHIBAKA 3 KWEENGWA KWAMIGAANO YAZYANSALUUZYO 160

Buvule bwamakkonsitichwensi amawaadi

(1) Kuchitila kuti kusalwe bantu batakayiminine bamwi muPalyamenti, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo yeelede kweenga migaano ipanga makkonsitichwensi asika kumyaanda yobile akkumi limwi (210) munyika yaZimbabwe yoonse.

(2) Kuchitila kuti kusalwe bantu batakayiminine bamwi kuKkansili yaMudolopo amuBbooma, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo yeelede kweenga migaano ipanga mawaadi ayendelana abuvule bwabantu bayanda kwiimikilila bamwi mudolopo naakuti mubbooma. 161

Kweengwa kwamigaano yazyansaluuzyo

(1) Choonse chiindi nikwiinda minyaka kkumi, bumwi buzuba bwaagalwa aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo bweelede kuba afwiifwi anikuzwa butebuzi bwakubalwa buvule bwabantu munyika, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo yeelede kweenga migaano yazyansaluuzyo itondeezya migaano mipya yama kkonsitichwensi amawaadi.

(2) Kuti kweengwa kwamigaano kwachitwa kakuli kwasyaala myeezi itasiki musanu awumwi kakuli kwasika mazuba ansaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene (general elections), migaano eeyo mipya tikonzyi kubelesegwa pe kunsaluzzyo zitanooli ziyanda kuchitwa, pesi kulabeleka migaano yansiku/ yakaliwo kale kunsaluuzyo ziyanda kuchitwa.

(3) Migaano yansaluuzyo (ma kkonsitichwensi) yeelede kuti muchiindi chakwiiyenga kwaabanwa kwabantu bakavoota mumakkonsitichwensi woonse kuleelene nyika yoonse yaZimbabwe.

(4) Migaano yansaluuzyo yamawaadi yeelede kuti muchiindi chakwiiyenga kwaabanisigwa kwabantu bakavoota mumawaadi woonse kweelana mubbooma lyoonse.

(5) Mukweenga migaano yazyansaluuzyo—

(a) migaano yamawaadi, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo, yeelede kuti yabona kuti taakwe waadi pe yapegwa migaano iyibikka mumabbooma aabili naakuti kwiinda;

(b) migaano yama kkonsitichwensi, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo, yeelede kuti yabona kuti taakwe waadi pe yapegwa migaano iyibikka muma kkonsitichwensi aabili naakuti kwiinda;

(6) Mukweenga migaano yansaluuzyo, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo, mubusena abusena kweelede ilangisisye zintu zitobela —

(a) zilengwa-leza zili mbuuli milonga, zilundu, mimpata azimwi;

(b) nzila zyakwaambuuzyania mubusena oobo;

(c) buyalwe bwambubakkede basikuvoota mubusena oobo;

(d) fumbwa zintu ziyandwa abantu basikuvoota mubusena oobo;

(e) aawo mpukweengwa migaano mipya, kweelede kwalangwa yansiku kuti yakayimvwi biyeni; kabiyo

(f) bantu bakkala mubusena oobo; mpawo balangisisye akubona kuti zintu eezi bazilangisisya mukweenga migaano yabo. IKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo ikonzya kusiyanisya bwiingi bwabantu bajanika muwaadi naakuti mu kkonsitichwensi pesi yeelede yabona kuti woonse mawaadi ama kkonsitichwensi alakonzyania.Kuti kaasiyene, asiyene munzila itayindi makumi aabili kuzwa mumwaanda (20%) bwiingi naakuti buche bwabantu.

(7) Niyamana kweenga migaano yamawaadi ama kkonsitichwensi, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo iyelede kupa Muzulwidi waNyika Ngambo/lipoti ijisi—

(a) mazina amawaadi amakkonsitichwensi woonse antoomwe abupanduluzi bwamigaano bwachimwi achimwi;

(b) mepu naakuti mamepu atondeezya mawaadi amakkonsitichwensi; kabiyo

(c) fumbwa luzibo iKkomishini nzyiibona kazyeelede kuti azibe Muzulwidi waNyika; Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kutola ngambo eeyo kuPalyamenti mukati kamazuba asika musanu aabili kuzwa buunsi nayitambula.

(8) Mukati kamazuba asika kukkumi amazuba aane kuzwa lipoti yamigaano niyakeetwa muPalyamenti —

(a) Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kwiibweedezya lipoti kuKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo kuti ikalangelange zimwi nzyiitanooli yalyiiba kuzilangisisya;

(b) fumbwa ng’anda yamulawu akati kazyo zyobilwe ikonzya kubweedezya lipoti kuKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo kuti ikalangisisye zimwi nzyiitalangisisisye mulipoti eeyo. Kuti kwaboobo, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kubweedezya lipoti kuKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo.

(9) Aawo lipoti yakweengwa kwamigaano mpuyabweedezegwa kuKkomishini, kwiinda mukabeela (8), iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo yeelede kubona kuti yatola mizeezo njiyapegwa kuti iyilangelange lubo pesi nziyazuminana Kkomishini eeyo nzizyo tazisanduki.

(10) Achakufwambaana kuzwa akutobelezya zyaambidwe mutubeela (7) a (9), iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo yeelede kweeta lipoti lyakumaninsizya kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

(11) Mukati kamazuba asika mukkumi aane kali watambula lipoti yaKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kudinda mupepa lyaluundu (Gazeti) koompolola mazina amigaano yamawaadi ama kkonsitichwensi mapya.

(12) Aawo mpukuli musiyano akati kabupanduluzi bwamugaano wawaadi naakuti kkonsitichwensi amepu/amamepu akabambwa aKkomishini, zili mubupanduluzi nzizitobelwa.

CHAANDAANO 8 BUBETESI BOONSE ANTOOMWE ANKUTA

CHIBAKA 1 BWIIME BWAZYANKUTA

162 Manguzu aBubetesi Bubetesi boonse bujana nguzu kuzwa kuluundu lwaZimbabwe kabiyo nguzu eezi zibikkidwe munkuta zisiyenesiyene zibikkilizya —

(a) Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu

(b) Nkuta Yaajulujulu

(c) Nkuta Yaajulu

(d) Nkuta Yambelekelano

(e) Nkuta Yanjendelezyo

(f) Nkuta yaMejastiliti

(g) and Nkuta Zyabaami aBasibbuku

(h) zimwi nkuta zipangwa aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti. 163

Bubetesi Boonse

(1) Bubetesi boonse bwamuZimbabwe bujisi bantu batobela —

(a) Duundumuntule waBabetesi, Mutobeli waDuundumuntule waBabetesi, abamwi Majaji aNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu;

(b) Majaji aNkuta Yaajulujulu;

(c) Muzulwidi waBabetesi baNkuta Yaajulu abamwi Majaji aNkuta Yaajulu;

(d) Muzulwidi waBabetesi baNkuta Yambelekelano abamwi Majaji aNkuta Yambelekelano;

(e) Muzulwidi waBabetesi baNkuta Yanjendelezyo abamwi Majaji aNkuta Yanjendelezyo; kabiyo

(f) bantu babeteka milandu muNkuta zyamaMejastiliti, amunkuta zyabaami abasibbuku azimwi nkuta zitanooli zyabumbwa kwiinda Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Duundumuntule waBubetesi nguwe silutwe wabubetesi boonse kabiyo nguuzulwida Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu aNkuta Yaajulujulu.

(3) Muzulwidi waNkuta Yaajulu ngusilutwe wankuta eeyo.

(4) Muzulwidi waNkuta Yambelekelano ngusilutwe wankuta eeyo.

(5) Muzulwidi waNkuta Yanjendelezyo ngusilutwe wankuta eeyo. [Aboobo mbambululo yachitwa ku163(1)(d) a (e) a 163 (4) a (5) mukwiinda mukusowa Muzulwidi Mupati”: Sikubambulula Mulawu]

”Muzulwidi waBabetes” a

Kulyiimikilila akulisanta kwaBubetesi Boonse

(1) Nkuta zyoonse zililyiimikilide akulisanta mukuchita milimu yazyo nkaako zyeelede kulemeka akutobelezya Kkata lyaMulawu luzutu antoomwe amilawu yoonse yamunyika. Zyeelede kubona kuti batyola milawu bapegwa zisubulo zyeendelene aakutyolwa kwamilawu kakutakwe muntu uyoowegwa naakuti ufwidwa luse.

(2) Bulyiimikilile, kulisanta akubeleka nchobeni kwankuta nkuko kusimatizya kulemekwa kwamulawu akusimya bulelo bwajandabanji munyika nkaako—

(a) taakwe fulumende naakuti mutabi wafulumende muzibeela zyoonse zisiyenesiyene munyika, kuzwa kujulu kuyoosika aansi, naakuti umwi biyo muntu, uyelede kunjilila mumabelekelo ankuta;

(b) Fulumende, kwiinda mukupanga milawu akutola zimwi ntaamo, uyelede kuti wagwasizya akukwabilila nkuta zyoonse kuchitila kuti kulisanta akulyiimikilila kwazyo, bulemu bwazyo, kusikika kwazyo akubeleka nchobeni kwazyo kuchitike, nkukuti nkuta zikonzye kuzuzikizya ndemeko zyaambidwe muchibeela 165 chaKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(3) Zyaambo zyaambwa ankuta antoomwe azisubulo zyapegwa ankuta zyeelede kulemekwa akuchitwa amuntu woonse munyika kubikkilizya fulumende, atubungabunga toonse tujanika munyika.

(4) Taakwe chaambidwe pe muchibeela eechi chinga chilabonwa aanga chikasya Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kuti upe nguzu antomwe amilimu iimbi yakuchita kububetesi aajulu kwayeeyo yakubeteka milandu, fumbwa kuti milimu iimbi njibwapegwa bubetesi tayichiti kuti buleke kulyiimikilila akulisanta mukuchita mulimu wakubeteka milandu. 165

Ndemeko zizulwida bubetesi

(1) Mukuchita mulimu wabo bubetesi, basikubeteka beelede kuzulwidwa aandemeko zitobela —

(a) bululami bweelede kuchitilwa kumuntu woonse nikuba abe muntu ulibiyeni muchisi;

(b) bululami tabweelede kunonokelwa pe nkaako babetesi beelede kuchita milimu yabo zyanchobeni akufwambaana kweelede;

(c) milimu yankuta mipati loko mukukwabilila zyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu antoomwe akubona kuti milawu yanyika yoonse yalemekwa akutobelezegwa.

(2) Babetesi, kalaalikke naakuti kabali banji, beelede kulemeka zyuuno zyabo zyabubetesi antoomwe kusyomwa nkubasyomwa aluundu. Beelede kubona kuti balilyiimikilide akulisanta munzila njibachita milimu yabo nkukuti luundu lwiinkilile nembo kalusyoma akulemeka bubetesi boonse.

(3) Kuti kabaamba nzibayeeya aatala atwaambo tweetwa kunkuta, babetesi bayelede kwaamba nzibayeeya kabaangunukide kakutakwe banjilila mumabelekelo aabo naakuti kabatakwe ubasungilizya kuti bachite mulimu wabo muliimbi nzila njibatayandi.

(4) Basikubeteka tabeelede pe—

(a) kunjila muli zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika (politikisi);

(b) kuba achuuno naakuti kuba umwi watubunga twakweendelezegwa kwanyika;

(c) kugwasizya mukulanguula mali yakupa tubunga twazyanyika naakuti kupa mali kukabunga kakweendelezegwa kwanyika; naakuti

(d) kwiinka kumiswaangano yatubunga twazyanyika.

(5) Bubetesi tabweelede pe kukumbila zipo kuzwa nikuba kulini zinga zilachita kuti kulyiimikilila akulisanta kwabo kunyonyongane.

(6) Bubetesi bweelede kutunzya nembo mulimu wabo wakubeteka milandu kabiyo tabweelede kuchita zimwi pe zinga zilanyonyongania kulyiimikilila akulisanta kwabo.

(7) Bubetesi bweelede kutola ntaamo zyakujana antoomwe akuyungizya luzibo lwamilimu yabo, kapati bweelede kubona kuti bulookujana luzibo aatala azilookuchitika kumilawu yanyika naakuti milawu yakunze kwanyika, 166

Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu

(1) Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu njiinkuta yakumamanino kuzyaambo zilaachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu kabiyo ijisi babetesi batobela —

(a) Duundumuntule waBabetesi awutobela Duundumuntule waBabetesi; kabiyo

(b) amajaji aali musanu aNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu;

(2) Kuti kwayandikana kuti kube Jaji wabujana bugwasizya kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kwachiindi chisyoonto, Duundumuntule waBabetesi ukonzya kugala uumwi Jaji naakuti Jaji wakasiya mulimu kuti agwasizye muNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kwachiindi chiyandikana.

(3) Milandu naakuti twaambo tweetwa kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu—

(a) tulaatala akutyolwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zyaambidwe muChaandaano 4 naakuti zyaambo zilaatala akusalwa kwaMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti uMutobela, zyeelede kubetekwa kaaliwo woonse majaji aNkuta yaZyaKkata lyaMulawu.

(b) Tumwi twaambo naakuti imwi milandu iteensi yeeyo yaambwa mufukalilaajulu, tweelede kubetekwa amajaji asika batatu naakuti biingi kwiinda batatu baNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu. Pesi Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti naakuti imwi miteto yaNkuta ikonzya kuzumizya kuti tumwi twaambo/milandu ibetekwe aJaji womwe naakuti banji baNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu.

(4) MaJaji aachibeleka naakuti maJaji akasiya mulimu banga bagalwa kuti bagwasizye kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, mbuuli zyaambwa mukabeela (2) bakonzya kwiinkilila nembo kabagwasizya muNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu naanka chasika chiindi chabo chakuti bayimikile nkukuti bakonzye kumaninsizya imwi milimu njibakatalika naakuti yakatalikwa kabatana galwa kuti bagwasizye. 167

Nzyiibeleka Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu

(1) Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu —

(a) njiinkuta yakumamanino kumilandu yoonse ilaachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu, nkaako nziyaamba atala atwaambo tweetwa kulinjiyo nkuta eeyi tazikazigwi pe kabiyo boonse bantu beelede kuzitobelezya akuzichita zyaambwa ankuta eeyi;

(b) ilangana atwaambo twazyaKkata lyaMulawu luzutu antoomwe atumwi twaambo tulaachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu, kapati eezyo zyaambidwe muchibeela 131(8)(b) afuka 9(2) lyaMpampikwa Yasanu ijanika kumamanino kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli; kabiyo

(c) nziyaamba ataala azyaambo zyaKkata lyaMulawu naakuti zimwi zyaambo zyeendelana aKkata lyaMulawu tazikazigwi pe iinga —.

(2) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, niNkuta yaZyaKkata lyaMulawu luzutu —

(a) ilaamba atala akweendelana naakuti kuteendelana kwamulawu mupya aKkata lyaMulawu eeli, pesi nkuta eeyi ikonzya kupa muzeezo wayo luzutu kuti kaambo aaka keetwa kuli njiyo kwiinda munzila zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(b) ilabeteka kaambo keendelene akukazyania kulaatala kwaMuzulwidi waNyika munsaluuzyo zyachitwa;

akusalwa

(c) ilabeteka kaambo keendelene akukazyania kulaatala kwaMutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika munsaluuzyo; naakuti

akusalwa

(d) ilaamba kuti Palyamenti naakuti Muzulwidi waNyika wakachilwa kuchita milimu yakwe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kwaamba zimwi nzyiyelede kuchita Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, nkaambo kaboobo mulawu ooyo ukonzya kupa manguzu akuti Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu ipange miteto itobelezegwa munkuta.

(4) Milawu yaNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu yeelede kuzumizya muntu, kuti kakuli kuchita boobo kuzuzikizya kuti muntu ajane bululami.

(5) Milawu yaNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu yeelede kuzumizya muntu, kuti kakuli kuchita boobo kuzuzikizya kuti muntyu ajane bululami—

(a) kuti muntu ayete kuNkuta yazyaKkata kalaazyakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu;

lyaMulawu

kaambo

(b) kukumbila kuti mulandu wakwe ukabetekwe aNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kuzwa kuliimwi nkuta nkuwali kubetekwa;

(c) kuboneka mbuuli mweenzinyina waNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu. 168

Nkuta Yaajulujulu

(1)

Nkuta Yaajulujulu ninkuta mpati ijisi babetesi batobela —

(a) Duundumuntule waBabetesi awutobela Duundumuntule waBabetesi;

(b) majaji abili naakuti ayinda babili baNkuta Yaajulujulu

(c) fumbwa majaji bagalwa kwiinda mukabeela (2)

(2) Kuti kwayandikana kuti kube Jaji wakujana bugwasizya Nkuta Yaajulujulu kwachiindi chisyoonto, Duundumuntule waBabetesi ukonzya kugala umwi Jaji uchibeleka naakuti Jaji wakasiya mulimu kuti agwasizye mu Nkuta Yaajulujulu kwachiindi chiyandikana.

(3)MaJaji bachibeleka naakuti maJaji bakasiya mulimu banga bagalwa kuti bagwasizye kuNkuta Yaajulujulu, mbuuli zyaambwa mukabeela (2) bakonzya kwiinkilila nembo kabagwasizya mu Nkuta Yaajulujulu naanka chasika chiindi chabo chakuti bayimikile nkukuti bakonzye kumaninsizya imwi milimu njibakatalika naakuti yakatalikwa kabatana galwa kuti bagwasizye. 169

Nzyiibeleka Nkuta Yaajulujulu

(1) Nkuta Yaajulujulu njiinkuta yakumamanino kuliimwi milandu yoonse atumwi twaambo tutalangene azyaKkata lyaMulawu.

(2) Pesi kwiinda mukabeela (1), Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa Nkuta Yaajulujulu iimbi milimu yakuchita antoomwe awambi manguzu.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa iimbi milimu Nkuta Yaajulujulu kubikkilizya manguzu akuchita miteto itobelezegwa muNkuta Yaajulujulu.

(4) Miteto yamunkuta ikonzya kupa nguzu kuli Mulembi Mupati waNkuta Yaajulujulu antoomwe ankuta lwayo kuti niilangalanga milandu yakubbadalisyania kwabantu—

(a) ipe mizeezo yamaboneno aayo kumilandu itendebete pesi kumilandu naakuti twaambo tulaachakuchita akubambwa kwabana nibakakana bazyali;

(b) ipe maboneno aayo atala atwaambo tumwi biyo pesi peepe kumilandu ilaachakuchita akwaangunuka kwamuntu; Pesi miteto eeyo iyelede kupa nguzu, muntu wajatwajatwa bubi azyaambwa a Mulembi Mupati waNkuta Yaajulujulu, zyakukumbila kuti Jaji waNkuta Yaajulujulu alangelange kaambo kakwe. Ooyu Jaji wakumbilwa ukonzya kusimatizya zyakaambwa aMulembi Mupati waNkuta Yaajulujulu naakuti kuzisandula zyakambidwe, naakuti kuzikaka zyoonse zyakaambidwe mpawo ape maboneno aakwe aasiyene mbuuli mbazibona zyaambo. 170

Nkuta Yaajulu Nkuta Yaajulu ninkuta mpati ijisi babetesi batobela —

(a) Duundumuntule waBabetesi, Mutobeli waDuundumuntule waBabetesi antoomwe aMuzulwidi waBabetesi baNkuta Yaajulu; kabiyo

(b) abamwi majaji baNkuta Yaajulu batanoogalwe muchiindi achiindi mbuuli mbukweelede.

171 Nzyiibeleka Nkuta Yaajulu

(1) Nkuta Yaajulu —

(a) njiyo ilangalanga yoonse milandu yamipati yeetwa kunkuta kuzwa munyika yoonse yaZimbabwe;

(b) ilaamukuli wakulangalanga mabelekelo ankuta zyabaMejasitiliti azimwi nkuta zilaansi antoomwe akulangalanga zisubulo zyakapegwa kumilandu yakabetekwa ankuta zyabaMejasitiliti.

(c) ikonzya kulanga zyaambo zilaachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu aanze kwazeezyo zyeelede kulangwalangwa aNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu;

(d) ikonzya kuchita zimwi mbuuli mbukutanooli kwaambwa aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa iimbi milimu Nkuta Yaajulu kubikkilizya manguzu akuchita miteto itobelezegwa muNkuta Yaajulu.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa manguzu kuNkuta Yaajulu kuti ipandulwe muzipanzi-panzi zilangisisya zintu zisiyenesiyene pesi chimwi achimwi chipanzi cheelede kukonzya kuchita mulimu ukonzya kuchitwa aNkuta Yaajulu yoonse kiijisini nikuba muli kali akali kaambo/mulandu weetwa kunembo lyabo.

(4) Milawu yamunkuta ikonzya kupa nguzu kuli Mulembi Mupati waNkuta Yaajulu kuti niilangalanga milandu yakubbadalisyania kwabantu—

(a) ipe mizeezo yamaboneno aayo kumilandu itendebete pesi kumilandu naakuti twaambo tulaachakuchita akubambwa kwabana nibalakana bazyali

(b) ipe maboneno aayo atala atwaambo tumwi biyo pesi peepe kumilandu ilaachakuchita akwaangunuka kwamuntu; Pesi miteto eeyo iyelede kupa nguzu, muntu wajatwajatwa bubi azyaambwa aMulembi Mupati waNkuta Yaajulu, zyakukumbila kuti Jaji waNkuta Yaajulu alangelange kaambo kakwe. Ooyu Jaji wakumbilwa ukonzya kusimatizya zyakaambwa aMulembi Mupati waNkuta Yaajulu naakuti kuzisandula zyakaambidwe, naakuti kuzikaka zyoonse zyakaambidwe mpawo ape maboneno aakwe asiyene mbuuli mbazibona zyaambo.

172 Nkuta Yambelekelano

(1) Nkuta Yambelekelano ijisi babetesi batobela —

(a) Mubetesi mupati; kabiyo

(b) abamwi mabetesi ba Nkuta Yambelekelano batanoogalwe muchiindi achiindi. [Kabela kakabambululwa achibeela 3 kaMulawu 10 wa2017]

(2) Nkuta Yambelekelano ijatajata zyaambo amilandu yazyakubelekelana kwabantu (babelesi ababelekelwa) mbuuli mbukunga kuli pandulwidwe muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa nguzu kuNkuta Yambelekelano kuti ilangelange zimwi zyaambo antoomwe akupanga milawu yamuNkuta Yambelekelano. 173

Nkuta Yanjendelezyo

(1) Nkuta Yanjendelezyo ninkuta yamalembe ijisi babetesi batobela —

(a) Mubetesi mupati; kabiyo

(b) abamwi babetesi baNkuta Yanjendelezyo batanoogalwe muchiindi achiindi. [Kabela kakabambululwa achibeela 3 chaMulawu 10 wa2017]

(2) Nkuta Yanjendelezyo ijatajata zyaambo zyakweendelezegwa kwazintu mbuuli mbukunga kuli pandulwidwe muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa nguzu kuNkuta Yanjendelezyo kuti ilangelange zimwi zyaambo antoomwe akupanga miteto yamuNkuta Yanjendelezyo. 174

Zimwi nkuta atubunga tubeteka

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kwaamba aatala akupangwa, bubumbwe bwazyo, antoomwe atwaambo/milandu tubetekwa ankuta zya —

(a) maMejastiliti, kuti balangelange misyobo yamilandu yoonse;

(b) nkuta zyabaami abasimabbuku zilangalanga miteto yazyatunsiyanisya ayachintu;

(c) zimwi nkuta zilaansi kwaNkuta Yaajulu; kabiyo

(d) atubunga tubumbwa kuti tulangelange mpyompyongano zyeetwa kuli ntuto.

(2) Naa kakulangidwe chibeela eechi alimwi achibeela171(1)(b), kulazibisigwa kuze kutabi kudoneeka kuti Nkuta yaKubeteka atumwi tubunga tubeteka aNkuta waNjendelezyo ninkuta zili munsi aNkuta Yaajulu.”

(3) (2) Kakutobelezegwa chibeela 188(4) chaKkata lyaMulawu, mifolo, luliyo alumwiluliyo lwamali zyaBabetesi baNkuta yaMbelekelano aBabetesi aNkuta yaBweendelezi bwaManguzu balazyuuno naakuti biimvwilidi uujisi chuunomubuzuba oobo Mulawu ooyu nuutalika kubeleka tiyooselusigwa. [Kabela (2) a(3) kakabikwa achibeela 5 chaMulawu 10 wa2017]


Nguzu zyankuta kuzyaambo zilachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu

(1) Aawo nkuta mpuyapa muzeezo wayo kuti mulawu tuuyendelani pe aKkata lyaMulawu, naakuti aatala anchito zyaMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti nchito zyaPalyamenti, muzeezo ooyo tukonzyi kubelesegwa kusikila wazuminwa aNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu.

(2) Nkuta yapa muzeezo aatala akuteendelana kwamulawu aKkata lyaMulawu mbuuli kwaambidwe mukabeela (1) aajulu, ikonzya kujana buumuzya machise luzutu naakuti

kwiimikizya meendelezyo azyaambo kusikila Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu yapa maboneno aayo aatala azyaambo naakuti nchito eezyo.

(3) Fumbwa muntu ukonzya kukumbila kuti zyaambo zyakwe zitolwe kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, naakuti kwiinka kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kuti muzeezo wakapegwa ankuta yaansi , mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (1) aatala azyaambo zilachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu, ukasimatizigwe, ukasandulwe naakuti kukakwa.

(4) Kuti zyaambo zilaachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu zyeetwa mukubetekwa kwamulandu umwi, mubetesi wankuta eeyo weelede kutola zyaambo eezyo kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, aanze kwakuti unooli wabona kuti chikumbizyo nchamayabwe.

(5) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti naakuti miteto yankuta yeelede kubikka antanganana kuti muzeezo wapegwa ankuta yaansi aatala akweendelana naakuti kuteendelana kwamulawu/nchito aKkata lyaMulawu, zyeelede kutolwa kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu.

(6) Kuti kiilangisisya zyaambo zilaachakuchita aatala azyaKkata lyaMulawu, inkuta yaansi ikonzya—

(a) kupa muzeezo wakuti mulawu/nchito tazyeendelani pe aKkata lyaMulawu;

(b) Kupa muzeezo wabwini, kubikkikizya kuzyotolola zintu zyakali zyabija kale nkaambo kazyaambo zyeetwa munkuta zyaambo nizichitolwa kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kuti zikazyotololwe. 176

Nguzu zijiswi aNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, Nkuta Yaajulujulu aNkuta Yaajulu

Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, Nkuta Yaajulujulu aNkuta Yaajulu zijisi nguzu zyakukwabilila akweendelezya milimu yazyo akusumpula mulawu wanyika amulawu wachintu, kakulangisisigwa mbukweelede kusumpula zyabululami antoomwe azili muKkata lyaMulawu.

CHIBAKA 2 KUGALWA KWABABETESI ACHIINDI NCHIBEELEDE KUKKALA MUZYUUNO ZYABO 177

Nzibeelede kubaazyo bantu kuti basalwe/bagalwe kuba maJaji aNkuta yaKkata lyaMulawu

(1) Muntu ukonzya kugalwa kuba Jaji waNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kuti kakuli munaZimbabwe, ujisi minyaka isika naakuti iyinda makumi aane kabiyo ujisi luzibo lunji aatala amulawu wazyaKkata lyaMulawu, kabiyo kajisi chimwi chazeezi zitobela—

(a) wakali waba Jaji kale wankuta ilangalanga milandu azyaambo zyiingi ku civil acriminal cases munyika ibelesya mulawu waRoman Dutch naakuti mulawu waChikuwa, kabiyo nyika eeyo kiibelesya mulaka wachikuwa mukweendelezya nkuta amilimu yafulumende; naakuti

(b) kwaminyaka isika naakuti iyinda kkumi atubili, kayijisini naakuti kayitajisini minyaka eeyi, wakali kubeleka zyabusimulawu—

(i) muZimbabwe; naakuti

(ii) munyika ibelesya mulawu waRoman Dutch naakuti mulawu waChikuwa , Chikuwa kakuli ngumulaka ubelesegwa munkuta amukweendelezya mulimu wafulumende kabiyo achiindi eecho ukonzya kuchita mulimu wasimulawu.

(2) Kuti ugalwe kuba Jaji waNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, muntu kweelede kakonzya kuchita mulimu wabuJaji.

Nzibeelede kubaazyo bantu kuti basalwe/bagalwe kuba maJaji aNkuta Yaajulujulu

(1) Muntu ukonzya kugalwa kuba Jaji waNkuta Yaajulujulu kuti kakuli munaZimbabwe, ujisi minyaka isika naakuti iyinda makumi aane, kabiyo kajisi zeezi zitobela —

(a) wakali waba Jaji kale wankuta ilangalanga milandu azyaambo zyiingi ku civil acriminal cases munyika ibelesya mulawu waRoman Dutch law naakuti mulawu waChikuwa, kabiyo nyika eeyo kiibelesya mulaka wachikuwa mukweendelezya nkuta amilimu yafulumende; naakuti

(b) kwaminyaka isika naakuti iyinda kkumi, kayijisini naakuti kayitajisini minyaka eeyi, wakali kubeleka zyabusimulawu—

(i) muZimbabwe; naakuti

(ii) munyika ibelesya mulawu waRoman Dutch naakuti mulawu waChikuwa,

(iii)Chikuwa kakuli ngumulaka ubelesegwa munkuta amukweendelezya mulimu wafulumende; kabiyo achiindi eecho ukonzya kuchita mulimu wasimulawu.

(2) Kuti ugalwe kuba Jaji waNkuta Yaajulujulu, muntu kweelede kakonzya kuchita mulimu wabubetesi/wabuJaji. 179

Nzibeelede kubaazyo bantu kuti babe maJaji aNkuta Yaajulu, Nkuta Yambelekelano aNkuta Yanjendelezyo

(1) Muntu ukonzya kugalwa kuba Jaji waNkuta Yaajulu, Nkuta Yambelekelano aNkuta Yanjendelezyo kuti kakuli, ujisi minyaka isika naakuti iyinda makumi aane, kabiyo kajisi zeezi zitobela —

(a) wakali waba Jaji kale wankuta ilangalanga milandu azyaambo zyiingi kumilandu mipati munyika ibelesya mulawu waRoman Dutch naakuti mulawu waChikuwa, kabiyo nyika eeyo kiibelesya mulaka wachikuwa mukweendelezya nkuta amilimu yafulumende; naakuti

(b) kwaminyaka isika naakuti iyinda musanu atubili, kayijisini naakuti kayitajisini minyaka eeyi, wakali kubeleka zyabusimulawu—

(i) muZimbabwe;

(ii) munyika ibelesya mulawu waRoman Dutch, Chikuwa kakuli ngumulaka ubelesegwa munkuta amukweendelezya mulimu wafulumende; naakuti

(iii) kuti kali munaZimbabwe, munyika kubelesegwa muawu waChikuwa kabiyo Chikuwa kakuli ngumulaka ubelesegwa munkuta amukweendelezya mulimu wafulumende kabiyo achiindi eecho ukonzya kuchita mulimu wasimulawu.

(2) Kuti ugalwe kuba Jaji waNkuta Yaajulu, Nkuta Yambelekelano aNkuta Yanjendelezyo, muntu kweelede kakonzya kuchita mulimu wabuJaji.


Kugalwa kwaBabetesi

(1) Dunduumuntule waBabeteis, Mutobeli waDunduumuntule waMubetesi alimwi aMuzulwidi wamaJaji baNkuta Yaajulu abamwi boonse majaji bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika nkakutobelezegwa chibaka eechi.

(2) Dundumuuntule waBabetesi, Mutobeli waDundumuuntule waMubetesi waNkuta Yaajulu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika musule akubonisyania aKkomishini yaMbelekelano yaMulawu.

(3) Naa kugalwa kwaDundumuuntule waBabetesi, Mutobeli waDunduumuntule waBabaetsi antoomwe aMuzulwidi waBabetesi baNkuta Yaajulu kuti kabateendelani amabambe kufumbwa atanoli abikwa aKkomishini yaMbelekelano waMulawu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe kuchibaka (2), Muzulwidi waNyika uzochita kuti Ng’anda yaMulawu yaSeneti izibisigwe aatala azeezi chakufwambana; Naa, kuchitila kuti kutabi kudoneeka, kulazibisigwa kuti ntaamo yaMuzulwidi waNyika tikoyosandulwa

(4) Kakutobelwa kabela 4(a), kufumbwa chiindi mpukuyandikana kuti kukapegwe Bubetesi uutali Dundumuuntule waBabetesi, Mutobeli waDundumuuntule waMubetesi antoomwe aMuzulwidi waMajaji kuNkuta Yaajulu, Kkomishini yaMbelekelano yaMulawu yeelede—

(a) yiitilile chuuno eecho; alimwi

(b) itambe Muzulwidi waNyika antoomwe aLuundu kuti basale mazina; alimwi

(c) ichite mbuzisisyo kunembo lyaLuundu abaabo bayanda mulimu ooyo; alimwi

(d) ibunganie mulongo wabantu botatwe basalwa mbuuli bantu beelede ooyo mulimu; alimwi

(e) ipe Muzulwidi waNyika mulongo ooyo; kakutobelezegwa kabela (5), Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede wapa uumwi wasalwa eecho chuuno. [Chibeela chakasowegwa achibeela 12 (a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(4a) Kakutakwe azyakuchita azyaambwa kukabela (4) Muzulwidi waNyika, katobela kubonisigwa kwaKkomishini yaMbelekelano ulakonzya, kufumbwa chiindi mpukuyandikana, kuti abike Mubetesi uuliwo kale waNkuta Mpati, Nkuta Yaajulu, Nkuta Yambelekelano naakuti Nkuta yaNjendelezyo kuti abe mubetesi wankuta iitobela yaajulu [Chibeela chabikwa achibeela 12 (b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(5) Naa Muzulwidi waNyika wazwa amuzeezo wakuti kunyina naba womwe kumulongo ngwaapegwa weelede mulimu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe kukabela (4)(e), weelede wachita kuti Kkomishini yaMibeleko yaMulawu imupe uumbi mulongo wabantu botatwe beeledwe ooyo mulimuMuzulwidi waNyika kuzwa waawo weelede kusala umwi wabaabo kuti ayendelezye eecho chuuno.

(6) Muzulwidi waNyika weelede wachitisya kuti kugwisigwa ngambo wakuzibya aatala akupegwa mulimu kwendelana acheechi chibaka muGazette.

(7) Zyuuno zyaDundumutuule wabaJaji waNkuta yaMbelekelano aMubetesi Mupati waNkuta yaNjendelezyo yaManguzu kweelede kweendelezegwa awumwi Jaji naakuti waajulu naakuti Jaji uchijisi chuuno, nkaziya akuti nchichili chiimo chiliwo, kunkuta yeelede, alimwi bapegwa mulimu aDundumuntuule waBabetesi musule akubonisyania aKkomishini yaMbelekelano yaMulawu. [Chibeela chakasowegwa achibeela 6 chaMulawu 10 wa 2017]


Kugalwa kwaBabetesi bajisilidi zyuuno

(1) Kuti chuuno chaDuundumuntule waBabetesi chasyaala kachitakwe muntu naakuti uliwo katachikonzyi kuchita milimu yachuuno eechi, Uumutobela ukonzya kunjila ajatile chuuno eechi. Pesi kuti zyuuno zyobilwe duundumuntule amutobeli wakwe kabatawo naakuti kabatachikonzyi kuchita milimu yabo, umwi Jaji ulachiindi chilamfu kabeleka muNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, ukonzya kujatila kubaDuundumuntule waBabetesi.

(2) Kuti wofesi ya—

(a) muzulwidi waBabetesi baNkuta Yaajulu;

(b) mubetesi mupati waNkuta Yambelekelano: naakuti

(c) mubetesi mupati waaNkuta Yanjendelezyo: yasyaala kiitachikwe muntu naakuti uuliwo katachikonzyi kuchita milimu yakwe, umwi Jaji, ulachiindi chilamfu kabeleka, munkuta eeyo ukonzya kujana bujatila kuba Dundumuntuule waBabetesi bankuta eeyo. [Kabela kabambululwa achibeela 7 kaMulawu 10 wa2021 ]

(3) Kuti kwayandikana lugwasyo lwawumbi Jaji kwachiindi chifwiifwi, aajulu kwabaabo baliwo muNkuta Yaajulu, Nkuta Yambelekelano, aNkuta Yanjendelezyo, Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kugala umwi wamaJaji akasiya mulimu. Mukugala kwakwe Muzulwidi waNyika upegwa mizeezo aKkomishini yaBubetesi. Ooyo Jaji wagalwa kuti ajane bujatilila ukonzya kubeleka kwachiindi chisika kumyeezi kkumi ayibili, nikuba kuti ukonzya kupegwa iimbi myeezi kkumi ayibili niyamana yakutaanguna.

(4) Bantu bagalwa kuti bajane bujatilila, kwiinda mukabeela (3), bakonzya kwiinkilila nembo kabagwasizya kubamaJaji naanka chasika chiindi chabo chakuti bayimikile kubeleka, nkukuti bakonzye kumaninsizya imwi milimu njibakatalika naakuti yakatalikwa kabatana galwa kuti bagwasizye. 182 Kugalwa kwabaMejasitiliti abamwi babeleka mububetesi Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kweelede waamba mbukweelede kugalwa baMejasitiliti antoomwe abamwi babelesi babubetesi bateensi maJaji, pesi —

(a) Mejasitiliti uyelede kugalwa aKkomishini yaBubetesi;

(b) babelesi babubetesi bateensi baMejasitiliti amaJaji kweelede bagalwa kwiinda mukuzumina kwaKkomishini yaBubetesi

(c) kugalwa kwababelesi ooku kweelede kuchitwa munzila ibalangala ilaantanganana kakutakwe kuyoosegwa akuchitilana luse. 183 Babelesi babubetesi tabeelede kugalwa kubelekela nkuta zyiinda iimwi Aanze kwazeezyo zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, muntu tayelede kugalwa kuba mubelesi wabubetesi wankuta zyiinda iimwi pe. 184

Kugalwa kwababelesi babubetesi kweelede kutondeezya bwiime bwachisi

Kugalwa kwababelesi babubetesi kweelede kutondeezya bwiime abube bwachisi kabiyo kakutondeezya buvule abuche bwabaalumi abanakazi muZimbabwe. 185 Chikonke chamulimu

(1) Katana talika mulimu Duundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti Uutobela Duundumuntule waBabetesi, kweelede wakonkezya kunembo lyaMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti kunembo lyawumwi muntu wazumizigwa aMuzulwidi waNyika. Bwiime bwachikonke nchibeelede kukonka mpuchili muMpampikwa Yatatu kumamanino kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(2) Katana talika mulimu Jaji, uteensi Duundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti Uutobela Duundumuntule waBabetesi, kweelede wakonkezya kunembo lya Duundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti kunembo lyawumwi Jaji ulachiindi chilamfu kabeleka. Bwiime bwachikonke nchibeelede kukonka mpuchili muMpampikwa Yatatu kumamanino kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ubelesegwa mukunjizya milimu maMejstiliti abamwi babelesi babubetesi, bateensi maJaji, uyelede kupandulula bwiime bwachikonke nchibeelede kukonkezya babelesi babubetesi aaba. 186

Chiindi nchibeelede kukkala muzyuuno maJaji

(1) Dundumuntuule wabaJaji aMutobeli wakwe baba achuuno kuzwa buzuba nibatalika mulimu wabo kuze bakabe amyaaka makkumi musanu aabili, aawo mpubeelede kusiya mulimu kunze kwakuti nkabatanasisya myaaka eeyo, basale kuti bayungizye myaaka musanu aajulu: Naa kakuli kuti wasala kuchita oobo ulatola ngambo yakuti uponede kabotu muboongo amubili alimwi ngambo eeyo itambulwe aMuzulwidi waNyika musule aakuti wabonisyania aKkomishini yaMibeleko yaMulawo kuti ayinkilile nembo akubeleka

(2) BaJaji baNkuta yaKkata lyaMulawu bagawla kwalweendo lomwe lwachiindi myaaka kkumi ilamusanu ikonzya kuyumgizigwa, pele —

(a) Kweelede basiye mulimu myaaka eeyo kiitanasika naa basisya myaaka yakuzyalwa makkumi musanu amakkumi obile kunze kwakuti, nibasisya myaaka eeyo, basale kwiinkilila nembo kwamyaaka musanu kabali aachuuno: Naa kakuli kuti uusala kuchita oobo ulabweza ngambo yakuti uponede kabotu muboongo amubili alimwi ngambo yakwe ikatambulwe aMuzulwidi waNyika musule akubonisyania aKkomishini yaMbelekelano yaMulawu kuti ayinkilile nembo kabeleka achuuno.

(b) musule akumana kwachiindi chabo chitalidwe, bakonzya kupegwa milimu mbuuli maJaji aNkuta Yaajulu naakuti Nkuta Mpati naa basala kuchita oobo.

(3) MaJaji baNkuta Yaajulu baba azyuuno kuzwa buzuba nibatalika kubeleka achuuno eecho kuze bakabe amyaaka makkumi musanu aamakkumi obile, aawo mpubeelede kusiya mulimu kunze kwakuti nkabatanasisya myaaka eeyo basala kwiinkilila nembo akubeleka kwamyaaka musanu: Naa nkakuli kuti Jaji ulatola ngambo yakwe kuMuzulwidi waNyika wakuti upona kabotu muboongo amubili mpawo ngambo ikatambulwe aMuzulwidi waNyika musule akubonisyania aKkomishini yaMibeleko yaMulawu kuti ayinkilile nembo.

(4) Kakutakwe kuti ninzi zilembedwe kukabela (7) kachibeela 328, mpandamayake akabela (1), (2) a(3) zyachibeela eechi zilabeleka kweendelezya chuuno chaDundumuntuule wamaJaji, Mutobeli waDundumuntuule, majaji aNkuta yaKkata lyaMulawu amajaji baNkuta Yaajulu.

(5) MaJaji baNkuta Mpati antoomwe abamwi majaji baba achuuno kuzwa buzuba mbubatalika mulimu kuze bakabe amyaaka makkumi musanu amakkumi obile, aawo mpubelede kusiya mulimu.

(6) Muntu ulakonzya kubikwa chuuno chakubajaji waNkuta Yaajulu naakuti kufumbwa nkuta kwachiindi chitalidwe pele naa muntu uubikidwe kuti achite oobo nkajisilidi uumwi, uliima kuba jaji wankali waba amyaaka makkumi musanu amakkumi obile amasanu (naa kali jaji waNkuta Yaajulu naa kufumbwa nkuta) nikuba kakuli kuti chiindi chakwe chitalidwe chakubeleka kachitanamana.

(7) Nikuba kuti Jaji wasiya mulimu naan kali wasisya myaaka yakuzyalwa makkumi musanu amakkumi obile naakuti, kakulangidwe Jaji waNkuta yaKkata lyaMulawu kuti wasisya kumanina chiindi chakwe chitalidwe chakubeleka, ulakkala achuuno chabuJaji kuchitila kuti amaninsizye kubeteka milandu iilimumaboko aakwe nakachili Jaji.

(8) Jaji ulakonzya kusiya chuuno chakwe kufumbwa chiindi musule akulemba chizibisyo chiya kuMuzulwidi waNyika chitolwa aKkomishini yaMibeleko yaMulawu.

(9) Chuuno chaJaji takweelede chiimikila chiindi chakugalwa kwakwe. [Kabela kasowelwa mbali abusena bwako kubikwa chibeela 13 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]


Kugwisigwa kwamaJaji amulimu

(1) Jaji ukonzya kugwisigwa aamulimu luzutu kuti —

(a) tachikonzyi kuchita milimu yakwe nkaambo kakuchembaala naakuti kusondoka/kupenga;

(b) watondeezya kutaziba mulimu wakwe munzila iiyindilide; naakuti

(c) wasunda mulandu mupati loko;

nkaako Jaji takonzyi kugwisigwa amulimu, aanze kwakuti kwatobelezegwa zilembedwe muchibeela eechi.

(2) Kuti Muzulwidi waNyika wayeeya kugwisya Duundumuntule waBabetesi kuzwa amulimu, uyelede kugala kabunga kanga kalalangisisya kabotu kaambo aako.

(3) Kuti Kkomishini yaBubetesi yapa Muzulwidi waNyika muzeezo wakuti kugwisigwe Jaji amulimu, kubikkilizya Duundumuntule waBabetesi, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kugala kabunga kanga kalalangisisya kabotu kaambo aako.

(4) Kabunga kagalwa kuti kalangelange zyakugwisigwa kwaJaji, kakutobelezegwa zyaambidwe muchibeela eechi, keelede kuba aabantu batatu naakuti bayinda batatu kabagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika, kakuli—

(a) umwi wabo mukabunga kagalwa kali muntu —

(i) wakabeleka kuba Jaji muNkuta Yaajulujulu naakuti muNkuta Yaajulu muZimbabwe; naakuti

(ii) nguJaji naakuti wakali Jaji wankuta ilanga yoonse misyobo yamilandu (civil acriminal cases) munyika ibelesya mulawu waRoman Dutch Law naakuti mulawu waChikuwa kabiyo Chikuwa kachili ngumulaka ubelesegwa mukweendelezya milimu yafulumende munyika eeyo.

(b) Umwi naakuti bamwi babo beelede kusalwa kuzwa kuli baabo basimulawu bakabeleka kwaminyaka musanu ayibili naakuti kwiinda, kabali bakasangwa aKabunga Kabasimilawu kaambidwe muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(5) Kabunga Kabasimilawu kaambidwe mukabeela (4)(b) keelede kuzwa amundanda wamazina uwambidwe mukabeela nkakaako kuti mundanda wamazina ooyo wayandwa aMuzulwidi waNyika.

(6) Muzulwidi waNyika kweelede wagala umwi wabantu aaba kuti abe sichuuno wakabunga kagalwa.

(7) Kabunga kagalwa kwiinda mukabeela (2) naakuti (3) keelede kulangisisya kuti agwisigwe na Jaji amulimu. Nibalangalanga kweelede balemba lipoti/ngambo bayipa Muzulwidi waNyika mpawo baamba maboneno aatala akuti agwisigwe naakuti tagwisigwi Jaji amulimu wakwe.

(8) Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kuchita mbuuli zyaambwa akabunga kakali kulangisisya kaambo kakugwisigwa kwaJaji kwiinda mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (7).

(9) Kabunga kagalidwe kwiinda mukabeela aaka, kajisi zyeelelo anguzu mbuuli makkomishina kakutobelezegwa Mulawu waKkomishini yaMfukununo [Chaandaano 10:07].

(10) Kuti kaambo kaJaji kakatolwa kukabunga (tribunal) mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela eechi, Jaji ooyo kweelede wajana busosoloka kuzwa amulimu wakwe kusikila Muzulwidi waNyika wamuboozya lubo naakuti wamugwisya achuuno kazyeendelana azeezyo zyaambwa akabunga kakagalidwe kuti kalangelange mabelekelo aJaji.

(11) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa nguzu Kkomishini yaBubetesi naakuti kabunga kagalwa kwiinda muchibeela eechi kuti kakumbile fumbwa naba uli Jaji kuti ayete malembe atondeezya bwiime bwabulondozi bwakwe kuzwa kuKabunga kaBasilisi yabumbwa nkaambo kakuyanda kuti kuchitwe mulimu ooyo, nkukuti kubonwe kuti nchobeni Jaji uyimvwi biyeni mubulondozi bwakwe mumubili amuboongo. 188

Bwiime bwamulimu wabubetesi achiindi nchibeelede kukkala muzyuuno zyabo

(1) MaJaji beelede kujana mifolo alumwi lugwasyo lwaamulimu lusiyenesiyene zitanoobikkwe chiindi achiindi, zyoonse eezi zilangwalangwa aKkomishini yaBubetesi kazizuminwa aMuzulwidi waNyika uyelede kutanguna kupooma aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende Wazyabululami akujana mizeezo kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende waZyamali.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti weelede kupandulula mifolo anzibeelede kujana bamwi babelesi babubetesi bateensi maJaji. Mulawu ooyo uyelede kubona kuti kusumpulwa kwababelesi mumilimu yabo, kulonzegwa kuzwa kuli bumwi busena kuya kuli bumbi akwaandwa kwababelesi mulimu antoomwe antaamo zyeelede kutolwa mukubeteka babelesi basunda, zyeelede kuchitwa—

(a) kwiinda mukuzuminana aKkomishini yaBubetesi; kabiyo

(b) munzila ibalangala, itakwe lusaluulo, kuyoowa akufwidilana luse.

(3) Mifolo alumwi lugwasyo lwaamulimu lusiyenesiyene zyeelede kupegwa babelesi babubetesi zyeelede kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende.

(4) Mifolo alugwasyo lwaamulimu lusiyesiyene lupegwa babelesi babubetesi talweelede kuchesegwa pe kuti kabachili muzyuuno zyabo.

CHIBAKA 3 KKOMISHINI YABUBETESI 189

Kubumbwa abwiime bwaKkomishini yaBubetesi

(1) Kuli Kkomishini yaBubetesi ilaabantu batobela —

(a) Duundumuntule waBabetesi;

(b) Mutobeli waDuundumuntule waBabetesi;

(c) Muzulwidi waBabetesi baNkuta Yaajulu;

(d) womwe Jaji wagalwa amaJaji akuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, kuNkuta Yaajulujulu, kuNkuta Yaajulu, kuNkuta Yambelekelano akuNkuta Yanjendelezyo;

(e) Simulawu/Loya Mupati waFulumende;

(f) Mupati wabaMejastiliti;

(g) Sichuuno waKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu; [Fuka libambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

(h) botatwe basimulawu bakabeleka kwachiindi chaminyaka musanu ayibili naakuti kwiinda basangwa akabunga kayiminina basimulawu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kuti kweelede kuba kabunga kali boobo;

(i) womwe muyiisi wazyamulawu (Siangwilima kuli zyamulawu) wagalwa akabunga kayiminina bayiisi bazyamulawu mumaYunivesiti

amuZimbabwe, naakuti kakatawo kabunga kali boobo, Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kugala muyiisi wazyamulawu;

(j) womwe muntu ulaluzibo lwazyakulangalanga mabelesezyo amali utanooli wakabeleka kwachiindi chaminyaka musanu ayibili naakuti kwiinda utanooli wasangwa akabunga kabayiminina, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kuti kube kabunga kali boobo; kabiyo

(k) womwe ujisi minyaka yakubeleka ili musanu ayibili naakuti kwiinda, muli zyakubamba bantu ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Duundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti Uumutobela kuti katawo, mbabo beelede kuba Sichuuno bamiswaangano yaKkomishini yaBubetesi, pesi kuti kabatawo bobile chiindi chimwi, aabo baliwo bakonzya kusala umwi wabo kuti abe sichuuno wamuswaangano.

(3) Bantu bali muKkomishini yaBubetesi baambwa mumafuka (d),(h),(i),(j) a (k) mukabeela (1) aajulu bagalwa kuti bakkale mukkomishini kwamweenya womwe luzutu ulaaminyaka musanu awumwi. 190 Milimu yaKkomishini yaBubetesi

(1) IKkomishini yaBubetesi ikonzya kupa mizeezo kuli fulumende aatala azyabubetesi naakuti kweendelezegwa kwabululami munyika kabiyo fulumende weelede kuswiilizya mizeezo izwa ku Kkomishini yaBubetesi.

(2) IKkomishini yaBubetesi yeelede kusumpula akugwasizya kuti kube kulyiimikila akulisanta kwabubetesi antoomwe akubona kuti bubetesi bulokubeleka changuzu, kabotu munzila ibalangala mukweendelezya bululami muZimbabwe kabiyo ijisi nguzu zyoonse kuti ichite zyoonse eezi.

(3) IKkomishini yaBubetesi, kwiinda mukuzuminana aSilutwe waBululami, ikonzya kubumba miteto izulwida kuchitwa kwafumbwa nchinzi mukweendelezegwa kwabululami.

(4) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa Kkomishini yaBubetesi milimu iyendelana akubona zyakunjizigwa milimu, kubetekwa kwababelesi amifolo yababelesi alumwi lugwasyo lwaamulimu ndubajana babelesi babeleka muNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, muNkuta Yaajulujulu, muNkuta Yaajulu, muNkuta Yambelekelano amuNkuta Yanjendelezyo azimwi nkuta. 191

Bubalangale mumabelekelo aKkomishini yaBubetesi

IKkomishini yaBubetesi yeelede kuchita milimu yayo munzila yabululami, itakwe lusaluulo yabubalangale kabiyo ilaantanganana.

CHIBAKA 4 ZYABIYOBIYO 192 Mulawu uyelede kweendelezegwa Mulawu uyelede kweendelezegwa akubelesegwa munkuta zyaZimbabwe ngooyo wakali kubeleka chiindi nilyakatalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu antoomwe ayeeyo itanoobambululwe milawu mukuya kwachiindi. 193

—

Nkuta zyeelede kubeteka milandu yachakuchita aatala abasimilandu Nkuta zitobela luzutu nzizyo zyeelede kubeteka milandu yatala abasimilandu

(a) Nkuta yaZyaKkata lyaMulawu, Nkuta Yaajulujulu, Nkuta Yaajulu aNkuta yaMejasitiliti;

(b) nkuta naakuti kabunga kabeteka aako kabeteka kakabelesya mulawu wazyakubeteka babelesi babisya nzizikonzya kubeteka milandu yamamipati kakuya akweelela kwamilandu kuliwo kuchitila kuti kube kulijata kabotu kwababelesi bali muli zyakukwabililwa kwanyika (mapulisa, basilumamba ababamba baange abamwi).

CHAANDAANO 9 NDEMEKO ZYAKWEENDELEZYA BULUMBU ANTOOMWE ABUZULUZI 194

Ndemeko mpati zizulwida kweendelezegwa kwabulumbu

(1) Kweendelezegwa kwabulumbu muzibeela zyafulumende zyoonse, kubikkilizya mbunga atubunga twafulumende, mitabi yafulumende azimwi zipanzi zyafulumende, kweelede kwazulwidwa aandemeko zyabulelo bwajandabanji mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kubikkilizya ndemeko zitobela—

(a) kulijata kwababelesi munzila itondeezya kuziba zyeelede kuchitwa mumulimu wabo kweelede kwasungwaazigwa akuchitwa choonse chiindi;

(b) kubelesya zibelesyo munzila yabunonoozi kweelede kwasungwaazigwa;

(c) kweendelezegwa kwabulumbu kweelede kwalemeka lusumpuko;

(d) lugwasyo lupegwa bulumbu kweelede lwachitwa kakutakwe lusaluulo, munzila ibalangala kabiyo itasumpuli bbazu limwi luzutu;

(e) mapenzi aabantu ayelede kwiingulwa muchiindi chisyoonto loko, kabiyo bulumbu ngabunjilingane mukupangwa kwamizeezo atulawulawu aawo mpukweelede;

(f) bweendelezi bwabulumbu bweelede kakulangwalangwa chiindi achiindi aPalyamenti antoomwe abantu lwabo;

(g) ntabi, mbunga atubunga twafulumende muzibeela zyoonse zyanyika kweelede zyabelekela aamwi;

(h) bubalangale mumachitilo amilimu kweelede kusungwaazigwa kwiinda mukupa bulumbu lugwasyo lwaluzibo lulaamunse kabiyo lubagwasizya muchiindi eecho;

(i) kugwasya babelesi munzila zisiyenesiyene zibagwasizya kusumpula mabelekelo aluzibo lwabo kweelede kwasungwazigwa kuchitila kuti babelesi babelesye zipo antoomwe aluzibo lwabo zizwide;

(j) kweendelezegwa kwabulumbu kweelede kwachitwa abantu bazwa mumisyobo bantu isiyenesiyene bachisi chaZimbabwe;

(k) kunjizya milimu, kuyiisigwa makkosi aamilimu akusumpulwa kwabantu muzyiiyo zyabo kweelede kwachitwa munzila ilangisisya kuziba akukonzya kwabantu, kakutakwe lusaluulo, bweelene akati kabaalumi abanakazi akubikkilizya bantu balemene. kabiyo fulumende uyelede kutola ntaamo, kubikkilizya kupanga milawu, mukusungwaazya kutobelezegwa akuzuzikizigwa kwandemeko eezi

(2) Kunjizya milimu mumawofesi ajanika muzibeela zisiyenesiyene zyafulumende, kubikkilizya mbunga atubunga twafulumende, mitabi yafulumende azimwi zipanzi zyafulumende, kweelede kwachitwa munzila ilangisisya kuziba akukonzya kwabantu.

195 Mabbizinesi antoomwe aMakkapani afulumende

(1) Makkapani awamwi mabbizinesi afulumende ayeelede kuti aajulu kwakutobelezya antoomwe akuchita ndemeko zyaambidwe muchibeela 194(1), ngaachite milimu yawo munzila iyeta mpindu nkukuti ayendelele nembo kaachiliwo kabiyo akutobelezya buzuluzi buzikitide mukweendelezya mabbizinesi aawo.

(2) Makkapani awamwi mabbizinesi afulumende awambwa mukabeela (1) ayelede kubikka nzila zitondeezya kubalangala akusungwaazya bantu/mabbizinesi amwi manji kuti ajatikane mukuula zintu zyawo. 196 Mikuli yaBabelesi Baluundu andemeko zyabuzuluzi

(1) Nguzu zipedwe Babelesi Baluundu zijanwa mulusyomo bulumbu ndulupede fulumende nkaako kweelede nguzu eezyo zyabelesegwa kabotu munzila —

(a) iyendelana ampalamo zyaKkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(b)itondeezya kulemeka bulumbu achiyandisyo chakugwasizya kutatondeezyi kuti mubelesi wafulumende nguwe ujatwa mbuuli mwaami biya abulumbu mbwayelede kubelekela; kabiyo

(c)isungwaazya kuti bulumbu bube alusyomo kuBabelesi Baluundu.

(2) Babelesi Baluundu beelede kulijata, aamulimu akunze kwamulimu, munzila ibapa bulemu alulemeko antoomwe akutatondeezya kusumpula nzibayanda beni babelesi pesi kusumpula ziyandikana aamulimu. Kabiyo beelede kusiyana anchito zyuubula chiimo chamawofesi aabo achiimo chafulumende.

(3) Babelesi Baluundu bajisi zyuuno zyabuzuluzi beelede kutobelezya ndemeko zyabuzuluzi zitobela—

(a) kuchita milimu munzila itakwe lusaluulo;

(b) kusyomeka mukuchita mulimu wabulumbu;

(c) kutondeezya kulemeka bulumbu akuchita zintu zyeelede aziyandwa aabulumbu; kabiyo

(d) kulijata akutondeezya kulipeda kumulimu nikugwasigwa bulumbu. 197

Chiindi cheelede kukkalwa abasilutwe batutabi tusiyene-siyene twafulumende muzyuuno zyabo

Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kufwiinsya chiindi cheelede kukkalwa aBabelesi Baluundu muzyuuno zyabo, Babelesi Baluundu bamumitabi yafulumende amumabbizinesi afulumende. 198 Mulawu weelede kubona kuzuzikizigwa Chaandaano 9 Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula ntaamo zyeelede kutolwa kuti eezi zyaambidwe muchaandaano eechi zyatobelezegwa antoomwe akuchitwa, kubikkilizya ntaamo zya —

(a) kuchita kuti Babelesi Baluundu kabazibisya bulumbu chiindi achiindi lubono lwabo;

(b) kubumba zizuminano zyakulijata kwababelesi zyeelede kutobelezegwa aBabelesi Baluundu;

(c) kubikka antanganana zyeelede kuchitwa mukutobelezya buzuluzi buzikitide mukweendelezya mabbizinesi afulumende;

(d) kuzwa antaamo zyeelede kutobelezegwa mukubeteka bantu batyola zyaambwa muChaandaano eechi naakuti fumbwa zizuminano zyakulijata kwababelesi zyaambidwe mufuka (b) lilaajulu.

CHAANDAANO 10 BABELESI BALUUNDU [Mutwe wamakani ubambululwa achibeela 14 chaMulawu 2 wa2021


]

Babelesi Baluundu [Mutwe wamakani wachibeela ubambululwa achibeela 14(a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021 ]

(1) kuliMutabi womwe waBabelesi baLuundu lwakweendelezya Nkunkume zyalubazu lwaFulumende kuchitila kuti lugwasye kweendelezya Zimbabwe alimwi akupa lugwasyo kuluundu. [kabela kasowegwa achibeela 14(b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021 ]

(2) Babelesi baluundu mbantu babelekela luundu aanze kwa — [Kabela kabambululwa achibeela 14( c) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(a) babelesi banjide mutubunga twakukwabilila nyika naakuti fumbwa kabunga kanga kalabumbwa kakukwabilila nyika;

(b) maJaji, maMejasitiliti abamwi babelesi bamunkuta zisiyenesiyene;

(c) bantu bajanika lyaMulawu eeli;

mumaKkomishini

aabumbwa

kwiinda

muKkata

(d) babelesi bamuPalyamenti; kabiyo

(e) fumbwa muntu ulaachuuno chaambidwe, muKkata Lyamilawu eeli naakuti aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti, kuti tayelede kubalilwa kuBabelesi Baluundu. [Fuka lyagwisigwa achibeela 14 (c) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula bwiime, bubumbwe, kweendelezegwa, kulijata kwababelesi anzibeelede kujana mukubeleka kwabo babelesi baluundu, kakulangwa zilembedwe muchibeela 203. [kabela kagwisigwa achibeela 14 (c) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]


Kulijata kwaBabelesi Baluundu

(1) Babelesi Baluundu beelede kulijata munzila zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli ayimwi milawu;

(2) Taakwe Mubelesi Waluundu uyelede kuswiilizya akutobelezya malayilile atali mumulawu pe.

(3) Mukuchita milimu yabo taakwe Babelesi Baluundu beelede—

(a) kuchita milimu yakabunga kakweendelezegwa kwanyika;

(b) kuzuzikizya mpalamo zyakabunga kakweendezya nyika;

(c) kudyaamizya ziyanda kuchitwa akabunga kazyanyika zili mumulawu;

(d) kutyola akutalemeka zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zyanikuba uuli muntu,

(4) Babelesi Baluundu tabeelede pe kunjila muzyuuno zyatubunga twakweendelezegwa kwanyika.

(5) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti weelede kutola ntaamo zyakubona kuti Babelesi Baluundu taakwe kabunga kakweendelezegwa kwanyika nkibadundiizya pe. [Kabela kabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyaBabelesi Baluundu

Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kugala Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBabelesi Baluundu. [Chibeela chibambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]


Kupangwa abubumbwe bwaKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu

(1) Kuli Kkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu ilaabantu batobela —

(a) Sichuuno antoomwe awutobela Sichuuno; kabiyo

(b) bambi bantu bayinda babili pesi kabatayindi musanu; bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Bantu bagalwa kunjila muKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu kweelede kabali bantu bajisi luzibo lwamulimu wakweendelezya bulumbu. [kabela kakabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

203 Milimu yaKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu

(1) Kkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu ijisi milimu itobela —

(a) kusalwa bantu beelede alimwi balaluzibo luliluzi lwakuba achuuno chaMibeleko yaLuundu kwakuti mweelo wakkumi kuzwa kumwaanda ube wabantu bapona abulema; [Kabela kabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

(b) kwiinda muchibeela 65(5), kuchita mabambe aamifolo alugwasyo lwaamulimu ndubeelede kujana Babelesi Baluundu;

(c) kuzyotolola akubeteka Babelesi Baluundu babisya mumilimu;

(d) kulangisisya ntongawuko akuyanduula nsandulo zyantongawuko zizwa kuBabelesi Baluundu;

(e) kutola ntaamo zinga zilagwasizya akusungwaazya Babelesi Baluundu mukuchita milimu yabo changuzu;

(f) kubona kuti Babelesi Baluundu balookubeleka kabotu milimu yabo mbuuli mbukweelede kakutakwe lusaluulo;

(g) kupa Muzulwidi waNyika antoomwe aSilutwe waMutabi waBabelesi Baluundu mizeezo abusongo aatala azintu zisiyenesiyene zyaatala aBabelesi Baluundu;

(h) kusumpula akusungwaazya kulemekwa kwa ndemeko akati kaBabelesi baBaluundu mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli; kabiyo

(i) kuchita niiba iili milimu mbuuli mbitanooli yapegwa manguzu aakuchita milimu eeyo aKkata lyaMulawu naakuti aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Kkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu ikonzya kupanga miteto, kwiinda mukuzuminana aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende, igwasizya kuzuzikizigwa kwampalamo zyamilimu yabo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (1).

(3) Kkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu yeelede kuchita milimu yayo kwiinda mumalembe aamalayilile aazwa kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende chiindi achiindi.

(4) Mukuzwa amabambe amifolo alumwi lugwasyo lwaamulimu lupegwa Babelesi Baluundu, iKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu iyelede kuchita milimu yayo kwiinda mukuzuminana aMuzulwidi waNyika kali alakwe wapegwa mizeezo abaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende babona zyaMali aBabelesi Baluundu. [Kabela kabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

Bayiminini baNyika yaZimbabwe kulaamwi maNyika

Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kugala bantu kuti bakayiminine nyika yaZimbabwe kulaamwi maNyika, naakuti kuli tumwi tubunga twamanyika andeene kabiyo Muzulwidi waNyika ujisi nguzu zyakubagwisya nichiba chili chiindi bayiminini aabo bagalwa. 204 A Mulembi Mupati waWofesi yaMuzulwidi waNyika aKabunga kaBazulwidi baMitabi yaFulumende

(1) Mulembi Mupati waWofesi yaMuzulwidi waNyika aBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende aBatobeli babo bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika musule akubonisyania aKKomishini yaBabelesi baLuundu.

(2) Mulembi Mupati waWofesi yaMuzulwidi waNyika aKabunga kabaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende mubelesi mupati kulibamwi baBabelesi baLuundu alimwi Balembi Bapati baMitabi basalwa kakutobelwa Chibeela 205 bali munsi lyaNabbatule waWofesi yaMuzulwidi waNyika akabunga kabaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende kufumbwa makani aali azyakuchita ambabo.

(3) Bulamfu bwachiindi chakubeleka alimwi azyiimo zyamubeleko zyaMulembi Mupati waWofesi yaMuzulwidi waNyika akabunga kabaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende antoomwe abatobeli babo bulaba mbuuli mbabikide Muzulwidi waNyika. [Chibeela chabikwa achibeela 15 chaMulawu 2 wa2021] 205 Beendelezi baMitabi yaFulumende

(1) Balembi baMitabi yaFulumende bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kajana mizeezo kuzwa kuKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu. [Kabela kabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Bulamfu bwachiindi cheelede kukkalwa muchuuno aMulembi waMutabi waFulumende minyaka isika kumusanu, kabiyo ukonzya kupegwa uumbi mweenya wakukkala muchuuno eecho kwachiindi chaminyaka musanu pesi kakulangisisigwa kuti ubeleka zidodonkene na milimu yakwe.

CHAANDAANO 11 BAKWABILILA NYIKA

CHIBAKA 1 ZYABIYOBIYO 206

Kukwabililwa Kwanyika

(1) Impalamo zyakukwabililwa kwanyika zyeelede kutondeezya chiyandisyo chabana baZimbabwe chakupona bweelene, mulwaangunuko, muluumuno, mukutayoowa kabiyo mulusumpuko.

(2) Kukwabililwa kwaNyika yaZimbabwe kweelede kwachitwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli antoomwe ayimwi milawu.

(3) Kapati, kukwabililwa kwanyika yaZimbabwe kweelede kuchitwa kakulemekwa eezi zintu zitobela—

(a) zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka antoomwe andemeko zyabulelo bwajandabanji bwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli; kabiyo

(b) kulemeka akutobelezya mulawu.

Tubunga tukwabilila nyika

(1) Tubunga tukwabilila nyika yaZimbabwe ntootu —

(a) Basilumamba

(b) Bakapokola

(c) Bufumfuzyi

(d) BaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange; kabiyo

(e) fumbwa kabunga kakukwabililwa kwanyika kabumbwa muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

kwiinda

(2) Tubunga tukwabilila nyika tuchita milimu yato katutobelezya Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, zyaambwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kabiyo mabelekelo aato alangisisigwa aPalyamenti.

(3) Bubumbwe abube bwatubunga twakukwabililwa kwanyika kweelede kutondeezya busiyane bwamisyobo yabantu bamuZimbabwe. 208

Kulijata kwababelesi batubunga twakukwabilila nyika

(1) Babelesi batubunga tukwabilila nyika beelede kulijata kwiinda muli zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli antoomwe ayimwi milawu.

(2) Taakwe kabunga naakuti mubelesi wakabunga kakukwabililwa kwanyika unga mukuchita mulimu wakwe—

(a) wachita katobelezya zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika;

(b) kuzuzikizya mpalamo zyakabunga kakweendelezegwa kwanyika;

(c) kudyaamizya ziyanda kuchitwa akabunga kazyanyika zili mumulawu;

(d) Kutyola akutalemeka zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zyanikuba uuli muntu.

(3) Babelesi batubunga tukwabilila nyika tabeelede pe kunjila muzyuuno zyatubunga twakweendelezegwa kwanyika naakuti imwi mbungano biyo.

(4) Babelesi batubunga tukwabilila nyika tabeelede pe kunjizigwa milimu mutubunga twabanasinakoma aanze kwazyiindi nizyayimikila luzeya zintu. 209

Kkansili yaKukwabililwa Kwanyika

(1) Kuli Kkansili yaKukwabililwa Kwanyika ilaabantu batobela: Muzulwidi waNyika kali nguSichuuno, Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika antoomwe abaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende ababelesi batubunga tukwabilila nyika abamwi bantu banga balaambwa muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Milimu yaKkansiliyaKukwabililwa Kwanyika njeeyi—

(a) kubumba mabambe ambiinga ilakwabililwa nyika yaZimbabwe;

(b) kuzibisya akupa mizeezo kuMuzulwidi waNyika aatala akukwabililwa kwanyika; kabiyo

(c) kuchita imwi milimu inga ilaambwa aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(3) Makkomanda aatubunga tukwabilila nyika ayelede kupa Kkansili yaKukwabilwa Kwanyika luzibo aatala abwiime bwakukwabililwa kwanyika chiindi achiindi mbuuli mpiyayandila Kkansili yaKukwabililwa Kwanyika. 210

Nzila ilyiimikilide yakutambula ntongawuko

Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti weelede kupandulula nzila ilomene kabiyo ilyiimikilide akulisanta yakutambula akuzilangisisya ntongawuko zizwa kubulumbu

zilaatala akutalijata kabotu kwababelesi batubunga tukwabilila nyika. Nzila eeyi yeelede kuzyotolola kubisya kutanooli kwachitwa.

CHIBAKA 2 BASILUMAMBA 211

Basilumamba

(1) Basilumamba baZimbabwe bajatikizya bali mutubunga tutobela: Balwanina Aansi

(Army) antoomwe aBalwanina Mumulengalenga atumwi tubunga twabasilumba tunga tulabumbwa aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Basilumamba baZimbabwe mbabo luzutu basilumamba bali mumulawu munyika.

(3) Basilumamba beelede kulemeka zyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu boonse kabiyo tabeelede kunjila muli zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika, pesi bapalame kukwabilila akulemeka nyika mbiizulwa, bayandisisye nyika yabo, bachite mulimu wabo munzila iyelede kabiyo kabaswiilila buzuluzi bwenyika buteensi basilumamba mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(4) Basilumamba beelede kukkala kabali babelesi bakwizi kulijata kabotu munchito zyabo.

(5) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula bwiime, bubumbwe, kweendelezegwa, kulijata kwababelesi anzibeelede kujana mukubeleka kwabo babelesi batubunga twabasilumamba, kakulangwa zilembedwe muchibeela 218. 212 Milimu yaBasilumamba Milimu yaBasilumamba nkukwabilila nyika yaZimbabwe, bulumbu bwayo, lukwabililo lwachisi anzyiyanda nyika amigaano yayo antoomwe akusumpula Kkata lyaMulawu. 213

Kutumwa kwaBasilumamba kumulimu

(1) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, Muzulwidi waNyika kali nguDuundumuntule waBasilumamba, nguwe luzutu uujisi nguzu —

(a) zyakutuma Basilumamba kunkondo; naakuti

(b) ulaanguzu zyakwaamba mbubeelede kuyoochita nkubatumwa kunkondo Basilumamba.

(2) Kwiinda mumvumo yaMuzulwidi waNyika, Basilumamba bakonzya kutumwa kumulimu mukati kanyika yaZimbabwe kuti —

(a) kabakwabilila nyika yaZimbabwe;

(b) kabagwasizya Bakapokola mukubamba luumuno lwabulumbu;

(c) kabagwasizya Bakapokola atumwi tubunga twabantu biyo muchiindi zintu nizyiimikila luzeya naakuti nikwaba chachitika chibi.

(3) Kwiinda mumvumo yaMuzulwidi waNyika, Basilumamba bakonzya kutumwa kumulimu aanze kwanyika yaZimbabwe —

(a) kukubamba luumuno kabalaansi kwaMbungano yaKujatana kwaManyika Woonse naakuti kamwi biyo kabunga kamuluwa kamanyikamanyika pesi nyika yaZimbabwe nkiilyidilana aankako.

(b) kukukwabilila migaano yayimwi nyika itali Zimbabwe;

(c) kukuzuzikizya mulimu wamanyikamanyika aandeene;

(d) kukukwabilila ziyandwa akugwasya nyika yaZimbabwe.

(4) Kwiinda mukuzuminana kwabayiminini bamuPalyamenti basika kubwiingi bwantompachaala mumuswaangano uswaanizya maanda amilawu obile (yaansi ayaajulu), Palyamenti ikonzya kuzuminana kwaamba kuti kutumwa kwaBasilumamba aanze kwanyika nkubakatumwa ngababoozegwe.

(5) Aawo Palyamenti mpuyazuminana kuti Basilumamba bakatumwa aanze kwanyika baboozegwe, Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kubweza ntaamo zyoonse zyakuboozya Basilumamba, pesi kalangisisya kukwabililwa kwabantu bamuZimbabwe azibelesyo zyabo. 214

Beelede kuzibisigwa nikwatumwa kwaBasilumamba Aawo Basilumamba nibatumwa kumulimu wabo —

(a) Basilumamba beelede kuba aansi kwaMupati waBasilumamba womwe; naakuti

(b) Yoonse milimu yabo Basilumamba naakuti yobile naakuti kwiinda mpubabelekela aamwi beelede kuba aansi kwaBapati baBasilumamba basiyene—

Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kuzibisya Palyamenti kufwambaana eezyo zyapa kuti atume Basilumamba kabiyo—

(i) kwaamba busena nkwaabatuma muZimbabwe;

(ii) kwaamba busena nkwabatuma aanze kwaZimbabwe. 215

Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBasilumamba

Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kugala Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBasilumamba. 216

Kuzulwidwa kwaBasilumamba

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupandulula kuti —

(a) Basilumamba beelede kuba aansi kwaMupati waBasilumamba womwe; naakuti

(b) Yoonse milimu yabo Basilumamba naakuti yobile naakuti kwiinda mpubabelekela aamwi beelede kuba aansi kwaBapati baBasilumamba basiyene.

(2) Woonse Mupati waBasilumamba awoonse Mupati wakapanzi kaBasilumamba ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kajana mizeezo kuzwa kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende waBasilumamba.

(3) Bapati baBasilumamba aBapati batupanzi twaBasilumamba bagalwa kuti babe muzyuuno zyabo kwabulamfu bwachiindi chisika kuminyaka musanu kabiyo muntu takonzyi kupegwa myeenya iyinda tubili kali muchuuno chomwe.

(4) Muntu wagalwa kuti abe Mupati waBasilumamba naakuti kapanzi kaBasilumamba ukonzya kugalwa kuba Mupati waBasilumamba, pesi muntu wakali Mupati waBasilumamba naakuti kapanzi kaBasilumamba takonzyi kugalwa kuba Mupati waBasilumamba naakuti Mupati wakapanzi kaBasilumamba naakuti kuba Mupati wakamwi kapanzi kakukwabililwa kwanyika.

(5) Woonse Mupati waBasilumamba awoonse Mupati wakapanzi kaBasilumamba weelede kubeleka mulimu wakwe katobelezya malayilile ngaapegwa aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBasilumamba kali alakwe uchita nzyachita kajana mvumo kuzwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

Kupangwa abubumbwe bwaKkomishini yaBasilumamba

(1) Kuli Kkomishini yaBasilumamba ilaabantu batobela; Sichuuno weelede kuba kali nguSichuuno waKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu, antoomwe abamwi bantu bayinda babili pesi kabatayindi musanu awumwi bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika. [Kabeela kabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Bajanika muKkomishini yaBasilumamba beelede kusalwa kwiinda muluzibo lwabo lwakweendelezya milimu, zyankondo, alwiiyo ndubalaalo naakuti kuba kwabo kupa kuti babe lugwasyo kuKkomishini; kabiyo—

(a) basika kuchisela chabantu bajanika muKkomishini kabali mbantu batana beleka mulimu waBasilumamba;

(b) uumwi naakuti kwiinda uumwi kali wakali waba umwi waBapati mumulimu waBasilumamba kwamweenya womwe naakuti yobile isisya minyaka musanu kaziya nembo. 218 Milimu yaKkomishini yaBasilumamba

(1) Kkomishini yaBasilumamba ijisi milimu itobela —

(a) kunjizya bantu bazi milimu kuti babweze zyuuno naakuti bapegwe nyembe mumulimu waBasilumamba;

(b) kubamba mabambe aamabelekelo aabasilumamba kakulangwa mifolo ampindu zyaandeene

(c) kubona kuti balookupona buumi buli kabotu akweendelezegwa kabotu mumilimu yabo Basilumamba;

(d) kubona kuti Basilumamba bazuzikizya ziyandwa achibeela 208;

(e) kusungwaazya luumuno akumvwanana akati kaBasilumamba abulumbu bwaZimbabwe;

(f) kupa Muzulwidi waNyika antoomwe aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende mizeezo abusongo aatala azintu zisiyenesiyene zyaatala aBasilumamba; kabiyo

(g) kuchita niiba iili milimu mbuuli mbitanooli yapegwa manguzu akuchita milimu eeyo aKkata lyaMulawu antoomwe aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Kkomishini yaBasilumamba ikonzya kupanga miteto, kwiinda mukuzuminana aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona Basilumamba, igwasizya kuzuzikizigwa kwampalamo zyamilimu yabo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (1)

(3) Mukuzwa amabambe amifolo alumwi lugwasyo lwaamulimu lupegwa yaBasilumamba, iKkomishini yaBasilumamba yeelede kuchita milimu yayo kwiinda mukuzuminana aMuzulwidi waNyika kali alakwe wapegwa mizeezo abaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende babona zyaMali aBasilumamba.

CHIBAKA 3 BAKAPOKOLA 219

Bakapokola amilimu yabo

(1) Kuli Bakapokola bajisi milimu iitobela —

(a) kubbutula, kupokoola akukwabilila kuchitika kwamilandu;

(b) kubamba luumuno lwabulumbu munyika yaZimbabwe;

(c) kukwabilila lubono abuumi bwabulumbu;

(d) kubona kuti mulawu ulokutobelezegwa akulemekwa; kabiyo

(e) kusumpula Kkata lyaMulawu akubona kuti mulawu ulokutobelezegwa kakutakwe kuyoowa naakuti lusaluulo.

(2) Bakapokola beelede kuchita milimu yabo kabaalamwi a —

(a) tubunga twatuswiilizya bavwiya tubumbwa kwiinda mumulawu;

(b) fumbwa muntu wabikkwa amulawu kuti agwasizye mukubbutula, kupokoola akukwabilila kuchitika kwayimwi misyobo yamilandu;

(c) tubunga twamuluwa kwamilandu.

atwamanyika

aandeene

alwana

kusundwa

(3) Bakapokola tabeelede kunjila mutubunga twazyakweendelezegwa kwanyika, pesi bapalame kulemeka nyika mbiizulwa, bayandisisye nyika yabo, bachite mulimu wabo munzila yeelede kabiyo kabaswiilila buzuluzi bwenyika butali bakapokola mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(4) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti weelede kupandulula bwiime, bubumbwe, kweendelezegwa, kulijata kwababelesi anzibeelede kujana mukubeleka kwabo babelesi baBakapokola, kakulangwa zilembedwe muchibeela 223. 220 Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBakapokola Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kugala Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBakapokola. 221

Nabbatule waBakapokola

(1) Bakapokola balaansi kwabweendelezi bwaNabbatule waBakapokola ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kagwasigwa aSilutwe waFulumende ubona zyaBakapokola.

(2) Nabbatule waBakapokola ugalwa kuti abe achuuno kwaminyaka musanu pesi ukonzya kupegwa uumwi biyo uumbi mweenya waminyaka musanu lubo.

(3) Muntu wakali wabeleka mulimu wabuNabbatule waBakapokola takonzyi kugalwa kuti akabe Nabbatule kuli kambi kabunga kakukwabililwa kwanyika.

(4) Nabbatule waBakapokola weelede kubeleka mulimu wakwe katobelezya malayilile ngaapegwa aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBakapokola kali alakwe uchita nzyachita kajana mvumo kuzwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika. 222

Kupangwa abubumbwe bwaKkomishini yaBakapokola

(1) Kuli Kkomishini yaBakapokola ilaabantu batobela; Sichuno uyelede kuba kali nguSichuuno waKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu, antoomwe abamwi bantu bayinda babili pesi kabatayindi musanu awumwi bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika. [Kabeela kabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Bajanika muKkomishini yaBakapokola beelede kusalwa kwiinda muluzibo lwabo lwakweendelezya milimu, zyankondo, alwiiyo ndubalaalo naakuti kuba kwabo kupa kuti babe lugwasyo kuKkomishini; kabiyo—

(a) basika kuchisela chabantu bajanika muKkomishini kabali mbantu batana beleka mulimu waBakapokola;

(b) uumwi naakuti kwiinda uumwi kali wakali waba umwi waBapati mumulimu waBakapokola kwamweenya womwe naakuti yobile isisya minyaka musanu kaziya nembo.

223 Milimu yaKkomishini yaBakapokola

(1) Kkomishini yaBakapokola ijisi milimu itobela —

(a) kunjizya bantu bazi milimu kuti babweze zyuuno naakuti nyembe mumulimu waBakapokola;

(b) kuchita mabambe aamifolo alugwasyo ndubeelede kujana lwaamulimu Bakapokola;

(c) kubona kuti balookupona buumi buli kabotu akweendelezegwa kabotu mumilimu yabo Bakapokola;

(d) kubona kuti Bakapokola bazuzikizya ziyandwa achibeela 208;

(e) kusungwaazya luumuno akumvwanana akati kaBakapokola abulumbu bwaZimbabawe bateensi Bakapokola;

(f) kupa Muzulwidi waNyika antoomwe aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende mizeezo abusongo aatala azintu zisiyenesiyene zyaatala aBakapokola; kabiyo

(g) kuchita niiba iili milimu mbuuli mbitanooli yapegwa manguzu akuchita milimu eeyo aKkata lyaMulawu antoomwe aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Kkomishini yaBakapokola ikonzya kupanga milawu, kwiinda mukuzuminana aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona Bakapokola, igwasizya kuzuzikizigwa kwampalamo zyamilimu yabo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (1).

(3) Mukuzwa amabambe amifolo alumwi lugwasyo lwaamulimu lupegwa yaBakapokola, iKkomishini yaBakapokola yeelede kuchita milimu yayo kwiinda mukuzuminana aMuzulwidi waNyika kali alakwe wapegwa mizeezo abaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende babona zyaMali azyaBakapokola.

CHIBAKA 4 BUFUMFUUZI 224

Kupangwa kwakabunga Kabufumfuuzyi

(1) Fumbwa kabunga kaFulumende kaBufumfuuzyi, katali kabunga kabufumfuuzyi kajanika muli Basilumamba amuli Bakapokola, keelede kubumbwa kwiinda mumulawu naakuti malayilile azwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti kuNkamu yabaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende (kkabbineti).

(2) Bafumfuzyi tabeelede pe kunjila mukweendelezegwa kwanyika, pesi bapalame kulemeka nyika mbiizulwa, bay andisisye nyika yabo, bachite milimu yabo munzila yeelede kabiyo kabaswiilila buzuluzi bwenyika butali bwa bufumfuuzyi pe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli. 225 Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBufumfuuzyi Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kugala Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaBufumfuuzyi. 226

Kweendelezegwa kwakabunga kaBufumfuuzyi

(1) Kabunga kaBufumfuzyi keelede kweendelezegwa aNabbatule waBufumfuuzyi (Director-General of Intelligence Services) weelede kugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika

kwaminyaka musanu, kakonzya kupegwa uumbi lubo mweenya waminyaka ikozyania bwiingi bwayo.

(2) Nabbatule waBufumfuuzyi weelede kubeleka mulimu wakwe katobelezya malayilile ngaapegwa aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zya Bufumfuuzyi kali alakwe uchita nzyachita kajana mvumo kuzwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

(3) Muntu wakali wabeleka mulimu wakuba Nabbatule waBufumfuuzyi takonzyi kugalwa kuti akabe Nabbatule kuli kambi kabunga kakukwabililwa kwanyika.

CHIBAKA 5 NTOLONGO/MAJELE AKUZYOTOLOLWA KWABAANGE 227 Milimu yakabunga kaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange

(1) Kuli kabunga kaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange kalaamilimu iitobela —

(a) kukwabilila luundu kuzwa kubantu basunda kwiinda mukubajalila mumajele akuzyotolola bantu bajanwa kabaalamilandu munkuta kabeelede kujalilwa akuzoogwisigwa lubo kuti babweede muluundu nikwiinda chiindi chaambwa aankuta; kabiyo

(b) kweendelezegwa kwamajele amasena akuzyotolola baange.

(2) Babelesi bakabunga kaNtolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange tabeelede pe kunjila mukweendelezegwa kwanyika, ngabapalame kulemeka nyika mbiizulwa, bayandisisye nyika yabo, bachite mulimu wabo munzila iyelede kabiyo kabaswiilila buzuluzi bwenyika butali bwatuswiilizya bavwiya mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula bwiime, bubumbwe, kweendelezegwa, kulijata kwababelesi anzibeelede kujana mukubeleka kwabo babelesi bakabunga kaNtolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange, kakulangwa zilembedwe muchibeela 231. 228

Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaNtolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange

Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kugala Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaNtolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange. 229

Nabbatule waNtolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange

(1) Babelesi bakabunga ka Ntolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange balaansi kwabweendelezi bwa Nabbatule wa Ntolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kagwasigwa aSilutwe waFulumende ubona zya Ntolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange.

(2) Nabbatule wa Ntolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange ugalwa kuti abe achuuno kwaminyaka musanu pesi ukonzya kupegwa uumbi mweenya waminyaka musanu lubo.

(3) Muntu wakali wabeleka mulimu wabuNabbatule wa Ntolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange takonzyi kugalwa kuti akabe Nabbatule kuli kambi kabunga kakukwabililwa kwanyika.

(4) Nabbatule wa Ntolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange weelede kubeleka mulimu wakwe katobelezya malayilile ngaapegwa aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zya Ntolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange kali alakwe uchita nzyachita kajana mvumo kuzwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

230 Kupangwa abubumbwe bwaKkomishini yaNtolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange

(1) Kuli Kkomishini ya Ntolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange ilaabantu batobela; Sichuuno uyelede kuba kali nguSichuuno waKkomishini yaBabelesi Baluundu, antoomwe abamwi bantu bayinda babili pesi kabatayindi musanu awumwi bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika. [Kabeela kakabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Bajanika muKkomishini yaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange beelede kusalwa kwiinda muluzibo lwabo lwakweendelezya milimu, zyankondo, alwiiyo ndubalaalo naakuti kuba kwabo kupa kuti babe lugwasyo kuKkomishini; kabiyo—

(a) basika kuchisela chabantu bajanika muKkomishini kabali mbantu batana beleka mukabunga kaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange;

(b) uumwi naakuti kwiinda uumwi kali wakali waba umwi waBapati mumulimu waMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange kwamweenya womwe naakuti yobile isisya minyaka musanu kaziya nembo. 231 Milimu yaKkomishini yaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange

(1) Kkomishini yaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange ijisi milimu itobela —

(a) kunjizya bantu bazi milimu kuti babweze zyuuno naakuti nyembe mumulimu wamu Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange;

(b) kuchita mabambe aamifolo alugwasyo ndubeelede kujana lwaamulimu wamuMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange;

(c) kubona kuti balookupona buumi buli kabotu akweendelezegwa kabotu mumilimu yabo babelesi bamuMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange;

(d) kubona kuti babelesi bamuMajele bazuzikizya ziyandwa achibeela 208;

aKuzyotololwa

Kwabaange

(e) kusungwaazya luumuno akumvwanana akati kaBakapokola abulumbu bwaZimbabwe;

(f) kupa Muzulwidi waNyika antoomwe aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende mizeezo abusongo aatala azintu zisiyenesiyene zyaatala ababelesi bamuMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange; kabiyo

(g) kuchita niiba iili milimu mbuuli mbuyitanooli yapegwa manguzu akuchita milimu eeyo aKkata lyaMulawu antoomwe aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Kkomishini yaNtolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange ikonzya kupanga miteto, kwiinda mukuzuminana aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyaNtolongo/Majele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange, igwasizya kuzuzikizigwa kwampalamo zyamilimu yabo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (1).

(3) Mukuzwa amabambe amifolo alumwi lugwasyo lwaamulimu lupegwa babelesi bamuMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange, iKkomishini yaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange iyelede kuchita milimu yayo kwiinda mukuzuminana aMuzulwidi waNyika kali alakwe wapegwa mizeezo abaSilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende babona zyaMali azyaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange.

CHAANDAANO 12 MAKKOMISHINI ALYIIMIKILIDE ASUMPULA BULELO BWAJANDABANJI

CHIBAKA 1 BWIIME BWACHIBAKA 1

232 Makhomishini akazwimila MaKkomishini alyiimikilide aswaanizya aaya aatobela —

(a) Kkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe;

(b) Kkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe;

(c) Kkomishini yaKweelanisigwa kwaMyeenya yaBanakazi aBaalumi MuZimbabwe;

(d) Kkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe; kabiyo

(e) Kkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika. 233 Mpalamo zyamaKkomishini Alyiimikilide MaKkomishini ajisi mpalamo zijanika kuli woonse alaajulu kwazeezyo ziluleme aayimwi ayimwi kkomishini—

(a) kusungwaazya akusukutizya zyeelelo zyabantu mubulelo bwajandabanji;

(b) kukwabilila kulilela antoomwe aziyandwa abantu;

(c) kusungwaazya kutobelezegwa akulemekwa kwaKkata lyaMulawu;

(d) kusungwaazya bubalangale mumachitilo atubunga twafulumende;

(e) kusimatizya kulemekwa kwabulelo andemeko zyajandabanji muli fulumende amutubunga twafulumende; kabiyo

(f) kubona kuti kudyamizigwa kwabantu kwazyotololwa. 234

Babelesi bamaKkomishini alyiimikilide

Makkomishini alyiimikilide ajisi nguzu zyakunjizya bantu milimu antoomwe akubapa mifolo alumwi lugwasyo lupegwa babelesi, zyoonse eezi kazichitwa kwiinda mumulawu. 235 Kulyiimikilila akulisanta kwamaKkomishini

(1) Makkomishini alyiimikilide —

(a) alilyiimikilide akulisanta kabiyo mabelekelo aawo taayelede kunjililwa awumwi muntu;

(b) ayelede kubeleka kaatobelezya Kkata lyaMulawu eeli; kabiyo

(c) ayelede kuchita milimu yawo katayoowi akutasaluula; nikuba kuti kubeleka kwawo kweelede kulangisisigwa chiindi achiindi aPalyamenti.

(2) Fulumende antoomwe atoonse tubunga amitabi yakwe zijanika kuzibeela zisiyenesiyene zyanyika, kwiinda mumilawu azimwi ntaamo, weelede kugwasizya maKkomishini alyiimikilide akukwabilila kulisanta akulyiimikilila kwawo, kubeleka kaatasaluuli, bulemu bwawo akubeleka zizikitide;

(3) Taakwe muntu uyelede kunjilila mumabelekelo amaKkomishini alyiimikililide pe. 236 Bantu bajanika mumakkomishini alyiimikilide tabeelede kunjila pe mutubunga twakweendezya nyika

(1) Mumabelekelo aabo bantu bajanika mumakkomishini tabeelede pe —

(a) kunjila zyatubunga twakweendelezya nyika;

(b) kuzuzikizya mpalamo zyakabunga kakweendelezegwa kwanyika;

(c) kudyaamizya ziyanda kuchitwa akabunga kazyanyika zili mumulawu;

(d) kutyola akutalemeka zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zyanaba uuli muntu.

(2) Bantu bajanika mutubunga twakweendelezya nyika nibagalwa kuti banjile mulimu mumaKkomishini alyiimvwilidi beelede kusiya zyuuno zyabo mutubunga twakweendelezegwa kwanyika achakufwambaana mukati kamazuba makumi otatwe kuzwa buunsi nibapegwa mulimu mukkomishini ilyiimikilide.

(3) Kuti umwi muntu ujanika muKkomishini ilyiimikilide—

(a) wapegwa chuuno mukabunga kakweendelezya nyika; naakuti

(b) wakali umwi wakabunga kakweendelezya nyika chiindi naapegwa mulimu akkomishini ilyiimikilide, mpawo wakachilwa kusiya chuuno chakwe kukabunga kazyanyika mukati kamazuba makumi otatwe; unooli wagwisigwa mukuba umwi waKkomishini eeyo yamupede mulimu 237

Kunjila akugwisigwa mulimu kwabantu bali muMakkomishini alyiimikilide

(1) Kuchitila kuti kuube kuzwa amazina abantu banga balanjizigwa mumaKkomishini alyiimikilide, iKkomiti ya Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti yeelede —

(a) kusambala zibaka ziliwo;

(b) yiitila bulumbu kuti bwaambe mazina aabantu mbibabona kuti ngabalakonzya kunjila mumakkomishini;

(c) chita mbuzisisyo antanganana kunembo lyabulumbu kakubuzisisigwa baabo bayanda kunjila mumakkomishini;

(d) lemba mazina abaabo beelela kugalwa kunjila mumakkomishini; kabiyo

(e) tola mazina aayo kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Muntu uli mukkomishini ilyiimikilide ukonzya kugwisigwa amulimu kuti kakuli muntu ooyo —

(a) tachikonzyi kuchita mulimu nkaambo kakulemana naakuti kusondoka muboongo;

(b) wakachilwa loko kuchita mulimu wakwe;

(c) wajanika kalaamulandu wakusunda kampatila; naakuti

(d) takonzyi kusalwa kuba umwi wamakkomishini alyiimikilide.

(3) Nzila yakugwisya Jaji njiyo ibelesegwa mukugwisya bantu bali mumakkomishini alyiimikilide.

CHIBAKA 2 KKOMISHINI YANSALUUZYO MUZIMBABWE 238

Kupangwa abubumbwe bwaKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe

(1) Kuli Kkomishini itanootegwa Kkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe ilaabantu batobela —

(a) sichuuno ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kagwasyania aKkomishini yaBubetesi aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(b) bamwi bantu bali musanu abatatu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuzwa kumazina alikkumi awabili naa kwiinda aazwa kuKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(2) Sichuuno waKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe uyelede kuba Jaji naakuti wakali Jaji naakuti muntu ukonzya kunjizigwa mulimu wabuJaji.

(3) Kuti kugalwa kwasichuuno waKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe kakuteendelani amizeezo yakazwa kuKkomishini yaBubetesi, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kwaambila Kkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti achakufwambaana.

(4) Bantu banjila muKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe beelede kuba banaZimbabwe basalwa kwiinda mubulemu mbubajisi aluzibo lwamulimu ndubalaalo lwakubeleka abulumbu, mumakkapani antoomwe amutubunga tuteensi twafulumende.

(5) Bantu banjizigwa muKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe bapegwa mweenya wakubeleka mukkomishini kwachiindi chisika minyaka musanu awumwi kabiyo bakonzya kupegwa uumbi mweenya waminyaka ikozyania. Pesi taakwe muntu unga ulagalwa lubo pe kuti anjile mumaKkomishini kuti wakali wabelekela iimwi Kkomishini kale kwaminyaka kkumi ayibili kiitungene mulongo naakuti kitatungene mulongo myaaka. 239 Milimu yaKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe IKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe ilaamilimu itobela —

(a) kubambila, kweendelezya akubona kuti ulookuchitwa kabotu mulimu —

(i) kunsaluuzyo zyakusala Muzulwidi waNyika abayiminini bamuPalyamenti;

(ii) kunsaluuzyo zyamumaKkansili aamuzilikiti awamumadolopo mapati antoomwe amaKkansili amabbooma;

(iii) kunsaluuzyo zyabantu banjila muKkansili yaBaami Yanyika Mbiizulwa mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 285; kabiyo

(iv) kweendelezya malefelendamu; akubona kuti nsaluuzyo eezi amalefelendamu zyachitwa kabotu , kabaangunukide bantu, kakutakwe lusaluulo, mububalangale kabiyo kakutobelezegwa mulawu;

(b) kweendelezya nsaluuzyo zyakusala Muzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu antoomwe akusala Nabbatule waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, kubona kuti zyachitwa kabotu kakutobelezegwa mulawu;

(c) kulembesya bayanda kuvoota;

(d) kubumba malembe abasikuvoota;

(e) kubamba akubambulula malembe abasikuvoota;

(f) kweenga migaano yamakkonsitichwensi, mawaadi ayimwi migaano yazyakuvoota;

(g) kupanga, kudinda akutola kumasena aandeene mapepa akuvootesya , kuzumina misyobo yamabbokesi mubavootela bantu, kuzwa aamasena akuvootela akuvootesya bantu;

(h) kweendelezya akubona machitilo alwiiyo lwakuvoota;

(i) kutambula akupa mvumo kubantu bayanda kubona mbuzyeendelezegwa nsaluuzyo amalefelendamu;
