(j) kupa malayilile kuBabelesi Baluundu naakuti babelesi bakkansili yabbooma/dolopo kuchitila kuti bagwasizye mukweendelezya nsaluuzyo/ malefelendamu munzila iwangunukide, itakwe lusaluulo kabiyo ibalangala akutandabala; kabiyo

(k) kutambula akulangisisya ntongawuko zizwa kubulumbu zyaatala ambuzyakachitwa nsaluuzyo antoomwe akuchita zyeelede kuchitwa kakweendelana antongawuko zyeetwa abantu kuKkomishini.

240 Kusiigwa aanze kwabantu bayanda kunjila muKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe Aajulu kwabantu baambidwe muchibeela 320(3), bantu batobela tabakonzyi kugalwa kuti banjile muKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe—

(a) Babelesi Baluundu, aanze kwamaJaji;

(b) babelesi bamakkansili azilikiti amadolopo mapati antoomwe ababelesi bamumabbooma;

(c) babelesi batubunga ambunga zisiyenesiyene zizulwidwa afulumende. 241 Kkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe yeelede kupa ngambo (lipoti) yabweendelezi bwansaluuzyo amalefelendamu Aajulu kwangambo njiyelede kutola kuPalyamenti, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 323, iKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe yeelede kuti achakufwambaana yatola lipoti kuPalyamenti, kwiinda kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende, aatala abweendelezi bwansaluuzyo zyoonse amalefelendamu aakachitwa. CHIBAKA 3 KKOMISHINI YAZYEELELO ZYABANTU MUZIMBABWE 242 Kupangwa abwiime bwa Kkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe

(1) Kuli Kkomishini itanootegwa niKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe ilaabantu batobela —

(a) sichuuno ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kagwasyania aKkomishini yaBubetesi aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(b) bamwi bantu bali musanu abatatu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuzwa kumazina ali kkumi awabili naa kwiinda aazwa kuKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(2) Sichuuno wa Kkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe uyelede kuba muntu wakabeleka muli zyamulawu kwachiindi chisika naakuti kwiinda minyaka musanu ayibili muZimbabwe.

(3) Kuti kugalwa kwasichuuno wa Kkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe kakuteendelani amizeezo yakazwa kuKkomishini yaBubetesi, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kwaambila Kkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti achakufwambaana.

(4) Bantu banjila muKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe beelede basalwa kwiinda mubulemu mbubajisi aluzibo lwamulimu ndubalaalo muli zyakusumpula zyeelelo zyabantu. 243 Milimu yaKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe

(1) IKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe ijisi milimu itobela —

(a) kusungwaazya kumwaywa kwaluzibo aatala akulemekwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu muzibeela zyoonse zyachisi;

(b) kusungwaazya kukwabililwa, kusumpulwa akuzuzikizigwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu;

(c) kulangisisya kulemekwa akuzuzikizigwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu;

(d) kutambula akulangisisya ntongawuko/nyontooko zizwa kubulumbu akutola ntaamo zyakuzyotolola bulewusi kakweendelana abulewusi bwakachitwa mbuuli mbiizibona Kkomishini;

(e) kukwabililwa kwabulumbu kuzwa kubazuluzi aBabelesi Baluundu babelesya zyuuno zyabo munzila italuleme mukudyaamizya bulumbu;

(f) kupokoola aatala anchito zyabantu bamang’alilwa naakuti kabunga/ tubunga twamang’alilwa nkaambo kakutyola zyeelelo angwangunuko zilembedwe muZyeelelo Zyaampandamunsaka;

(g) kugwasizya bantu batyolelwa zyeelelo zyabo kuti bajane bululami, kubikkilizya kutola basikutyola zyeelelo kunkuta aawo zyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu mpuzyatyolwa;

(h) kwaambila Nabbatule waBakapokola kuti apokoole milandu yakutyolwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko mpawo ape butebuzi bwabupokoozi kuKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu;

(i) kupa mizeezo kuPalyamenti aatala antaamo anzila zinga zilatobelezegwa kusungwaazya kulemekwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu;

(j) kuchita mfukununo aatala azintu zyeendelene akulemekwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu antoomwe abukkale bwabantu; kabiyo

(k) kuswaya akulangalanga bwiime—

(i) bwamajele, masena abambilwa baange, masena kukkala basikuchija lumamba awamwi masena ayendelana awaaya;

(ii) masena kubambwa bantu bakasondoka muboongo; kuchitila kuti kuzibikane bube abwiime bwamasena aaya mukkala bantu mpawo Kkomishini ipe maboneno aayo atala abwiime bwamasena aaya kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyakweendelezegwa kwamulawu ulangene aamasena aayo.

(2) Nabbatule waBakapokola weelede kulemeka akutobelezya malayilile ngaapegwa aKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela

(1)(h). 244 Ngambo/Malipoti aaya kuKomishini antoomwe awalembwa aKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe

(1) IKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe ikonzya kuyanda muntu, kabunga, tubunga, twafulumede naakuti tuteensi twafulumende kuti—

(a) aambile Kkomishini ntaamo zyakatolwa kukusungwaazya akusumpula kuzuzikizigwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu mbuuli mbuziyalidwe muZyeelelo Zyaampandamunsaka;

(b) kupa Kkomishini luzibo njiluyanda mukubambila kulemba malipoti/ ngambo zitolwa nikuba kuli mbuuli kutubunga twamanyika aandeene twamuluwa naakuti tubunga twamanyika andeene aansi lyoonse tubona zyazyeelelo zyabantu.

(2) Aajulu angambo njiyelede kupa Palyamenti, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 323, iKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe yeelede kuti achakufwambaana yatola lipoti kuPalyamenti, kwiinda kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende wayo, aatala azimwi zyakachitikana zyazyeelelo angwangunuko zyabantu nzyibona lwayo Kkomishini kuti zyeelede kuzibisigwa Palyamenti.

CHIBAKA 4 KKOMISHINI YAKWEELANISIGWA KWAMYEENYA YABANAKAZI ABAALUMI MUZIMBABWE 245 Kupangwa abwiime bwa Kkomishini yaKweelanisigwa kwaMyeenya yaBanakazi aBaalumi MuZimbabwe

(1) Kuli Kkomishini itanootegwa Kkomishini yaKweelanisigwa kwaMyeenya yaBanakazi aBaalumi muZimbabwe ilaabantu batobela —

(a) sichuuno ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kagwasyania aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(b) bamwi bali musanu abatatu, kakuli —

(i) bali musanu ababili bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuzwa kumazina abantu aali kkumi awabili ngapegwa aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(ii) uumwi ugalwa aKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse, ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Bantu banjila muKkomishini yaKweelanisigwa kwaMyeenya yaBanakazi aBaalumi muZimbabwe beelede basalwa kwiinda mubulemu mbubajisi aluzibo lwamulimu ndubalaalo muli zyabukkale, tunsiyansiya, zyabuvubi, azyakweendelezegwa kwanyika antoomwe azyakusiyana kwamyeenya yabanakazi abaalumi mubuumi. Buvule bwabaalumi abanakazi bweelede kweelana muKkomishini eeyi. 246 Milimu yaKkomishini yaKweelanisigwa kwaMyeenya yaBanakazi aBaalumi MuZimbabwe IKkomishini yaKweelanisigwa kwaMyeenya yaBanakazi aBaalumi muZimbabwe ijisi milimu itobela—

(a) kulangisisya zyaambo zyeendelene akweelanisigwa kwamyeenya yabanakazi abaalumi mubuumi mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu;

(b) kulangisisya kutyolwa kwazyeelelo zyabantu kubbazu lyakweelansigwa kwamyeenya yabanakazi abaalumi mubuumi;

(c) kutambula akulangisisya ntongawuko/nyontooko zizwa kubulumbu akutola ntaamo zyakuzyotolola bulewusi kakweendelana abuleswusi bwakachitwa mbuuli mbiizibona Kkomishini;

(d) kuchita mfukununo aatala azintu zyeendelene akweelana kwabanakazi abaalumi mumyeenya yabo mubuumi antoomwe akupa mizeezo atala amasandu anga alachitwa amilawu amachitilo abantu zinga zilachesya lusaluulo lwabantu aatala akweelana kwabanakazi abaalumi mubuumi;

(e) kupa mizeezo kutubunga twafulumende atuteensi twafulumende aatala antaamo zinga zilatolwa kuti kube kweelana kwamyeenya akati kabaalumi abanakazi mubuumi;

(f) kupa mizeezo atala amabambe agwasizya kweelanisigwa kwamyeenya yabanakazi abaalumi mubuumi;

(g) kupa mizeezo aatala akubetekwa kwabantu batyola zyeelelo zigwasizya kweelanisigwa kwamyeenya yabanakazi abaalumi mubuumi;

(h) kugwasizya bantu batyolelwa zyeelelo zyabo zyeendelene akweelanisigwa kwamyeenya yabanakazi abaalumi mubuumi; kabiyo

(i) kuchita nichiba chili chigwasizya kuti kuzuzikizigwe kweelana kwamyeenya yabanakazi abaalumi mubuumi.

247 Ngambo/Malipoti alembwa aKkomishini yaKweelanisigwa kwaMyeenya yaBanakazi aBaalumi MuZimbabwe Aajulu kwalipoti/ngambo njiyelede kupa Palyamenti, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 323, Kkomishini yaKweelanisigwa kwaMyeenya yaBanakazi aBaalumi muZimbabwe yeelede kuti achakufwambaana yatola lipoti kuPalyamenti, kwiinda kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende wayo, aatala azimwi zyakachitikana mukweelanisigwa kwamyeenya yabanakazi abaalumi nzyibona lwayo Kkomishini kuti zyeelede kuzibisigwa Palyamenti.

CHIBAKA 5 KKOMISHINI YAZIMWAYA MILUMBE MUZIMBABWE 248 Kupangwa abwiime bwaKkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe

(1) Kuli Kkomishini itanootegwa Kkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe —

(a) sichuuno ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kagwasyania aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(b) bali musanu abatatu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuzwa kumazina abantu aali kkumi awabili ngaapegwa aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(2) Bantu banjila muKkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe beelede basalwa kwiinda mubulemu mbubajisi aluzibo lwamulimu ndubalaalo muli zyazyeelelo zyabantu, amachitilo mabotu muli zyakumwaywa kwamilumbe. 249 Milimu yaKkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe

(1) IKkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe ijisi milimu itobela —

(a) kusungwaazya akusumpula kwaangunuka kwazimwaya milumbe munyika;

(b) kusungwaazya akubona kuti zilookuchitwa zigwasizya kubeleka kabotu kwazimwaya milumbe;

(c) kulangisisya mabelekelo anzila njimwaywa aayo milumbe kakutobelezegwa zintu zigwasya bulumbu, kapati kubona kuti takukwe lusalulano akutambula kusiyana kwamizeezo yaluundu lwaZimbabwe;

(d) kusungwaazya kupangwa kwazyeelede kutobelezegwa ababelesi bali mumilimu yakumwaywa kwamilumbe. Aawo mpuzitawo zyeelede kuchitwa Kkomishini yeelede kuzipanga akubona kuti zilookulemekwa;

(e) kutambula akulangisisya ntongawuko zizwa kubulumbu akutola ntaamo zyakuzyotolola basimutola twaambo bachita bulewusi mukuchita milimu yabo naakuti batyola milawu/zyeelede kuchitwa ambabo;

(f) kubona kuti bantu bamuZimbabwe bajana luzibo amilumbe minji munzila itakwe lusalulano;

(g) kusungwaazya kubelesegwa akusumpulwa kwayoonse milaka izibikene zili mumulawu muZimbabwe;

(h) kusungwaazya kubelesegwa kwazyabusongo (technology) mukumwaywa kwamilumbe aluzibo;

bwamazuba

aano

(i) kuzumizya aksusungwaazya mbunga/ng’anda zyandeene zimwaya milumbe; kabiyo

(j) kuchita mfukununo aatala azintu zyeendelene akwaangunuka kwazimwaya milumbe akwaangunuka kwanzila zyakwaambuula, kabiyo akusungwaazya kubambululwa kwamilawu iyanda kubambululwa kuti kumwaywa kwamilumbe kuchitwe kabotu.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa nguzu ku Kkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe kuti —

(a) ipokoole akulangalanga —

(i) fumbwa nchito abube bwazintu bukachizya kwaangunuka kwazimwaya milumbe; kabiyo

(ii) machitilo azimwaya milumbe; kabiyo

(b) kutola ntaamo naakuti kupa mizeezo atala azinga zilachitwa mukuzyotolola basimutola twaambo abamwi babelesi bajanika muzimwaya milumbe batanooli batyola milawu naakuti kutalemeka zyeelede kuchitwa ababeleka muzimwaya milumbe.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kubikka nzila zyeelede kutobelezegwa ababeleka muzimwaya milumbe. 250 Ngambo zilembwa aKkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe Aajulu kwalipoti/ngambo njiyelede kupa Palyamenti, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 323, Kkomishini yaZimwaya Milumbe muZimbabwe yelede kuti achakufwambaana yatola lipoti kuPalyamenti, kwiinda kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende wayo, aatala azimwi zyakachitikana zyakumwaywa kwamilumbe nzyibona lwayo Kkomishini kuti zyeelede kuzibisigwa Palyamenti.

CHIBAKA 6 KKOMISHINI YALUUMUNO ALULEKELELANO MUNYIKA 251

Kupangwa abubumbwe bwaKkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika

(1) Kwachiindi chaminyaka ili kkumi kuzwa buunsi nilitalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, kunooli kkomishini itegwa niKkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika ijisi bantu batobela —

(a) sichuuno ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kagwasyania aKkomishini yaBubetesi antoomwe aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(b) bali musanu abatatu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuzwa kumazina abantu aali kkumi awabili ngapegwaa aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(2) Sichuuno wa Kkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika uyelede kuba muntu wakabeleka muli zyamulawu kwachiindi chisika naakuti kwiinda minyaka musanu ayibili muZimbabwe.

(3) Kuti kugalwa kwasichuuno waKkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika kakuteendelani amizeezo yakazwa kuKkomishini yaBubetesi, Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kwaambila Kkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti achakufwambaana.

(4) Bantu banjila mu Kkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika beelede basalwa kwiinda mubulemu mbubajisi aluzibo lwamulimu ndubalaalo akumvwisisisya zyakulekelelana, koomboozyania, kumana mpyopyongano, kulekelelana kwabantu nilwamana luzyokola antoomwe anzila zyakubumba luumuno.

252 Milimu yaKkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika IKkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika ijisi milimu itobela —

(a) kubona kuti kwaba bululami nilwamana lupyopyongano/luzyokola, koomboozyania/kuumuzya aachisa akulekelelana kwabantu;

(b) kuchita mabambe asumpula kuumuzya aachisa kwanyika, kumvwanana lukamantano muZimbabwe akubelesya nzila zyaluumuno mukumana mpyopyongano zyabawo;

(c) kweeta lulekelelano munyika kwiinda mukusungwaazya bantu kuti baambe nchobeni aatala azyakachitikana nkukuti bagwasigwe mukujana lulekelelano abululami;

(d) kupanga nzila atubunga twanyika yoonse tusungwaazya kwaambuuzyania akati katubunga twazyakweendelezya nyika, luundu, mbungano antoomwe atumwi tubunga, nkukuti kuchesegwe kubawo kwampyopyongano muchiindi chiza;

(e) kupanga mabambe akubona kuti bantu bakasonsegwa mubuumi, bajatwa buzike akukoloonsegwa bajana lugwasyo lwakubusilisigwa, kusilikwa alugwasyo mubuumi;

(f) kutambula akulangisisya ntongawuko/nyontooko zizwa kubulumbu akutola ntaamo zyakuzyotolola butongawusi abulewusi munzila iyelede;

(g) kupanga nzila zyakubbutula mpyopyongano chakufwambaana kuchitila kuti luzyokola taluyaluuki mbuuli mulilo mpawo akutola ntaamo zyakuchesya kubawo kwampyopyongano;

(h) kuchita zyoonse zikonzeka kuchitwa kukwabilila mpyopyongano akusungwaazya luumuno;

(i) kumana mpyopyongano kwiinda mukwaambuzyania kwabantu muluundu, mumbungano, mutubunga antoomwe amuluumwi awumwi wabantu; kabiyo

(j) kupa mizeezo atala amilawu iyelede kubikkwa kuchitila kuti lugwasyo, kubikkilizya zilembwa, lwapegwa kubantu bakaswaanana ampyopyongano, luzyokola abumwi buyumuyumu. 253 Ngambo/Malipoti alembwa aKkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika Aajulu kwalipoti/ngambo njiyelede kupa Palyamenti, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 323, Kkomishini yaLuumuno aLulekelelano Munyika yelede kuti achakufwambaana yatola lipoti kuPalyamenti, kwiinda kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende wayo, aatala azimwi zyakachitikana zyaluumuno alulekelelano munyika nzyibona lwayo Kkomishini kuti zyeelede kuzibisigwa Palyamenti.

CHAANDAAN0 13 TUBUNGA TWAKULWANA BUBOLI ANTOOMWE AMILANDU

CHIBAKA 1 KKOMISHINI YANKAKA BUBOLI MUZIMBABWE 254 Kupangwa abwiime bwa Kkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe

(1) Kuli Kkomishini itanootegwa Kkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe —

(a) sichuuno ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kajana mizeezo kuzwa kuKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(b) musanu abatatu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuzwa kumundanda wamazina usika kukkumi awabili naakuti kwiinda, mazina aayo kaali ayetwa aKkomiti yaNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Bantu beelede kujanika muKkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe beelede kusalwa nkaambo kabulemu mbubajisi, luzibo lwabo azyamulimu abuumi mukweendelezya akupokoolwa kwamilandu naakuti kulangika kwabo kabeelela kugalwa; kabiyo—

(a) uumwi naakuti banji babo kweelede kabali bakayiila mulimu wazyabusimulawu kabiyo ukonzya kubeleka zyabusimulawu muZimbabwe, kali wakali kubeleka zyabusimulawu kwaminyaka isika musanu ayibili naakuti kwiinda;

(b) uumwi naakuti banji babo beelede kuti kabali bantu bakayiila mulimu wazyakulangalanga mabelesezyo amali naakuti kufunkulula mabelesezyo amali (public accountant /public auditor), kabiyo wakali kubeleka muZimbabwe zyakulangalanga/kufunkulula mabelesezyo amali kwachiindi chisika naakuti chiinda minyaka musanu ayibili; kabiyo

(c) uumwi naakuti banji babantu kabali bajisi minyaka isika naakuti kwiinda kkumi muli zyakupokoola akuyanduulilisya bwiime bwamilandu. 255 Milimu yaKkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe

(1) IKkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe ijisi milimu itobela —

(a) kupokoola akulangisisya bwiime bwamilandu yazyabuboli mumitabi atubunga twafulumende antoomwe amumakkampani alyiimikilide;

(b) kulwana buboli, bubbi, kubbigwa kwamali azintu, kutabelesegwa kabotu manguzu kwabazuluzi muzyuuno zyabo mumitabi yafulumende amutubunga /makkampani alyiimikilide;

(c) kusungwaazya busichaamba, mabelesezyo mabotu amali, bubalangale mumitabi atubunga twafulumende antoomwe amutubunga tulyiimikilide;

(d) kutambula, kulangisisya ntongawuko/nyontooko zizwa kubulumbu akutola ntaamo zyakuzyotolola ntongawuko mbuuli mbukweelede;

(e) kwaambila Nabbatule waBakapokola kuti apokoole akulangisisya milandu ilaatala abuboli mpawo akwaambila Kkomishini butebuzi butanooli bwazwa mubupokoozi bwachitwa;

(f) kutola zyaambo amilandu kuNkamu yaZyakusosela Milandu kuti milandu itolwe kunkuta;

(g) kujana lugwasyo kuzwa kuli Bakapokola ayimwi mitabi yafulumedne ipokoola milandu; kabiyo

(h) kupa mizeezo kuli fulumende abamwi bantu aatala akusungwaazya kulijata kuli kabotu kwatubunga amitabi yaFulumende antoomwe amutubunga tulyiimikilide.

(2) Nabbatule waBakapokola weelede kuswiilizya malayilile aazwa ku Kkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (1) (e).

(3) Fulumende weelede kubona kuti, kwiinda mukupanga mulawu azimwi ntaamo, Kkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe ilaamanguzu aakupa kuti muntu asungwe antoomwe akubetekwa ooyo wayeeyelwa kuti wakachita zyabuboli, watabelesya kabotu manguzu achuuno chakwe naakuti kutalijata kabotu kwamuntu munzila ichita mulandu mumeso aKkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe.

256 Kuchita zyaambidwe muChaandaano 12 kumulimu waKkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe Zyaambidwe muzibeela 234, 235, 236 a 237 zyeelede kuchitwa kuKkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe antoomwe abantu bajanika mukkomishini eeyi mbukunga nikkomishini ilyiimikilide ipangwa kwiinda muChaandaano 12. 257 Ngambo zilembwa aKkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe Aajulu kwalipoti njiyelede kupa Palyamenti, mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 323, iKkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe yeelede kuti, kwiinda kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende, yatola lipoti kuPalyamenti, aatala akulijata kwababelesi mumitabi atubunga twafulumende amumakkampani alyiimikilide nzyiibona kazyeelede kwaambilwa Palyamenti.

CHIBAKA 2 NKAMU YABASIKUSOSELA MILANDU MUNKUTA 258 Kupangwa amilimu yaNkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta Kuli Nkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta ilaamulimu wakutalika akweendelezya kusosela milandu munkuta kayiimikilila fulumende mumilandu yacriminal cases antoomwe akuchita imwi milimu iyelede nikusoselwa milandu munkuta eeyo itanooli yasundwa. 259 Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta abamwi babelesi

(1) Kuli Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta kakuli ngusilutwe waNkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta.

(2) Chuuno chabuNabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta nchuuno chababelesi baluundu pesi teensi bamwi abaBabelesi Baluundu. [Kabela 2 kakabambululwa achibeela 16 (b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(3) Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta ugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kapegwa mizeezo aKkomishini yaBubetesi kuti anjile mulimu. [Kabela 3 kakabambululwa achibeela 16 (c ) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(4) Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta uyelede kuba muntu ukonzya kupegwa mulimu wabuJaji bwaNkuta Yaajulujulu.

(5) Bulamfu bwakukkala muwofesi bwa Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu minyaka musanu awumwi pesi ukonzya kupegwa uumbi mweenya wabulamfu bwaminyaka bweelene abwakutaanguna.

(6) Katana talika kubeleka Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta weelede kukonka chikonke kunembo lyaMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti kunembo kwawumwi muntu wapegwa mvumo aMuzulwidi waNyika, bwiime bwachikonke buli muMpampikwa Yatatu.

(7) Nabbatule waBasikusosela ulagwisigwa achuuno akaambo kazitobela—

(a) kukachilwa kweendelezya mulimu wakwe weendelana achuuno chakwe akaambo kakukuchiswa boongo naakuti mubili; naakuti

(b) kukachilwa kuchita mulimu wakwe kwiindilide; naakuti

(c) kutalichata kabotu kwiindilide. [ Kabela 7 kalabambululwa chibeela 16 (c ) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(7a) Naa Muzulwidi waNyika wabona kuti kugwisigwa kwaNabbatule waBasikusosela Kunkuta achuuno yeelede kulangisisigwa, Muzulwidi waNyika weelede kugala kabunga kamaJaji kuti balangisisye nkani eeyo.

(7b) Kabunga kamaJaji kagalwa munsi akabela (7a) keelede kabe aabantu batali munsi abatatu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika, kakweelede kuti aakati kabo —

(a) bobile babe kabaliluzi naakuti kabali majaji kuNkuta Yaajulu; alimwi

(b) womwe kali aziyandikana naa kali wakali wabajaji waNkuta Mpati naakuti wakali waba jaji wankuta iitakwe mweelo wamanguzu kumakani aamilandu akutyola mulawu muchisi aawo mulawu uutobelwa kuuli waRoman-Dutch naakuti waChikuwa, alimwi Chikuwa kachibwezegwa mbuuli mulaka mukoloondo wakubelesya mumasena asiyene.

(7c) Muzulwidi waNyika weelede wagala um wi wakabunga kamaJaji kuti abe sichuuno wakabunga.

(7d) Kabunga kamaJaji keelede kulangisisya kaambo kakugwisigwa kwaDundumuntule wabaJaji achuuno, alimwi musule akuchita oobo, keelede kazibisya Muzulwidi waNyika aatala azeezyo nzikajana alimwi kabambe kuti Dundumuntule wabaJaji agwisigwe na naakuti pe achuuno.

(7e) Kabunga kamaJaji kabikwa munsi achibeela kali azyeelelo amanguzu ayelene awamaKkomishina munsi aMulawu wamaKkomishina aaKudunamina [Chaandano 10:07] naa umwi mulawu uubikwa aabusena bwayooyo.

(7f) Naa makani akugwisigwa achuuno kwaNabbatule waBasikusosela kuNkuta lwatolwa kukabunga kamaJaji munsi achaandaano eechi, Nabbatule waBasikusosela kuNkuta ulakonzya kwiimikizigwa kuchuuno kunze kukabe kuti Muzulwidi waNyika, akubonisyania akabunga kamaJaji, usowe kwiimikizigwa naa agwisye Nabbatule waBasikusosela achuuno.

(7g) Mulawu uubikwa aPalyamenti kwaaambuulwa awo munsi akabela (10) uuulimunsi yaBboodi yaKabunga kaChisi kaBasikusosela naakuti kabunga kababetkesi kabikwa munsi kwaChibeela eechi kuti kalangisisye kuti Nabbatule waBasikusosela eete ngambo yakwe yakulangwa kwakwe akabunga kazyabulondosi katanoli kapegwa mukuli ooyu, kuti kube lusinzyo aatala akupona kwakwe kwaboongo amubili [Kabela 7(a)-7(g) kabikwa achibeela 16( c) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(8) Mifolo alumwi lugwasyo lupegwa babelesi amulimu lweendelene a Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta, zyeelede kupandululwa kabotu aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti. Pesi mufolo wakwe tuuyelede kuselusigwa pe Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta kachili achuuno.

(9) Mufolo wa Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta uzwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende.

(10) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kwaamba aatala akupangwa kwaBboodi lyeelede kupandulula kunjizigwa milimu kwababelesi bagwasizya Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta—

(a) nzibeelede kubaazyo kuti banjile milimu;

(b) (b)mifolo yabo ambubeelede kulijata amulimu;

(c) (c) akuti nibachita milimu yabo kweelede babelesi aabo kabalyiimikilide akulisanta kabatobelezya mulawu luzutu, kuswiilizya malayilile anzyotololo zizwa kuli Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta;

(d) (d)zyabwiime abubumbwe Munkuta; kabiyo

bwaNkamu

(e) (e)kugwasizya mumabelekelo Milandu Munkuta.

aabo

yaBasikusosela

bamuNkamu

Milandu

yaBasikusosela

(11) Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta ukonzya kwaambila Nabbatule waBakapokola kuti apokoole akulangisisya mulandu.Butebuzi mbwatanooli wajana mukupokoola kwakwe aambile Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta. Kabiyo

Nabbatule waBakapokola uyelede kutobelezya malayilile aazwa kuli Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta. 260 Kulyiimikilila akulisanta kwa Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu

(1) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta—

(a) uli lyiimikilide kabiyo tayelede kuchelezegwa pe mukubeleka kwakwe awumwi muntu; kabiyo

(b)

weelede kuchita milimu yakwe kalyiimvwilidi akulisanta katayoowi akufwida bamwi bantu luse.

(2) Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta uyelede kuchita akumwaya kuluundu ndemeko nzyatobelezya mukulangisisya aakupokoolwa kwamilandu yabantu. 261 Mbubeelede kulijata babelesi baNkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta

(1) Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta ababelesi bamuNkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta beelede kulijata munzila yaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli ayimwi milawu.

(2) Taakwe mubelesi waNkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta uyelede kuti nachita milimu yakwe—

(a) kachita zyakabunga kakweendelezya nyika;

(b) kuzuzikizya mpalamo zyakabunga kakweendelezya nyika;

(c) kudyaamizya ziyanda kuchitwa akabunga kazyakweendelezya nyika zili mumulawu;

(d) Kutyola akutalemeka zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zyanaba uuli muntu.

(3) Babelesi baNkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta tabeelede pe kunjila muzyuuno zyatubunga twazyakweendelezya nyika naakuti imwi mbungano biyo.

(4) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kwaamba zimwi zinga zilagwasizya mukuchita kuti babelesi baNkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta tabanoonjili munchito amilimu yazyatubunga twazyakweendelezya nyika. 262 Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta weelede kupa lipoti kuPalyamenti munyaka amunyaka Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta weelede kupa Palyamenti lipoti lyazyakachitika mumunyaka woonse, kwiinda muli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende wakwe. Lipoti eeli lyamunyaka wayinda lyeelede kupegwa Palyamenti mukati kamyeezi musanu awumwi kuzwa nuwatalika munyaka mupya. 263

Amwi manguzu aakusosela milandu

Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa manguzu aakusosela milandu kuli bamwi bantu batajaniki muNkamu yaBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta pesi nguzu eezyo tazyeelede kwiimpana pe anguzu zipedwe Nkamu yaBasikusosela Milandi muNkuta mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muChibaka eechi.

CHAANDAANO 14 FULUMENDE WACHILIKITI AWABBOOMA NTALISYO Nikuba kuti kulayandikana kubona kuti:

(a) kwakwabililwa lukamantano lwanyika yaZimbabwe akulwana zyoonse nzila zinga zilapa kutamvwanana kwabantu akupambuka kwachimwi chipanzi chanyika;

(b) kugwasizya akunjilingana kwabenichisi munyika yabo yaZimbabwe; kabiyo

mubulelo

bwajandabanji

(c) kwaabilwa kweelene kwazibelesyo azilengwa-leza zijanika munyika antoomwe akunjilingana kwabulumbu mumabambe alusumpuko lwabusena; kweelede kwaba kuselusigwa kwanguzu zyakulela nyika kuzwa kuli fulumende mupati wakujulu kwachisi kuza kuli fulumende waansi kuchilikiti akubbooma munyika yaZimbabwe. CHIBAKA 1 NTALISYO 264

Kwaabwa kwanguzu zyakulela nyika kuzwa kuli fulumende mupati kusika kuli fulumende waansi, nguzu zipedwe antoomwe amilimu yeelede kuchitwa

(1) Aawo mpukwabonwa kakweelede, nguzu antoomwe amilimu yafulumende, zyaabwa akuselusigwa kumakkansili azilikiti, madolopo mapati antoomwe amabbooma kuti eezi zibeela kazikonzya kubelesya nguzu akuchita milimu iyelede kuchitwa munzila iidodonkene.

(2) Impalamo zyakwaabwa akuselusigwa kwanguzu zyafulumende kuzwa kujulu kusika kumakkansili azilikiti, madolopo mapati amabbooma nzezi—

(a) kupa nguzu kubantu zyakweendelezya bukkale bwabo muzibeela zyazilikiti, madolopo mapati amabbooma kabiyo kabanjilingana mukweendelezegwa kwamasena aabo akupa mizeezo mulusumpuko lwamasena aabo;

(b) kusungwaazya bulelo bwajandabanji, bulelo budodonkene, bubalangala, bukamantene munyika yaZimbabwe mbiizulwa;

(c) kubutilizya akusungwaazya luumuno, abugodobwiime bwanyika yaZimbabwe;

lukamantano

lwachisi

(d) kubona kuti kweelede bulumbu bwajana cheelelo chakulyeendelezya mubulelo bwabo akusumpula chisi munzila njibayanda;

(e) kubona kuti kuli kwaabana kweelene kwazilengwa-leza zijanika munyika mbiizulwa amumasena aamuluwa; kabiyo

(f) kutola zibelesyo antoomwe amikuli iyelede kuchitwa kuzwa kuli fulumende waajulu kuya kuli fulumende wazibeela zilaansi kuchitila kuti kupangwe mabelesezyo azyamali muli chimwi achimwi chilikiti , dolopo pati abbooma. 265 Ndemeko zyeelede kujanika muli fulumende wachilikiti, dolopo abbooma

(1) Makkansili azilikiti, madolopo mapati amabbooma ayelede kuti mukati kanguzu nzyaapegwa —

(a) babona kuti kuli bweendelezi buzikitide mumasena aabo kwiinda mukudodonkania machitilo abweendelezi bwabo munzila iibalangala yakuchita akulemeka ziyandwa abulumbu;

(b) bachita milimu eeyo njibakapegwa luzutu aKkata lyaMulawu eeli naakuti aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti;

(c) kuchita milimu yabo munzila itakolonganyi akunjilila mumabelekelo azimwi zibeela zyafulumende;

(d) babelekela aamwi azimwi zibeela zyafulumende munyika, kapati kwiinda munzila—

(i) yakuzibisyania akugwasizyania mumizeezo aatala azimwi zintu nzibeelede kubelekelaamwi;

(ii) kuchita milimu yabo ipa kuti kube kubelekelaamwi akumvwanana;

(e) kukwabilila luumuno, lukamantano lwachisi abugodobwiime bwanyika yaZimbabwe

(f) kubona kuti bulumbu buleledwe akweendelezegwa kabotu; kabiyo

(g) kubona kuti bulumbu bwayimikililwa munzila ibukkutya muzintu nzibuyanda mumasena aabo.

(2) Boonse bayiminini mumakkansili amadolopo amabbooma kweelede kabasalwa aabantu balembesezye kuvoota mumasena aayo;

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula mbukweelede kuchitwa kuti kube kubelekelana kuli kabotu akati ka fulumende mupati wanyika abafulumende bamumakkansili. 266 Kulijata kwaBabelesi Baluundu wachilikiti mumadolopo mapati amumabbooma

(1) Babelesi bamukkansili yachilikiti, mumadolopo mapati amumabbooma beelede kulijata munzila yaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli antoomwe ayimwi milawu.

(2) Taakwe mubelesi wamukkansili yamuchilikiti, mudolopo pati amubbooma uyelede kuti naachita milimu yakwe—

(a) wanjizya zyakamwi kabunga kakweendezya nyika;

(b) wazuzikizya mpalamo zyakabunga kakweendelezya nyika;

(c) wadyaamizya ziyanda kuchitwa akabunga kazyanyika zili mumulawu;

(d) watyola akutalemeka zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zyanikuba uuli muntu.

(3) Babelesi bamukkansili yachilikiti, mudolopo pati amubbooma tabeelede pe kunjila muzyuuno zyatubunga twazyakweendelezya nyika naakuti imwi mbungano biyo.

(4) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kwaamba zimwi zinga zilagwasizya mukuchita kuti babelesi bamukkansili yachilikiti, mudolopo pati amubbooma tabanoonjili munchito amilimu yazyatubunga twakweendelezya nyika.

CHIBAKA 2 ZILIKITI AMAKKANSILI AAZILIKITI AMADOLOPO-MAPATI 267 Zilikiti amabbooma amuZimbabwe

(1) Zilikiti mwigaanwidwe nyika yaZimbabwe nzezi —

(a) Chilikiti chaDolopo pati lyaBulawayo

(b) Chilikiti chaDolopo pati lyaHarare

(c) Chilikiti chaManicaland

(d) Chilikiti chaMashonaland Central

(e) Chilikiti chaMashonaland East

(f) Chilikiti chaMashonaland West

(g) Chilikiti cha Masvingo

(h) Chilikiti chaMatabeleland North

(i) Chilikiti chaMatabeleland South; kabiyo

(j) Chilikiti chaMidlands; migaano yazyo zilikiti eezi ilipandulwidwe muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti —

(a) weelede kupandulula kugaanunwa kwazilikiti kakuchitwa migaano yamabbooma; kabiyo

(b) kupandulula mbukunga migaano yazilikiti amabbooma ilabambululwa; kwiinda mukupana mizeezo aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe abantu bajanika muzilikiti amumabbooma alaamigaano iyanda kubambuululwa. 268 Makkansili amuZilikiti zyamumyuunzi amadolopo mapati

(1) Kulikkansili yachilikiti muchilikiti chimwi achimwi akkansili yadolopo pati muchilikiti chadolopo pati limwi alimwi iiswanizya —

(a) Swichuunoakkansili, wasalwa ampandamayake zyabandawukwa kuChibeela 272; alimwi

yamulawu

(b) Bameya abasichuuno, kakutalangwi kuti biitwa biyeni, bamakkansili woonse amadolopo mapati azilikiti zyamumyuunzi kubusena oobo; alimwi

(c) Bachikayintu balikkumi basalwa anzila yakweelenisya kwiiminina kuzwa kumyeelwe iijanwa abiiminini batubunga twazyabuleli kunsaluuzyo yaambwa kukabela (3).

(2) Wachikayintu weelede kusalwa kuti abe umwi wakabunga kakkansili yachilikiti naakuti dolopo pati kakutobelwa kabela (1)(c) naa kaliluzi kuti asalwe kuba Mwiiminini waNg’anda yaBiiminini.

(3) Nsaluuzyo yamakkansili aazilikiti ayamadolopo mapati kweelede yeendelezegwa kakutobelwa Mulawu waNsaluuzyo, wonya weeelede ubike lusinizyo lwakuti bachikayintu baambwa kukabela (1)(c) basalwe kumpampikwa lwakweelanisya—

(a) luyeme kumavoti ajanwa abiiminini batubunga twazyabuleli bwachisi kunsaluuzyo iikamantene lwaBiiminini baNg’nda yaMulawu kuchilikiti eecho; alimwi

(b) luswaanizya bachikayintu bapona abulema.

(4) Chuuno chamwiiminini lilokwaambuulwa aatala andilyo —

wakkansili

kuchilikiti

naakuti

kudolopo

pati

(a) kufuka (b) lyakabela (1) chisyaala kiitakwe muntu naa mwiiminini wasiya chuuno chabumeya naa chubusichuuno wakkansili yachilikiti eecho;

(b) kufuka (c) lyakabela (1) chisyaala kachitakwe muntu naa kwaba zyaambwa kuchibeela 129, mbuuli kuti wakali Mwiiminini waNg’anda yaMulawu. [Kabela kabambululwa achibeela 17 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

269 … [Kabela kasowegwa achibeela 17 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

270 Milimu yamaKkansili muzilikiti zyamadolopo mapati

(1) Ikkansili yachilikiti naakuti dolopo pati ilaamulimu wakubona kuti busena bwayo bulookujana lusumpuko lwabantu mubukkale amubuvubi bwabo, kubikkilizya —

(a) kuchita mabambe akwaayendelezya mukusumpula bukkale abuvubi bwabantu muchilikiti;

(b) kweendelezya mabambe alusumpuko aafulumende wanyika mbiizulwa mubusena bwayo;

(c) kuchita mabambe akwaayendelezya alaatala akukwabilila kubotya akujata kabotu zilengwaleza zijanika muchilikiti eecho;

(d) kusungwaazya kuswaya kwabantu bayanda kubona masena aswaayilwa muchilikiti antoomwe akwaasumpula masena aayo;

(e) kuchita ndangemusule akulangisisya mabelesezyo azibelesyo zijanika muchilikiti; kabiyo

(f) kuchita nuuba uuli mulimu , kubikkilizya kupanga milawu, iinga ilapegwa makkansili kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zyakupangwa, bwiime ababelesi bajanika mukkansili yachilikiti amukkansili lyadolopo pati antoomwe ambubanga baleendelezya milimu yabo.

(3) Bantu bajanika mukkansili yachilikiti amudolopo pati balaabulambe, kabalaamwi naakuti umwi awumwi, bwakubelekela bulumbu bukkala muchilikiti mbubeelede kuchitilwa akuchitila fulumende wanyika mbiizulwa nzyayelede kuchitilwa. 271 Makkomiti aamakkansili amuzilikiti zyamumyuunzi amadolopo mapati Kuchitila kuti abeleke kabotu milimu yawo, makkansili aamuzilikiti amumadolopo mapati akonzya kubumba makkomiti pesi makkomiti aayo ayelede kuzulwidwa abantu baambidwe muchibeela 268(1)(c). [Kabela kabambululwa achibeela 18 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

272 Basichuuno bamakkansili amuzilikiti zyamumyuunzi amadolopo mapati [Mutwe wamakani wachibeela wabambululwa achibeela 19(a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021 ]

(1) Kumuswaangano mutaanzi nikwazwa aakuchitwa nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene nyika mbiizulwa, ikkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naakuti yadolopo pati yeelede kusala sichuuno kuzwa kumazina ngibapegwa aayinda aabili aabantu bakonzya kusalwa—

[kabela kabambululwa achibeela 19(b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(a) akabunga kazyakweendelezya nyika kakajana mavooti manji kwiindila mukusalwa kwabayiminini baNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu bamuchilikiti eecho; naakuti

(b) kuti kakutakwe kabunga kakweendelezya nyika mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mufuka (a), kabunga kakweendelezya nyika kakajana mavooti manji mukusalwa kwabazulwidi bayiminina Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(2) Muntu ukonzya kusalwa kuba sichuuno wakkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi na dolopo pati kuti kakuli ukonzya kusalwa kuba mwiiminini wamuNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu (Seneti). [Kabela kabambululwa achibeela 19(c ) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(3) Wofesi yaSichuuno wakkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naakuti dolopo pati njakubelekela luundu pesi lwakwe sichuuno teensi mubelesi waluundu [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 19(d )(ii) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(4) Katana talika kubeleka, sichuuno wakkansili yamumyuunzi naa dolopo pati yachilikiti uyelede kuchita chikonke kunembo kwaMulembi wakkanisli yachilikiti, chikonke chakulibombya achamulimu wakwe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yatatu. [kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 19( e) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(5) Sichuuno wakkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naa dolopo pati ukonzya kusiya mulimu kwiinda mukuzibisya kkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naa dolopo pati lwakwe mwini. [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 19 (e ) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(6) Sichuuno wakkansili yachilikitichamumyuunzi naa dolopo pati uyelede kusiya chuuno chakwe— [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 19 (f) (i) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(a) buzuba kkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naa dolopo pati niyaswaanana kwalutaanzi nikwazwa kusalwa bazulwidi basiyenesiyene munsaluuzyo zyanyika yoonse;

(b) kuti katachikonzyi kuba umwi wabayiminini bakkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naa dolopo pati;

(c) kuti kkansili yachilikiti yazuminana kuti agwisigwe kuzwa muwofesi kwiinda mukuzuminana kwabantu basika kuntompa-chaala kwabantu bajanika mukkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naa dolopo pati; [Mafuka (a) – ( c) abambululwa achibeela 19(f) (ii) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(d) wagwisigwa muwofesi akabunga kakasalwa kuti kalangelange mabelekelo aakwe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (7).

(7) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zyakupangwa kwakabunga kalyiimikilide kakonzya kugwisya basichuuno bamakkansili aamuzilikiti zyamumyuunzi amadolopo mapati kuzwa mumawofesi, pesi kugwisigwa kwabasichuuno muzyuuno zyabo kweelede kuchitwa kakutobelezegwa zezi zitobela— [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 19(g) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(a) kutakonzya kuchita milimu yakwe nkaambo kakusondoka naakuti kulemana;

(b) kutakonzya mulimu wakwe munzila iiyindilide;

(c) kusunda mulandu mupati loko;

(d) kujanwa kalaamulandu ankuta, mulandu ulaachakuchita akuubula bulemu bwakwe, buboli akutabelesya kabotu chuuno chakwe; naakuti

(e) kutyola mulawu achaali kubikkilizya tulawulawu twakabikkwa akkansili.

(8) Sichuuno wakkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naa dolopo pati takonzyi kuzwa achuuno domane kwiinda mumalayilile awambidwe muchibeela eechi. [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 19(h) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

273 Zimwi zinga zilaambwa aatala amakkansili amuzilikiti amumadolopo mapti

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti weelede wapandulula, kazyeendelana aChaandaano eechi, zyakupangwa antoomwe amilimu yamakkansili yamuzilikiti amumadolopo mapati, kapati aatala —

(a) akweendelezegwa kwamakkansili aazilikiti amadolopo mapati;

(b) milimu yabasichuuno bamakkansili aamuzilikiti amumadolopo mapati;

(c) mifolo amabelekelo abantu amumadolopo mapati; kabiyo

bajanika

mumakkansili

aamuzilikiti

(d) kunjizigwa milimu, mifolo, mabelekelo akugwisigwa kwababelesi bamukkansili yachilikiti amudolopo pati.

milimu

(2) Mulawu waNsaluuzyo uyelede kupandulula, kazyeendelana aazili muChaandaano eechi, kuzuzikizigwa kwazimanga zyachitika kubantu batanooli bazwa mumakkansili aazilikiti zyaambwa muchibeela 268(1)(f) akunjila muchuuno chasichuuno kuti wazwa sichuuno, kabiyo chuuno chasyaala kachitachikwe muntu chikonzya kuzuzikizigwa—

(a) amuntu uzwa kukabunga kazyakweendelezya nyika kakabikkide ooyo wasiya chuuno naakuti wofesi; kabiyo

(b) aanze kwabusichuuno, zimwi zyuuno zyeelede kutolwa amuntu ukozyania awakasiya chuuno eecho, kuti wakali mwaalumi ngakunjizigwe mwaalumi kabiyo kuti wakali mwanakazi ngakunjizigwe mwanakazi.

CHIBAKA 3 FULUMENDE WADOLOPO/WABBOOMA 274 Fulumende/kkansili yadolopo

(1) Kuli makkansili amadolopo aakwiimikilila akweendelezya bukkale bwabantu bajanika mumadolopo munyika yoonse yaZimbabwe.

(2) Makkansili amadolopo ayendelezegwa amakkansili aajisi makkansela basalwa abantu balembesezye kuvoota mumasena aayo mubakkala mumadolopo kaazulwidwa abaMeya basalwa naakuti basizyuuno, fumbwa zina ndibapegwa.

(3) Misyobo yamakkansili yamumadolopo asiyenesiyene ikonzya kupangwa kabiyo madolopo aabili naakuti kwiinda aabili akonzya kubikkwa mubweendelezi bwakkansili yomwe.

(4) Ziyandikana azyeelede kutobelezegwa mukuchita nsaluuzyo yabantu baambidwe mukabeela (2) zyeelede kudondomekwa muMulawu wazyaNsaluuzyo.

(5) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa nguzu kumaMeya abasichuuno bamakkansili amadolopo, pesi fumbwa Meya naakuti sichuuno wapegwa nguzu eezi weelede kusalwa abantu bakalembesya kuvoota mubusena mujanika kkansili eeyo. 275 Kkansili yabbooma yamumyuunzi

(1) Kuli makkansili amumyuunzi apangwa kwiinda muchibeela eechi, kuchitila kuti ayiminine akweendelezya bukkale bwabantu bamuluzeva.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zya —

(a) kupangwa kwamakkansili amumyuunzi;

(b) kusalwa, abantu bakalembesya kuvoota mubbooma limwi alimwi, kwamakkansili beelede kweendelezya bukkale bwamabbooma awambidwe mufuka (a) aajulu;

(c) kusalwa kwabasichuuno, naakuti zimwi zyuuno nzibitwa aazyo, kuti bazulwide makkansili awambwa mufuka (b); kabiyo

(d) ziyandikana kuti muntu asalwe kuba umwi wamakkansili awambwa mufuka (b).

(3) Misyobo isiyenesiyene yamakkansili ikonzya kupangwa yamasena asiyenesiyene amumyuunzi, kabiyo aabili naakuti aayinda aabili makkansili akonzya kubikkwa mubweendelezi bwakkansili yomwe. 276 Milimu yamakkansili amumabbooma/mumyuunzi

(1) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli amuMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti, kkansili ilaanguzu zyakweendelezya busena, ayilikke, amakkalilo abantu bajanika mubusena muyakapangwa, kabiyo ijisi woonse manguzu akuchita mulimu ooyu.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa nguzu amilimu kukkansili yabbooma, kubikkilizya—

(a) manguzu aakuchita milawu, ayimwi milawu-lawu igwasizya kweendelezegwa kwamasena ngiyakabumbilwa kkansili eeyo;

(b) nguzu zyakwaamba mali zyeelede kubbadalwa abantu bakkala mubusena oobo kuchitila kuti ijane mali zyakweendelezya akusumpula busena oobo. 277 Kusalwa kuba kkansela

(1) Kusalwa kwamakkansela kweelede kuchitwa —

(a) munsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene munyika yoonse nikusalwa maMeya amakkansela abayiminini bakuPalyamenti antoomwe aMuzulwidi waNyika;

(b) amukusala kkansela nikutali chiindi chakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene kuzuzikizya chuuno chasiigwa awumwi kkansela/bamwi makkansela basiya zyuuno achakufwambaana.

(2) Nsaluuzyo zyamaMeya abasichuuno bamakkansili aamabbooma amadolopo, aanze kwamaMeya abasichuuno bapegwa nguzu mpati kwiinda muchibeela 274(5), zyeelede kuchitwa mumuswaangano mutaanzi wamakkansili aayo nikwazwa kuchitwa nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi basiyenesiyene nyika yoonse.

(3) Aanze kwawaawo mpukwambidwe mukabeela (2) naakuti muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti, maMeya, basichuuno amakkansela bamadolopo amabbooma beelede kutalika mulimu wabo mubuzuba bwamusanu amazuba aane kuzwa buunsi nikwaambilizigwa bakazunda munsaluuzyo zyakusalwa kwabazulwidi basiyenesiyene mubakasalwa abalabo.

(4) Mulawu Wabikwa aPalyamenti ulakonzya kuchita kuti kube nsaluuzyo, kakubelesegwa mpampikwa lwatubunga twazyabuleli bwachisi yakweelanisya yaambwa kukabeela (5), akuti mweelo wamakkumi otatwe kuzwa kumwaanda chabiiminini boonse akkansili mumawaadi ube wabachikayintu.

(5) Lusaluulo lwamakkansela amuzilikiti zyakumyuunzi amumadolopo mapati lweelede lweendelezegwa kakutobelwa Mulawu waNsaluulo, ooyo weelede wasinizya kuti bantu baambwa kukabeela (4) basalwe munsi ampampikwa yatubunga twazyabuleli bwanyika luyeme kumavoti ajanwa abiiminini batubunga kunsaluuzyo iikamanteneya Biiminini baNg’anda yaMulawu kuchilikiti eecho.

[Kabeela 4 a5 kabikwa achibeela 20 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

278 Bulamfu bwachiindi nchibanga balakkala muzyuuno makkansela

(1) Chuuno chaMeya, sichuuno naakuti kkansela wadolopo naakuti bbooma chisyaala kachitachikwe muntu luzutu kwiinda mumalayilile awambidwe muchibeela 129, aanga mwiiminini waNg’anda Yamilawu (Palyamenti), fumbwa kwaamba Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu naakuti Muzulwidi waSenenti muchibeela 129(1)(k), zinooli kakwaambwa Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyabafulumende bazilikiti madolopo amabbooma.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula kubumbwa kwakabunga kalyiimikilide kakonzya kuchita milimu yakugwisya aazyuuno maMeya, sichuuno amakkansela, pesi kugwisigwa kwabantu aaba muzyuuno kweelede kuchitwa kwiinda muli zeezi zyaambidwe aansi—

(a) kutakonzya kuchita milimu yakwe nkaambo kakusondoka naakuti kulemana;

(b) kutakonzya mulimu munzila iiyindilide;

(c) kusunda mulandu mupati loko;

(d) kujanwa kalaamulandu ankuta, mulandu ulaachakuchita akuubula bulemu bwakwe, buboli akutabelesya kabotu chuuno chakwe; naakuti

(e) kutyola mulawu achaali kubikkilizya tulawulawu twakabikkwa akkansili.

(3) Meya, sichuuno naakuti kkansela wakkansili yadolopo naakuti bbooma takonzyi kuzwa achuuno domane kwiinda mumalayilile awambidwe muchibeela eechi. 279 Meendelezyo aaKkansili yabbooma mumyuunzi Mulawu upangwa aaPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zyameendelezyo ayelede kuchitwa amakkansili amuluzeva.

CHAANDAANO 15 BAZULUZI BABULONGO 280 Buzuluzi bwabulongo

(1) Bube bwabuzuluzi bwabulongo, bwiime bwabo amilimu yabo kwiinda mumilawu yansiku /chintu buzibikene zili mumulawu.

(2) Muzuluzi wabulongo ulaabulambe bwakweendelezya tunsiyansiya, milawu yansiku yabulongo amilimu yaMwaami, 281

Ndemeko zyeelede kulangisisigwa abazuluzi babulongo

(1) Bazuluzi babulongo beelede—

(a) kuchita milimu yabo kabatobelezya Kkata lyaMulawu eeli antoomwe amilawu yamuZimbabwe yoonse;

(b) kulemeka tunsiyansiya tulangene abuzuluzi bwabulongo akuchita milimu yabo izuzikizya bube bwabo mbuuli mbubazibikene bazuluzi babulongo muKkata lyaMulawu eeli; kabiyo

(c) kujata bweelene bantu boonse bali mumasena alaansi lyabo.

(2) Bazuluzi babulongo tabeelede pe —

(a) kunjila mutubunga twakweendelezya nyika naakuti kunjilingana mumilimu yatubunga twazyanyika;

(b) kuchita zyakabunga kakweendelezya nyika;

(c) kuzuzikizya mpalamo zyakabunga kakweendelezya nyika; naakuti

(d) kutyola akutalemeka zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka zyanikuba uuli muntu. 282 Milimu yabazuluzi babulongo

(1) Bazuluzi babulongo bajisi milimu itobela mumasena aabo mubajanika —

(a) kusungwaazya akubona kuti tunsiyansiya twaluundu tulookutobelezegwa, kapati kusungwaazya kwiimikila akujatwa kabotu kwampuli;

(b) kutola ntaamo zyakukwabilila akusungwaazya kutobelezegwa kwatunsiyansiya, zyansiku, ngano, azyakatusiila bensiku zijanika muluundu kubikkilizya malende aatondwa;

(c) kubona kuti lusumpuko lulookuchitika mubusena bwabo;

(d) kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti, kweendelezya busena anyika yamumaluzeva akukwabilila zituzyungulukide azilengwa-leza zyamubusena bwabo;

(e) kumana mpyopyongano zichitika akati kabantu mbibazulwida kakutobelezegwa milawu atunsiyansiya twaluundu lwabo; kabiyo

(f) kuchita imwi milimu njibapegwa aMulawu upangwa a Palyamenti.

(2) Aanze kwazyaambidwe muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti, bazuluzi babulongo bajisi nguzu zyakweendelezya maluzeva/ nyika yamuluzeva naakuti masena ngibapegwa kuti beendelezye antoomwe aboonse bantu bajanika mumasena aayo.

(3) Mukuchita milimu yabo, bazuluzi babulongo tabeelede kunjililwa aabamwi bantu pe naakuti tumwi tubunga nikuba twafulumende, aanze kwawaawo mpukwaambidwe aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(4) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula mbubeelede kulijata bazuluzi babulongo. 283 Kunjizigwa akugwisigwa kwabazuluzi babulongo muzyuuno Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zitobela kawutobelezya tunsiyansiya abukkale bujanika mubusena bumwi abumwi—

(a) kunjizya muzyuuno, kujana bwiimikizya kubeleka kwabazuluzi babulongo, kusalwa kwabazuluzi nikwaba chimanga antoomwe akugwisigwa achuuno kwabazuluzi babulongo;

(b) kupangwa akubusilizigwa kwabwaami; kabiyo

(c) kumana mpyopyongano zyeendelene akunjizigwa muzyuuno kwabazuluzi babulongo, kwiimikizigwa kubeleka kwabo, kusalwa kwabazuluzi babulongo akugwisigwa achuuno kwabazuluzi babulongo; pesi—

(i) kunjizigwa muzyuuno kwabazuluzi babulongo, kugwisigwa achuuno akwiimikizigwa kubeleka kwabo baami kweelede kwachitwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuuli muzeezo wazwa kuNkamu Yabaami Yamuchilikiti kwiinda muNkamu Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse kiibelekela aamwi aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyabazuluzi babulongo. Zyoonse eezi zyeelede kuchitwa kakutobelezegwa tunsiyansiya abukkale bwamuluundu mwajanika mwaami ooyo;

(ii) mpyopyongano zilaatala akunjizigwa muzyuuno kwabazuluzi babulongo, kwiimikizigwa kubeleka kwabo antoomwe akugwisigwa milimu kwabazuluzi babulongo zyeelede kumaninsigwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kwiinda muNkamu Yabaami Yamuchilikiti , aNkamu Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse kiibelekela amwi aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende ubona zyabazuluzi babulongo;

(iii) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula kabotu ntaamo zyeelede kutolwa kubona kuti zyoonse zyaambo zyajatwa kabotu munzila itakwe lusaluulo kabiyo itanjizyi zyaambo Zyatubunga twabulei bwanyika

(iv) Mulawu weelede kubikka ntaamo zyakukwabilila kuzikatila alimwi akukkalinkana kwatubunga twababulongo antoomwe akulyiimikilila akulisanta kwabo kuzwa kuli zyakweendelezegwa kwanyika mumabelekelo aabo. 284 Mifolo alumwi lugwasyo lupegwa bazuluzi babulongo

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zyaatala amifolo antoomwe alumwi lugwasyo lweelede kupegwa bazuluzi babulongo kazili zyazumininwa aMuzulwidi waNyika muzeezo ooyo kuuli wazwa kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyabazuluzi babulongo.

(2) Mifolo yabazuluzi babulongo iyelede kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende (Consolidated Revenue Fund).

(3) Mifolo yabazuluzi babulongo tiiyelede kuselusigwa pe kuti kabachili achuuno naakuti kabachijatilide chuuno eecho. 285 Kkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse aNkamu Yabaami Yamuchilikiti

(1) Kuli Kkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse ijisi bantu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti, kiili njakwimikilila baami boonse muZimbabwe.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupanga Nkamu Yabaami Yamuchilikiti, muzilikiti zyoonse aanze kwazilikiti zyamadolopo mapati. Nkamu yabaami eeyi ijisi baami bamuchilikiti eecho.

(3) Kweelede baami bamuzilikiti zyoonse bayimikililwa muKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse.

(4) Kusalwa kwaMuzulwidi aWumutobela, baKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse abamwi bajanika mukkansili eeyi kweelede kwachitwa aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe.

(5) Imwi ayimwi Nkamu Yabaami Yamuchilikiti iyelede kusala, kakutobelezegwa Mulawu waNsaluuzyo, baami bayiminina chilikiti muNg’anda Yamilawu Yaajuli kwiinda muchibeela 120(1)(b).

(6) Muzulwidi aWumutobela, baKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse basalwa kuti bakkale muzyuuno eezi kwachiindi chisika kuminyaka musanu kabiyo bakonzya kusalwa kwachibili kwaminyaka iyelene pesi muntu wanjila tubili muchuuno chakuba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waKkansili Yabaami , ukonzya kwiimikila kuti asalwe kuba Muzulwidi waKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse.

(7) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zyaatala—

(a) akusalwa kwabaami kuti banjile muKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse antoomwe anzibeelede kuba aazyo azinga zilabakachizya aabo bayanda kusalwa kunjila;

(b) achikonke cheelede kukonkwa abaabo baami banjila muKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse antoomwe amuNkamu Yabaami Yamuzilikiti;

(c) abulamfu bwachindi nchibeelede kukkala muzyuuno zyamuKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse antoomwe amuNkamu Yabaami Yamuzilikiti;

(d) amifolo, pencheni alumwi lugwasyo lweelede kupegwa Muzulwidi aWumutobela, baKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse abamwi baami bajanika mukkansili eeyi;

(e) nzila zyeelede kutobelezegwa mukuchita miswaangano yaKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse amuNkamu Yabaami Yamuzilikiti;

(f) akunjizigwa milimu babelesi bagwasizya Kkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse aNkamu Yabaami Yamuzilikiti. 286 Milimu yaKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse aNkamu Yabaami Yamuchilikiti

(1) IKkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse kabiyo muli chimwi chilikiti, Nkamu Yabaami Yamuchilikiti ilaamilimu itobela —

(a) kukwabilila, kusungwaazya twanyika yaZimbabwe;

akusumpula

bukkale

atunsiyansiya

(b) kwiimikilila ziyeeywa abazuluzi babulongo antoomwe akubona kuti buntususu abwiime bwabazuluzi babulongo bulizikitide;

(c) kukwabilila akusungwaazya ziyandwa abazuluzi babulongo;

(d) kutambula ntongawuko azyaambo zyeetwa kuli mbabo kazizwa kubazuluzi babulongo;

(e) kudondolola akubona kuti zilookutobelwa nchito akulijata kwabazuluzi babulongo antoomwe akubagwasizya zyabuzuluzi bwabo;

(f) kugwasizya kumana mpyopyongano akati kabazuluzi babulongo naakuti zilaatala abazuluzi babulongo;

(g) kuchita milimu isiyenesiyene njibatanooli bapegwa aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti wabikkwa.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kubona kuti —

(a) Kkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse aNkamu Yabaami Yamuzilikiti zyakonzya kuchita milimu yazyo kazilyiimikilide;

(b) bantu babelekela Kkansili Yabaami Yanyika Yoonse amuNkamu Yabaami Yamuzilikiti bachita milimu yabo kabotu kabatakwe lusaluulo. 287 Kkomiti yazyabuntususu akulijata kwaBaami Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula zyaatala akupangwa kwa kkomiti yazyabuntususu akulijata, bantu beelede kunjila mukkomiti ambiyeelede kubeleka kkomiti yazyabuntususu akulijata kwabaami kuti ichite milimu itobela—

(a) kupanga akubona kuti zilokutobelezegwa zyabuntususu akulijata kwabazuluzi babulongo;

(b) kumana kutamvwanana akati kwabazuluzi babulongo;

(c) Kulangisisya ntongawuko/nyontooko zilaatala abazuluzi babulongo.

CHAANDAANO 16 NYIKA YAKULIMA 288 Bupanduluzi bwaChaandaano 16 Muchaandaano eechi— “nyika yakulima” iwamba mbuuli mbukupandulwidwe muchibeela 72. 289 Ndemeko zitobelwa mumabelesezyo aanyika yakulima Kuchitila kululamika bulewusi bwakaliwo mukupana nyika yakulima muchiindi nyika niyakaleledwe aamakuwa, kabiyo kweeta masandu mumabelesezyo anyika yakulima akuchita kuti banaZimbabwe babelesye zilengwaleza bweelene, milawu yamabelesezyo akupana nyika yakulima yeelede kutobelezya ndemeko eezi—

(a) nyika yakulima nchilengwa chaLeza chibumba chipanzi chalukono lwabana banyika yaZimbabwe;

(b) kwiinda muchibeela 72, woonse munaZimbabwe ulacheelelo chakujana, kujata, kukkala, kubelesya, kupa bamwi bantu, nyika yakulima kakutalangwi kuti musyobo nzi pe;

(c) kupana kwanyika yakulima kweelede kuchitwa kakutakwe lusaluulo, kuchitwe bweelene kakutalangwi kuti mwanakazi naakuti mwaalumi sikupegwa antoomwe azyiingi zilangililwa aluundu mukupana nyika;

(d) nzila yakupana nyika yakulima yeelede kusungwaazya kuti bantu babelesye nyika munzila igwasya luundu antoomwe anyika yaZimbabwe mbiizulwa;

(e) kubelesegwa kwanyika yakulima kweelede kusungwaazya kujanika kwachakulya munyika, bulondozi buzwide akupanga milimu kabiyo kakukwabililwa akujata kabotu zituzyungulukide kuchitila kuti zyalane lyajuunza lizoobelesye alilayo;

(f) taakwe muntu uunga uliimwa cheelelo chakubelesya nyika yakulima naakuti kuyaka aanyika njaapegwa. 290 Kwiinkilila nembo kwazyeelelo zyafulumende kunyika yakulima

(1) Yoonse nyika yakulima yakali —

(a) yalembwa muMpampikwa Yamusanu Atubili muKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi; naakuti

(b) kalitana beleka pe Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, yakabonwa kuti kwiinda muchibeela 16B(2)(a)(ii) naakuti (iii) muKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi; Iya kunembo kiili mumaanza afulumende.

(2) Fumbwa kuteendelana akati kazijanika mu —

(a) chizibisyo chijanika muMpampikwa Yamusnau Atubili muKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi; naakuti

(b) chizibisyo chilaachakuchita anyika yakulima kabiyo chakadindwa kwiinda muchibeela 16B(2)(a)(ii) naakuti (iii) muKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi; Kabiyo mapepa atondeezya kuti ngwani mweni nyika yakulima eeyo naakuti uyelede, kabiyo fumbwa bulewusi bwakachitwa muchizibisyo eecho, tazikachizyi kubeleka kwakabeela (1) pe naakuti kugwisya cheelelo chafulumende mukuba mweni nyika yakulima eeyo kwiinda mukabeela aako.

291 Kwiinkilila nembo kwazyeelelo zyabantu bakkede aanyika yakulima Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, fumbwa muntu wakakkede aanyika yakulima naakuti wakali kubelesya nyika yakulima naakuti wakajisi mapepa atondeezya kuti njiyakwe nyika yakulima kwiinda mumapepa akubelesya kwachiindi chisyoonto naakuti kwiinda muli chimwi chizuminano afulumende, uliinkilila nembo kabelesya naakuti kakkede aanyika yakulima eeyo kuzwa buunsi nilitalika kubeleka Kkata Lyamualwu eeli. 292 Kukwabililwa kwanzila njibakapegwa aayo bantu bakkede aanyika yakulima Fulumende weelede kutola ntaamo, kubikkilizya kupanga mulawu, kuchitila kuti bantu bapegwa nyika yakulima bajane lukwabililo luchita kuti balimvwe kabali beni nyika yakulima akwiibelesya munzila yakazuminanwa afulumende. 293

Kupambula kwafulumedne nyika yakulima

(1) Fulumende ukonzya kupambula fumbwa nyika, kwiinda mukupa umwi muntu nyika yakulima kuti ibe yakwe naakuti ajane bubelesya, pesi nyika eeyo yapambulwa kweelede yachitwa boobo kwiinda mundemeko zyaambidwe muchibeela 289.

(2) Fulumende takonzyi kupambula akupa uumwi muntu naakuti bana bakwe, nyika zyakulima zyiinda yomwe.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti yeelede kupandulula mpambwido amapedo aanyika yakulima kumuntu afulumende, pesi fumbwa mulawu uwamba boobo uyelede kweendelana andemeko zyaambidwe muchibeela 289. 294 Kupambulwa kwanyika yakulima abeni nyika naakuti bakkede mpawo Kwiinda munzila zyaambidwe aayimwi milawu, mweni nyika yakulima naakuti uukkede aanyika yakulima ulacheelelo chakupa umwi muntu chipanzi chanyika, kupa muntu kuti ajane bubelesya naakuti kuuzya cheelelo chakwe chakubelesya nyika yakwe yakulima. 295

Kubbadalwa kwabantu bakanyangwa nyika yakulima afulumende muchiindi chakiinda

(1) Fumbwa munaZimbabwe wakanyangwa nyika yakulima afulumende kalitana tanguna kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli ulacheelelo chakubbadalwa, kabbadalwa nyika antoomwe azimwi nzyatanooli wakayaka akuchita aanyika yakulima eeyo njaakanyangwa;

(2) Fumbwa muntu ulaanyika yakulima yakatolwa afulumende kalitana tanguna kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli kabiyo kakuli cheelelo chakuba mweni nyika yakulima chakali kwabilidwe azizuminano zyakachitwa aakati kaFulumende waZimbabwe aFulumende wayimwi nyika, ulacheelelo chakubbadalwa, kabbadalilwa nyika antoomwe azimwi nzyatanooli wakayaka akuchita aanyika yakulima eeyo njaakanyangwa;

(3) Fumbwa muntu, aanze kwawooyo uwambidwe mukabeela (1) naakuti (2), ulaanyika yakulima yakatolwa afulumende kalitana talika pe kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli ulacheelelo chakubbadalwa afulumende, kabbadalwa zintu nzyaakayaka akuchita aanyika yakulima muchiindi niyakatolwa.

(4) Miliyo iliigwa aansi kwazibeela (1)(2) a(3) iyelede kulangisisigwa kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

296 Kupangwa antoomwe abwiime bwa Kkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe

(1) Kuli kkomishini itanootegwa Kkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe ijisi bantu batobela —

(a) sichuuno awutobela sichuuno; kabiyo

(b) bantu babili naakuti bayinda babili pesi kabatayindi musanu ababili; basalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Bantu bajanika muKkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe beelede kuti —

(a) bakasalwa nkaambo kabuntususu bwabo, kukonzya mulimu aluzibo, kumvwisisisya akuziba kwiindilide muli zyakweendelezegwa akubambwa kwazintu; kabiyo

(b) kutondeezya kusiyana kwamisyobo yabantu bajanika munyika yaZimbabwe, kapati ziyandwa azipanzi zisiyenesiyene zyanyika yaZimbabwe akusiyana kwamilimu ichitwa abaalumi abanakazi.

(3) Chibeela 237 chilabeleka mukugwisigwa kuzwa muzyuuno kwabantu bali muKkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe mbuuli kuti nikkomishini ilyiimikilide yakapangwa kwiinda muchibeela 12. 297 Kkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe

(1) Milimu yaKkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe njeeyi iitobela —

(a) kubona kuti takukwe lusaluulo, kuli kubalangala mukweendelezegwa kwanyika yakulima yafulumende;

(b) kuchita ndangisisyo yamabelesezyo aanyika yakulima chiindi achiindi;

(c) kupa mizeezo kuli fulumende aatala —

(i) akutolwa kwanyika yakulima ili mumaboko abantu kuti ibe mumaboko afulumende igwasizye bulumbu;

(ii) kubona kuti nyika yakulima ilajanika abantu boonse bayanda kwiijana , kapati— A. kugwisigwa kwamisyobo yalusaluulo yoonse mukupana kwanyika yakulima, kapati lusaalulo lulaatala akuti mwanakazi naakuti mwaalumi uyanda kupegwa nyika yakulima; B. kubona kuti mulawu wabeleka ukasya kupegwa kwamuntu uumwi naakuti mpuli iimwi nyika yakulima iyindilide,

(iii) kubona mabelesezyo anyika yakulima abukomene bwanyika yakulima bweelede kupegwa bantu basiyenesiyene;

(iv) kuubya machitilo akulonzya nyika yakulima kuzwa mumaboko aawumwi muntu kuya kuluumbi;

(v) kubona nzila zisiyenesiyene zyakubelesya nyika yakulima antoomwe akubona kuti muntu uyipedwe kwachiindi chilamfu biyeni nyika yakulima;

(vi) kubbadalwa kuli kabotu kweendelene anzyeelede kubbadalwa muntu aansi kwamilawu isiyenesiyene ilaatala anyika yakulima antoomwe azitanooli zyakachitwa akuyakwa aanyika yakulima muntu njaakatolelwa;

(vii) kupegwa kwabantu antoomwe akupambulwa kwanyika yakulima;

(d) kulangisisya akupokoola aatala antongawuko/nyontooko ampyopyongano zilaatala akubona mabelesezyo, kweendelezegwa akupegwa kwabantu nyika yakulima.

(2) IKkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe, kwiinda mukupegwa mvumo aSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende wazyanyika yakulima, ikonzya kuchita fumbwa miteto igwasizya kuzuzikizya mpalamo zyaambidwe mukabeela (1).

(3) IKkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe iyelede kuchita milimu yayo kwiinda mukutobelezya malembe aamiteto atanoolembwe aSilutwe waMitabi yaFulumende ubona zyanyika yakulima.

(4) Mukubeleka kwayo Kkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe iyelede kutobelezya ndemeko zyaambidwe muchibeela 289.

(5) Fulumende atumwi tubunga ambungano zyafulumende muzibeela zyoonse zyanyika, kwiinda mukupanga milawu naakuti kutola zimwi ntaamo, uyelede kugwasizya Kkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe mukubeleka kwayo antoomwe akukwabilila kulyiimikilila akulisanta kwayo mumabelekelo aayo alaabuntususu.

(6) Fulumende uyelede kuchita zyoonse nzyakonzya, kwiinda mukupanga milawu naakuti kutola ntaamo zisiyenesiyene, kubona kuti—

(a) Kkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe yakonzya kubeleka kabotu munzila ilisantide akulyiimikilila; kabiyo

(b) babelesi baKkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe babeleka munzila ilaantanganana kabiyo itakwe lusaluulo.

CHAANDAANO 17 ZYAMALI

CHIBAKA 1 MABELESEZYO AAMALI 298 Ndemeko zilaatala amabelesezyo aamali yabulumbu

(1) Ndemeko zitobela zyeelede kutobelezegwa mukubelesegwa kwamali yabulumbu munyika yaZimbabwe —

(a) kweelede kwaba bubalangale mumabelesezyo aamali;

(b) zyamali yabulumbu zyeelede kugwasizya lusumpuko lwanyika, kapati —

(i) mukuli wakuliya tekisi yanyika weelede kwaabanwa abantu boonse;

(ii) mali yajanwa kuzwa kutekisi yanyika yoonse yeelede kwaabanwa akati kafulumedne wanyika yoonse abafulumende bazilikiti abamabbooma; kabiyo

(iii) kubelesegwa kwamali kweelede kulanzigwa kulusumpuko lwanyika yoonse, kapati kugwasizya baabo badyaamizidwe mubuumi amumasena aabo;

(c) mukuli antoomwe amabelesezyo aazilengwaleza kweelede kwaabanwa bweelene akati kamazyalane aasunu amazyalane ajuunza;

(d) mali yabulumbu yeelede kubelesegwa kabotu, munzila iibalangala kabiyo iizikitide;

(e) kubelesegwa kabotu kwamali ngakuchitwe munzila ilaantanganana kiibalangala; kabiyo

(f) kuchenekwa kwamali yabulumbu antoomwe akubelesegwa kwamali yaluundu ngazichitwe munzila ibalangala munzila iswaanizya akulemeka njando zyachisi chaZimbabwe.

(2) Taakwe tekisi yeelede kuliisigwa pe aanze kwakuti nguzu zyakuliisya bantu zyazwa muKkata lyaMulawu eeli naakuti muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

299 Kubayila kwaPalyamenti kumabelesesyo akujanwa kwamali afulumende

(1) Palyamenti iyelede kubayila akulangisisya mabelesezyo aamali afulumende antoomwe amaKkomishini, tubunga ambunga zyafulumende zisiyenesiyene muzibeela zyanyika zyoonse, kubikkilizya tubunga twakaanzwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu, bafulumende bamuzilikiti, mumabbooma, kuchitila kuti—

(a) mali zyoonse zibelesegwe kabotu;

(b) mabelesezyo amali woonse aachitwa aleelede kuchitwa; kabiyo

(c) eezyo ziyanda kubelesegwa bunonooono zichitwe mbubo mbuuli mbukweelede.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kuchita kuti Palyamenti ibayile antoomwe akulangisisya mabelesezyo amali mbuuli mbukwaambwa mukabeela (1) aajulu. 300

Kuganunwa kwanguzu zyaFulumende zyakucheneka mali achikwelete chabulumbu

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kubikka mugaano a —

(a) kucheneka mali kwaFulumende;

(b) chikwelete chaFulumende;

(c) chikwelete amukuli wakuliya chikwelete zyeelede kuchitwa aFulumende;

mugaano ooyo tuyelede kwiindwa pe kakutakwe mvumo yazwa kuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kwaamba nzila azeezyo zyeelede kupa kuti Fulumende akwelete.

(3) Mukati kamazuba makumi musanu alimwi kali watola chikwelete fulumende naakuti wachita chizuminano chachikwelete, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali uyelede kumwaya akudinda zyaambo eezyo muGazette.

(4) Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali uyelede kuti—

(a) tubili naakuti kwiinda tubili amunyaka, wapa lipoti kuPalyamenti aajulu amabelekelo—

(i) achikwelete chakatolwa afulumende; kabiyo

(ii) achikwelete chakazuminanwa afulumende;

(b) achiindi chimwi nikwaambilwa Palyamenti aatala amali iliwo amali yakabelesegwa kwiinda muchibeela 305, kweelede kwaapegwa lipoti iizwide aatala abwiime bwachikwelete chanyika. 301

Kupegwa mali kwabafulumende bazilikiti abamabbooma

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula kabotu —

(a) kwaabanisigwa kweelene kwamali yakubelesya mulusumpuko akati kazilikiti amakkansili amadolopo mapati antoomwe amabbooma;

(b) fumbwa zimwi mali zyaabanisigwa kuzilikiti akumabbooma , antoomwe anzila zisiyenesiyene zyakwaabanisya mali eezi.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti waambwa mukabeela (1) uyelede kutobelezya zintu zitobela akati kazimwi zyiingi—

(a) njando zyachisi choonse;

(b) fumbwa chaabilo chabikkwa abbazu cheelede kubelesegwa kubbadala chikwelete chanyika azimwi zintu ziyanda kubbadalwa anyika;

(c) njando zyafulumende wanyika yoonse zilangisisya mpalamo zyanyika;

(d) chiyandisyo chakugwasizya bantu munzila zisiyenesiyene, kubikkilizya zyalwiiyo, bulondozi, maanzi, migwagwa, magesi kumasena adyaamikizidwe;

(e) majanino aamali kuli bafulumende bazilikiti, mumadolopo mapati amumabbooma;

(f) ziyanda kusumpulwa azimwi zintu ziyanda mali muzilikiti , mumadolopo mapati amumabbooma; kabiyo

(g) kusiyana kwalusumpuko lujanika mumasena asiyenesiyene atoomwe aakati kazilikiti zyanyika.

(3) Mali ipegwa zilikiti amabbooma munyaka amunyaka tayeelede kuba aansi kwachibeela musanu kuzwa mumwaanda (5%) kumali yabulumbu yajanwa anyika yoonse.

CHIBAKA 2 NKOMO MPATI YAFULUMENDE 302 Nkomo Mpati Yafulumende Kuli Nkomo Mpati yaFulumende iyelede kunjila mali zyafulumende zyoonse mbuuli zijanwa kutekisi, fumbwa nkuyazwa, aanze kwakuti Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti—

(a) uzumizya kuti mali ibikkwe muliimbi nkomo yakaanzilwa kuti ibeleke umwi mulimu; naakuti

(b) wazumizya kuti nkamu eeyo yatambula mali ibelesye mali yoonse naakuti chipanzi chamali yatambulwa kuti izuzikizye mpalamo yazyo. 303 Kugwisigwa kwamali kuzwa muNkomo Mpati yafulumende

(1) Taakwe mali inga ilagwisigwa pe muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende aanze kwakuti iyanda kugwasizya kubbadala mulimu wazumininwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu naakuti aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Mali yagwisigwa kuzwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende iyelede kubbadalwa kumbungano, kabunga naakuti muntu uyelede kwiipegwa mali eeyo.

(3) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula nzila aatala—

(a) ambiiyelede kugwisigwa mali kuzwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende antoomwe azimwi nkomo zyamali yaluundi; kabiyo

(b) mali ili muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende mbiiyelede kubambwa akubelesegwa kuti izyale iimbi. 304 Zibbadalwa zilangene aNkomo Mpati yaFulumende

(1) Zyoonse mali zibbadalwa zikwelete zyafulumende zyeelede kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende

(2) Iluliyo luswaananwa aalo mukubungilizya mali injila muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende nduulumwi luliyo lutaanzi lweelede kuzwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende.

(3) Kuchitila zeezyo zyaambwa mukabeela (1)— “luliyo lulangene aachikwelete” lubikkilizya mali yakazyalwa, kuliigwa kwamali akubbadalwa kwachikwelete antoomwe azimwi mbeelesezyo zyamali mukujana chikwelete ibayilwa aNkomo Mpati yaFulumende antoomwe akubbadalwa kwachikwelete.

CHIBAKA 3 KUZUMIZIGWA KWAMABELESEZYO AMALI IZWA MUNKOMO MPATI YAFULUMENDE 305

Kwaabanisya mali iizwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende

(1) Mwaaka amwaaka Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali weelede kupa lipoti/ngambo, kuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, ilaatala amali zilangililwa kujanwa ambuzitazoobelesegwe afulumende mumunyaka uuza.

(2) Malangililo akujanwa antoomwe ambelesezyo zyamali zyafulumende kweelede kuyoomwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kwiinda mukabeela (1) mubuzuba Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu mbuyakkala kakutaniinda mazuba aasika makumi musanu alimwi kawuli watalika munyaka mupya wazyakubelesegwa kwamali. Pesi kuti Palyamenti kiitalookuswaanana mazuba aayo kweelede malembe aayo aayoomwa aPalyamenti mukati kamazuba aali makumi aatatu kuzwa buzuba mbuyataanguna kuswaanana Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kuzwa mpuyakamwayidwe.

(3) Malangililo aamali itazoojanwe amalangililo amabelesezyo amali ayelede kujanwa kuzwa kuzibeela zisiyenesiyene zitobela—

(a) kuliimwi ayimwi Kkomishini yakaanzwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(b) ku wofesi yaSikulingula Mabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende;

(c) Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta;

(d) Kkansili yaBaami Yanyika Yoonse; kabiyo

(e) atumwi tubunga twaambidwe muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(4) Kuti Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu yazumizya akutambula malembe alaatala azilangililwa mumbelesezyo zyamunyaka uuza, aanze kwaluliyo luchitwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali uyelede kuchita mabambe aKwaaba Mali litegwa lyeelede kunjizigwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, kabiyo imalembe amulawu eelyo ayeelede kuti—

(a) lyapandulula aatala amali ijanwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende kiiliya luliyo lweelede kuchitwa;

(b) kupa mali kuti eezyo ziyanda kuliigwa ziliigwe kabotu kwiinda muzintu zyeelede kuti ziliigwe.

(5) Kuti kakuli mali yabikkwa aabbazu kwiinda muMulawu waKwaaba Mali kiili yacheya naakuti kuyandikana kuti kuliigwe mali kuchintu chakata bambilidwe mali pe, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali uyelede kuchita aambi mabambe aanjiliko atondeezya mali ilangililwa kuti iyilikwe mpawo kuliigwe. Mpawo mabambe aayo ayelede kutolegwa kuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu. Kuti Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu yazitambula zyaambwa, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali uyelede kupanga Malembe aaMulawu waKwaaba Mali - weelede kunjizigwa kabiyo muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu likayoomwe kalipandulula bwiime bwamali iimbi iyelede kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende. 306 Kuzumizigwa kubelesya mali kiitana kwaabanisigwa

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa nguzu kuMuzulwidi waNyika kuti azumizye kugwisigwa kwamali kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati Yafulumende kuti ibeelesegwe kuliya ziyanda kuliigwa zyakatali kulangililwa kuti zizooyanda mali chakufwambaana naakuti zyakatalangililidwe kabiyo zyakatabambilidwe mali yazyo, pesi—

(a) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ooyu tuuyelede kuzumizya kugwisigwa kwamali pe iyinda kamwi achisela kuzwa mumwaanda (one and half percent) yayeeyo yakaabanisidwe mumulawu wakachitidwe chiindi chayinda;

(b) fumbwa mali yagwisigwa kwiinda muMulawu waambwa iyelede kwiilikizigwa kumali yanjiliko ilangililwa kubelesegwa kabiyo yeelede kubikkwa kunembo lyaNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kuchitila kuti iyoomwe. Kuti Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu yazumina njiliko ilaangililwa, mali eeyo ikonzya kugwisigwa kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati yaFulumende kwiinda mumabambe anjiliko naakuti njiliko yaMulawu waKwaaba Mali

(2) Kuti mMulawu waKwaaba Mali- Appropriation Act yamunyaka wakubelesegwa kwamali kiitana beleka kumatalikilo kwamunyaka wakubelesegwa kwamali, Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kuzumizya Muzulwidi waNyika kuti ape mvumo yakugwisigwa kwamali kuzwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende kuchitila kuti kuliigwe chiyanda kuliigwa chamulimu wafulumende mukati kamyeezi iine yakutaanguna kwamunyaka wamabelesezyo aamali, pesi—

(a) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ooyu tuuyelede kuzumizya kugwisigwa kwamali pe iyinda fuka lyomwe kuzwa kumafuka otatwe yakaabanisidwe muzilangililo zyakubelesegwa kwamali zyakachitidwe munyaka wayinda;

(b) fumbwa mali yagwisigwa kwiinda muMulawu waambwa iyelede kwiilikizigwa kumali yanjiliko ilangililwa kubelesegwa kabiyo yeelede kubikkwa muzintu ziyanda kuliigwa zisiyenesiyene zyamabelesezyo aamali.

(3) Kuti Palyamenti yamwayigwa kakutana chitwa mabambe akuti fulumende akonzye kuchita milimu yakwe, Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa Muzulwidi waNyika nguzu kuti azumizye kugwisigwa kwamali kuzwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende kuti kuliigwe kuchitila kuti milimu yafulumende iye nembo kusikila myeezi itatu kakuli Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu yaswaanana kuzwa niyakamwayidwe. Pesi fumbwa mali yagwisigwa kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ooyu iyelede kunjizigwa muMulawu wakwaaba Mali muAppropriation Act kazili nzintu ziyanda kuliigwa zilyiimvwilidi mumabelesezyo aamali. 307 Kubelesegwa kwamali kutapedwe mvumo

(1) Kuti kakuli kwaboneka kuti yavula mali yakabelesegwa aachintu kutali mbuuli mbukwakaambidwe kwiinda mumabambe awambidwe muchibaka eechi, naakuti mali yabelesegwa achintu chakatapedwe mali kwiinda muchibaka eechi, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali uyelede kunjizya Appropriation Bill muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kakuyandwa kunyontooka aatala amabelesezyo akatapedwe mvumo.

(2) IBill yaambidwe mukabeela (1) iyeelede kunjizigwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu achakufwambaana kakutaniinda mazuba makumi musanu akkumi limwi (60) kuzwa nikwakabelesegwa mali yakatapedwe mvumo.

CHIBAKA 4 KUKWABILILWA KWAMALI ANTOOMWE AMBONO ZYABULUMBU 308 Milimu yabantu bakwabilila mali ambono zyabulumbu

(1) Muchibeela eechi— “mali yabulumbu” zibikkilizya fumbwa mali yafulumende naakuti iibambidwe afulumende naakuti fumbwa kabunga /mbunga

yafulumende, kubikkilizya zijanika muzilikiti, mumabbooma amutubunga twafulumende; “mbono zyabulumbu” zyaamba fumbwa lubono lwafulumende naakuti lubambidwe afulumende naakuti fumbwa kabunga /mbunga yafulumende, kubikkilizya zijanika muzilikiti, mumabbooma amutubunga twafulumende;

(2) Mukuli wamubelesi woonse uubelesya mali yabulumbu kuti akwabilile mali yabulumbu antoomwe akubona kuti yabelesegwa kabotu kabiyo yabelesegwa kuzintu nzyiyelede kubelesegwa mali yakazuminanwa.

(3) Mukuli wawoonse muntu uubambinde lubono lwafulumende kukwabilila mbono kuchitila kuti tazisweeki, kujeegwa, kupwachuulwa naakuti kunyonyonwa akutabelesegwa kabotu.

(4) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula mbukunga kulafwambaanwa kubonwa kuti tubeela (2) a (3) twatyolwa kabiyo ntaamo zyakuzyotolola muntu ooyo watyola mulawu zyachitwa, mpukweelede sikusunda ukonzya kutegwa aluboozye lubono lwatakanika.

CHIBAKA 5 MUPATI UULANGA MABELESEZYO AAMBONO AMALI ZYALUUNDU 309 Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/ Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu antoomwe amilimu yakwe

(1) Kweelede kwaba Nabbatule waKulanga Mabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu, ujisi wofesi yabulumbu pesi tali uumwi waBabelesi Baluundu. [Kabeela kakabambululwa aMulawu 2 wa2021]

(2) Milimu yaNabbatule waKulanga Mabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu—

(a) kulangisisya mabelesezyo antoomwe anzila zyakubambwa kwamali muzibeela amitabi yoonse yafulumende, kuzilikiti, mumadolopo mapati amabbooma woonse.

(b) kuti wakumbilwa afulumende, kulangisisya aakabunga nikaba kali kafulumende;

mabelesezyo

amali

(c) kupa malayilile akuti kuzyotololwe bulewusi abubeya mukweendelezegwa, kukwabililwa kwamali ambono zyabulumbu;

(d) kuchita fumbwa aPalyamenti.
