(3) Bantu babelekela bulumbu naakuti luundu beelede kutobeelezya zeezyo zyaambwa aMupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe mukabeela (2)( c). 310

Kunjizigwa mulimu kwaMupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/ Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu

(1) Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu unjizigwa mulimu aMuzulwidi waNyika kazuminana aPalyamenti.

(2) Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu kweelede kali muna Zimbabwe wasalwa nkaambo kabuntususu bwakwe kabiyo kweelede kali muntu ukonzya kuchita mulimu

wazyakulangalanga akulangisisya mabelekelo amabelesezyo amali kwaminyaka isika kkumi naakuti kwiinda.

(3) Chiindi ncheelede kukkala achuuno Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu nchiindi chitayindi minyaka musanu awumwi kabiyo tayelede pe kusalwa kuba Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu kuti kakuli wakali wabeleka mulimu ooyu kale, kakutobelene naakuti muzidyunku zyaminyaka zisika kukkumi ayibili.

(4) Katana kutalika kubeleka mulimu, Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu, weelede kukonka zikonke kunembo lyaMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti umwi muntu wagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika, zyamulimu akulipeda kunyika yaZimbabwe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yatatu. 311 Kulyiimikilila akulisanta kwaMupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu Mukweendelezya milimu yakwe Nabbatule waKulanga Mmabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende Uulanga Mabelesezyo aaMbono aMali zyaluundu l ulilyiimikilide akulisanta, pesi uyelede kutobeelezya mulawu mumabelekelo aakwe. 312 Mufolo waMupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/ Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kupandulula mufolo alumwi lugwasyo ndwayelede kujana Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/ Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu, zyoonse eezi zyeelede kuzuminanwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kwiinda mumizeezo izwa kuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali.

(2) Mali yamufolo waNabbatule waKulanga Mmabelesezyo aMmali yaFulumende Uulanga Mabelesezyo aaMbono aMali zyaluundu iyelede kuzwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende kabiyo tayiyelede kuchesegwa pe kuti kwachiindi choonse nachili aamulimu wakwe. 313 Kugwisigwa mulimu kwaMupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/ Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu

(1) Nabbatule waKulanga Mabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende Uulanga Mabelesezyo aaMbono aMali zyaluundu ukonzya kugwisigwa mulimu wakwe kuti kakuli wa —

(a) wakachilwa kubeleka mulimu wakwe nkaambo kakusondoka naakuti kulemana;

(b) kukachilwa mulimu wakwe zyiindilide; naakuti

(c) kusunda kwiindilide.

(2) Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende uubona zyamali, kabeleka aamwi akkomiti yaPalyamenti ibona zyamali yabulumbu, uwambila Muzulwidi waNyika kuti kuli zyaambo zyakuyanda kugwisigwa Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu amulimu wakwe kabiyo zyeelede kulangisisigwa kabotu. Muzulwidi waNyika uyelede kugala kabunga keelede kulangisisya kabotu mabelekelo aNabbatule waKulanga Mmabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu;

(3) Kabunga kagalwa, kwiinda mukabeela (2), keelede kuba abantu batatu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika, kakuli kuti—

(a) uumwi wabo naakuti bayinda uumwi wakali Jaji; kabiyo

(b) uumwi naakuti uyinda uumwi kubantu batatu naakuti bayinda batatu bagalwa akabunga kali mumulawu kayiminina bantu bachita milimu yakulangisisya mabelesezyo akubambwa kwamali.

(4) Kabunga kaambwa mukabeela (3)(b) keelede kugala bantu banga baambidwe mukabeela aako kuti bakumbilwa kuti bachite boobo aMuzulwidi waNyika.

(5) Kabunga kagalwa kwiinda mukabeela (2) keelede kulangisisya kabotu zyaambo zyakugwisigwa kwaAuditor General/Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu kuzwa amulimu, kuti bachita boobo, kweelede bapa zeezyo nzibajana mubulangisisi bwabo kuMuzulwidi waNyika mpawo akupa muzeezo wakuti agwisigwe naakuti tagwisigi Auditor General/Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu kuzwa achuuno.

(6) Kabunga kagalwa kwiinda mukabeela (2) kalaacheelelo anguzu zyamakkomishini kwiinda muMulawu wakKkomishini yaMfukununo [Chaandano 10:07], naakuti fumbwa mulawu uunjila mubusena bwawooyu uliwo. 314

Babelesi mbabeleka aabo Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu

Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti weelede kupandulula aatala akugalwa kwa Bboodi lin jizya milimu bantu babeleka aMupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu mumilimu yakwe, kabiyo kweelede kwaamba aatala—

(a) anzibeelede kubaazyo bantu aabo;

(b) mifolo alugwasyo ndubeelede kupegwa babelesi aaba ambubeelede kulijata amulimu;

(c) kulyiimikilila akulisanta abuntususu mbubeelede kubaabo bantu aaba; kabiyo

(d) bwiime, mabelekelo mabotu bwa wofesi yaMupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu/Mupati uulanga mabelesezyo aambono amali zyaluundu.

CHIBAKA 6 BWIIME BWACHIBAKA 315 Mawulilo aazintu antoomwe azimwi zizuminano zyafulumende

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kwaamba nzila zyeelede kutobelezegwa mukuyanda kuula zintu zyafulumende amitabi, tubunga ambunga zyafulumende muzibeela zyoonse zyafulumende zijanika mumasena aandene kuchitila kuti mawulilo azintu abe antanganana, kakutakwe lusaluulo pesi kakuli muzundano usungwaazya kuti bantu bawuzye akuula kabotu zintu.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uyelede kwaamba ntaamo zinga zilatolwa mukwaambulilana mukujanwa zizuminano zyakuulwa akuuzigwa kwazintu zyafulumende—

(a) zizuminano zibikkilizya banji;

(b) zizuminano zilaatala akuyakwa kwamayake; kabiyo

(c) zizuminano zyaatala akusyigwa kwazilengwaleza zijanika aansi kwanyika kuchitila kuti kakuli bubalangale, buntususu mukuchita akweendelezya zintu eezi munzila isumpula zyaluzundano. 316 Kweendelezegwa kwatubunga twakabumbwa kwiinda mumulawu Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kweelede wapandulula aatala ambukweelede kweendelezegwa tubunga twakabumbwa kwiinda mumulawu, kapati kweelede kwabonwa kuti basilutwe batubunga ooto bapegwa myeenya yakubelekela tubunga

ootu iyendelana amabelekelo aabo. Kupegwa uumbi mweenya kuti babe basilutwe batubunga ooto kweelede kweendelana akukonzya naakuti kukachilwa kwabo mumweenya mutaanzi ngubakapedwe kukweendelezya tubunga ooto. 317 Bbenki Pati lyaNyika yaZimbabwe

(1) Kuli bbenki pati lyeelede kutegwa ndi Bbenki Pati lyaNyika yaZimbabwe, lilaampalamo zitobela —

(a) kubayila bweendelezegwe amabelesezyo aamali munyika;

(b) kukwabilila mali yanyika akubona mbiinga ilagwasizya kusumpula buvubi bwanyika; kabiyo

(c) kupanga akubona mabelekelo amilawu ibona mabelesezyo aamali.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupandulula zyaatala abwiime amabelekelo aaBbenki Pati lyaNyika yaZimbabwe antoomwe akupa iimbi milimu yalyo bbenkipati eelyo.

CHAANDAANO 18 ZYABIYOBIYO AZIMWI NJILIKIZYO

CHIBAKA 1 ZYABIYOBIYO ZILANGENE AMAKKOMISHINI

318 Mabelesezyo aaChibaka 1 Zili muChibaka eechi zilaatala amaKkomishini akaanzwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli antoomwe abantu bajanika mumaKkomishini aayo. 319 MaKkomishini tube tubunga tulyiimvwilidi atulikke MaKkomishini ntubunga tulyiimvwilidi atulikke tulaabantu banjila antoomwe akuzwa muli ntuto, tukonzya kwaanzigwa akwaanzya muntu muzina lyato. 320 Bajanika mumaKkomishini amabelekelo aabo

(1) Aanze kwawaawo mpukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, boonse banjila muma Kkomishini banjizigwa kuti babeleke kwaminyaka musanu ikonzya kuyungizigwa ayimbi minyaka musanu luzutu.

(2) Bajanika mumaKkomishini, aanze kwa—

(a) maKkomishini alyiimikilide

(b) Kkomishini yaBubetesi

(c) Kkomishini yaNkaka Buboli muZimbabwe; kabiyo

(d) Kkomishini yaNyika yakulima muZimbabwe. bakkala muzyuuno zyamulimu kuti Muzulwidi waNyika kachiyanda kuti babewo nkaambo ukonzya kubagwisya mpaayandila

(3) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, Bayiminini bamuPalyamenti abayiminini bajanika muKkansili yazilikiti amadolopo mapati, mumabbooma amutubunga twafulumende boonse tabakonzyi kusalwa kuti banjile mumaKkomishini.

(4) Aawo Kkomishini mpiijisi sichuuno awutobela sichuuno, bantu aaba kweelede umwi kali mwanakazi umwi kali mwaalumi.

(5) Kabatana talika pe kubeleka, bantu banjila mumaKkomishini beelede kukonka zikonke kunembo lyaMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti umwi muntu wagalwa aMuzulwidi

waNyika. Zikonke zyabo chimwi nchamulimu antoomwe achakulemeka nyika mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muMpampikwa Yatatu.

(6) Bantu bajanika mumaKkomishini beelede kujana mufolo, alumwi lugwasyo lwaamulimu lupegwa babelesi mbuuli mbukutanoowambwe chiindi achiindi muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kabiyo mifolo yabo tiiyelede kuselusigwa kuti kabachili aamulimu.

(7) Mifolo alumwi lugwasyo lupegwa babelesi aamulimu zipegwa bantu banjila mumaKkomishini zizwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende. 321 Milimu ameendelezezyo amaKkomishini

(1) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupa iimbi milimu kumaKkomishini kabiyo ukonzya kupa malayilile ambaayelede kubeleka makkomishini fumbwa kulyiimikilila akulisanta kwawo kwalemekwa.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti waambwa mukabeela (1) ukonzya kuzumizya Kkomishini kuti ipe umwi muntu naakuti kabunga nguzu zyakuchita milimu yayo, pesi Kkomishini tiikonzyi kupa manguzu aayo uumbi muntu naakuti kambi kabunga mukuli wayo wakunjizya bantu milimu babelesi bayo naakuti kupa mizeezo aatala akamwi kaambo.

(3) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, fumbwa chizuminano chachitwa aKkomishini cheelede kuti chazuminanwa amaKkomishina banji baliwo nikuchitwa chizuminano eecho.

(4) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kwaamba aatala anzila zinga zilatobelwa aKkomishini mukuchita umwi mulimu wayo, pesi aawo aatakwe nzila. zyaambidwe Kkomishini ikonzya kupanga nzila zyayo. Nikuba boobo, zyoonse nzila zyakuchita mulimu wayo nziyapanga Kkomishini zyeelede kubalangala mumachitilo, kazitakwe lusaluulo mukubeleka kwaKkomishini 322 Kupegwa mali kwaKkomishini Palyamenti iyelede kubona kuti mali iizulide yapegwa kumaKkomishini nkukuti akonzye kweendelezya milimu yawo mbuuli mbukweelede. 323 MaKkomishini ayelede kupa lipoti kuPalyamenti lumwi amunyaka

(1) Woonse maKkomishini kweelede apa Palyamenti lipoti lumwi amunyaka, kwiinda muli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ngubabeleka aawe. Lipoti eeyi iyelede kusika kuPalyamenti kutana mana mweezi waMiyoba wamunyaka uli nembo kwamunyaka walipoti eeyo.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kwaamba kuti Kkomishini ilembe aambi malipoti aajulu kwayeeyo ngambo njiiyelede kulemba kumamanino kwamunyaka mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (1), kabiyo mulawu ooyo ukonzya kwaamba bwiime bwamalipoti aalembwa akati kamwaaka.

CHIBAKA 2 ZYABIYOBIYO 324 Kubelekwa chalusinizyo kwamilimu yaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu Yoonse milimu amikuli yaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli iyelede kubelekwa munzila izikitide achakufwambaana. 325 Kupegwa kwamali kwatubunga twakalengwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu antoomwe atumwi tubunga

(1) Fulumende kweelede wabona kuti kuli mali iizulide ipegwa —

(a) kuma Kkomishini antoomwe atumwi tubunga twakalengwa aKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kuchitila kuti tukonzye kubeleka milimu yato zizikitide;

(b) kuPalyamenti , kuchitila kuti lwayo antoomwe amakkomiti ikonzye kuswaanana fumbwa chili chiindi mpukwayandikana kuti iswaanane; kabiyo

(c) kutubunga toonse twafulumende, kuchitila kuti tukonzye kuchita milimu amikuli yato yaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

(2) MaKkomishini antoomwe atumwi tubunga twakapangwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kweelede twapegwa mweenya wakuyooma aKkomiti yaPalyamenti aatala amali njikayanda kamwi akamwi kabunga mumwaaka amwaaka. 326 Mulawu wachintu wamanyika aandeene

(1) Mulawu wachintu wamanyika aandeene ubumba chipanzi chamulawu wamuZimbabwe, aanze kwakuti kawudinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu naakuti Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(2) Kuti kakupandululwa mulawu, nkuta zyoonse atubunga twabumbwa twalubeta kweelede twatola nzila ili mbubombubo mukupandulula mulawu kakweendelana amulawu wachintu wamanyika aandeene uyendelana amulawu wamuZimbabwe, kakulangwa akutola bbazu zimwi zinga zilalangisisigwa mukupandululwa kwakutayendelana kwamulawu ooyo. 327 Zizuminano zyamanyika aandeene

(1) Muchibeela eechi — “Kabunga kamanyika aandeene” chaamba kabunga kajisi manyika aabili naakuti aayinda aabili aamanyika alilelede naakuti kabunga mujanika bantu bayimvwilidi manyika akalilela aabili naakuti aayinda aabili; “Chizuminano Chamanyika aandeene” chaamba fumbwa zizuminano zisiyenesiyene zyakachitwa aakati kamanyika naakuti bafulumende bamanyika aandeene naakuti atubunga twamanyika aandeene.

(2) Chizuminano chaManyika aandeene chakachitwa aMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti amuntu wakapedwe mvumo aMuzulwidi waNyika —

(a) tachaangilili nyika yaZimbabwe pe domane chazuminwa aPalyamenti; kabiyo

(b) tachibi chipanzi chamulawu wamuZimbabwe pe domane chanjizigwa mumulawu waZimbabwe kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti.

(3) Aanze aazizuminano zyakuboleka mali alusinizyo lwaambuulwa aatala andulo kuzibela 300(3) a (4) chizuminano chitali chizuminano chamanyika aandeene pele —

(a) kachili chazuminanwa kale naakuti kachili chachitwa aMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti munsi amanguzi aMuzulwidi waNyika akabunga kamanyika amwi yomwe naakuti zyyinda alimwi zipa mukuli wakuti chisi chaZimbabwe chiliye mali; alimwi

(b) chipa mukuli kuchisi chaZimbabwe kuti chiliye imali tachaangili Zimbabwe kunze kwakuti kwazumiinanwa aPalyamenti. [Kabeela kabambululwa achibeela 21 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(4) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kwaambwa kuti zyaambidwe mutubeela

(2) a (3)—

(a) tazichitiki pe kuchizuminano chamanyika aandeene wamusyobo nikuba uuli;

(b) zichitike kakuli kwaba kubambululwa kwamalembe kuli chimwi chizuminano chamanyika aandeene nikuba chili chizuminano chakachitwa.

(5) Palyamenti ikonzya, kwiinda muchizuminano, kwaamba kuti fumbwa chizuminano chamanyika aandeene tachiyandi kuzuminwa aanjiyo pe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (2), pele chizuminano eecho tachijatikizyi zizuminano ziyanda kuti—

(a) kugwisigwe mali kuzwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende; naakuti

(b) kubambululwe fumbwa mulawu wamu Zimbabwe.

(6) Kuti kakupandululwa mulawu, nkuta zyoonse antoomwe atubunga twakabumbilwa kubeteka kweelede twatola bupanduluzi bwamulawu bweendelena achizuminano chamanyika aandeene chaangilila nyika yaZimbabwe, kakulangwa akutola bbazu zimwi zinga zilalangisisigwa mukupandulula kweendelana kwachizuminano eecho. 328 Kubambululwa kwaKkata lyaMulawu

(1) Muchibeela eechi — “Malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu” chaamba malembe aalatala akubambululwa kwachipanzi chaKkata lyaMulawu eeli; “term-limit provision” chaamba malembe aali muKkata lyaMulawu atondeezya kufwiinsya kwachiindi muntu nchayelede kukkala muchuuno eecho chilookwaambwa.

(2) Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ubambulula Kkata lyaMulawu eeli uyelede kuchita mulimu ooyo munzila ifwambaana.

(3) Malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu taakonzyi kuyoomwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamulawu amuNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 131 adomane Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu wapa chizibisyo chamazuba ali makumi musanu amakuni aane mupepa lyaGazette aatala aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu.

(4) Mbatape chizibisyo luzutu Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu aatala aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (3), Palyamenti kweelede yatamba bulumbu kuti buzoope mizeezo yabo aatala azilookuyanda kubambululwa , kwiinda mumiswaangano yabulumbu, kwiinda mumalembe kabiyo miswaangano iyelede yachitwa aPalyamenti kuchitila kupa bulumbu mweenya wakwaamba nzibuyanda bulumbu aatala amasandu ayanda kuchitwa.

(5) Malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu ayelede kuzuminwa, buunsi naabalwa kwakumaninsizya muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu amuNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu, abantu basika kuntompa-chaala yabantu bajanika muliimwi ayimwi ng’anda yamilawu.

(6) Aawo malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu mpaayanda kubambulula zilembedwe muChaandaano 4 naakuti Chaandaano 16—

(a) mukati kamyeezi yotatwe kaali azuminwa, aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu antoomwe aNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu, malembe masandu alookubambululwa mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mukabeela (5), ayelede kutolwa kulefelendamu yanyika yoonse.

(b) kuti azuminanwa masandu aayo abantu banji kubantu batanooli bavoota boonse mulefelendamu, Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, uyelede kutola malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu kuMuzulwidi waNyika, ooyo uyelede kuzumina akusayina mpawaawo.

(7) Katutalangi zimwi zyaambidwe muchibeela eechi, kuti katulanga kubambululwa kwachiindi cheelede kukkalwa amuntu muchuuno chaamulimu mwabede (term limit provision), kuti kakuli kubambululwa ooko nkwakulansya bulamfu bwachiindi cheelede

kukkalwa amuntu muchuuno chakwe aamulimu, zibambululwa eezyo tazimujatijati pe muntu wajanika kali muchuuno kale naachitwa mabambe akubambulula mulawu.

(8) Tubeela (6) a (7) tatweelede kubambululwa chiindi chomwe pe mumalembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu naakuti tatweelede kubikkwa kunembo lyabulumbu pe kuti kuchitwe lefelendamu muchiindi chomwe.

(9) Chibeela eechi chikonzya kubambululwa luzutu kuti kwatobelezegwa nzila zyaambidwe mutubeela (3), (4), (5) a(6) mbuuli kuti chibeela eechi chijanika muChaandaano 4.

(10) Kuti malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu apegwa Muzulwidi waNyika kuti awazumine akwasayina, ayelede kwiinka a—

(a) setifiketi izwa kuli Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, itondeezya kuti nikwakali kuvootwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu akazuminanwa abantu basika kuntompa-chaala yabantu bajanika muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu; kabiyo

(b) setifiketi izwa kuMuzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu, itondeezya kuti nikwakali kuvootwa muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu, malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu akazuminanwa abantu basika kuntompa-chaala yabantu bajanika muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu. 329 Kutaanguna kubelesegwa kwaKkata lyaMulawu azitobelwa mukuzubuka Mpampikwa Yamusanu Akamwi ilaatala akutaanguna kubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kusweegwa kwaKkata lyaMulawu Lyansiku abweendelezegwe bwakutaanguna kubelesya Kkata lyaMulawu Pya eeli.

CHIBAKA 3 BUPANDULUZI 330 Mbelesezyo yaChibaka 3 Chibaka eechi chibeleka mukupandulula Kkata lyaMulawu eeli aanze kwawaawo malembelo mpaawambide zisiyene. 331 Ndemeko zyabiyobiyo zyabupanduluzi bwaKkata lyaMulawu Chibeela 46 chilabeleka, aamwi akubambululwa kunga mukupandulula Kkata lyaMulawu eeli aanze kwaChaandaano 4.

kulachitwa,

332 Bupanduluzi MuKkata lyaMulawu eeli “ Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti” chaamba—

(a) malembe amulawu azuminanwa aPalyamenti mpawo azuminwa akusayinwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kuti ube mulawu kwiinda muli zyaambwa aKkata lyaMulawu eeli; naakuti

(b) malembe amulawu aakaliwo kale muZimbabwe kaali Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti buzuba nilyakatalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, kubikkilizya—

(i) malembe amilawu yakabambululwa muZimbabwe mu1996 aansi kwaMulawu waStatute Law Compilation and Revision [Chaandano 1:03]; kabiyo

(ii) malembe amilawu, akatabambululwa nikwakabambululwa imwi milawu yaambwa mufuka (i), pesi yakali kubeleka kale kiili milawu; kabiyo

(iii) malembe akazuminanwa aPalyamenti yaZimbabwe nikwakamana kubambululwa kwamilawu yaambidwe mufuka

(i) pesi kalitana talika pe kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli; “nchito yabweendelezi” chaamba nchito zyachitwa mukweendelezya mulimu, kukachilwa kweendelezya mulimu naakuti kukaka kweendelezya mulimu kwamubelesi wafulumende. “kubambulula” kubikkilizya kusandula bwiime, kusandula bube, kuyungizya, akuchitola mbuchibede; “nsaluuzyo yachitwa kuzuzikizya chimanga” chaamba nsaluuzyo zyachitwa kuti kubikkwe uumbi mwiiminini wabusena nkaambo wakaliwo wakazwa aachuuno; “ Mwaami” chaamba mwaami mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muChaandaano 15; “Babelesi Baluundu” … [Bupanduluzi bwabambululwa achibeela 22 (a) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

“Kkomishini” chaamba kkomishini yabumbwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli; “Kkomiti yaNzila Zyakweendelezya muPalyamenti” chaamba kkomiti yazina eeli yabumbwa aansi kwaChibeela 151; “Nyika yaluundu” chaamba nyika yakabikkwa kubbazu kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kiiyendelezegwa kwiinda mumiteto atunsiyansiya twachiindi kabiyo kiibelesegwa abantu bazulwidwa aMwaami. “Malembe aZiyanda kubambululwa muKkata lyaMulawu” chaamba malembe amulawu ayanda kubambulula zili muKkata lyaMulawu eeli. “Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu” chaamba Nkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu yakaanzwa kwiinda muchibeela 162(a); “Makani alaachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu” chaamba makani alaachakuchita aatala akupandululwa kwaKkata lyaMulawu naakuti kukwabililwa kwazili muKkata lyaMulawu; “Mulawu watunsiyansiya” chaamba mulawu watunsiyansiya wafumbwa nikuba uuli musyobo wabantu bajanika muZimbabwe; “mulawu wakulijata” chaamba mulawu ugwasizya basilumamba kuti balijate kabotu aamulimu wabo. Mulawu ooyu ubikkilizya baabo banga bagwasizya basilumamba kwachiindi chifwiifwi mumulimu wabusilumamba; “basikukwabilila nyika” chaamba—

(a) basilumamba bamumaanzi, basilumamba naakuti balwanina kujulu;

(b) bakapokola;

(c) aabo babamba baange; naakuti

(d) fumbwa chipanzi chalengwa kugwasizya luundu kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kabiyo choompololwa amulawu ooyo kuti mbaasikukwabilila nyika;

“buzuba bwakutalika kubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu” chaamba buzuba nilitalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli lyoonse kwiinda mufuka 3(2) lyaMpampikwa Yamusanu Akamwi; “Mulawu waNsaluuzyo” chaamba Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ugwasizya kweendelezegwa kwansaluuzyo mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli; “Munyaka wamabelesezyo amali” chaamba myeezi kkumi ayibili kusika kuli Nalupale 31; “milimu” zibikkikizya nguzu, mibeleko azimwi zichitwa; “Gazette” chaamba pepatwaambo lyakuzibisya luundu libelesegwa afulumende; “Nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi banji” chaamba nsaluuzyo zyakusala bazulwidi banji bazibeela zyoonse zyachisi bali mbuuli—

(a) Muzulwidi waNyika, Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Bayiminini bamuNg’anda yaPalyamenti;

(b) maKkansela bamumabbooma; “Fulumende” kwambwa Fulumente weZimbabwe; “fulumende” chaamba fulumende waZimbabwe “Kabunga kalaansi kwafulumende” chaamba kabunga kalyiimikile kajisi milimu yafulumende kabiyo kakalaansi kwafulumende naakuti muntu uyiminina fulumende. “Ng’anda” aanze kwawaawo mpukwaambidwe chaamba Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu naakuti Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu; “Kkomishini Ilyiimikilide” chaamba kkomishini mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muchibeela 232; “Jaji” chaamba Jaji waNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu, Nkuta Yaajulujulu, Nkuta Yaajulu, Nkuta Yazyambelekelano aNkuta Yanjendelezyo; “Kkomishini yaBubetesi” chaamba Kkomishini yabubetesi yakaanzwa kwiinda muchibeela 189; “Mulawu” chaamba—

(a) nichiba chili chipanzi chaKkata lyaMulawu eeli naakuti Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti;

(b) nikuba chili chipanzi chamulawu watulawulawu (statutory instrument);

(c) fumbwa mulawu utalembedwe pesi uubeleka muZimbabwe kubikkilizya mulawu watunsiyansiya kabiyo zili mumulawu zyeelede kumvwika akuzibikana mbuboobo; “Simulawu” chaamba muntu ulaamvumo yakubeleka mbuuli simulawu munyika yaZimbabwe; ‘Bbooma” chaamba Kkansili mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muChibaka 3 chaChaandaano 14; “umwi wabo” kakwaambwa—

(a) muKkomishini naakuti mbunga yakaanzwa aKkata lyaMulawu eeli kazibikkikizya Sichuuno awutobela Sichuuno muKkomishini;

(b) kabunga kalyiimikilide mumulawu, kkansili yachilikiti, yadolopo pati naakuti umwi muntu wagalwa kuti abe umwi wakabunga—

(i) kabunga kalyiimikilide mumulawu, kkansili yachilikiti, yadolopo pati naakuti yabbooma;

(ii) ukonzya kweendelezya kabunga kalyiimikilide mumulawu kkansili yachilikiti, yadolopo pati naakuti yabbooma; ““Mwiiminini wamuPalyamenti” chaamba Seneta naakuti Mwiiminini wamuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

“Kkansili yaDolopo pati” chaamba Kkansili yakaanzwa kwiinda muChibeela 269 yadolopo pati libumba chilikiti alilikke mbuuli Bulawayo naakuti Harare; “Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende” chaamba muntu uzulwida mutabi wafulumende; “Mulawu wachisi” chaamba Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti naakuti atulawulawu; “chikonke” chibikkikizya nzumino; “mulandu” chaamba mulandu/criminal offence; “chiindi chalupyopyongano” chaamba aawo nikoompololwa chiindi chalupyopyongano kwiinda muChibeela 113; “muntu” chaamba muntu lwakwe naakuti mubili wamuntu, muntu uli muchiimo chakabunga; “Muzulwidi waNyika” chaamba Muzulwidi waNyika yaZimbabwe; “Muzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu” chaamba Muzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu kwiinda muchibeela 122; “Kkansili yachilikiti” chaamba kkansili yakaanzwa kwiinda muChibeela 268 yachilikiti chitali chadolopo pati; “Wofesi yabulumbu” chaamba wofesi ilaamuntu ubbadalwa afulumende; “Mubelesi waLuundu” chaamba bupanduluzi bupedwe achibeela 199; [Bupanduluzi bwabikwa achibeela 22 (b) chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

“Bazyakukwabilwa kwanyika” chaamba baabo babeleka mukukwabilila nyika kwiinda muChibeela 207; “‘Mwami kali Seneta” chaamba mwaami uyimikilila bamwi baami muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu; “Kuswaanana muNg’anda Yamilawu’ chaamba kuswaanana kwabayiminizi bamasena mumaanda aamilawu yaansi ayaajuulu; “Buunsi bwakuswaanana” chaamba buunsi nikuba buli bwamvwiki bayiminini bamumaanda amilawu nibaswaanana mumaanda amilawu obile naakuti yomwe; “Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu” chaamba Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu; “Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti” kuti kazyaambwa aazimwi ziyanda kuchitwa zyaamba —

(a) Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu zyaamba Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti;

(b) Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu zyaamba Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti zyamuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(c) maanda obile kasweenene yaajuulu ayaansi zyaamba Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti zyakachitwa kwiinda muChibeela 139; “Kabunga kalyiimikilide mumulawu” chaamba—

(a) kkomishini yakaanzwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli; naakuti

(b) kabunga kali mumulawu kakaanzwa aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti keendelezegwa abantu bagalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika, Uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waFulumende waNyika Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende, naakuti kkomishini yakaanzwa aKkata lyaMulawu eeli. “Mpandamayake yaMulawu” chaamba tulawulawu twakaliwo atuchiliwo atuchibumbwa aMuzulwidi waNyika; Uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende; “Muzulwidi wabulongo” chaamba muntu wakagalwa kwiinda muChibeela 283; “Zimbabwe” chaamba nyika yakalilela yaZimbabwe;

“Kkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe” chaamba Kkomishini mbuuli mbuyakaanzwa muChibeela 238; “Kkomishini yaZyeelelo zyaBantu muZimbabwe” chaamba Kkomishini mbuuli mbuyakaanzwa muChibeela 242. 333 Aawo mpukwadomwa zyaandaano, zibeela, azimwi MuKkata lyaMulawu eeli, aawo mpukwaadomwa kakutaambidwe zimwi, kakwaambwa—

(a) Chaandaano, Chibeela naakuti Mpampikwa, aawo kweelede kwazibikana kuti kwaambwa Chaandaano, Chibeela naakuti Mpampikwa zyaKkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(b) kabeela, aawo kweelede kwazibikana kuti kwaambwa kabeela kachibeela chilokudomwa;

(c) fuka, aawo kweelede kwazibikana kuti kwaambwa fuka lya Mpampikwa, chibeela, kabeela naakuti mbuuli mbukupandulwidwe;

(d) mwaneefuka aawo kweelede kwazibikana kuti kwaambwa mwaneefuka wafuka naakuti mwaneefuka wadomwa. 334 Mabala aali mubuche awamba bwiingi kabiyo aali mubwiingi awamba mubuche MuKkata lyaMulawu eeli, mabala aali mubuche awamba bwiingi kabiyo aali mubwiingi awamba buche. 335 Malembe aazili mukati antoomwe amitwe yamakani Malembe akumataangunino atondeezya Zili Mukati antoomwe amitwe yamakani yazyaandaano, zibaka, zibeela azimwi zilembedwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli teensi nziizimwi zyaKkata lyaMulawu eeli pesi zibikkilidwe kuti kuube kubelesya Kkata lyaMulawu eeli luzutu. 336 Kudomwa kwachiindi

(1) MuKkata lyaMulawu eeli, aawo mpukwaambwa chiindi chibikkidwe mumazuba—

(a) mpukutegwa chiindi chitalika buunsi buteedenene, oobo buunsi tabweelede kubalizigwa pe mukubala mazuba aawambwa;

(b) mpukutegwa chiindi chimana buunsi buteedenene, oobo buunsi bweelede kubikkilizigwa mukubala mazuba aawambwa;

(2) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli aawo chiindi chakuchita mulimu wategwa uchitwe kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeeli, kuti chiindi eecho kachimana muMugibelo, Sondo naakuti mubuzuba bwakulyookezya, chiindi chakuchita mulimu ooyo ngachalomponoka kuyoosika mubuzuba butobela buteensi Mugibelo, Nsondo abuunsi bwakulyookezya.

(3) MuKkata lyaMulawu eeli aawo mpukwaambwa mweezi, kunooli kwaamba mweezi wamazuba ngituzi wakkalenda kabiyo chiindi chategwa chitola myeezi yongane kubalwa kwamyeezi eeyo kweelede kutalika buunsi nikwaambwa kuyoosika zuba naazulila mazuba apanga mweezi/myeezi yaambwa.

(4) MuKkata lyaMulawu eeli aawo mpukwaambwa munyaka, kunooli kwaamba munyaka ulamyeezi kkumi ayibili.

337 Kudomwa kwababelesi bamumawofesi Woonse aawo Kkata lyaMulawu mpulyaamba muntu ubeleka muwofesi abulamfu mbwayelede kukkala muwofesi eeyo, eezi zyaamba boonse bantu banga badomwa kuti bajane bujatilila kuchita milimu yamuwofesi eeyo. 338 Kudomwa kwaPalyamenti Woonse aawo Kkata lyaMulawu mpuliyanda ngambo/lipoti naakuti fumbwa malembe kuti atolwe kuPalyamenti, lipoti eeyo naakuti malembe aayo anooli ayeledee kupegwa ng’anda zyobile zyamilawu (yaansi ayakujulu). 339 Kuyanduula akujana busongo

(1) Woonse aawo Kkata lyaMulawu mpuliyanda fumbwa nabani muntu kuti ayanduule lugwasyo lwamizeezo kuzwa kuli bamwi bantu/tubunga, muntu /mubelesi ooyo weelede—

(a) kwaambila umwi wakwe, kwiinda mumalembe, eezyo nzyayanda kuchita kabiyo ape mweenzinyina luzibo loonse ndwanga ulayanda kuchitila kuti mweenzinyina amvwisisizye eezyo ziyanda kuchitwa; kabiyo

(b) kupa mweenzinyina chiindi chizulide chakuti ape mizeezo amaboneno aakwe; kabiyo ooyo sikupegwa mizeezo uyelede kwiitobelezya mizeezo njaapegwa awumwi wakwe.

(2) Woonse aawo Kkata lyaMulawu mpuliyanda muntu /kabunga kuti kakumbile busongo kuzwa kuluumwi muntu/kabunga, ooyo sikuyanduula busongo uyelede —

(a) kwaambila umwi wakwe, kwiinda mumalembe, eezyo nzyayanda kuchita kabiyo ape mweenzinyina luzibo loonse ndwanga ulayanda kuchitila kuti mweenzinyina amvwisisizye eezyo ziyanda kuchitwa; kabiyo

(b) kupa mweenzinyina chiindi chizulide chakuti ape mizeezo amaboneno aakwe aatala azilokwaambwa; kabiyo;

(c) alangisisyse kabotu mizeezo abusongo mbwaapegwa awumwi wakwe aatala aziyanda kuchitwa; pesi muntu/kabunga kayanda busongo takaangilwi pe kutola mizeezo/busongo bwazwa kumuntu wali kukumbilwa busongo. 340 Kugalwa kwabantu kuti banjile muzyuuno zyamilimu

(1) Aanze kwawaawo mpukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu ooyu, manguzu ngapedwe muntu kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, aakugala bantu kuti banjile muzyuuno zyamilimu zibikkilizya manguzu aakozyania aaku —

(a) gala lubo muntu ooyo kuti ayendelele nembo kachibelekela muchuuno eecho;

(b) kugala muntu antoomwe akumusumpula kuzwa achuuno eecho naakuti kumugwisya kumutola kuli chimbi chuuno;

(c) kugala muntu kuti ajane bujatila umwi muchuuno eecho;

(d) kugala muntu kuti abeleke muchuuno eecho kuti mweni chuuno wajana busosolosegwa kuti kulangwelangwe mabelekelo aakwe;

(e) kudondomeka akusiyanisya kuzwa chiindi achiindi mufolo alumwi lugwasyo lupegwa babelesi nalaamulimu anaasiya mulimu;

(f) kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kugwisya muntu achuuno chakwe naakuti kujana bumwiimikizya mulimu.

(2) Aawo bantu babili naakuti biinda babili mpubakagalwa kuti babeleke mulimu womwe nkaambo kakuti umwi naakuti bamwi bakayimikizigwa mulimu, muntu wasyaalila kubeleka muchuuno eecho nguwe mweni chuuno.

(3) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, Mulawu upangwa aPalyamenti ukonzya kupandulula manjizizyo abantu babeleka kabatobela umwi muntu kabiyo mulawu ooyo uyelede kwaamba aatala amufolo an1t5o7omwe alumwi lugwasyo lupegwa babelesi balaamulimu.

(4) Aawo mpukwagalwa muntu kuti abeleke kali mutobeli wawumwi muntu, ooyo ubeleka kali mutobeli wawumwi muntu ukonzya kuchita milimu yoonse njachita ngwatobela aawo mupati wakwe mpatachikonzyi kuchita mulimu naakuti mpaamukumbila kuti achite mulimu.

(5) Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli likonzya kufwiinsya chiindi nchanga ulabeleka muntu mumulimu, muntu utanooli wasiya chuuno chakabikkwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kuti kachikonzya ulabona wagalwa lubo kuti anjile muchuuno chakwe. 341 Kusiya mulimu

(1) Fumbwa muntu wagalwa kuti anjile mulimu muchuuno chakaanzwa aKkata lyaMulawu eeli ukonzya kusiya mulimu kwiinda mukulemba chizibisyo chiya kumuntu wakamugala kuti anjile mulimu ooyo naakuti kuli baabo bakamusala kuti anjile muchuuno eecho—

(a) kuMuzulwidi waNyika chizibisyo chakwe chakusiya mulimu uchilanzya kuli Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(b) Muzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu naakuti Umutobela chizibisyo chakwe chakusiya mulimu uchilanzya kuMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti naakuti kwaambila Bayiminini bamuNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu;

(c) Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu naakuti Umutobela chizibisyo chakwe chakusiya mulimu uchilanzya kuMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti naakuti kwaambila Bayiminini bamuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(d) Seneta chizibisyo chakwe chakusiya mulimu uchilanzya kuMuzulwidi waNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu;

(e) Mwiiminini wamuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu chizibisyo chakwe chakusiya mulimu uchilanzya kuli Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(f) mwiiminini wakkansili yachilikiti chamumyuunzi naa wadolopo pati, mulumbe wakuzibisya ulanzya kuli sichuuno wakkansili; [Fuka ligwisigwa achibeela 23 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(g) … [Fuka lisowegwa achibeela 23 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(h) Umwi wakkansili yabbooma chizibisyo chakwe chakusiya mulimu uchilanzya kuMupati waKkansili.

(2) Kusiya mulimu kwamuntu kuzwa muchuuno chakalengwa aKkata lyaMulawu eeli kutalika buunsi bwaambidwe mumalembe akusiya mulimu kwakwe pesi aawo buzuba achiindi mpuzitaambidwe, kusiya mulimu kwakwe kutalika aawo naatambulwa malembe aakwe sikusiya mulimu.

342 Kuchita milimu, azimwi

(1) Nguzu zyakuchita mulimu zipedwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli zyeelede kubelesegwa aawo mpuzyeelede kubelesegwa.

(2) Toonse tubunga twakalengwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli tujisi nguzu zyeelede kuti tuchite milimu yato mbuuli mbukweelede.

(3) Aawo manguzu mpaapedwe aKkata lyaMulawu eeli kuti muntu achite mulimu ngwaapegwa, zyoonse zyeelede kubawo kuti kubelekeke nga azilazyo muzili mumanguzu ngapedwe. 343 Aawo muntu mpategwa tachibeleki Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, bantu tabayelede kutegwa tabachibeleki aawo nibanga bachita chakutambula ndyabukkede (mpencheni) iyendelana amulimu ngubakali kuchita. 344 Bwiingi bwabantu bweenzya muswaangano azimanga zijanika mutubungabunga twakalengwa aKkata lyaMulawu

(1) Fumbwa kabunga kakalengwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli kakonzya kuchita milimu yako nikuba kuti bantu bakabunga aako tabazulide fumbwa aabo baliwo bakonzya kweenzya muswaangano zili mumulawu.

(2) Aanze kwakuti Kkata lyaMulawu eeli naakuti imwi milawu iibona mabelekelo aakabunga aako yaamba zisiyene, pesi kwaambidwe kuti fumbwa kakuli bwiingi bwabantu busika chisela chabantu bajanika mukabunga aako, kakaanzwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu, bantu baliwo bakonzya kweenzya muswaangano mbuuli mbukuyandikana.

(3) Fumbwa mpukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli aatala amavooti aa—

(a) chisela chabwiingi bwabantu pesi kabunga koonse kakajisi bwiingi busika kumweelo unjilwa amweelo wabili.

(b) ntompachaala pesi kabunga koonse kakajisi bwiingi bwabantu busika kumweelwe unjilwa a3.

(c) buvule bwamavooti asika kuntompa chaala yabantu bajanika mukabunga kumweelew uutanjilwi a 4; zyeelede kupandululwa kazyaamba kuti bwiingi bwamavooti taayelede kuba aansi kwamyeelwe iyinda chisela , ntompachaala naakuti three quarters.

(4) Fumbwa kwaamba bwiingi bwabayiminini bamung’anda zyamilawu (Palyamenti) zyaamba bwiingi bwabayiminini boonse bajanika muPalyamenti kwachiindi eecho. 345 Kuteendelana kwazyaambwa aamabala muKkata lyaMulawu Kuti kwaba kuteendelana kwazyaambwa aamabala aKkata lyaMulawu, eezyo zyaambwa aamabala achikuwa nzizyo zyeelede kutobelezegwa.

MPAMPIKWA NTAANZI (Chibeela 4) NDEMBELA YACHISI, LWIIMBO LWACHISI, PANDE LYACHISI A CHIDINDO CHACHISI

CHIBAKA 1 NDEMBELA YACHISI

CHIBAKA 2 LWIIMBO LWACHISI 1. Amunyampule Ndembela Yesu yaZimbabwe Yakazyalwa aNkondo yaLwaangunuko Abulowa bunji bwabasilumamba Tuyitabilile kuli basinkondoma Ngayilongezegwe nyika yaZimbabwe. 2. Amulange Zimbabwe Yo iidobeekedwe Amalundu Amilonga iiyebeka Ngayiwe Mvula ngayiwe zilimwa zivule Babeleki Bajane, Abantu Bakkute Ngayilongezegwe Nyika yaZimbabwe. 3. Mwami longezya nyika Yesu yaZimbabwe Nyika yabaMataata besu Iswe Toonse Kuzwa kuli Zambezi kuya kuli Limpopo Bazulwidi Babe chitondeezyo Ngayilongezegwe Nyika yaZimbabwe.

CHIBAKA 2 LWIIMBO LWACHISI
