CHIBAKA 4 CHIDINDO CHACHISI

MPAMPIKWA YABILI (Chibeela 87) KUTYOLWA KWAZYEELELO MUCHIINDI CHAMPYOPYONGANO Bupanduluzi muMpampikwa Ntaanzi 1. MuMpampikwa eeyi — “mwaange” chaamba muntu waangwa muchiindi champyopyongano kwiinda mumulawu uubelesegwa kwaanga bantumuchiindi champyopyongano; “mulawu wachiindi champyopyongano” chaamba mulawu ulaatala azyeelede kuchitwa muchiindi chalupyopyongano; “zyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka naakuti ngwangunuko” chaamba zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaambidwe muChibaka Chabili muChaandaano 4; “Kabunga kakulangalanga” chaamba kabunga kaambidwe mufuka 3(1). Mbuzinga zilaleka kuzuzikizigwa zyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka 2. (1) Mulawu wachiindi champyopyongano ukonzya kuzumizya kuleka kuzuzikizigwa kwazyeelelo angwangunuko zyaampandamunsaka pesi kakutobelezegwa akusimatizya zyaambidwe muchibeela 87.

(2) Kuti koompololwa chiindi champyopyongano kwiinda muchibeela 113 kuchipanzi chimwi chanyika yaZimbabwe, kuleka kuzuzikizigwa kwazyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka antoomwe angwangunuko takweelede pe kuchitwa munyika yoonse yaZimbabwe, ikapati kwatawompololwa lupyopyongano. Kabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano 3. (1) Mulawu wamuchiindi champyopyongano uuzumizya kwaangwa kwabantu muchiindi eecho kweelede wapandulula kubumbwa kwakabunga katanoolangisisye milandu yabaange baangwa muchiindi eecho.

(2) Kabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano keelede kugalwa aMuzulwidi waNyika kapegwa busongo aKkomishini yaBubetesi antoomwe akumvwa maboneno aKkomiti ibona Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti.

(3) Kabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano keelede kubumbwa aabantu batobela —

(a) sichuuno unooli nguJaji naakuti wakali Jaji; kabiyo

(b) abambi babili bantu, kakuli umwi wabo —

(i) nguJaji naakuti wakali Jaji naakuti ukonzya kuchita mulimu wabuJaji;

(ii) wakali waba Mejasitiliti muZimbabwe kwaminyaka ili musanu ayibili naakuti iyinda waawo; naakuti

(iii) wakabeleka zyabusimulawu kwaminyaka ili musanu ayibili naakuti iyinda waawo muZimbabwe. Zyeelelo zyabaange 4. (1) Boonse baange bajalilidwe —

(a) beelede kwaambilwa, chakufwambaana mukati kamazuba musanu awabili, eecho chapa kuti baangwe akujalilwa;

(b) beelede kuzumizigwa achakufwambaana —

(i) kababelesya mali yabo kuti bazuwe mukavwindi asimulawu/loya wabo ngubalisalila; naakuti

(ii) kuzuwa mukavwindi asimulawu ngubapegwa afulumende kabiyo simulawu ooyo kabbadalwa afulumende; kabiyo beelede kwaambilwa achakufwambaana zyeelelo zyabo eezi zyaambidwe mufuka eeli; kabiyo

(c) mubwaange bwabo kweelede bajatwa mululemeko kabiyo kazitondeezya kuti mbantu.

(2) Aawo fuka mpuliyanda kuti baange bapegwe luzibo oolu —

(a) luzibo oolo lweelede kupegwa kubaange mumulaka ngubamvwisisisya; kabiyo 130

(b) kuti mwaange katakonzyi kubala akulemba , fumbwa malembe ngaapegwa ayelede kupandululwa kabotu kulinguwe kuchitila kuti amvwisisisye zyaambwa. Kulangwalangwa kwamilandu yabaange 5.(1) Umwi awumwi mulandu wabaange bajalilidwe uyelede kweetwa kuKabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano mukati kamazuba kkumi kuzwa buzuba mbubaangwa kabiyo kabunga aaka keelede kwaambilwa zina lyamwaange, busena nkwaajalilidwe acheecho chakapa kuti ajalilwe/awangwe.

(2) Mulandu wawoonse mwaange uujalilidwe uyelede kweetwa lubo kukabunga kalangalanga zyamilandu yabaange muchiindi champyopyongano nikwiinda mazuba makumi otatwe kuzwa buzuba nuwakalangidwe mulandu umwi awumwi naakuti mukati kamazuba mache kuti kakuli kabunga kalokulangalanga milandu kaamba boobo.

(3) Kabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano keelede kulangisisya milandu yeetwa kuli nkako kakatanonoki pe.

(4) Mukulangalanga milandu yoonse kuchitwa aKabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano, boonse baange beelede kuzumininwa kulyaambwiida lwabo beni, naakuti kabayanda —

(a) kwaambwidwa asimulawu ngubapegwa kabbadalwa afulumende; naakuti

afulumende

kabiyo

(b) kwaambwidwa asimulawu ngubalilangwiida akulibbadalila lwabo beni.

(5) Eezi zyaambwa mumwaneefuka (1) zyamazuba asika kukkumi zilaamba kabiyo kuchiindi chisyoonto chakujalilwa chitasiki kumazuba kkumi. Eezi zilaamba kulaabo baange banga bagwisigwa kaatana mana mazuba kkumi kabali baangwa mpawo baangwa lubo akujalilwa lubo mukati kamazuba kkumi kuzwa nibagwisigwa. Eezyo zyaambwa aKabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano 6. Kabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano keelede kulemba maboneno aako kakapa malembe aayo kuli baabo bakawanga akujalila baange. Malembe aaya aleelede kwaamba kuti mwaange umwi awumwi uyelede kuyanembo kajalilidwe na naakuti agwisigwe mubwaange. Aabo bapegwa malembe

azwa kuKabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano beelede kutobelezya zyaambwa amalembe aachitwa akabunga aaka. Mwaange wagwisigwa tayelede kwaangwa lubo pe kaangilwa mulandu ngwaagwisilwa kale. 7. (1) Mwaange wagwisigwa kuzwa mubwaange/mukujalilwa nkaambo kakuti kwajanika kuti malembe aKabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano awamba kuti ngagwisigwe nkaambo taakwe bukamboni pe bwakwaangwa akujalilwa kwakwe, tayelede kujalilwa lubo pe kakubelesegwa mulandu ngwaagwisigilwa.

(2) Kuchitila kuti fuka eeli limvwisisike kabotu, mwaange uwambwa kuti wajalilwa mulandu ngunguwo ngwaajalilidwe do mane Kabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano kaamba kuti, mukubona kwako, kubaanga kuluumbi mulandu ngwanga ulaangilwa mwaange ooyu. Kuzumizigwa kwamwaange kuti abelesye nkuta 8. Mpampikwa eeyi tiiyelede kubonwa aanga itolela mwaange cheelelo chakubelesya nkuta kukaka kwaangwa antoomwe amulandu wakwe muchiindi kuutana langwalangwa aKabunga kalangalanga zyabaange muchiindi champyopyongano naakuti kuuli walangwalangwa kale akabunga aaka.

MPAMPIKWA YATATU (Zibeela 94, 104, 114, 122, 123, 126, 127, 128, 185, 259, 272, 310 a 320) ZIKONKE ANZUMINO Malayilile: 1. Woonse muntu uyelede kuchita chimwi chazikonke, kuti kayanda, ukonzya kuchita nzumino aleke kuchita chikonke kabelesya mabala aapedwe. 2. Kuti muntu wachita chimwi chazikonke eezi naakuti kuchita imwi yanzumino eezi wabelesya mabala asiyene achikonke anzumino, musiyano wamabala aabelesegwa tuukonzyi kuubula bulemu bwachikonke anzumino yachitwa nkaambo zichinoomusungilide sikukonka/sikuzumina eezyo nzyaakonka/ zumina. CHIKONKE ANZUMINO YAKULIPEDA AKUSWIILILA Mebo ……………………………………...ndakonka [naakuti ndazumina] kuti njoosyomeka akulipeda kunyika yaZimbabwe antoomwe akulemeka milawu yaZimbabwe. Nkaako ndigwasye Leza. [zilasotokwa kuti kakuchitwa nzumino] Chikonke eechi naakuti nzumino eeyi iyelede kuchitwa kunembo lyamuntu uuwambidwe mumulawu uyendelana aanzizeezi. CHIKONKE NAAKUTI NZUMINO CHAWOFESI Mebo ……………………………………...ndakonka [naakuti ndizooyibelekela Zimbabwe, akulipeda mu wofesi yabu.

ndazumina]

kuti

Nkaako ndigwasye Leza. [zilasotokwa kuti kakuchitwa nzumino] Chikonke eechi naakuti nzumino eeyi iyelede kuchitwa kunembo lyamuntu uuwambidwe mumulawu uyendelana aanzizeezi. CHIKONKE NAAKUTI NZUMINO YAMUZULWIDI WANYIKA NAAKUTI BAMUTOBELA

Mebo ………………………………………ndakonka [naakuti ndazumina] kuti mbuuli Muzulwidi waNyika [naakuti Utobela Muzulwidi waNyika] yaZimbabwe, njoosyomeka kabiyo njooswiilila, kusumpula akukwabilila Kkata lyaMulawu antoomwe ayimwi milawu yaZimbabwe, kabiyo— njoosumpula zyoonse zigwasya nyika yaZimbabwe antoomwe akukaka zitagwasyi nyika yaZimbabwe; • • •

njookwabilila akusumpula zyeelelo zyabantu bamuZimbabwe; njoochita milimu yangu munguzu zyangu zyoonse aluzibo ndundaalo loonse. ndal ipeda lwangu mukugwas izya bukkale bwanyika yaZimbabwe abantu bayo.

Nkaako ndigwasye Leza. [zilasotokwa kuti kakuchitwa nzumino] Chikonke eechi naakuti nzumino eeyi iyelede kuchitwa kunembo lyaDuundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti kuti katawo, kunembo lyawumwi mubetesi utobela Duundumuuntule waBabetesi. CHIKONKE NAAKUTI NZUMINO YASILUTWE WAMUTABI WAFULUMENDE Mebo ………………………………………ndakonka [naakuti ndazumina] kuti njoosyomeka kunyika yaZimbabwe , mu wofesi yaSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende (Minista) njooswiilila, kusumpula akukwabilila Kkata lyaMulawu antoomwe ayimwi milawu yaZimbabwe, kabiyo; njoogwasizya Muzulwidi waNyika kumupa luzibo abusongo Muzulwidi waNyika yaZimbabwe mbuuli mbuntanoozibone aawo mpuntanookumbilwe kupa mizeezo yangu kuchitila kuti kweendelezegwa kwanyika yaZimbabwe kuchitwe kabotu, kabiyo nsikoyoobbongonona nsiswa zyaambuulwa muKkabbineti naakuti niiba iili nsiswa njindapegwa mukuchita mulimu wangu mbuuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende, kabiyo njoochita milimu yangu mukuziba kwangu koonse. Nkaako ndigwasye Leza. [zilasotokwa kuti kakuchitwa nzumino] Chikonke eechi naakuti nzumino eeyi iyelede kuchitwa kunembo lyaMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti katawo, kunembo lyaDuundumuntule waBabetesi naakuti kunembo lyawumwi mubetesi utobela Duundumunutle waBabetesi. CHIKONKE NAAKUTI NZUMINO YAWUTOBELA SILUTWE WAMUTABI WAFULUMENDE

Mebo……………………ndakonka [naakuti ndazumina] kuti njoosyomeka kunyika yaZimbabwe , mu wofesi yaMutobeli waSilutwe waMutabi waFulumende njooswiilila, kusumpula akukwabilila Kkata lyaMulawu antoomwe ayimwi milawu yaZimbabwe, kabiyo; njoogwasizya Muzulwidi waNyika kumupa luzibo abusongo Muzulwidi waNyika yaZimbabwe mbuuli mbuntanoozibone aawo mpuntanookumbilwe kupa mizeezo yangu kuchitila kuti kweendelezegwa kwanyika yaZimbabwe kuchitwe kabotu, kabiyo nsikoyoobbongonona nsiswa zyaambuulwa muKkabbineti naakuti niiba iili nsiswa njindapegwa mukuchita mulimu wangu mbuuli Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende, kabiyo njoochita milimu yangu mukuziba kwangu koonse. Nkaako ndigwasye Leza. [zilasotokwa kuti kakuchitwa nzumino] Chikonke eechi naakuti nzumino eeyi iyelede kuchitwa kunembo lyaMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti katawo, kunembo lyaDuundumuuntule waBabetesi naakuti kunembo lyawumwi mubetesi utobela Duundumunutle waBabetesi. CHIKONKE NAAKUTI NZUMINO YAMWIIMININI MUNG’ANDA YAPALYAMENTI Mebo………………………………….Ndakonka [naakuti ndazumina] kuti njoosyomeka kunyika yaZimbabwe, njoosumpula Kkata lyaMulawu antoomwe ayimwi milawu yaZimbabwe, kabiyo ndiloochita milimu yangu nkindi Seneta/ Mwiiminini muNg’anda

Yaansi Yamilawu [fumbwa zili nzizyo] mukulipeda kwangu kabiyo muluzibo loonse ndundaalo. Nkaako ndigwasye Leza. [zilasotokwa kuti kakuchitwa nzumino] Chikonke eechi naakuti nzumino eeyi iyelede kuchitwa kunembo lyaMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti. CHIKONKE NAAKUTI NZUMINO YABUBETESI Mebo……………………ndakonka [naakuti ndazumina] kuti njoosyomeka kunyika yaZimbabwe kabiyo nkindi muwofesi ya…………….Ndiloosumpula akukwabilila Kkata lyaMulawu kabiyo ndiyooyendelezya bululami kubantu boonse munzila ikozyania kakutakwe kuyoowa naakuti kufwida luse bamwi bantu mbuuli mbuzyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu antoomwe ayimwi milawu. Nkaako ndigwasye Leza. [zilasotokwa kuti kakuchitwa nzumino] Aawo muntu uchita chikonke eechi naakuti nzumino eeyi kali Jaji kweelede wachita kunembo lya Duundumuntule waBubetesi naakuti uutobela Duundumuntule wabubetesi. Muli zyoonse zimwi kweelede chikonke eechi chachitwa kunembo lyamuntu uuwambidwe amulawu uyendelana amulimu wakwe. CHIKONKE NAAKUTI NZUMINO YAMWIIMININI MUKKANSILI YACHILIKITI/DOLOPO PATI Mebo…………………ndakonka [naakuti ndazumina] kuti njoosyomeka kunyika yaZimbabwe , mu wofesi yakuba umwi waKkansili yaChilikiti [naakuti yaDolopo Pati], kkansili yachilikiti cha [wamba zina lyachilikiti] njoosumpula akukwabilila Kkata lyaMulawu antoomwe ayimwi milawu yaZimbabwe, kabiyo; Njoogwasizya kumizeezo Sichuuno waKkansili yaChilikiti [naakuti Meya] wa [wamba zina lyachilikiti] mbuuli mbuntanoozibone aawo mpuntanookumbilwe kupa mizeezo yangu kuchitila kuti kweendelezegwa kwachilikiti/dolopo pati kuchitwe kabotu, kabiyo nsikoyoobbongonona nsiswa ziyoomwa mukkansili yachilikiti [dolopo pati] naakuti niiba iili nsiswa njindapegwa mukuchita mulimu wangu mbuuli umwi wakkansili yachilikiti , kabiyo njoochita milimu yangu mukuziba kwangu koonse. Nkaako ndigwasye Leza. [zilasotokwa kuti kakuchitwa nzumino] Chikonke eechi naakuti nzumino eeyi iyelede kuchitwa kunembo lyamuntu uwambidwe mumilawu ilangene aamulimu ooyu.

MPAMPIKWA YANE (Zibeela 92, 121, 124, 125 a158) NZIBEELEDE KUBAAZYO KUTI BANTU BALEMBESYE KUVOOTA Nzuweelede kubaazyo kuti ulembesye kuvoota 1. (1) Kwiinda mumwaneefuka (2) kabiyo afuka (2), muntu ukonzya kulembesya kuvoota mumalembe abasikuvoota bajanika mubusena bwaakagalwa (kkonsitichwensi) kuti kakuli —

(a) ujisi minyaka isika naakuti iyinda kkumi lilaamusanu atutatu; kabiyo

(b) ujisi bwenichisi bwaZimbabwe.

(2) Mulawu waNsaluuzyo ukonzya kuyungizya zimwi ziyandikana zili mbuuli bukamboni bwampokkala kuchitila kuti bantu balembesye mumalembe aabusena bwabo nchobeni, pesi fumbwa eezyo zyayungizigwa kweelede kazyeendelana aziyandwa aKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kapati chibeela 67. Kukasigwa kulembesya kuvoota 2. Muntu ukonzya kukasigwa kulembesya kuvoota —

(a) kuti kakuli ubambidwe muchibbadela chabantu basondokede zili mumulawu upangwa aPalyamenti uubona zyabulondozi bwamuboongo;

(b) kuti kakuli wakoompololwa ankuta kuti tachikonzyi kweendelezya zintu zyakwe, fumbwa moompololo ooyo kuchibeleka; naakuti

(c) kuti kakuli muntu wakali wajanika kalaamulandu ujanika muMulawu waNsaluuzyo kabiyo wakoompololwa aNkuta Yaajulu kuti akasigwe kulembesya kuvoota naakuti wakakasigwa kuvoota kwachiindi chaambidwe pesi chiindi eecho chakukasigwa kuvoota tacheelede kwiinda minyaka musanu pe.

MPAMPIKWA YASANU (Zibeela 130 a131) ZITOBELEZEGWA MUKUCHITA MABILLS AZIMWI ZYAAMBO MUPALYAMENTI

KUBAMBWA KWAMAFUKA CHIBAKA 1 KUNJIZYA MAMALEMBE AAKUBAMBA MULAWU, MITWE YANJOOMO AMA PETISHINI Ifuka 1. Bupanduluzi muMpampikwa Yasanu. 2. Ng’anda Yamilawu nkuyazwa Bill. 3. Bayiminini bang’anda bayeta maBills aMitwe Yanjoomo (Motions). 4. Palyamenti tiikonzyi kuzuwa aatala azyaMoney Bills naakuti Fiscal motions or petishin aanze kwakuti zyakumbilwa aMutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende.

CHIBAKA 2 NGAMBO ZILEMBWA AKKOMITI YAZYAKULANGALANGA MILAWU MUPALYAMENTI 5. Kweenzegwa kwama Bill akati kaMaanda amilawu. 6. Kukazyania kwaMaanda aaMilawu.

7\. Ma Bill alaatala azyamali. CHIBAKA 3 MALIPOTI ZILEMBWA AKKOMITI YAZYAKULANGALANGA MILAWU MUPALYAMENTI 8. Malipoti alembwa a Kkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti. 9. Malipoti alembwa aKkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti aatala amaStatutory Instruments.

CHIBAKA 1 KUNJIZYA MABILLS, MITWE YANJOOMO AMA PETISHINI

Bupanduluzi muMpampikwa Yasanu 1. Mumpampikwa eeyi — “Malembe aakubamba Mulawu waMali” chaamba Bill lilaachakwaamba aatala a—

(a) kubikka, kuyungizya naakuti kuchesya mali yatax ibbadalwa

(a) kuchitila kuti fulumende agwasikane;

(b) kujana mali kuzwa, naakuti kubikka, kuyungizya naakuti kuchesya mali iyanda kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati Yafulumende naakuti zimwi nkomo zyafulumende;

(c) kuliya chikwelete chiya kuli fulumende;

(d) kusola kukachilwa kutolwa kwamutelo iyelede kuti iye kuli fulumende

(e) kusola mabelesezyo aamali atazumizigwe pe afulumende. Ng’anda yamilawu kwazwa malembe akubamba mulawu 2. (1) Fumbwa Bill likonzya kutaanguninwa muNg’amda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(2) Fumbwa Bbili, aanze kwaBbili lyaMali, likonzya kutaanguninwa muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu Bakonzya kutaanguna njoomo yamaBbili 3. Kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli aNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti —

(a) fumbwa Seneta ukonzya kunjizya Bbili muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu naakuti kukosola njoomo yaatala aBbili muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu naakuti kweeta petishini muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu;

(b) fumbwa Mwiiminini wamuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu ukonzya kunjizya Bill muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu naakuti kukosola njoomo yaatala aBill muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu naakuti kweeta petishini muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(c) fumbwa Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende ukonzya kunjizya Bill, naakuti kukosola njoomo yaatala aBill naakuti kweeta petishini muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamulawu naakuti Ng’anda Yaansi Yamulawu;

(d) Fumbwa muntu wanjizya Bill muliimwi Ng’anda Yamilawu ukonzya kuti, nilyazuminwa Bbili eelyo ang’anda eeyo mwaalinjizya walitola kuliimbi Ng’anda Yamilawu. Palyamenti tiikonzyi kuzuwa aatala azyamaBbili aMali naakuti Mabambe aZyaambo aatala aMali naakuti petishini aanze kwakuti zyakumbilwa aMutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende 4. (1) Aanze kwakuti bakumbilwa aMutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende, taakwe ng’anda yamilawu iinga—

(a) ilayooma Bbili, nikuba liyanda kubambululwa, lilaachakuchita azyaBbili lyaMali mumeso aMuzulwidi waSeneti naakuti Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(b) yooma mutwe wanjoomo , nikuba wakabambululwa, ulaachakuchita azintu aMuzulwidi waSeneti naakuti Sichuuno Yamilawu—

mutwe wanjoomo zitobela mumeso waNg’anda Yaansi

(i) kubikka, kuyungizya naakuti kuchesya mali yatax ibbadalwa kuchitila kuti fulumende agwasikane;

(ii) kujana mali kuzwa, naakuti kubikka, kuyungizya naakuti kuchesya mali iyanda kuzwa kuNkomo Mpati Yafulumende naakuti zimwi nkomo zyafulumende;

(iii) kuliya chikwelete chiya kuli fulumende;

(iv) kusola kukachilwa kutolwa kwatax iyelede kuti iye kuli fulumende

(v) kusola mabelesezyo aamali atazumizidwe pe afulumende naakuti

(c) ilatambula petishini inga mumeso aMuzulwidi waSeneti naakuti Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, iyanda kuti kuchitwe nikuba chinzi chaambidwe mumwaneefuka (b)

(2) Mwaneefuka (1) takonzyi kubelesegwa kuBbili lyanjizigwa, mutwe wanjoomo naakuti petishini yeetwa aMutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende naakuti Uutobela Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende. CHIBAKA 2 MEENDELEZYO AMALEMBE ABBILI Kweenzegwa kwamaBbili aakati kaMaanda amilawu 5. (1) Bbili lyataangunwa akuzuminwa muliimwi ng’anda yaPalyamenti lyeelede kutolwa kuliimbi ng’anda yaPalyamenti achakufwambaana kabiyo buunsi nilyatolwa kuliimbi ng’anda yaPalyamenti kweelede kwalembwa mumalembe ang’anda nkulyazwa Bill.

(2) Bbili lyeetwa mung’anda yamilawu lyeelede kutaangunwa kuyoomwa achakufwambaana mpawo balizumine mbuuli mbulibede naakuti balibambulule nibatalizumine.

(3) Bbili lyakatumizigwa kuliimbi ng’anda yamilawu kwiinda muli zyaambidwe mufuka eeli, kuti lyaakubambululwa kung’anda eeyo lyeelede lyaboozegwa nkung’anda nkulyakazwide kabiyo eezyo zyakabambululwa muBill kazili zyazuminanwa aMulembi Mupati waPalyamenti. Nikuba boobo ng’anda yamilawu yakatumizizye Bbili lyakabambululwa ikonzya kukaka, kuzumina naakuti kubambulula lubo eezyo zyakaakubambululwa kuliimbi ng’anda yamilawu.

(4) Kuti Bbili lyaboozegwa kung’anda yamilawu yakalitaanguna, kwiinda mulizyaambwa amwaneefuka (3), mpawo eezyo zyakali zyabambululwa ayimbi ng’anda yamilawu zyakakwa naakuti zyabambululwa lubo ang’anda yamilawu yakataanguna Bbili, imwi ng’anda yakali yabambulula Bill ikonzya kutumizya mulumbe kung’anda yamilawu yakataanguna Bbili kakugwisigwa eezyo zyakali zyabambululwa ang’anda yakatali taanguna Bbili naakuti kakuzuminwa zyakabambululwa ang’anda yakataanguna Bbili. Kukazyania kwaMaanda aaMilawu 6. (1) Kwiinda mufuka eeli, kuti —

(a) Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu teezyazuminana aatala aziyanda kubambululwa muBill lyakataangunwa aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu mukati kamazuba makumi musanu alaamakumi aane (90) Bbili kalili lyanjizigwa muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu;

(b) Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamulawu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu teezyazuminana aatala aziyanda kubambululwa muBill lyakataangunwa aNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu mukati kamazuba makumi musanu alaamakumi aane (90) Bbili kalili lyabweedezegwa kuNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu; naakuti

(c) Bbili lyakataangunwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu lyakakwa naakuti teelyazuminwa aNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu mukati kamazuba ali makumi musanu amakumi aane (90) Bbili kalili lyanjizigwa muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu; Bbili eelyo likonzya kutumizigwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika kuti likazuminwe akusayinwa muchiimo mbulyakabede nilyakazuminwa aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, aanze kwaziyandikana kubambululwa ziche/ziniini mukuya kwachiindi antoomwe azimwi zyakabambululwa zyakazuminanwa amaanda aamulawu obile.

(2) Kuti, mukubona kwaSichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, Bill lya —

(a) kataanguninwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu; kabiyo

(b) lyakanjizigwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu mukati kamazuba makumi musanu amakumi aane (90) kuzwa limwi Bbili lyakataangunwa muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu lyakanjizigwa muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu kalijisi malembe aakozyania awaayo aali muBbili lyakaliwo kale, aanze kwamasandu maniini loko ayandikana mukuya kwachiindi, mwaneefuka (1) ulabeleka kuBill aali boobo kwabaanga chiindi chamazuba aasika kumakumi musanu amakumi aane (90 days) zyaambidwe mubanabefuka (a) a (c) bamwaneefuka zyaamba chiindi nchaswaanana maanda aamilawu amazuba musanu awatatu.

(3)Bbili lyaambidwe mubanabefuka (1) a (2) talikonzyi kutolwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika kuti likazuminwe akusayinwe aanze kwakuti, nikwayinda chiindi chamazuba makumi musanu amakumi aane (90) naakuti mazuba musanu awatatu chiindi chaambidwe mumwaneefuka wakkona, mpawo Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu yazuminana kuti Bbili litumizigwe kuMuzulwidi waNyika kuyoozuminwa akusayinwa.

(4)Bbili lyatumizigwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika kuti likazuminwe akusayinwa kwiinda mulizyaambidwe mufuka eeli lyeelede kusindikilwa asetifiketi kuzwa kuli Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu iiwamba kuti Bill eeli ndelimwi lyakeendelezegwa kwiinda mufuka eeli kabiyo eezyo ziyandikana kuchitwa afuka eeli zyakachitwa mbuuli mbukweelede.

(5)Bbili lyatumizigwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika, kwiinda muli zyaambidwe mufuka eeli, nga lyabwezegwa aanga lyakazuminwa aPalyamenti muchiimo mulyatumizigwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika.

(6) Kuchitila fuka eeli —

(a) Bbili lyakataangunwa aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu ngalyatolwa mbuuli Bill lyakanjizigwa muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu mpawompawo mubuunsi mbwiiswaanana Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu kuzwa buunsi nilyakatumizigwa kuya kung’anda eeyi yamulawu mbuuli mbukulembedwe mumalembe aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kwiinda muli zyaambidwe mufuka 5(1);

(b) Bbili lyakataangunwa aNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu ngalyatolwa mbuuli Bill lyakaboozegwa kuNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu mpawompawo mubuunsi mbwiiswaanana Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamulawu kuzwa buunsi nilyakaboozegwa lutaanzi kuza kuNganda Yaajulu Yamilawu kwiinda muli zyaambidwe mufuka 5(3).

(7) Fuka eeli talitobelezegwi pe kukweendelezegwa kwamaBbili aKkata lyaMulawu naakuti Bbili lyaMali. MaBbili alaatala azyamali 7. (1) (Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu tiijisi manguzu aakubambulula Bbili lyaMali pesi ikonzya kukumbila Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kuti ibambulule ziyanda kubambululwa.

(2) Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti uyelede kubona masandu alokukumbilwa aNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu kuti achitwe kuBbili lyaMali kabiyo uyelede kwaatumizya masandu ayanda kuchitwa kuNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(3) Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu iyelede kwalangalanga masandu ngiyakakumbilwa kuti iwachite akatumizigwa kuli njiyo kwiinda mufuka eeli kabiyo ikonzya kwaanjizya muBbili lyaMali masandu aayo.

(4) Kuti Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu teeyazumina Bbili lyaMali mukati kamazuba musanu awatatu (8) kiiswaanana kubalwa kuzwa buunsi Bill nilyakanjizigwa muNg’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu, iNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu ikonzya kuzuminana kuti Bill eelyo ngalitumizigwe kuMuzulwidi waNyika nkaako Bill eelyo likonzya kutumizigwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika muchiimo mulyakabede nilyakazuminanwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(5) Bbili lyaMali lyatumizigwa kuMuzulwiid waNyika kalili lyazuminanwa kwiinda muli zeezyo zyaambidwe mumwaneefuka (4)—

(a) ngalyatolwa mbuuli kuti lyakazuminanwa aPalyamenti muchiimo mulyakabede nilyakazuminanwa muNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu; kabiyo

(b) kuti lyatumizigwa kuMuzulwidi waNyika kuti likazuminwe akusayinwa, lyeelede kusindikilwa asetifiketi lizwa kuli Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu lyaamba kuti Bbili eelyo lyakazuminanwa kwiinda mufuka eeli. CHIBAKA 3 MALIPOTI ALEMBWA AKKOMITI YAZYAKULANGALANGA MILAWU MUPALYAMENTI Malipoti alembwa a Kkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti 8. (1) Kwiinda muli zyaambidwe mufuka eeli, taakwe ng’anda yamilawu ikonzya kumaninsizya meendelezezyo aBbilil ilipoti izwa kuKkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti kiitana balwa mung’anda eeyo yamilawu.

(2) Mwaneefuka (1) tabelesegwi pe —

(a) kuBbili lyaKkata lyaMulawu; naakuti

(b) fumbwa Bill lyakalembelwa lipoti kale aKkomiti yaZyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti aanze kwakuti Bill eelyo lyakazoobambululwa kuzwa niyakali kulangalanga Kkomiti yaZyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti.

(3) Kuti Kkomiti yaZyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti yakachilwa kulemba lipoti aatala aBill mukati kachiindi chaambidwe muNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti naakuti mukati kachiindi chayungizigwa kachili chazuminwa kwiinda muNzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti, nga kwayeeyelwa kuti Kkomiti yakabona kuti taakwe bubi budinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu pe bujanika muBill eelyo kuti lyaba mulawu nkaako eeyo ng’anda yamulawu iyelede kwiinkilila nembo akuzumina Bill eelyo mbolibede aanga Kkomiti yakapa lipoti yayo.

(4) Kwiinda mubanabefuka (5) a (6), kuti Kkomiti yaZyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti yaamba kuti zimwi zipanzi zyaBbili zidinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu, eeyo ng’anda yamilawu ilokweendelezya Bbili eelyo iyeelede kubweza mizeezo yazwa kuKkomiti yaZyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti akuchita mbuuli mbuyaambilwa mulipoti. Ng’anda yamulawu eeyo tiikonzyi kuzumina Bill eelyo lyategwa lijisi zipanzi zidinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu kusikila zyabambululwa zipanzi eezyo zitalomene.

(5) Kuti ng’anda yamilawu yajana lipoti lizwa kuKkomiti yaZyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mumwaneefuka (4) aatala aBill lyakanjizigwa Mutobela waMuzulwiid waNyika, Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende

naakuti Uumutobela, muntu wakeeta Bill mung’anda yamulawu uyelede kupegwa chiindi chakuti asandule zyaambidwe mulipoti yaKkomiti yaZyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti.

(6) Kuti ng’anda yamilawu yazuminana, kwiinda mulizyaambidwe amumwaneefuka (4), kuti nchobeni zimwi zipanzi zyaBbili zidinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu, Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika naakuti Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende kuti kayanda ukonzya kutola kaambo kakwe kuNkuta yaZyaKkata lyaMulawu mukati kamazuba kkumi amazuba aane (14 days) kuzwa buunsi ng’anda yamulawu niyakakaka kuzumina Bill eelyo. Pesi kuti Nkuta yaZyaKkata lyaMulawu yatuzumina tupanzi twaBill twali kutegwa tudinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu, ng’anda yamilawu yalikweendelezya Bbili eelyo ikonzya kuzumina Bill eelyo kalijisi tupanzi twakali kutegwa tudinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu. Malipoti alembwa aKkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti 9. (1) Kkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti ikonzya kugwisya lipoti lyayo mung’anda yamilawu kuti yabona kuti zipanzi zidinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu zyabambululwa, iStatutory Instrument kiitana langwalangwa pe aMaanda aMilawu obile.

(2) Kuti, Maanda aMilawu obile alangalanga lipoti yakazwa ku Kkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti iwamba kuti muli zipanzi zidinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu muMpandamayake yaMulawu , Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti uyelede kwaambila baabo bakeetede Statutory Instrument mung’anda yamilawu kuti Statutory Instrument ijisi zipanzi zidinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu nkaako tiikonzyi kuzuminwa. Mutabi wakeetede mpandamayake yamulawu eeyo uyelede kuti mukati kamazuba makumi obile alimwi kali waambilwa, wachita chimwi chazeezi—

(a) walembela kuNkuta yaZyaKkata lyaMulawu kakumbila kuti ilangelange kuti nchobeni na kuli zipanzi zidinkaana aKkata lyaMulawu muStatutory Instrument; naakuti.

(b) Ayibambulule Statutory Instrument yakwe.

(3) Aawo mutabi wakeetede Mpandamayake yaMulawu kuti walembela kuNkuta yaZyaKkata lyaMulawu kakumbila kuti ilangwelangwe mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mumwaneefuka (2)(a), maba,be amulawu ooyo ngawabikkwa kumbali kakwiimikililwa mazembe atazwe kuNkuta yaZyaKkata lyaMulawu.

(4) Kuti nikwamana kulangwalangwa lipoti ya Kkomiti Yazyakulangalanga Milawu muPalyamenti kakuli kwajanwa kuti zimwi zipanzi zyaStatutory Instrument zidinkaana aMulawu uzyala Mabambe aMulawu, Maanda aMilawu obile akonzya kuzuminana kuti zipanzi eezyo zidinkaana aMulawu uzyala Mpandamayake yaMulawu nkaako—

(a) zipanzi zidinkaana aMulawu uzyala Mpandamayake yaMulawu nga tazichibeleki pe; kabiyo

(b) Mulembi Mupati waPalyamenti ulalemba chizibisyo muGazette achakufwambaana kazibisya bulumbu eezyo zyakazuminanwa aMaanda aMilawu obile anchizyaamba chizuminano chawo maanda.

MPAMPIKWA YAMUSANU AKAMWI (Zibeela 329 a332) KUTALIKA KUBELEKA KWAKKATA LYAMULAWU EELI AKUTAMBIKIZYANIA NGUZU KWAMAWOFESI BWIIME BWAMAFUKA CHIBAKA 1

NTALISYO

Fuka 1. Kutalika kubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli. 2. Kusweegwa KwaKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi. CHIBAKA 2 KUTAANGUNA KUBELEKA KWAKKATA LYAMULAWU EELI ANTOOMWE AKUGWISIGWA KWAKKATA LYAMULAWU WACHIINDI

3\. Kutalika kubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli. 4. Kusweegwa KwaKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi. CHIBAKA 3 NSALUUZYO NTAANZI 5. Kubelesya migaano iiliwo kale yazilikiti amakkonsitichwensi. 6. Kulembesegwa kwabasikuvoota. 7. Kukazya masalilo aMuzulwidi waNyika kunsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna. 8. Mulawu wansaluuzyo. 9. Kutambikizyania nguzu kwafulumende 10. Kuyanembo kwamilawu iliwo kale CHIBAKA 4 KUTAMBIKIZYANIA NGUZU KWAFULUMENDE 11. Bupanduluzi bwamilawu iliwo kale. 12. Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti. 13. Babelesi bajanika muzyuuno. 14. Zyaambo zyaatala ansaluuzyo antoomwe abulamfu bwachiindi Muzulwidi waNyika nchayelede kukkala muwofesi. 15. Kweendelela nembo kwamawofesi amwi. 16. Mukwabilili waBulumbu. 17. Kulonzegwa kwamali ili muNkomoMpati Yafulumende yachiindi. 18. Nkuta ameendelezyo azyamulawu. 19. Zyaambo zyaMupati waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta. 20. Zyeelelo zyabantu kulizya Ndyabukkede. CHIBAKA 1 NTALISYO Bupanduluzi muMpampikwa Yamusanu Akamwi 1. Mumpampikwa eeyi, aanze kwakuti kazisiyene azyaambidwe mumalembe alokwaambwa —

“buunsi bwakutalika” chaamba buunsi Kkata lyaMulawu eeli mbuli talika kubeleka kwiinda muli zyaambidwe mufuka 3(2); “milawu iliwo kale” chaamba mulawu uli mumalembe wakali kubeleka kale muZimbabwe buunsi mbulyakatalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, kuuli muchiimo chaMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti naakuti Mayake aMulawu; “mulawu uliwo kale” chaamba mulawu wakaliwo kale naakuti umwi mulawu, fumbwa muchiimo muwakabede, pesi kuuli wakali kubeleka muZimbabwe buunsi mbulyakatalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli; “Nsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna” chaamba—

(a) nsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna zyakusala kakubelesegwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli;

Muzulwidi

waNyika

(b) nsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna zyakusala biiminini bamung’anda yaPalyamenti kakubelesegwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli; kabiyo

(c) nsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna zyakusala bantu banjila mumakkansili aamuzilikiti, mumadolopo mapati amumabbooma; “Kkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi” chaamba Kkata lyaMulawu lyaZimbabwe lyakatalika kubeleka mu18 Mukubwekaangala 1980 ambambululo zyamulawu ooyu zyakachitwa zisiyenesiyene; “zuba lyakudindwa” chaamba buzuba mbulyakadindwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli mupepa lyaGazeti kwiinda muli zyaambidwe muchibeela 51(5) chaKkata lyaMulawu wachiindi. Manguzu aMpampikwa Yamusanu Akamwi 2. Mpampikwa Yamusanu Akamwi eeyi ilaamanguzu kuli zyoonse zibeela zijanika muKkata lyaMulawu eeli, kapati aawo mpukutamvwisisiki kabotu zyaambwa achimwi chibeela. CHIBAKA 2 KUTALIKA KUBELEKA KWAKKATA LYAMULAWU EELI AKUSWEEGWA KWAKKATA LYAMULAWU LYACHIINDI

Kutalika kubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli 3. (1) Mpampikwa eeyi, antoomwe a —

(a) Chaandaano 3, chilaatala azyabunichisi bwaZimbabwe;

(b) Chaandaano 4, kakuli Nzyeelelo Zyaampandamunsaka;

(c) Chaandaano 5, chilaatala akusalwa kwaMuzulwidi waNyika akutalika kwakwe mulimu;

(d) Chaandaano 6, chilaatala akusalwa kwaBiiminini bamung’anda yaPalyamenti antoomwe akwiitwa kwaPalyamenti nikwazwa kuchitwa nsaluuzyo akuzumina kwaMuzulwidi waNyika Milawu ipangwa aPalyamenti;

(e) Chaandaano 7, chilaatala ansaluuzyo, aanze kwazibeela 158, 160 a161;

(f) Chaandaano 8, chilaatala amulimu antoomwe amanguzu aNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu;

(g) Chaandaano 9, chilaatala andemeko zyameendelezyo abulumbu antoomwe abuzuluzi;

(h) Chibeela 208, chilaatala akulijata kwaBasikukwabilila Nyika;

(i) and Chaandaano muZimbabwe; kabiyo

12,

chilaatala

aKkomishini

yaNsaluuzyo

(j) Chaandaano 14, chilaatala abafulumende bamuzilikiti amumabbooma. zitaanguna kubeleka buunsi lyakudindwa kwaKkata lyaMulawu.

(2) Aanze kwawaawo mpukwaambidwe muMpampikwa eeyi, zipanzi zyoonse zyaKkata lyaMulawu zitalika kubeleka buunsi Muzulwidi waNyika wakasalwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli naatalika mulimu.

(3) Aakati kabuunsi bwakudindwa abuunsi bwakutalika kubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli, zipanzi zyaKkata lyaMulawu eeli zyaambidwe mubanabefuka (a) kuloosika ku (j) zyamwaneefuka (1) zitola busena bwazipanzi zikozyania zijanika muKkata lyaMulawu lyachiindi. Kusweegwa kwaKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi 4. Kwiinda muMpampikwa eeyi, Kkata lyaMulawu wachiindi lyasweegwa kuzwa buunsi nilyatalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli pya.

CHIBAKA 3 NSALUUZYO ZYAKUTAANGUNA 5. Kubelesya migaano iiliwo kale yazilikiti amakkonsitichwensi Miganhu yematunhu, yemitunhu neyemawadi sema kwayokusanhu kukaswika hhuba ledusiwa patjena ndiyo inohinga muhalulo dzekutanga. Kulembesegwa kwabasikuvoota 6. (1) Woonse muntu wakalembesezye kuvoota mumalembe akuvoota kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli unoochilembedwe mumalembe kuchitila nsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna.

(2) Kuchitila nsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna, Mulembi Mupati waZyakuvoota (Registra General of Voters) ngutanoolembe mazina abasikuvoota antoomwe akubamaba malembe abasikuvoota kalangwalangwa aKkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe.

(3) Nabbatule waBasikulemba Basikuvoota kalangwalangwa akkomishini yaNsaluuzyo muZimbabwe uyelede kuchita jendengano lyakulembesya bantu basikuvoota antoomwe akubona kuti bantu basika kuzoobona kuti mazina aabo mpaali mumalembe kwamazuba makumi aatatu kuzwa buunsi nilyadindwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli. Kukazya zyakazwa munsaluuzyo zyakusala Muzulwidi waNyika munsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna 7. Fumbwa wakazyania azyakazwa munsaluzzyo zyakusala Muzulwidi waNyika kunsaluuzyo zyachitwa kutaanguna, kaambo aaka kweelede kamaninsigwa akupetwa kwiinda muli zeezyo zyaambidwe muchibeela 93 chaKkata lyaMulawu eeli. Mulawu wansaluuzyo 8. Nsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna zyeelede kuchitwa kwiinda mumulawu wazyansaluuzyo uyendelana azyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli.

CHIBAKA 4 KUTAMBIKIZYANIA NGUZU KWAFULUMENDE

Kutambikizyania nguzu kwafulumende 9. Fulumende uubumbwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli nguwe utola chibaka chafulumende wakaliwo kale. Kuyanembo kwamilawu iiliwo kale 10. Kwiinda muMpampikwa eeyi, yoonse milawu iliwo kale ileendelela nembo kiibeleka pesi yeelede kulangisisigwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu pya eeli. Bupanduluzi bwamilawu iiliwo kale 11. (1) Aanze kwakuti zilisiyene azyaambidwe chiindi achiindi, zyaambwa mumilawu iiliwo kale aatala a —

(a) Muzulwidi waNyika nga zyaamba Muzulwidi waNyika uubikkidwe kwiinda muli zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(b) Palyamenti kwaambwa —

(i) Ng’anda Yaajulu Yamilawu, aawo mpukwaambwa milimu iichitwa ang’anda eeyi yamilawu ayilikke mbuuli mbukwaambwa muKkata Lyamilawu;

(ii) Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu, aawo mpukwaambwa milimu iichitwa ang’anda eeyi yamulawu iteensi yeeyo iwambidwe mu mwaneefuka

(1) naakuti mumulawu umwi wapangwa;

(c) Ng’anda Yaansi nga kwaambwa Ng’anda Yaansi Yamilawu;

(d) …

(e) … [Tufuka (d) a (e ) tusowegwa achibeela 24 chaMulawu 2 wa2021]

(f) Zyamajele kwaambwa Zyamajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange;

(g) Kkomishini yaZyamajele kwaambwa Kkomishini yaZyamajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange;

(h) Nabbatule waZyamajele kwaambwa Nabbatule wazyaMajele aKuzyotololwa Kwabaange;

(i) Sikulingula Mabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende aNabbatule Uulingula Mmabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende kwaambwa Nabbatule waKulingula Mmabelesezyo aMali yaFulumende

(j) Attorney General kwaambwa Mupati waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta.

(2) Aawo Kkata lyaMulawu eeli mpulipa nguzu kumuntu kuti naakuti kabunga kuti kapange milawu aatala azimwi zintu, kakuli zintu eezyo zilaambidwe kale mumilawu iiliwo kale yakachitwa abamwi bantu/tumwi tubunga, milawu iiliwo kale ijisi nguzu zikozyania amilawu yakaanzwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu pya eeli. Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti 12. Nzila Zyameendelezyo aPalyamenti zyakaliwo kale kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli yamuNg’anda Yaajulu aNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu kusikila zyabambululwa naakuti kugwisigwa kwiindila muKkata Lyamilawu eeli. Babelesi bajanika muzyuuno

13\. Fumbwa muntu wakajisi chuuno mumulimu wafulumende, kalitana talika pe kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, uyanembo kajisi chuuno chakwe naakuti kubelesya limbi zina lyawofesi lyakabumbwa amulawu mupya pesi kalyelene awofesi yakwe yansiku. Mufolo alumwi lugwasyo lupegwa babelesi aamulimu zinookozyania anzyaakali kujana kale kusikila bulamfu bwakukkala muwofesi eeyo bwasika mpubumana, kusikila wasiya mulimu, naakuti kwaandwa mulimu. Zyaambo zyaatala ansaluuzyo antoomwe abulamfu bwachiindi Muzulwidi waNyika nchayelede kukkala muwofesi 14. (1) Katutalangisisyi zyaambidwe muchibeela 92, munsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna kuzwa kutalika kwakubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli, antoomwe amunsaluuzyo zitobela zyakusala Muzulwidi waNyika mukati kakkumi lyaminyaka kuzwa kunsaluuzyo zyakutaanguna, basikuvootelwa kuba Muzulwidi waNyika tabeelede kusala uumwi muntu kuti avootelwe kuba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika.

(2) Achakufwambaana, muntu wasalwa kuba Muzulwidi waNyika munsaluuzyo zyaambidwe mumwaneefuka (1) uyelede kugala bantu batayindi bobile pe kuti babe Batobeli baMuzulwidi waNyika, bakkala muzyuuno eezi kwiinda muluse lwaMuzulwidi waNyika.

(3) Aawo—

(a) uumwi Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika mpaagalwa kwiinda mumwaneefuka (2), ooyo muntu uba Mutobeli waMuzulwidi waNyika Mutaanzi kakutobelwa zyaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu eeli;

(b) Babili Batobeli baMuzulwidi waNyika mpubagalwa kwiinda mumwaneefuka (2), Muzulwidi waNyika ukonzya kuti kuzwa chiindi achiindi wagala umwi kuti abe Simujatila Chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika aawo Muzulwidi waNyika natawo munyika naakuti natachikonzyi kuchita milimu yawofesi yakwe kwiinda mukuchiswa azimwi zinga zilachitika mubuumi.

(4) Katutalangisisyi zyaambidwe muchibeela 101 pesi katulanga zili mubanabefuka (5) a (6), kuti muntu wakasalidwe kuba Muzulwidi waNyika munsaluuzyo zyaambidwe mumwaneefuka (1), wafwa, wasiya chuuno naakuti wagwisigwa achuuno—

(a) Uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti aawo mpukuli Batobela Muzulwidi waNyika babili, ooyo Uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika wakamaninsizya kugalwa kuti ajane bujatila chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika kwiinda mumwaneefuka (3(b), nguwe ujana bujatila chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika kusikila Muzulwidi waNyika mupya watalika mulimu mbuuli mbukwaambidwe mumwaneefuka

(5); kabiyo

(b) Chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika chasyaala kachitakwe muntu cheelede kupegwa uumbi muntu wagalwa kuzwa kukabunga kakweendelezya zyanyika kakalaaMuzulwidi waNyika wakafwa, wakasiya naakuti wakazwa achuuno.

(5) Kabunga kakweendelezya zyanyika keelede kugala muntu utanjile muchuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika kwiinda mumwaneefuka (4)(b), keelede kuzibisya Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu izina lyamuntu ngukagala mukati kamazuba makumi musanu amakumi aane (90 days) kuzwa buunsi chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika nichasyaala kachitachikwe muntu. Mpawo muntu wagalwa utalika mulimu naamana kuchita chikonke chawofesi eeyi kwiinda muchibeela 94. Sikunjila muchuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika uyelede kuchita chikonke chawofesi eeyo mukati kamahola makumi aane alaamusanu aatutatu (48) naakuti kakutana yinda mazuba obile kuzwa nikwakazibisigwa zina lyamuntu ooyo Sichuuno waNg’anda Yaansi Yamilawu.

(6) Kuti muntu wali Muzulwidi waNyika wakasalidwe kwiinda mumwaneefuka wafwa, wasiya naakuti waandwa achuuno, kakuli muntu ooyo taakalikwe kabunga kakweendelezya zyanyika nkaakanjide, Uutobela Muzulwidi waNyika naakuti kabali babili ooyo wakamaninsizya kujatila chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika kwiinda mumwaneefuka

(3)(b), nguwe uutola chuuno chaMuzulwidi waNyika. Kweendelela nembo kwamawofesi amwi 15. Katutalangi zimwi zipanzi zyaKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi, ma wofesi aatobela aakaliwo kale buunsi lyakudindwa kwaKkata lyaMulawu kwiinda muMpampikwa Yamusanu Atutatu ijanika muKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi, kaali ngaaya—

(a) Muzulwidi waNyika aWutobela Muzulwidi waNyika;

(b) Nabbatule waBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende awutobela Nabbatule waBasilutwe baMitabi yaFulumende; kabiyo

(c) Silutwe waMutabi waFulumende aWumutobela; Beendelela nembo kabaliwo kusikila buunsi Muzulwidi waNyika mutaanzi aansi kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli watalika mulimu wakwe kabiyo aabo bakajisi zyuuno zyaambwa aajulu balasyaala kabali muzyuuno zyabo mbuuli mbukweelede. Mukwabilili waBulumbu 16. (1) Mulawu waMukwabilili waBulumbu [Chaandano 10:18]) wagwisigwa akusweegwa.

(2) Fumbwa zyaambo zyakali kujatwa aMukwabilili waBulumbu kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli zyeelede kutolwa kuKkomishini yaZyeelelo Zyabantu muZimbabwe kuti zikamaninsigwe nkukooko. Kulonzegwa kwamali iili muNkomoMpati Yafulumende yachiindi 17. Imali zyakeelede kubikkwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende, yakabikkidwe kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi, iyelede kubikkwa muNkomo Mpati yaFulumende yakaanzwa kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu eeli. Nkuta ameendelezyo azyamulawu 18. (1) Mufuka eeli — “ kaambo katanamana kalaachakuchita aKkata lyaMulawu” zyaamba—

(a) kaambo kalaachakuchita aatala akutyolwa kwazyeelelo zyaampandamunsaka mbuuli mbukwaambidwe muKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi; naakuti

(b) fumbwa kaambo kalaachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu mbuuli mbukupandulwidwe muchibeela 332 chaKkata lyaMulawu eeli pya; kakuli buunsi nilyakadindwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli kaambo aako kakachili muNkuta Yaajulujulu yaZimbabwe yakaanzidwe kwiinda muKkata lyaMulawu Lyachiindi.

(2) Katutalangi zili muchibeela 166, kwaminyaka musanu ayibili kuzwa buunsi nilyadindwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, iNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu iijisi bantu aaba —

(a) Duundumuntule waBubetesi aWumutobela; kabiyo

(b) maJaji aali musanu awabili bakuNkuta Yaajulujulu; beelede kukkala kabali boonse kuti baswiilizye kaambo keetwa muNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu.

(3) Kuti chuuno chawumwi Jaji wakuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu chasyaala kachitakwe muntu mukati kaminyaka musanu ayibili kuzwa buunsi

nilyakadindwa Kkata lyaMulawu, chuuno eecho cheelede kuzuzikizigwa kwiinda mukunjizya uumbi Jaji, naakuti kubikka Jaji uujatilide chuuno eecho pesi Jaji ooyo kazwa kuNkuta Yaajulujulu. Pesi kuti chuno chaUutobela Dundumuntule waBubetesi chayanda muntu, muntu ooyo uyelede kunjizigwa kakutobelezegwa chibeela 180(2)(3). Naa kakuli kuti chuuno chaMutobeli waDundumuntuule waBabetesi chaaseme kwachiindi eecho chizopegwa muntu mukwiinda mukutobela chibeela 180 (2) a(3) [Kabeela kakasekkelwa akabeela 8 kaMulawu 10 wa2017]

(4) Kusikila kwalembwa zyaambo zisiyene kwiinda muMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti—

(a) miteto ikonzya kupangwa kwiinda muMulawu waNkuta Mpati [Chaandano 7:13] kuti miteto eeyo igwasizye kuubya meendelezyo azintu muNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu;

(b) milawu yaNkuta Yaajulujulu ilabeleka, nikuba kuti masandu akonzya kuchitwa aayelede, mumeendelezyo azintu muNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kuzyaambo zitavumbidwe amiteto yachitwa kwiinda mumwaneefuka (a); pesi milawu eeyo iyelede kubeleka muNkuta yazyaKkuta lyaMulawu, iyelede kweendelana azyaambidwe muchibeela 85 aChaandaano 8.

(5) Nkuta Yaajulujulu yaZimbabwe, Nkuta Yaajulu yaZimbabwe, Nkuta Yambelekelano, aNkuta Yanjendelezyo, mbuuli mbuzyakabede muchiindi kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, zyakabambululwa boobu Nkuta Yaajulujulu (Sureme Court), Nkuta Yaajulu, Nkuta Yambelekelano (Labour Court), Nkuta Yanjendelezyo aansi kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli. Pesi fumbwa mizeezo njiyakapa kumilandu atwaambo musule kwakutalika kubeleka kwaKkata lyaMulawu eeli mizeezo eeyo ichibeleka kabiyo ichilaamunse.

(6) Woonse muntu wakali kubeteka milandu muNkuta Yambelekelano amuNkuta Yanjendelezyo kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli waba Jaji waNkuta Yambelekelano naakuti Nkuta Yanjendelezyo uujana mufolo azimwi zipegwa babelesi zyeelene aJaji waNkuta Yaajulu. Kabiyo bulamfu bwakukkala muwofesi yamulimu kali Muzulwidi waNkuta Yambelekelano naakuti Nkuta Yanjendelezyo kabukozyania aboobo bwaJaji wakuNkuta Yaajulu.

(7) Inkuta zyaba Mejasitiliti, nkuta zyabaami azimwi nkuta zyakapangwa kwiinda mumulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, zyeelede zyeendelela nembo kazibeleka mbuuli zyakabikkwa aMulawu upangwa aPalyamenti kwiinda muchibeela 174, kabiyo zyakaambwa ankuta eezi kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli zichibeleka kabiyo zichilaamunse.

(8) Fumbwa lyaMulawu—

kaambo

katalina

maninsigwa

keendelana

azyaKkata

(a) kakatana kubetekwa buunsi kalitana dindwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli, kaambo aako ngakatolwe kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu yakabumbwa kwiinda mumwaneefuka (2);

(b) kuti kakuli kaambo kakali katalika kubetekwa buunsi nilyakadindwa Kkata lyaMulawu, kaambo aako keelede kutolwa kuNkuta Yaajulujulu, aanze kwakuti aabo beni twaambo bazuminana kukatola kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu yakabambwa kwiinda mumwaneefuka (2), nkaako kuti kwaboobo Nkuta Yaajulujulu ilakatola kuNkuta yazyaKkata lyaMulawu kaambo aako.

(9) Yoonse milandu, aanze kwayeeyo ilaachakuchita azyaKkata lyaMulawu, yakatalina maninsigwa munkuta buunsi nilyakatalina dindwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli ikonzya kwiinkilila nembo kilookubetekwa munkuta eezyo naakuti azimwi zyeelene

nkuta zyakabikkwa aKkata lyaMulawu pya eeli kakunga yakali yatalika kubetekwa milandu eeyo, pesi—

(a) meendelezyo atwaambo/milandu eeyo ayelede kuba ngawo akali kubelesegwa kale kalitana dindwa Kkata lyaMulawu eeli; kabiyo

(b) meendelezyo amilandu/twaambo aawambwa mumwaneefuka (a) alabeleka kumilandu ilaachakuchita azilembedwe muChaandaano 4 chaKkata lyaMulawu eeli pya.

(10) Kuchitila zyaambidwe mumwaneefuka (9)—

(a) mulandu ulaachakuchita azya criminal case uwambwa kuti wakali watalika kubetekwa kuti kakuli sikutamizigwa wakali watalika kuzumina mulandu wakwe;

(b) mumulandu muniini uwambwa kuti wakali watalika kubetekwa kuti kakuli mapepa akwiita sikusunda kunkuta akali atumizigwa kuli nguwe, naakuti mulandu wakali waba mumalembe eenkuta. Zyeendelana aNabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta 19. (1) Fumbwa zyakaambwa akuchita a Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu kuNkuta musule kwabuunsi katlitanatalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli aatala amilandu yaMipati zitolwa kuti zyakachitwa kazichitilwa awofesi mpya yaNabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu Munkuta.

(2)Muntu wakajisi chuuno chaDundumuntuule waBasikusosela Milandu kuNkuta musule kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu uyendelela nembo muwofesi kali waba Nabbatule waBasikusosela Milandu muNkuta kuzwa buunsi mbolitalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMualwu. Zyeelelo zyabantu kuli zyaNdyabukkede 20. Cheelelo chakujana penshini chakaliwo kale kalitana talika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu kabiyo kabiyo kachili cheelelo eecho chakali kwabililidwe aKkata lyaMulawu lyachiindi, eecho cheelelo cheelede kuya nembo kachizuzikizigwa kuzwa buunsi nilitalika kubeleka Kkata lyaMulawu eeli
